145		kāṇmi ṉōveṉak kaṇṭuniṟ kunarum
virāṭaṉ pērūr vicayaṉām pēṭiyik
kāṇiya cūḻnta kampali mākkaḷiṉ
maṇimē kalitaṉi vantupuṟañ cuṟṟi
aṇiyami tōṟṟat taruntavap paṭuttiya
150		tāyō koṭiyaḷ takavilaḷ īṅkivaḷ
māmalar koyya malarvaṉan tāṉpukiṉ
nalliḷa vaṉṉam nāṇā tāṅkuḷa
valluna kollō maṭanti taṉṉaṭi
māmayi lāṅkuḷa vantumuṉ niṟpaṉa
155		cāyalkaṟ paṉakolō tiyal taṉṉuṭaṉ
piṅkiḷi tāmuḷa pāvitaṉ kiḷavik
keñcala kollō iciyuna valla
eṉ ṟivi colli yāvarum iṉintukac
centaḷirc cēvaṭi nilamvaṭu vuṟāmal
160		kuravamum maravamuṅ kuruntuṅ koṉṟiyum
tilakamum vakuḷamuṅ ceṅkāl veṭciyum
narantamum nākamum parantalar puṉṉiyum
piṭavamun taḷavamum muṭamuṭ ṭāḻiyum
kuṭacamum vetiramuṅ koḻuṅkā lacōkamum
165		ceruntiyum vēṅkiyum peruñcaṇ pakamum
erimala rilavamum virimalar parappi
vittaka riyaṟṟiya viḷaṅkiya kiviṉic
cittirac ceykip paṭāmpōrt tatuvē
oppat tōṉṟiya uvavaṉan taṉṉit
170		toḻutaṉaḷ kāṭṭiya cutamati taṉṉoṭu
malarkoyyap pukuntaṉaḷ maṇimē kaliyeṉ.
uri  
1-4.		vayanta mālikku mātavi uritta - vayanta māliyiṉiṭam mātavi kūṟiya, uyaṅku nōy varuttattu urimuṉ tōṉṟi - piṟarum varuntutaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya mikka tuṉpantarum moḻi yiṉiṭamāka utittu, māmalar nāṟṟampōl-ciṟanta malariṭattu nāṟṟampōl, maṇimēkalikku ētu nikaḻcci etirntuḷatu ātaliṉ-maṇimēkalikku ētu nikaḻcci etirppaṭṭatātalāl; 
uritta uri yeṉka. ētu nikaḻcci-payaṉukku ētuvākiya viṉiyiṉ nikaḻcci; viṉi payaṉkoṭuttaṟkut tōṉṟuvatu eṉṟa paṭi; 1"ētu nikaḻcci yīṅkiṉ ṟātaliṉ" 2"ētu nikaḻcci yāvum palavuḷa" eṉa innūluḷḷē piṉṉarum ippeyar payiṉṟu varukiṉṟatu. māmalar nāṟṟampōl eṉṟa uvamiyāl nalviṉi payaṉaḷittaṟ ketirntuḷa teṉpatu peṟṟām. malariṉkaṇ nāṟṟam aṭaṅkiyiruntu piṉ veḷippaṭutal pōl kaṉmamum maṟintiruntu payaṉaḷikkuṅ kālam vantuḻi veḷippaṭu meṉka.
 5-10.	tantiyum tāyum tām naṉi uḻanta - taṉ tanti tāyarāṉa kōvalaṉum kaṇṇakiyum uṟṟu mika varuntiya, ventuyar iṭumpi ceviyakam vetuppa-koṭiya tuyariṉi viḷikkum tuṉpa moḻikaḷ kātiṉuḷḷē cuṭa, kātal neñcam kalaṅkik kāriki - maṇimēkali avarpāl aṉpu mikka uḷḷaṅ kalakka muṟṟu, mātar ceṅkaṇ vari vaṉappu aḻittu - aḻakiya civanta kaṇkaḷil mi eḻutiṉa aḻakiṉic citittu, pulampu nīr uruṭṭi-varuttattā luṇṭākiya kaṇṇīri utirttu, poti aviḻ naṟumalar ilaṅku itaḻ māliyi iṭṭu nīrāṭṭa - kaṭṭa viḻntu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya naṟiya malar māliyiṉ mītu vīḻtti ataṉi nīrāṭṭa; 
iṭumpi-mātavi kūṟiya tuṉpa moḻikaḷ, mātar-kātal; īṇṭu aḻaku. vari-miyeḻutiya kīṟṟu; 1"varivaṉap puṟṟa" eṉpatu puṟam. pulampunīr-taṉittu vīḻkiṉṟa nīrt tuḷiyumām. nīrāṭṭa - naṉikka eṉṟapaṭi.
11-17.	mātavi maṇimēkali mukam nōkki-mātavi maṇimēkaliyiṉ mukattiṉip pārttu, tāmari taṇmati cērntatupōlak kāmar ceṅkiyil kaṇṇīr māṟṟi-centāmarimalar kuḷircci poruntiya muḻumatiyic cērntatupōlat taṉ viruppam poruntiya civantakiyiṉāl maṇimēkaliyiṉ kaṇṇiṉiṉ ṟoḻukum nīrit tuṭittu, tūnīr māli tūttaki iḻantatu - tūya nīrmiyiyuṭiya naṟumalar māli kaṇṇīrāl naṉintu tūya taṉmiyi iḻantatu ākaliṉ, nikarmalar nīyē koṇarvāy eṉṟalum-vēṟu māli toṭuttaṟku oḷi poruntiya malarkaḷi nīyē ceṉṟu koṇṭuvaruka eṉa urittalum, matumalark kuḻaliyoṭu māmalar toṭukkum-tēṉporuntiya malar kaḷi yaṇinta kūntaliyuṭiya maṇimēkaliyuṭaṉ malar toṭuttuk koṇṭirukkum, cutamati kēṭṭut tuyaroṭum kūṟum - cutamati yeṉpavaḷ ataṉik kēṭṭut tuyaruṭaṉ kūṟukiṉṟāḷ; 
mātavi nōkki, māṟṟi, koṇarvāy eṉṟalum, cutamati kēṭṭuk kūṟumeṉka.  pōla - pōlumpaṭi, tāmari malar mātavi kikkum, mati maṇimēkali mukattiṟkum uvami. kāmar - viruppam; uṭiyāḷatu viruppam kiyiṉmēl ēṟṟappaṭṭatu; aḻakumām. nikar-oḷi; 2"nīrvār nikar malar" 3“arumpaviḻ mulli  nikar malar" eṉpaṉa kāṇka. nīyē koṇarvāy eṉṟatu kaḻuvāy kūṟuvatu pōṉṟu avaḷatu tuyariṉi māṟṟuvatō rupāyaṅ karutiyām.
 18-25.	kuravarkku uṟṟa koṭun tuyar kēṭṭu-tāy tantiyarkku nērnta koṭiya tuṉpattiṉik kēṭṭu, taṇiyāt tuṉpam talittali eytum-āṟāt tuyariṉi mēṉmēl aṭiyum, maṇimēkali taṉ matimukam taṉṉuḷ-maṇimēkaliyiṉatu matipōlum mukattiṉuḷ, aṇitikaḻ nīlattu āymalar ōṭṭiya-aḻaku viḷaṅkukiṉṟa melliya nīlamalari veṉṟa, kaṭimaṇi ukunīr kaṇṭaṉaṉ āyiṉ - kaṇṇiṉatu karumaṇiyiṉ kaṭiyiṉiṉṟu cintukiṉṟa nīrik kaṇṭaṉaṉāyiṉ, paṭiiṭṭu naṭuṅkum kāmaṉ-kāmaṉ taṉ paṭiyiṉi eṟintu naṭuṅkuvaṉ, pāviyi āṭavar kaṇṭāl akaṟalum uṇṭō - pāviyaṉiyāḷi āṭavar kāṇiṉ viṭṭu nīṅkutalum uṇṭō?, pēṭiyar aṉṟō peṟṟiyiṉ niṉṟiṭiṉ - aṅṅaṉam tam iyaṟki tiriyāmal niṟparēl avartām pēṭiyar allarō; 
 āy-ārāynteṭutta eṉṟumām. 1"āviṉ kaṭimaṇi yukunīr" eṉpatu īṇṭu aṟiyaṟpālatu. avaḷāṟ pakivellak karutiyirunta kāmaṉ iṉi vellutalariteṉṟu paṭiyi eṟintu naṭuṅkuvā ṉeṉka. uṇṭō, ō ; etirmaṟi. peṟṟi - iyalpu. peṟṟiyil niṟṟalāvatu - ivaḷi virumpātu niṟṟal. āṭavar eṉṟatu īṇṭu iṉpattuṟi niṟpārik karutiṟṟu. pēṭiyar - peṇṇiṉpam tuykkun takutiyillātavar.
 26-27.	āṅṅaṉam aṉṟiyum - aḵtaṉṟiyum, aṇiyiḻi kēḷāy īṅku innakarattu yāṉ varuṅ kāraṇam - innakarattiṉ kaṇ yāṉ vanta kāraṇatti mātavi kēṭpāyāka;
āṅṅaṉam: cuṭṭu. īṅku: aci.
 28-32.	pārāvāram palvaḷam paḻuniya-kaṭal tarum pala vaḷaṅ kaḷum mutirnta, kārāḷar caṇpiyil - kārāḷaruṭiya caṇpi nakarattil, kucikaṉ eṉpōṉ iru piṟappāḷaṉ oru makaḷ uḷḷēṉ-kucikaṉ eṉṉum antaṇaṉukku orē makaḷāyuḷḷayāṉ, oru taṉi añcēṉ ōrā neñcamōṭu - taṉṉantaṉiyē celvataṉi añcāmal oṉṟiyum ārāyāta uḷḷattōṭu, ārāmattiṭi alar koyvēṉtaṉi-upa vaṉattilē malar koyvēṉāyiṉēṉ; aṅṅaṉam koyta eṉṉi; 
pārāvārap palvaḷam eṉpatu melintu niṉṟatu. kārāḷar-ōr cātiyār; vēḷāḷarumām. caṇpi - oru nakaram; cīkāḻiyumām.
33-41. 	mārutavēkaṉ eṉpāṉ ōr viñciyaṉ - mārutavēkaṉ eṉṉum oru vittiyātaraṉ, tiru viḻi mūtūr tēvarkōṟku eṭutta - tirumakaḷum virumpum ik kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam intiraṉukkuc ceyyaluṟṟa, peruviḻāk kāṇum peṟṟiyiṉ varuvōṉ - periya viḻāviṉik kāṇum niṉivuṭaṉ varukiṉṟavaṉākiya, tāraṉ māliyaṉ tamaṉiyap pūṇiṉaṉ pārōr kāṇāp palar toḻu paṭimiyaṉ - pūmāliyi yuṭiyavaṉum maṇimāliyiyuṭiyavaṉum poṟpūṇiṉi yuṭiyavaṉum pūmiyiluḷḷōr, kāṇalākāta palarun toḻum teyva vaṭiviṉi yuṭiyavaṉumākiya avaṉ, eṭuttaṉaṉ eṟkoṇṭu eḻuntaṉaṉ vicumpil paṭuttaṉaṉ - eṉṉi eṭuttuk koṇṭu vicumpil eḻuntu akap paṭuttik koṇṭāṉ; āṅku avaṉ pāṉmiyēṉ āyiṉēṉ - appoḻutu yāṉ avaṉuṭiya iṉpattiṉ pakutiyi yuṭiyēṉāyiṉēṉ; āṅkavaṉ īṅku eṉi akaṉṟu kaṇmāṟi nīṅkiṉaṉ taṉpati neṭṭiṭi āyiṉum-avviñciyaṉ taṉ ūr cēymikkaṇ uḷḷatāṉālum viḻitta kaṇ imikkum aḷavilē eṉṉi innakaril viṭṭu maṟintu nīṅkiṉaṉ;
tāraṉ māliyaṉ pūṇiṉaṉ paṭimiyaṉāy varuvōṉākiya viñciyaṉ eṉṟiyikka. 1"tāraṉ ......paṭimiyaṉ," eṉṉum ivviraṇṭaṭiyum cilappatikārattum vantuḷḷami aṟika. kāṇāp paṭimiyaṉ palartoḻu paṭimiyaṉ eṉka. kaṇmāṟi - kaṇṇōṭṭamiṉṟi eṉṟumām.
 42-43.	maṇippūṅ kompar maṇimēkalitāṉ - māṇikkap pūṅkompaṉiya maṇimēkali, taṉittu alarkoyyum takimiyaḷ allaḷ - taṉiyē ceṉṟu malar koyyum takuti vāyntava ḷallaḷ;
nīṅkiṉaṉ; makaḷirkku iṅṅaṉam iṭukkaṇ nikaḻvatu uṇṭākaliṉtaṉit talar koyyun takimiya ḷallaḷ eṉa viritturikka.
 44-46.	paṉmalar aṭukkiya naṉmarap pantar - pala malarkaḷi niritta nalla maraniḻaliyuṭiya, ilavantikiyiṉ eyiṟpuṟam pōkiṉ - ilavantikiyiṉ matiṟpuṟattē ceṉṟāl, ulaka maṉṉavaṉ uḻiyōr āṅkuḷar - cōḻa maṉṉaṉatu maruṅkiluḷḷōr āṇṭuṟivar ; 
marap pantar - maraṅkaḷākiya pantar eṉṟum, cōli eṉṟumām. ilavantiki - iyantira vāvi; 1"niṟikkuṟi ṉiṟintu pōkkuṟiṟ pōkum, poṟippaṭi yaminta poṅkila vantiki," eṉpatu kāṇka, 2"ilavantiki-nīrāviyic cūḻnta vayantac cōli; aḵtu aracaṉum urimiyumāṭum kāvaṟcōli," eṉpar aṭiyārkku nallār, uḻiyōr-urimi makaḷir; kāvalāḷarumām. uḻiyōr āṅkuḷar eṉṟatu ataṉkaṭ pōkalākātu eṉṟapaṭiyām.
 47-52.	viṇṇavar kōmāṉ viḻākkoḷ nalnāḷ - intiraṉukku viḻācceyyum nalla nāṭkaḷil, maṇṇavar viḻiyār vāṉavar allatu - tēvariyaṉṟi makkaḷ virumpār ātaliṉ, pāṭu vaṇṭu imirā-pāṭukiṉṟa vaṇṭukaḷ olikkātaṉavāy, paṉmaram yāviyum -āṇṭuḷḷa pala maraṅkaḷum, vāṭā māmalar mālikaḷ tūkkaliṉ - perumi poruntiya vāṭāta pūmālikaḷit toṅka viṭutaliṉāl, kipey pācattup pūtam kākkum eṉṟu - kiyiṉiṭattuk koṇṭa pācattiṉiyuṭiya pūtam kākkumeṉṟu, uyyāṉattiṭi uṇarntōr cellār - uyyāṉattiṉkaṇ aṟintōr cellamāṭṭārkaḷ;
vāṉavar viḻitalaṉṟi maṇṇavar viḻiyār eṉka. vāṉavar virumputalāl vaṇṭu imirāvāyiṉa; 3"curumpu mūcāc cuṭarppūṅkāntaḷ" eṉpatu kāṇka. vāṭāta malar mālikaḷ tēvarkaḷāl nirumikkappaṭṭaṉa. mālikaḷ tūkkaliṉ pūtaṅ kākkumeṉṟu karutuvārāvar. uyyāṉam - uttiyāṉam ; aracar viḷiyāṭuñcōli.
53-58. veṅkatir vemmiyiṉ viriciṟi iḻanta-ñāyiṟṟiṉ katir veppattāl virinta ciṟakiṉi iḻanta, campāti irunta campāti vaṉamum-campāti eṉpavaṉ irunta campāti vaṉamum, tavā nīrk kāvirip pāvi taṉ tāti - aḻiyāta nīrpperuk kiṉiyuṭiya kāviri naṅkiyiṉ tantiyākiya, kavēraṉ āṅku irunta kavēra vaṉamum - kavēraṉ eṉapavaṉ irunta kavēra vaṉamum, mūppuṭi mutumiya tākkaṇaṅku uṭiya - mikka mutumiyuṭi yaṉavākiya tīṇṭi varuttumpeṇ teyvaṅkaḷi uṭiyaṉavātaliṉ, yāppuṭittāka aṟintōr eytār - aṟivuṭiyōr avaṟṟi uṟutiyuṭiyavākak karuti avaṟṟiṉkaṭ cellār;
campāti - kaḻukaracaṉ ; caṭāyuviṉuṭaṉ piṟantavaṉ; ivaṉ vāṉilē paṟantu ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilattiṉ aṇimiyiṟ ceṉṟa kāli ñāyiṟṟiṉ vemmiyāṟ ciṟi tīyappeṟṟaṉaṉ eṉpar. campāti, caṭāyu eṉṉum iruvarum iṟivaṉip pūcitta iṭam tiruppuḷ ḷirukkuvēḷūr eṉṉum patiyākum; 1"taḷḷāya campāti caṭāyeṉ pār tāmiruvar, puḷḷāṉārk kariyariṭam puḷḷirukku vēḷūrē," eṉpatu kāṇka. pukār nakaram appatikku aṇittākaliṉ campāti āṇṭu vantiruttal iyalpēyākum. vēttu muṉiyākiya kavēraṉ eṉpavaṉ piramaṉik kuṟittu aruntavam purintu avaṉaruḷāl viṭṭuṇu māyiyip putalviyākap peṟṟu mutti yeytiṉaṉeṉṟum, piṉpu  akkaṉṉi piramaṉ kaṭṭaḷiyāl nati vaṭivuṟṟamiyāl annati kāvēriyeṉap peyar peṟṟateṉṟum purāṇaṅ kūṟumeṉpar. yāppuṭittāka-iḻukkillaṉavāka; yāppuṭittāka aṇaṅ kuṭiya eṉṟiyittalumām.
 59-62.	aruḷum aṉpum āruyir ōmpum oru perum pūṭkiyum oḻiyā nōṉpiṉ - karuṇiyum nēyamum āruyirkaḷip pātukākkum oppaṟṟa periya mēṟkōḷum nīṅkāta nōṉ piṉiyuṭiya, pakavaṉatu āṇiyiṉ palmarampūkkum uvavaṉam eṉpatu oṉṟu uṇṭu - putta tēvaṉatu āṇiyāl pala maraṅkaḷum iṭiyaṟātu pūkkiṉṟa uvavaṉam eṉṉum peyaruḷḷa cōli oṉṟuṇṭu; 
aruḷ - aṉpu kāraṇamākat tōṉṟum aḷi. aṉpu - aruṭku mutalāki maṉattil nikaḻum nēyam. pūṭki - mēṟkōḷ. 
 62-66.	ataṉ uḷḷatu-accōliyiṉ uḷḷiṭattatākiya, viḷippu aṟi pōkātu meypuṟattu iṭūum paḷikkaṟi maṇṭapam uṇṭu - ōci veḷiyē pōkāmal taṉṉic cārntāruṭampi maṭṭum puṟattē tōṟṟuvikkum paḷikku maṇṭapam oṉṟu uṇṭu; ataṉ uḷḷatu - ammaṇṭapattiṉ uḷḷiṭattatākiya, tūniṟa māmaṇic cuṭaroḷi virinta tāmarippīṭiki tāṉ uṇṭu- tūya niṟamuṭiya māṇikkattiṉ mikka oḷiparanta patumapīṭam oṉṟuṇṭu;
ataṉ uḷḷatākiya maṇṭapam eṉṟum, ataṉ uḷḷatākiya pīṭiki eṉṟum oṭṭuka. aṟi pōtal-veḷiyē pōtal. taṉṉuḷ eytiṉavaratu ōciyi veḷippaṭuttātu, uruviṉi maṭṭum veḷip paṭuttuvateṉṟu paḷiṅku maṇṭapattiṉ iyalpu kūṟiyapaṭi. paḷikkaṟi maṇṭapam-paḷiṅkaṟiyākiya maṇṭapam; paḷiṅkup pāṟiyālākiya maṇṭapamumām. tāmarip pīṭiki-puttaṉ pātapīṭam. "puttaṉ pātatti maṇipatmam eṉṟalum, ataṉi vaḻipaṭuvōr atupaṟṟi, ‘ōm maṇipatmē hum' eṉṟa mantiratti japittalum putta camaya marapu," eṉpar.
66-72.	āṅku iṭiṉ arumpu aviḻ ceyyum-appīṭattil iṭṭāl arumpukaḷ malarā niṟkum, alarntaṉa vāṭā curumpiṉam mūcā tol yāṇṭu kaḻiyiṉum-alarnta malarkaḷ pala āṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟālum vāṭamāṭṭā, avaṟṟiṉ kaṇ vaṇṭiṉaṅkaḷum moykkā, maṟantēṉ ataṉ tiṟam mātavi kēḷāy - mātavi ataṉ iyalpu oṉṟiṉi naṉku maṟantēṉ ippoḻutu ataṉik kēṭpāyāka, kaṭam pūṇṭu-kāṇikki celuttali mēṟkoṇṭu, ōr teyvam karuttiṭi vittōr - oru teyvatti maṉattilē vittu, āṅku avar aṭikku iṭiṉ avar aṭi tāṉ uṟum- appīṭattiṉkaṇ avaraṭiyiṉ poruṭṭu malari iṭṭāl ammalar atteyvattiṉaṭiyic ceṉṟu cērum; nīṅkātu yāṅkaṇum niṉip pilarāy iṭiṉ - niṉip poṉṟumiṉṟi iṭṭāl ammalar yāṇṭum cellātu avaṇ taṅkum;
tol yāṇṭu-pal yāṇṭu; kaḻinta āṇṭukaḷ toṉmiyavātaliṉ 'tol yāṇṭu', eṉṟār - tolyāṇṭu kaḻiyiṉum vāṭā mūcā veṉka. maṟantēṉ eṉṟatu ulaka vaḻakkup paṟṟi. kaṭam - kaṭaṉ; kāṇikki. vittōr ; muṟṟeccam. malari iṭiṉ eṉka.
 73-79. 	īṅku itaṉ kāraṇam eṉṉi eṉṟiyēl-itaṟkuriya kāraṇam yāteṉa viṉavutiyāyiṉ, cinti iṉṟiyum ceyviṉi uṟum eṉum-maṉattoṭu kūṭāta vaḻiyum ceytaviṉi piṉvantu payaṉṟarum eṉkiṉṟa, ventiṟal nōṉpikaḷ viḻumam koḷḷavum -valiyiṉiyuṭiya nōṉpikaḷ varuttaṅ koḷḷavum, ceyviṉi cinti iṉṟeṉil yāvatum eytātu eṉpōrkku ētuvākavum- maṉattoṭu kūṭāviṭiṉ ceyta viṉi ciṟitum vantu payaṉaḷiyātu eṉpōrkkuk karuviyākavum, payamkeḻu mā malar iṭṭuk kāṭṭa mayaṉ paṇṭu iḻitta marapiṉatu atutāṉ - malarkaḷi iṭṭuk kāṭṭumāṟu mayaṉ eṉ pōṉāl muṉṉarc ceyyappaṭṭa payaṉ ciṟanta muṟimiyi yuṭiyatākum attāmarip pīṭam;
payaṅkeḻu marapiṉatu eṉak kūṭṭuka. maṉattoṭu kūṭāvaḻi viṉi payaṉṟarā teṉpataṉi, 1"iṉṉā veṉattā ṇuṇarntavi tuṉṉāmi vēṇṭum" 2"maṉattāṉām māṇā ceyyāmi tali" eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷkaḷāṉum, avaṟṟiṟku muṟiyē, "aṟamum pāvamum uḷavāvatu maṉamuḷaṉāya vaḻiyākalāṉ "uṇarntavi eṉṟār" eṉavum, "īṇṭu maṉattā ṉākāta vaḻip pāvamilli yeṉpatu peṟṟām" eṉavum parimēlaḻakar kūṟiya uriyāṉum aṟika.
80-85	avvaṉam allatu-accōliyiṉkaṇallatu, aṇiyiḻi - mātaviyē, niṉmakaḷ cevvaṉam cellum cemmitāṉ ilaḷ- niṉmakaḷ vēṟākac cellum takutiyillātavaḷ; maṇimēkali yoṭu māmalar koyya - maṇimēkaliyuṭaṉ malar koyyumāṟu, aṇiyiḻi nallāy yāṉum pōval eṉṟu-aḻakiya aṇikalaṉiyuṭiya mātavi, yāṉum celvēṉ eṉṟu, aṇippūṅ kompar avaḷoṭum kūṭi - aḻakiya pūṅkompaṉiya maṇimēkaliyuṭaṉ cērntu, maṇittēr vītiyil cutamati celvuḻīi - maṇikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya tērkaḷ cellum vītiyiṉkaṇ cutamati cellumpoḻutu;
cevvaṉam - vēṟāka; 3"tirumaka ḷirukki cevvaṉaṅ kaḻintu" eṉpuḻi, cevvaṉam kaḻintu eṉapataṟku, vēṟākak kaḻintu eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār poruḷurittuḷḷami kāṇka; nērāka eṉṟumām. cemmi - īṇṭut takuti.
86-103. cimilik karaṇṭiyaṉ-uṟiyilē vitta kuṇṭikiyi yuṭiyavaṉum, nuḻikōl pirampiṉaṉ-nuṇṇitāyt tiraṇṭa pirampiṉi yuṭiyavaṉum, tavalarum ciṟappiṉ arāntāṇattu uḷōṉ - kēṭillāta mēṉmiyiyuṭiya arukaṉ kōṭṭattē yuḷḷavaṉum, nāṇamum uṭiyum naṉkaṉam nīttu - nāṇattiyum āṭiyiyum aṟavē akaṟṟi, kāṇā uyirkkum kiyaṟṟu ēṅki- kaṇkaḷāṟ kāṇa viyalāta ciṟṟuyirkkum naṭattalāti taṉ ṟoḻilkaḷāl tuṉpamuṇṭāmō eṉac ceyalaṟṟu ēṅki, uṇṇā nōṉ pōṭu - uṇṇā viratattuṭaṉ, uyaval yāṉiyiṉ maṇṇā mēṉiyaṉ varuvōṉ taṉṉi- varuntu taliyuṭiya yāṉiyip pōla varuvōṉumākiya kaḻuvāta uṭali yuṭiyavaṉi, vantīr aṭikaḷ num malaraṭi toḻutēṉ - vārum aṭikēḷ nummuṭiya malarpōlum tiruvaṭikaḷi vaṇaṅkiṉēṉ, entam aṭikaḷ emuri kēṇmō- emmuṭiya perumāṉē aṭiyēṉ moḻiyik kēḷum, aḻukkuṭi yākkiyil pukunta num uyir - aḻukkuc ceṟinta uṭaliṉkaṭ pukunta nummuṭiya uyirāṉatu, puḻukkaṟip paṭṭōr pōṉṟu uḷam varuntātu - puḻukkat tiṉit tarum aṟiyil akappaṭṭōrippōla uḷḷam varuntā vaṇṇam, immiyum maṟumiyum iṟutiil iṉpamum taṉ vayiṉ tarūum eṉ talimakaṉ urittatu-em talivarurittatākiya immiyiṉpattiṉiyum maṟumi yiṉpattiṉiyum muṭivillāta mutti yiṉpattiṉiyum taṉṉiṭattiruntu tarukiṉṟatāṉa, koliyum uṇṭō koḻumaṭal teṅkiṉ viḷipūn tēṟaliṉ - koḻuviya maṭalkaḷiyuṭiya teṉṉiyiṉ kaṇ viḷikiṉṟa iṉiya matuvil koliyeṉpatum uṇṭō?, meyt tavattīrē - uṇmit tavamuṭiya aṭikaḷē, uṇṭu teḷintu ivyōkattu uṟupayaṉ kaṇṭāl - itaṉi uṇṭu teḷivu peṟṟu it tavaneṟiyil itaṉiṉ mikka payaṉik kaṇṭāl, emmiyum kiyutirk koṇmeṉa - tēṟaliyaṉṟi emmiyum akaṟṟiviṭum eṉṟu kūṟi, uṇṇā nōṉpi taṉṉoṭum cūḷuṟṟu - uṇṇā nōṉpiyākiya ciṉa muṉivaruṭaṉ capataṅ kūṟi, uṇm eṉ irakkum ōr kaḷimakaṉ piṉṉarum - uṇṇum eṉṟu irakkiṉṟa oru kaṭkuṭiyaṉ piṉ niṟpōrum;
cimili-uṟi. karaṇṭi - karaṇṭakam; kuṇṭiki; 1"kalpoḷin taṉṉa viṭṭuvāyk karaṇṭip palpuric cimili nāṟṟi" eṉpatu kāṇka. arāntāṇam - arukattāṉam. vantīraṭikaḷ, entamaṭikaḷ, meyttavattīr eṉak kaḷimakaṉ palakāṟ kūṟuvatu ikaḻcci. maṇṇā mēṉiyaṉ ākaliṉ aḻukkuṭi yākki eṉṟāṉ. talimakaṉ - ācāṉ. koliyumuṇṭō eṉṟatu koli yoṉṟumē kaṭiyaṟpālateṉpataṉi uṭaṉpaṭṭapaṭi. teṅkiṉ viḷipūn tēṟal eṉṟatu tēṟaliṉ tūymi kūṟiyapaṭiyām. iv yōkattu - nummuṭiya tavattil. itaṉiṉum uṟupayaṉ eṉa virikka. iṉi, yōkam - tēṟaliṉ cērkki yeṉṟumām : itaṟku, kaṇṭāl akaṟṟum eṉṟatu kaṇṭapiṉ akaṟṟakillīr eṉṉum kuṟippiṟṟu. kiyutir koḷḷutal-kiyi.acittu vilakkutal; cīlamillārik kāṇiṉ avaruṭaṉ pēcātu avarip pōmpaṭi kiyacittuk kuṟippittal ciṉa muṉivarkku iyalpu. uṇṇā nōṉpi - iraṇṭuvāvum aṭṭamiyum paṭṭiṉi viṭṭuṇṇum virati; 1"paṭṭiṉi nōṉpikaḷ' eṉpataṉuriyum, 2"ōvā tiraṇṭuvavu maṭṭamiyum paṭṭiṉiviṭṭoḻukkaṅ kāttal, tāvāttava meṉṟār" eṉpatum kāṇka. karaṇṭiyaṉum pirampiṉṉum ākiya ārāntāṇattuḷḷōṉ nōṉpōṭu mēṉiyaṉāy nīttu ēṅki varupavaṉi eṉṟiyittulumām. taruvatum urittatumākiya tēṟalil koliyumuṇṭō eṉak kūṭṭuka. tarūum talimakaṉ eṉalumām. koḷḷum, uṇṇum eṉṉum ēval muṟṟukkaḷil īṟṟumici yukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭatu. peruṅ katiyiṟ kaḷimakaṉ iyalpākak kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷa, 3"tuṟakkam kūṭiṉun tuṟantiva ṇīṅkum, piṟappō vēṇṭēṉ yāṉeṉak kūṟi, ārtta yāya ṉūrkkaḷi mūrkkaṉ, cevvaḻik kītañ citiyap pāṭi, avvaḻi varumō rantaṇāḷaṉic, cella lāṇi nilliva ṇīyeṉa, eytac ceṉṟu vitavaṇ vilakki, vaḻuttiṉē muṇṇumiv vaṭinaṟun tēṟalip, paḻittuk kūṟu niṉ pārppaṉak kaṇamatu, col
lāyāyiṟ pulluveṉ yāṉeṉak, kiyalit tōṭumōr kaḷimakaṟ kāṇmiṉ" eṉpatu īṇṭu aṟiyaṟpālatu.
104-115.	kaṇaviri māli kaṭṭiya tiraṇiyaṉ-alarippūvāl tiraṇiyākak kaṭṭappaṭṭa māliyi yuṭiyaṉāy, kuvimukiḻ erukkil kōtta māliyaṉ-erukkiṉatu kuvinta mukikaḷāṟ kōkkap peṟṟa tāriṉi yuṭiyaṉāy, citaval tuṇiyoṭu cēṇōṅku neṭuñciṉit tatarvīḻpu oṭittuk kaṭṭiya uṭiyiṉaṉ-citariṉa tuṇiyōṭu vāṉilē uyarnta periya marak kiḷikaḷiluḷḷa ceṟinta cuḷḷikaḷi oṭittuk kaṭṭiya uṭiyi yuṭiyaṉāy, veṇpali cāntam meymuḻutu uṟīi-veṇṇīṟuñ cantaṉamum uṭal muḻutum pūcik koṇṭu, paṇpil kiḷavi palaroṭum urittāṅku - payaṉillāta coṟkaḷip palaroṭuṅ kūṟik koṇṭu, aḻūum viḻūum araṟṟum kūum-aḻutum viḻuntum araṟṟiyum kūviyum, toḻūum eḻūum cuḻalalum cuḻalum - toḻutum eḻuntum cuḻalutalic ceytum,. ōṭalum ōṭum - ōṭiyum, oruciṟi otuṅki nīṭalum nīṭum-oru pakkamāka otuṅki neṭitu niṉṟum, niḻaloṭu maṟalum-niḻaluṭaṉ pakimi koṇṭum niṟkiṉṟa, miyaluṟṟa makaṉpiṉ varuntik kiyaṟu tuṉpam kaṇṭu niṟkunarum- pittuṟṟavaṉatu ceyalaṟṟa tuṉpattiṉik kaṇṭu varunti avaṉ piṉṉē niṟpōrum; 
kaṭṭiya tiraḷ puyaṉ eṉpatum, mey muḻuturīi eṉpatum pāṭam. urīi-uruvi; pūci. tatar-kottumām; 1"citarnaṉi murukkiṉ cēṇōṅku neṭuñciṉit tatar" eṉpatum kāṇka. veṇpali-veṇ ṇīṟu; cāmpal. veṇpaliyākiya cāntameṉak koṇṭu valloṟṟu vikārattāṟ ṟokka teṉiṉumām. cuḻalalum cuḻalum-cuḻalutaliyum ceyyum; piṉvaruvaṉa vaṟṟiṟkum iṅṅaṉam poruḷ koḷka; 2"iyaṅkalu miyaṅku mayaṅkalu mayaṅkum" eṉpatūum atu. aḻūum mutaliyavaṟṟi viṉiyeccappaṭuttu, niṟkiṉṟa eṉa oru col virittu muṭikka; iṉi, miyaluṟṟa makaṉ eṉpataṉi eḻuvāyākki, aḻūum mutaliya viṉikaḷōṭu taṉittaṉi muṭittu, avaṉ eṉa oru col varuvitturittalumām.
116-125.	curiyal tāṭi maruḷpaṭu pūṅkuḻal - curuṇṭa tāṭiyum iruṇṭa aḻakiya kūntalum, pavaḷac cevvāy tavaḷa vāḷnaki-pavaḷam pōṉṟa civanta vāyum veṇmi mikka oḷḷiya paṟkaḷum, oḷ ari neṭuṅkaṇ-oḷiporuntiya aripaṭarnta periya kaṇkaḷum, veḷḷiveṇ tōṭṭu-veḷḷiya caṅkiṉāṟ ceyta kātaṇiyum, karuṅkoṭip puruvattu maruṅkuvaḷi piṟi nutal - kariyatāy vaḷinta puruvattiṉ pakkalil vaḷintuḷḷa piṟi pōṉṟa neṟṟiyum, kāntaḷ am ceṅki - kāntaḷ malar pōluñ civantakiyum, ēntu iḷa vaṉamuli - ēntiya aḻakiya iḷaṅ koṅkikaḷum, akaṉṟa alkul am nuṇ maruṅkul - periya alkulum aḻakiya nuṇṇiya iṭiyum, ikanta vaṭṭuṭi - muḻantāḷaḷavāka uṭukkappaṭum uṭiyum, eḻutuvarik kōlattu - tōḷ mutaliyavaṟṟil eḻutappaṭṭa pattik kīṟṟum uṭiya, vāṇaṉ pērūr maṟukiṭit tōṉṟi-vāṇaṉ eṉṉum acuraṉatu periya cōnakarattiṉ vītiyil niṉṟu, nīṇilam aḷantōṉ makaṉmuṉ āṭiya - nilamaḷanta neṭumāliṉ makaṉākiya kāmaṉ muṉṉar āṭiya, pēṭik kōlattup pēṭu kāṇkunarum- pēṭik kōlattiyuṭiya pēṭu eṉṉuṅ kūttiṉik kāṇpōrum:
curiyal - curuḷal; tāṭi - mōvāy mayir. maruḷ-iruḷ. koṭi oḻuṅkumām. puruvamum iraṇṭu pakkattum vaḷintu nutalum eṉṟurittalum poruntum. ikanta-tāḷ muḻuvatum illāta. tāṭi mutaliyavaṟṟiyuṭiya kōlam eṉavum, āṭiya pēṭik kōlam eṉavum oṭṭuka; aṉṟi, tāṭi mutaliya vaṟṟiyuṭiya pēṭik kōlattuṭaṉ āṭiya pēṭu eṉṟumām. muṉ āṭiya pēṭu ippoḻutu naṭikkappaṭuva taṉi yeṉka. pēṭu-patiṉōrāṭaluḷ oṉṟu;ap patiṉoṉṟiyum, avaṟṟi nikaḻttiyavarkaḷiyum.
"kaṭiyamayi rāṇimarak kālvinti kantaṉ, kuṭituṭimāl alliyamaṟ kumpam - cuṭar viḻiyāṟ, paṭṭamataṉ pēṭutirup pāviyaraṉ pāṇṭaraṅkam, koṭṭiyivi kāṇpatiṉōr kūttu" eṉṉum veṇpāvāṉaṟika. pēṭu - uḻi kāraṇamāka vāṇaṉāṟ ciṟivikkappaṭṭa taṉ makaṉ aniruttaṉic ciṟimīṭṭuk kāmaṉ cō nakarat tāṭiya āṭal; uḻi-vāṇaṉ makaḷ. 1"āṇmi tirinta peṇmik kōlattuk, kāma ṉāṭiya pēṭi yāṭalum" 2"curuḷiṭu tāṭi maruḷpaṭu pūṅkuḻal, ariparan toḻukiya ceḻuṅkaya ṉeṭuṅkaṇ, viriveṇṭōṭṭu veṇṇakit tuvarvāyc, cūṭaka varivaḷi yāṭamip paṉittōḷ, vaḷariḷa vaṉamulit taḷariyaṉ miṉṉiṭip, pāṭakac cīṟaṭi yāriyap pēṭi" eṉpaṉa aṟiyaṟpālaṉa.
 126-131.	vampamākkaḷ kampali mūtūr-putiyarāy varum makkaḷiṉ muḻakkam mikunta toṉmiyuṭiya pukār nakaril, cuṭumaṇ ōṅkiya neṭunili maṉitoṟum-ceṅkalāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭu uyarnta periya māṭaṅkaḷtōṟum, miyaṟu paṭivattu vāṉavar mutalā evvaki uyirkaḷum uvamam kāṭṭi- kuṟṟamaṟṟa teyva vaṭiviṉiyuṭiya viṇṇavar mutalāka evvakippaṭṭa uyirkaḷiyum oppumi kāṭṭi, veṇ cuti viḷakkattu vittakar iyaṟṟiya - viḷakkattiyuṭiya veḷḷiya cutiyiṉāl kitērnta ōviyakkārar ceyta, kaṇkavar ōviyam kaṇṭu niṟkunarum - kaṇkaḷik kavarum vaṉappuṭiya ōviyaṅkaḷik kaṇṭu niṟpōrum ;
cuṭumaṇ-ceṅkal; 3"cuṭuma ṇōṅkiya neṭunakar varippu" eṉpatu perumpāṇ. paṭivam - teyva vaṭivam. uvamam-oppumi; 4"evvakic ceytiyu muvamaṅ kāṭṭi, nuṇṇiti ṉuṇarnta nuḻinta nōkkiṟ, kaṇṇuḷ viṉiñarum," 1"mayaṉeṉak koppā vakutta pāviyiṉ, nīṅkē ṉiyāṉ" eṉpataṉa kāṇka. vittakar-ciṟpācāriyar; 2"vittaka riyaṟṟiya" eṉpar piṉṉum.
132-145.	viḻavu āṟṟuppaṭutta kaḻiperu vītiyil - viḻaviṉik koṇṭāṭiya mikapperiya vītiyil, poṉ nāṇ kōtta naṉ maṇik kōvi-poṉṉālākiya kayiṟṟiṟ kōtta nalla maṇik kōviyākiya, aiyavi appiya ney aṇi mucci-veṇ ciṟu kaṭukiṉi appiya ney yaṇinta ucciyil, mayirppuṟam cuṟṟiya kayiṟkaṭi mukkāḻ-mayirppuṟattiṟ cuṟṟappaṭṭa kokkiyiṉiyuṭiya mūṉṟu caraṅkaḷ, polam piṟic ceṉṉi nalampeṟat tāḻa - aḻakiya piṟi pōṉṟa aṇiyiṉi yaṇinta ceṉṉiyil aḻaku peṟat toṅkavum, cevvāyk kutali meypeṟā maḻali - vaṭivutiruntāta maḻaliyākiya iḷañ colli yuṭiya civanta vāyiṉiṉṟum, cintupu ciṉṉīr aimpaṭi naṉippa-ciṟitākiya nīrcinti mārpilaṇinta aimpaṭit tāliyi naṉikkavum, aṟṟam kāvāccuṟṟuṭip pūntukil - aṟṟattiṉi maṟik kāmaṟ cuṟṟappaṭṭa aḻakiya āṭi, toṭutta maṇikkōvi uṭuppoṭu tuyalvara - toṭukkappaṭṭa maṇikaḷiṉ kōviyākiya uṭuppoṭu aciyavum, taḷarnaṭi tāṅkāk kiḷarpūṇ putalvari-taḷarnta naṭiyiyum poṟukkalāta oḷi poruntiya aṇikalaṉ aṇinta putalvari, polantēr mīmicip pukarmuka vēḻattu- poṟṟēriṉ mītuḷḷa puḷḷi poruntiya mukattiṉi yuṭiya yāṉiyiṉ mēl, ilaṅkutoṭi nallār cilar niṉṟu ēṟṟi-viḷaṅkukiṉṟa vaḷiyalkaḷi yaṇinta makaḷir cilar niṉṟu ēṟṟi, āl amar celvaṉ makaṉ viḻāk kālkōḷ kāṇmiṉō eṉ-āliṉ kīḻamarnta civaperumāṉ tirumintaṉākiya murukavēḷiṉ viḻāk kāl koḷḷutalik kāṇpīrāka eṉṟu kūṟa, kaṇṭu niṟkunarum-ataṉik kaṇṭu niṟpōrum;
kōviyākiya mukkāḻ eṉka. pēy tīṇṭāvaṇṇam kuḻantikaḷiṉ muṭiyil neyyaṇintu veṇciṟu kaṭuki apputal marapu; ataṉālē veṇciṟu kaṭukiṟkuk kaṭippaki eṉpatum peyarāyiṟṟu; kaṭi-pēy. kayiṟkaṭi-kokki ; kokkiṉ vāypōlvatākaliṉ kokki eṉappaṭṭatu. piṟic ceṉṉi-piṟivaṭuvākiya aṇiyiṉ iru kōṭukaḷil eṉṟurip pārumuḷar. mey - colliṉ vaṭivu, kutali- iḷañcol; 1"kutalic cevvāyk kuṟuntoṭi makaḷir, mutiyōr moḻiyiṉ muṉṟi ṉiṉṟaḻa" eṉpatu kāṇka. ciṟitāya nīric ciṉṉīr eṉṟal ōr ilakkaṇi vaḻakku. aimpaṭi - kāttaṟ kaṭavuḷāṉa tirumāliṉ caṅku, cakkaram, kati, vāḷ, vil eṉṉum aintu paṭikaḷiṉ vaṭivākiya aṇi; itu tāli eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum; itaṉic ciṟuvarkku aṇital marapu; 2"tāli kaḷin taṉṟu milaṉē pālviṭ, ṭayiṉiyu miṉṟayiṉṟaṉaṉē" eṉpatu kāṇka. tērmici yāṉiyi vittu ūrtal 3"poṟciṟu tēr micip pimpoṟ pōtakam, naṟciṟā rūrtaliṉ" eṉpataṉāṉum aṟiyappaṭum kālkōḷ- toṭakkam. vītiyil nallār cilar, kōviyiyum mucciyiyum kutaliyiyum maḻaliyiyum pūṇiyumuṭiya putalvari vēḻattu mukkāḻ tāḻac ciṉṉīr naṉippat tukil tuyalvara ēṟṟi eṉak kūṭṭi yurippārumuḷar
 146-150.	virāṭaṉ pērūr vicayaṉām pēṭiyik kāṇiya cūḻnta kampali mākkaḷiṉ-virāṭaṉatu periya nakarattiṉkaṇ aruccuṉaṉākiya pēṭiyik kāṇumāṟu cūḻnta muḻakkat tiṉiyuṭiya makkaḷip pōla, maṇimēkalitaṉi vantu puṟam cuṟṟi - vantu maṇimēkaliyic cuṟṟilum cūḻntu, aṇi ami tōṟṟattu aruntavap paṭuttiya tāyō koṭiyaḷ takavilaḷ - eḻilaminta nalluruvattiṉi ariya tavaneṟiyiṟ paṭuttiya tāyō koṭiyavaḷum takutiyillāta vaḷumāvaḷ;
pāṇṭavar aivarum pāñcāliyum virāṭaṉatu nakarattiṟ karantuṟintakāli aruccuṉaṉ pēṭiyuruk koṇṭu pirukannaḷi eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ āṇṭiruntami pāratattāl aṟiyalāvatu. kampali mākkaḷ - vēṟu cila kāṭci kaṇṭu muḻaṅkit tiriyumavar. aṇi ami tōṟṟattu - aṇikaḷ illāta tōṟṟattuṭaṉ eṉṟumām. cīvaka cintāmaṇiyil 4"uppami kāmattuppiṉ" "nikaraminta muḻantāḷum" eṉṉumiṭaṅkaḷil ‘ami' eṉpataṟku ipporuḷ kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
150-158. 	īṅkivaḷ mā malar koyya malarvaṉam tāṉ pukil - im maṇimēkali ciṟanta malar koytaṟkup pūmpoḻilil nuḻintāl, nal iḷa aṉṉam nāṇātu āṅkuḷa valluna kollō maṭanti taṉ naṭi-aṅkēyuḷḷa nalla iḷiya aṉṉap paṟavikaḷ nāṇāmal ivaḷ naṭipōl naṭakka vallaṉavō, māmayil āṅkuḷa vantu muṉniṟpaṉa cāyal kaṟpaṉa kolō tiyal taṉṉuṭaṉ - āṇṭuḷḷa aḻakiya mayilkaḷ innaṅkiyiṉ etirē vantu niṟpaṉavāki ivaḷuṭaṉ cāyalik kaṟpaṉavākumō, piṅkiḷi tām uḷa pāvitaṉ kiḷavikku eñcala kollō - aṅkirukkum paciya kiḷikaḷ immaṅkiyiṉ moḻikaṭkut tōṟpaṉa vallavō, iciyuna alla - ivi yāvum oppaṉavalla, eṉṟu ivi colli yāvarum iṉintu uka-eṉṟu ivikaḷik kūṟi aṉivarum varuntik keṭa,
āṅkuḷa aṉṉam nāṇātu valluna kollō eṉavum, āṅkuḷa māmayil vantu muṉṉiṟpaṉavāyk kaṟpaṉakolō eṉavum iyiyum. mayilkaḷ kaṟṟaṟkuttāṉum muṉvantu niṟkamāṭṭā eṉṟapaṭi. cāyal - meṉmi. eñcutal - kuṟital; tōṟṟal.
159. 		centaḷirc cēvaṭinilam vaṭu uṟāmal - civanta taḷir pōlum civanta aṭikaḷāl nilattilē vaṭuvuṇṭākāmaṟ ceṉṟu; 
vaṭu - cuvaṭu aṭiyum nilamum vaṭuvuṟāmal eṉṟumām. ceṉṟu eṉa oru col varuvikka.
160-171. 	kuravamum maravamum kuruntum koṉṟiyum - kurāvum veṇkaṭampum kuruntum koṉṟiyum, tilakamum vakuḷamum ceṅkāl veṭciyum - mañcāṭiyum makiḻum civanta kāliyuṭiya veṭciyum, narantamum nākamum parantu alar puṉṉiyum - nārattiyum curapuṉṉiyum parantu malarkiṉṟa puṉṉiyum, piṭavamum taḷavamum muṭamuḷ tāḻiyum-piṭavamum cemmulliyum vaḷinta muḷḷiyuṭiya tāḻiyum, kuṭacamum vetiramum koḻuṅkāl acōkamum- veṭpāliyum mūṅkilum parutta aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya acōkamum, ceruntiyum vēṅkiyum peruñ caṇpakamum- ceruntiyum vēṅkiyum periya caṇpakamum, erimalar ilavamum virimalar parappi-neruppippōṟ civanta pūkkaḷiyuṭiya ilavamum ākiyavi virinta malarkaḷip parappa, vittakar iyaṟṟiya viḷaṅkiya kiviṉi - kit toḻilil mēmpaṭṭa vittakarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa, cittirac ceykip paṭām pōrttatuvē oppat tōṉṟiya-cittirat toḻilaminta tukir pōrttatupōlat tōṉṟiya, uvavaṉam taṉṉi - uvavaṉatti, toḻutaṉaḷ kāṭṭiya cutamati taṉṉoṭu - toḻutu kāṭṭiya cutamatiyuṭaṉ, malar koyyap pukuntaṉaḷ maṇimēkali - maṇimēkali malar koytaṟku aṭintaṉaḷ eṉka,.
piṭavam-kuṭṭip piṭavam eṉṉum koṭi; 1"putaṉmicit taḷaviṉitaṉmuṭ cennaṉi, neruṅkukulip piṭavamō ṭoruṅku piṇiyaviḻa" eṉpatu akam. parappi - parappa veṉka. puttaṉ pātapīṭikiyuḷḷa tākaliṉ vaṉattit toḻutu kāṭṭiṉaḷ eṉka.
 mātavi yuritta urimuṉ ṟōṉṟi maṇimēkalikku ētu nikaḻcci etirntuḷa tākaliṉ, akkāriki vetuppak kalaṅki uruṭṭi āṭṭa, mātavi nōkki māṟṟik koṇarvāy eṉṟalum, ataṉik kēṭṭut tuyaroṭu kūṟum cutamati, 'kaṇṇīrik kaṇṭaṉaṉāyiṉ kāmaṉ naṭuṅkum; āṭavar akaṟalumuṇṭō; niṉṟiṭiṟ pēṭiya raṉṟō ; aṉṟiyum, yāṉ varuṅ kāraṇam kēḷāy; koyvēṉi viñciyaṉ eṭuttaṉaṉ, eḻuntaṉaṉ, paṭuttaṉaṉ; āyiṉēṉ; avaṉ akaṉṟu nīṅkiṉaṉ; ātalāl maṇimēkali taṉmiyaḷallaḷ; pōkiṉ, uḻiyōr āṅkuḷar; cellār; eytār; uvavaṉameṉpa toṉṟuṇṭu; uṇṭu; uṇṭu; marapiṉatu atutāṉ; cemmi tāṉilaḷ; yāṉum pōval; eṉṟu kūṭic celvuḻi, piṉṉarum niṟkunarum kāṇku narum niṟkunarum niṟkunarum maṇimēkaliyic cuṟṟi iṉintuka, maṇimēkali cutamati taṉṉoṭu malar koyyap pukuntaṉaḷ eṉa muṭikka. 
malarvaṉam pukka kāti muṟṟiṟṟu. 
 4. paḷikkaṟi pukka kāti 
uvavaṉattilum, ataṉkaṇ uḷḷa poykiyilum nikaḻum iyaṟkik kāṭcikaḷic cutamati kāṭṭa maṇimēkali kāṇpāḷāyiṉāḷ. avaḷ āṅku avvāṟirukka, mataveṟiyāl vītitōṟum kalakkuṟuttut tirinta kālavēkameṉṉum yāṉiyiṉ matatti yaṭakkit tērilēṟit tāṉi cūḻa varum utayakumaraṉ kaṇikiyar teruvil oru māṭattil malaraṇi mēṟ kātaṟparatti yoruttiyuṭaṉ makara yāḻiṉ kōṭṭiṉit taḻuvik koṇṭu mayaṅkip pāvipōl acivaṟṟirunta eṭṭikumaraṉic cāḷara vaḻiyāṟ kaṇṭu, ‘nī iṅṅaṉam mayaṅki
yirukkum kāraṇam yātu,' eṉṟu viṉaviṉaṉ. atu kēṭṭalum avaṉ virinteḻuntu arukaṇintu toḻutu, ‘vāṭiya aḻakuṭaṉ ivvīti vaḻiyē malarvaṉattiṟkuc cellum maṇimēkaliyik kaṇṭēṉ; kāṇṭalum, avaḷ tantiyuṟṟa koṭuntuyar eṉ niṉivilē tōṉṟi eṉ maṉavuṟutiyi māṟṟi, ivviyāḻiṉ paki narampil eṉ kiyic celuttiyatu; ituvē yāṉuṟṟa tuṉpam,' eṉṟaṉaṉ. eṉṟalum pala nāḷāka maṇimēkaliyi virumpiyirunta utayakumaraṉ makiḻcciyuṟṟu, ‘avaḷi eṉ tērmītēṟṟikkoṇṭu varuvēṉ,' eṉṟu avaṉukkuk kūṟiviṭṭut tēruṭaṉ ceṉṟu uvavaṉattiṉ matilvāyili aṭintāṉ.
maṇimēkali attēroliyik kēṭṭuc cutamatiyi nōkki, ‘utayakumaraṉ eṉpāl mikka viruppa muṭiyaṉeṉṟu muṉpu mātavikku vayantamāli kūṟiyatik kēṭṭuḷēṉ; iḵtu avaṉ tēroli pōlum; itaṟkiyātu ceyvēṉ,' eṉṟāḷ. atikkēṭṭa cutamati avaḷi āṅkuḷḷa paḷikkaṟiyiṟ pukutti, uḷḷē tāḻiṭṭukkoṇṭu irukkac ceytu, tāṉ ap paḷikkaṟikku aintu viṟkiṭi tūrattē niṉṟāḷ. aṅṅaṉam niṉṟavaḷit tēri niṟuttic cōliyiṉūṭē vanta utayakumaraṉ kaṇṭu, ‘nī maṇimēkaliyuṭaṉ vantāy eṉpataṉi aṟintēṉ,' eṉṟu kūṟi, maṇimēkaliyik kuṟittut taṉatu vēṭki pulappaṭap palavāṟu viṉāviṉaṉ. appoḻutu cutamati potiyaṟippaṭṭōr pōṉṟuḷam varunti, avaṉi nōkki, "iḷami nāṇi mutumi yeyti, uri muṭivu kāṭṭiya uravōṉ marukaṉākiya niṉakku aṟivum cālpum araciyal vaḻakkum makaḷir kūṟumāṟum uṇṭō? āyiṉum oṉṟu kūṟuvēṉ; makkaḷ yākki ivviyalpiṉi uṭiyatu; ataṉip puṟamaṟiyākap pārāy," eṉṟu ataṉ iḻiviṉi yeṭutturittāḷ; avvuri utayakumaraṉ ceviyi aṭiyumuṉ paḷikkaṟiyiṉuḷḷirunta maṇimēkaliyiṉ uruvam avaṉ kaṇṇetirppaṭṭatu. (ikkātiyiṉ mutaṟkaṇ uḷḷa iyaṟkip puṉivukaḷ iṉpam viḷippaṉa. iṟutikkaṇ uṭampiṉ iḻi takimi naṉku viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.)  
		paritiyañ celvaṉ virikatirt tāṉikku
iruḷvaḷip puṇṭa maruḷpaṭu pūmpoḻil
kuḻalici tumpi koḷuttik kāṭṭa
maḻali vaṇṭiṉam nalliyāḻ ceyya
5		veyil nuḻi paṟiyāk kuyilnuḻi potumpar
mayilā ṭaraṅkiṉ mantikāṇ paṉakāṇ
mācaṟat teḷinta maṇinī rilañcip
pācaṭip parappiṟ paṉmala riṭiniṉ
ṟorutaṉi yōṅkiya virimalart tāmari
10		araca vaṉṉam āṅkiṉi tiruppak
kariniṉ ṟālum orumayil taṉakkuk
kampuṭ cēvaṟ kaṉikuraṉ muḻavāk
kompa riruṅkuyil viḷippatu kāṇāy
iyaṅkutēr vīti yeḻutukaḷ cērntu
15		vayaṅkoḷi maḻuṅkiya mātarniṉ mukampōl
virimalart tāmari kariniṉ ṟōṅkiya
kōṭuṭit tāḻik koḻumaṭa laviḻnta
vālveṇ cuṇṇam āṭiya titukāṇ
mātar niṉkaṇ pōteṉac cērntu
20		tātuṇ vaṇṭiṉa mītukaṭi ceṅkiyiṉ
añciṟi viriya alarnta tāmaric
ceṅkayal pāyntu piṟaḻvaṉa kaṇṭāṅku
eṟintatu peṟāa tiriyiḻantu varunti
maṟintu nīṅku maṇicciral kāṇeṉap
25		poḻilum poykiyuñ cutamati kāṭṭa
maṇimē kaliyam malarvaṉaṅ kāṇpuḻi
matimaruḷ veṇkuṭi maṉṉavaṉ ciṟuvaṉ
	utaya kumaraṉ urukeḻu mītūr
	mīyāṉ naṭuṅka naṭuvuniṉ ṟōṅkiya
30			kūmpumutaṉ muṟiya vīṅkupiṇi yaviḻntu
	kayiṟukāl pariya vayiṟupāḻ paṭṭāṅku
	iticiti tārppat tiriporu munnīr
	iyaṅkutici yaṟiyā tiyāṅkaṇu mōṭi
	mayaṅkukāl eṭutta vaṅkam pōlak
35			kāḻōr kiyaṟa mēlō riṉṟip
	pākiṉ piḷaviyiṟ paṇimukan tuṭittuk
	kōviyaṉ vītiyum koṭittēr vītiyum
	pīṭikit teruvum peruṅkalak kuṟuttāṅku
	irupāṟ peyariya vurukeḻu mūtūr
40		orupāṟ paṭāa toruvaḻit taṅkātu
	pākum paṟiyum paruntiṉ pantarum
	ātula mākkaḷum alavuṟṟu viḷippa
	nīla mālvari nilaṉoṭu paṭarnteṉak
	kāla vēkaṅ kaḷimayak kuṟṟeṉa
45			viṭuparik kutiriyiṉ virintuceṉ ṟeytik
	kaṭuṅkaṇ yāṉiyiṉ kaṭāttiṟa maṭakki
	aṇittērt tāṉiyo ṭaraciḷaṅ kumaraṉ
	maṇittērk koṭiñci kiyāṟ paṟṟik
	kāralar kaṭampaṉ alla ṉeṉpatu
50			āraṅ kaṇṇiyiṟ cāṟṟiṉaṉ varuvōṉ
	nāṭaka maṭantiyar nalaṅkeḻu vīti
	āṭakac ceyviṉi māṭat tāṅkaṇ
	cāḷaram poḷitta kālpōku peruvaḻi
	vītimaruṅ kiyaṉṟa pūvaṇip paḷḷit
55			takarak kuḻalāḷ taṉṉoṭu mayaṅki
makara yāḻiṉ vāṉkōṭu taḻīi
	vaṭṭikic ceytiyiṉ varinta pāviyiṉ
	eṭṭi kumaraṉ iruntōṉ ṟaṉṉi
	mātar taṉṉoṭu mayaṅkiṉi yiruntōy
60			yātunī yuṟṟa iṭukkaṇ eṉṟalum
āṅkatu kēṭṭu vīṅkiḷa muliyoṭu
	pāṅkiṟ ceṉṟu taṉṟoḻu tētti
	maṭṭavi ḻalaṅkaṉ maṉṉa kumaraṟku
	eṭṭi kumaraṉ eytiya turippōṉ
65			vakivaric ceppiṉuḷ vikiya malarpōl			takinalam vāṭi malarvaṉam pukūum
	mātavi payanta maṇimē kaliyoṭu
	kōvala ṉuṟṟa koṭuntuyar tōṉṟa
	neñciṟi koṇṭa nīrmiyi nīkki
70			vempaki narampiṉ eṉkic celuttiyatu
	ituyā ṉuṟṟa iṭumpi yeṉṟalum
	matumalart tārōṉ maṉamakiḻ veyti
	āṅkavaḷ taṉṉiyeṉ aṇittē rēṟṟi
	īṅkiyāṉ varuvēṉ eṉṟavaṟ kurittāṅku
75			ōṭumaḻi kiḻiyum matiyam pōla
	māṭa vītiyiṉ maṇittēr kaṭiik
	kāraṇi pūmpoḻiṟ kaṭimukaṅ kuṟukavat
	tēroli mātar cevimutal icittalum
	cittirā patiyō ṭutaya kumaraṉuṟ
80			ṟeṉmēl vitta uḷḷat tāṉeṉa
	vayanta māli mātavik korunāḷ
	kiḷanta māṟṟam kēṭṭē ṉātaliṉ
	āṅkavaṉ tēroli pōlum āyiḻi
	īṅkeṉ cevimutal icittateṉ ceykeṉa
85		amutuṟu tīñcol āyiḻi yurittalum
cutamati kēṭṭut tuḷakkuṟu mayilpōl 
paḷikkaṟi maṇṭapam pāviyip pukukeṉ
ṟoḷittaṟi tāḻkōt tuḷḷakat tirīi
āṅkatu taṉakkōr aiviliṉ kiṭakki
90		nīṅkātu niṉṟa nēriḻi taṉṉik
kalleṉ tāṉiyoṭu kaṭuntēr niṟuttip
paṉmalarp pūmpoḻiṟ pakaṉmuḷit tatupōl
pūmarac cōliyum puṭiyum poṅkarum
tāmaric ceṅkaṇ parappiṉaṉ varūum
95		araciḷaṅ kumaraṉ ārumil oruciṟi
orutaṉi niṉṟāy uṉṟiṟa maṟintēṉ
vaḷariḷa vaṉamuli maṭanti melliyal
taḷariṭi yaṟiyun taṉmiyaḷ kollō
viḷiyā maḻali viḷintu melliyal
100		muḷiyeyi ṟarumpi muttunirit taṉakol
ceṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇ cevimaruṅ kōṭi
veṅkaṇi neṭuvēḷ viyappurik kuṅkol
mātava ruṟiyiṭam orīimaṇi mēkali
tāṉē tamiyaḷiṅ keytiya turiyeṉap
105			potiyaṟip paṭṭōr pōṉṟuḷam varunti
matumalark kūntaṟ cutamati yurikkum
iḷami nāṇi mutumi yeyti
urimuṭivu kāṭṭiya uravōṉ marukaṟku
aṟivuñ cālpum araciyal vaḻakkum
110		ceṟivaḷi makaḷir ceppalu muṇṭō
aṉiya tāyiṉum yātoṉṟu kiḷappal
viṉiviḷaṅku taṭakki viṟalōy kēṭṭi
viṉiyiṉ vantatu viṉikkuviḷi vāyatu
puṉivaṉa nīṅkiṟ pulālpuṟat tiṭuvatu
115		mūttuviḷi vuṭiyatu tīppiṇi irukki
paṟṟiṉ paṟṟiṭaṅ kuṟṟak koḷkalam
puṟṟaṭaṅ karaviṟ ceṟṟac cēkki
avalak kavali kiyā ṟaḻuṅkal
tavalā uḷḷan taṉpā luṭiyatu
120		makkaḷ yākki ituveṉa uṇarntu
mikkōy itaṉip puṟamaṟip pārāy
eṉṟava ḷuritta icipaṭu tīñcol
ceṉṟava ṉuḷḷañ cērā muṉṉarp
paḷiṅkupuṟat teṟinta pavaḷap pāviyiṉ
125		iḷaṅkoṭi tōṉṟumāl iḷaṅkō muṉṉeṉ.
uri 
1-6.		paritiyañ celvaṉ virikatirt tāṉikku - ātittaṉatu virinta katirkaḷākiya cēṉikku, iruḷ vaḷippuṇṭa maruḷpaṭu pūmpoḻil-iruḷ muṟṟappaṭṭā lotta maruṭkiyuṇṭākum aḻakiya poḻiliṉkaṇ, kuḻalici tumpi koḷuttik kāṭṭa- tumpikaḷ vēyṅkuḻaliṉiciyip poruttik kāṭṭa, maḻali vaṇṭiṉam nalliyāḻ ceyya-iḷiya vaṇṭiṉaṅkaḷ nalla yāḻiṉiciyi murala, veyil nuḻipu aṟiyāk kuyil nuḻi potumpar-ñāyiṟṟiṉ katir tōṉṟuṅkāl toṭṭu maṟiyum vari ciṟitum cellutal aṟiyāta kuyilum nuḻintu cellumāṟu aṭarnta iḷamarak
kāvākiya, mayil āṭu araṅkil manti kāṇpaṉakāṇ - araṅkil mayilkaḷ āṭuvatik kāṇpaṉavākiya manti
kaḷik kāṇpāyāka ;
paritiyākiya celvaṉ eṉka. tāṉikku-tāṉiyāl, ñāyiṟṟiṉ katirāl turattappaṭṭa ulakiṉiruḷellām pukuntiruntāṟ pōlum iruṭciyiyuṭiya poḻil eṉka. maruḷ-maruṭki; viyappu. tumpi - vaṇṭiṉ ōr kiḷi. 1"kompart tumpi kuḻalici kāṭṭap, poṅkar vaṇṭiṉa nalliyāḻ ceyya" eṉap piṉṉarum varutal kāṇka. ili kaḷiṉ ceṟivālē kuyilkaḷ nuḻintu cellum iḷamarakkā veṉka; 2"veyiṉuḻi paṟiyāk kuyiṉuḻi potumpar" eṉpatu perumpāṇ. kāṇpaṉa : kāṇṭal eṉat toḻiṟ peyarumām.
 7-13. 	mācaṟat teḷinta maṇinīr ilañci-maṟuvaṟṟut teḷinta paḷiṅkupōlum nīriṉiṭiya poykiyil, pācaṭip parappil paṉmalar iṭiniṉṟu-paciya ilikaḷiṉ parappiṉ kaṇuḷḷa pala malarkaḷiṉiṭiyē niṉṟum, oru taṉi ōṅkiya viri malart tāmari - taṉiyē uyarntu viḷaṅkiya maṇam mikka oru tāmari malaril, araca aṉṉam āṅkiṉitu iruppa - aṉṉap puḷ iṉitāka aracu vīṟṟiruppa, kari niṉṟu ālum oru mayil taṉakku - avvilañciyiṉ kariyil niṉṟu aracaṉ muṉ āṭum melliyal pōla āṭukiṉṟa oppaṟṟa aḻakiṉiyuṭiya mayilukku, kampuṭ cēval kaṉikural muḻavā - campaṅ kōḻic cēvaliṉatu mikka kural muḻavoliyāka, kompar iruṅkuyil viḷippatu kāṇāy - kiḷiyiṉkaṇ uḷḷa kariya kuyil pāṭuvatik kāṇ pāyāka;
maṇi - nīlamaṇiyumām. oru taṉi - oppaṟṟa taṉi; taṉṉan taṉi; "oru taṉi niṉṟāy," "oru taṉi yirunta, "oru taṉi yēṟi" (4 : 96 : 16 : 33 ; 17:86.) eṉa innūluḷḷē oru taṉiyeṉpatu payiṉṟu varukiṉṟatu. mayil taṉakku-mayiliṉatu āṭṭattiṟ kiyiya. viḷittal-pāṭutal; 1"viḷiyātāṉ kūttāṭṭu" 2"viḷittaliṉṉamir tuṟaḻ kītam" eṉpuḻi viḷittal ipporuḷpaṭutal kāṇka.
14-18,	iyaṅku tēr vīti eḻu tukaḷ cērntu - tērkaḷ cañcarikkiṉṟa vītiyiṉiṉṟum eḻunta tukaḷ cērntu, vayaṅku oḷimaḻuṅkiya mātar niṉmukampōl - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa oḷi maḻuṅkappeṟṟa niṉatu aḻakiya mukattippōla, viri malarttāmari kari niṉṟu ōṅkiya kōṭu uṭit tāḻik koḻumaṭal aviḻnta vālveṇ cuṇṇam āṭiyatu itukāṇ - kariyil niṉṟu vaḷarnta kiḷikaḷiyuṭiya tāḻiyiṉatu koḻuviya maṭaliṉiṉṟum aviḻnta veḷḷiya makarantap poṭi aḷinta maṇam poruntiya it tāmari malarik kāṇpāyāka;
mātar - aḻaku. 3"kōṭuṭit tāḻi" eṉṉum pāṭattiṟkuc  caṅku uṭintāṟ pōlum tāḻiyiṉ maṭal eṉka; vāl veṇ: oru poruḷirucol; tūya veḷḷiya eṉṟumām; 4"ūrti vāl veḷ ḷēṟē" eṉpatu kāṇka. itu malar eṉak kūṭṭi im malar eṉa urikka.
 19-26.	mātar niṉkaṇ pōtu eṉac cērntu tātu uṇ vaṇṭiṉam mītu kaṭi ceṅkiyiṉ - niṉṉuṭiya aḻakiya kaṇkaḷi nīlamalareṉa niṉintu tātuṇṇumāṟu aṭinta vaṇṭiṉaṅkaḷi akaṟṟukiṉṟa niṉ civanta kiyippōla, am ciṟi viriya alarnta tāmaric ceṅkayal pāyntu piṟaḻvaṉa kaṇṭu āṅku eṟintu - malarnta tāmari malarilē pāyntu piṟaḻvaṉavākiya ceṅkayalkaḷik kaṇṭu aḻakiya ciṟaku viriyumāṟu appoḻutē pāyntu, atu peṟāatu iri iḻantu varunti - am mīṉkaḷip peṟāmal iriyi iḻantu varunti, maṟintu nīṅku maṇicciral kāṇ - mīṇṭum nīṅkukiṉṟa nīlamaṇipōlum cicciliyik kāṇpāyāka; eṉa - eṉṟu colli, poḻilum poykiyum cutamati kāṭṭa - cutamati poḻiliyum poykiyiyum kāṭṭāniṟka, maṇimēkali am malarvaṉam kāṇpuḻi - maṇimēkali  appūmpoḻiliṉ vaḷattiḷik kāṇumpoḻutu;
añciṟi viriya eṟintu eṉaviyiyum. atu, cāti yorumi; piṟaḻvaṉaveṉa mēl vantamiyiṉ. iri iḻantu - peṟāmiyāl avviriyi yiḻanteṉka. maṇi - nīlamaṇi; 1"pulavukkaya leṭutta poṉvāy maṇicciral" eṉpatu kāṇka. ciral - ciccilippuḷ; itu mīṉ kotti yeṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. mukam tāmarikkum, kaṇ ceṅkayalukkum, ki ciralukkum uvamam; 2"poruḷē yuvamañ ceytaṉar moḻiyiṉum, maruḷaṟu ciṟappiṉaḵtuvama mākum" eṉpataṉāl poruḷkaḷ uvamamākak kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. poyki vaṉattiṉ uṟuppākaliṉ, vaṉaṅkāṇpuḻi yeṉa atilaṭakkik kūṟiṉar.
 27-28. 	matimaruḷ veṇkuṭi maṉṉavaṉ ciṟuvaṉ utayakumaraṉ - tiṅkaḷi yotta veṇkuṭiyiyuṭiya aracaṉ putalvaṉākiya utayakumaraṉ; 
maruḷ : uvamavurupu ; 3"matimaruḷ veṇkuṭi" eṉpatu puṟam. maṉṉavaṉ - cōḻaṉ.
28-34.	urukeḻumītu ūr mīyāṉ naṭuṅka - accam poruntiya mēliṭattiṉ ūrntirukkiṉṟa mālumi naṭuṅka, naṭuvuniṉṟu ōṅkiya kūmpu mutal muṟiya - naṭuvil niṉṟu ōṅkiya pāymaram aṭiyilē muṟiya, vīṅkupiṇi aviḻntu kayiṟu kāl pariya - iṟukiṉa piṇippu aviḻntu pāy kaṭṭiṉa kayiṟu aṟupaṭa, vayiṟu pāḻpaṭṭāṅku iti citintu ārppa - appoḻutu naṭuviṭam pāḻākip pāy pīṟuṇṭu olippa, tiriporu munnīr iyaṅku tici aṟiyātu - alikaḷ porukiṉṟa kaṭaliṉkaṇ cellun tici aṟiyāmal, yāṅkaṇum ōṭi mayaṅku kāl eṭutta vaṅkam pōla - evviṭattum ōṭi mayaṅkukiṉṟa kaṭuṅkāṟṟiṉālalikkap paṭṭa marakkalam pōla;
mūtūr eṉṉum pāṭattiṟkup pukāriṉkaṇ eṉṟurikka,  mīyāṉ - mālumi ; nīyāṉ eṉpatum pāṭam. naṭuṅka - muṟiyap pariya ārppa ōṭiyeṉka : ōṭi mayaṅku vaṅkam eṉṟiyikka ; mayaṅku kāl - cuḻal kāṟṟu eṉalumām ; itaṟku, cuḻal kāṟṟiṉāl eṭukkappaṭṭu ōṭiya vaṅkampōla eṉappirittuk kūṭṭuka.
35-44.	kāḻōr kiyaṟa - kuttukkōṟkārar ceyalaṟa, mēlōr iṉṟi - pākarum iṉṟi, pākiṉ piḷaviyiṟ paṇimukam tuṭittu - pākaṉāṟ kuttappaṭṭa piḷaviyiṉiyuṭiya pariya mukattik kiyāṟṟuṭittu, kōviyaṉ vītiyum koṭittēr vītiyum pīṭikit teruvum peruṅkalak kuṟuttu - aracar perunteruviyum koṭi yaṇinta tērōṭum vītiyiyum kaṭi vītiyiyum periya kalakka muṇṭākki, irupāṟ peyariya urukeḻu mūtūr orupāṟpaṭātu oruvaḻit taṅkātu-iruvakip peyariṉiyuṭiyatāyp pakivarkku accattit tarum mūtūriṉkaṇ oru neṟip paṭāmal ōriṭattiṟ ṟaṅkāmal, pākum paṟiyum paruntiṉ pantarum - pākaṉum paṟiyaṭippōrum paruntiṉ tokutiyum, ātula mākkaḷum alavuṟṟu viḷippa - mikka vaṟumi yuṭiyōrum cuḻaṉṟu kūppiṭa, nīla mālvari nilaṉoṭu paṭarn teṉak kālavēkam kaḷimayakku uṟṟeṉa - periya nīla mali nilattiṉ kaṇ tirivatupōlak kālavēkam eṉṉum paṭṭattiyāṉi mata mayakkuṟṟut tirintatāka;
mēlōr iṉṟi-pākarivīci yeṉṟapaṭi. piḷavi -aṅkucat tāṟ piḷakkappaṭṭa puṇ. pākiṉ piḷavi-pākaṉuṭiya uṭaṟ piḷavu eṉṟumām. iruvakip peyariṉi, 1"irupāṟ peyariya vurukeḻu mūtūr" eṉpataṉ uriyā laṟika. pāku - pākaṉ paṟi - paṟiyaṭip pōr; yāṉikku muṉpu paṟiyaṭikkappaṭutal marapeṉpataṉi, 2"niṟiyaḻi kolyāṉi nīrkkuviṭ ṭāṅkup. paṟiyaṟin tallatu cellaṟka veṉṉā, iṟiyē tavaṟuṭi yāṉ" eṉpataṉālaṟika. ātulamākkaḷ teruvilē tirivarākaliṉ avariyuṅ kūṟiṉār. paruntiṉ pantar - uṇaviṉporuṭṭup pantariṭṭāṟpōl mēlē cellum paruntiṉam, vari nilaṉoṭu paṭarnteṉa eṉpatu il poruḷuvamam. nilaṉoṭu - nilamici. kaḷi - matam. 
vaṅkampōlak kāḻōr kiyaṟap pākaṉ mutaliyōr  alavuṟṟu viḷippa mūtūriṉkaṇ mēlōriṉṟit tuṭittup peruṅ kalakkuṟuttup paṭātu taṅkātu paṭarnteṉak kālavēkam kaḷimayakkuṟṟatāka eṉa muṭikka; vaṅkampōlak kālavēkam kaḷimayakkuṟṟeṉa eṉa viyiyum. ivvāṟē maturikkāñciyil, 3"vīṅkupiṇi nōṉ kayiṟāiyiti puṭiyūk, kūmpumutaṉ muruṅka veṟṟik kāyntuṭaṉ, kaṭuṅkāṟ ṟeṭuppak kalporu turii, neṭuñcuḻip paṭṭa nāvāy pōla, irutalip paṇila mārppac ciṉañciṟantu, kōlōrkkoṉṟu mēlōr vīci, meṉpiṇi vaṉṟoṭar pēṇātu kāḻcāyttuk, kantunīt tuḻitaruṅ kaṭāa yāṉiyum" eṉa vantiruntal kāṇka.
45-50.	viṭuparik kutiriyiṉ virintu ceṉṟu eyti-celuttu kiṉṟa virinta celaviṉiyuṭiya kutiriyiṉmītu kaṭitiṟ ceṉṟu cērntu, kaṭuṅkaṇ yāṉiyiṉ kaṭāttiṟam aṭakki- taṟukaṇmiyuṭiya yāṉiyatu matattiṉ kūṟupāṭṭiṉi aṭakki, aṇittērt tāṉiyoṭu - aḻakiya tēr mutaliya paṭiyuṭaṉ, araciḷaṅ kumaraṉ-, maṇittērkkoṭiñci kiyālpaṟṟi - tāṉ ēṟiyuḷḷa tēriṉkaṇ uḷḷa aḻakiya koṭiñciyik kiyāṟ piṭittukkoṇṭu, kāralar kaṭampaṉ allaṉ eṉpatu āraṅkaṇṇiyil cāṟṟiṉaṉ varuvōṉ - tāṉ kārkālattu malarkiṉṟa kaṭappa māliyiyuṭiya murukavēḷ allaṉ eṉpataṉittāṉ aṇinta ātti māliyiṉāl aṟivittu varupavaṉ;
pari - celavu, utayakumaraṉākiya araciḷaṅkumaraṉ eṉak  kūṭṭuka. ‘koṭiñci-tāmarippū vaṭivamākap paṇṇit tērt taṭṭiṉ muṉṉē naṭuvatu,' eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. 1"koṭiñci neṭuntēr," eṉpataṉ uri kāṇka. itu tāmari moṭṭu vaṭivamuḷḷateṉṟu kūṟuvārumuḷar. ic colliṉuruvam koṭuñci yeṉavum kāṇappaṭum. aḻaku, iḷami, āṇmikaḷāl ivaṉi murukakkaṭavuḷeṉṟeṇṇi, kaṭappa māliyiṉmiyāṉum āttimāli yuṇmiyāṉum ivaṉ murukaṉallaṉ, cōḻaṉmakaṉ eṉṟu kaṇṭōr tuṇivareṉa utayakumaraṉiṉ aḻaku mutaliyaṉa ciṟappikkap paṭṭaṉa veṉka. ār - ātti.
51-58.	nāṭaka maṭantiyar nalaṅkeḻu vīti-nāṭakak kaṇikiyaratu aḻaku poruntiya vītiyil, āṭakac ceyviṉi māṭat tāṅkaṇ - āṭakappoṉṉāṟ ceytoḻilaminta māḷikiyiṉ kaṇ, cāḷaram poḷitta kālpōku peruvaḻi-kāṟṟu cellu kiṉṟa periya vaḻiyākiyatuḷiceyyappaṭṭa cāḷarattiṉiṭa māka, vīti maruṅku iyaṉṟa pūvaṇippaḷḷi - vītiyiṉ pakkalil iyaṟṟiya malaraṇic cēkkiyil, takarakkuḻalāḷ taṉṉōṭu mayaṅki - mayirccāntiṉi yaṇinta kuḻaliṉi yuṭiya kaṇikiyoruttiyuṭaṉ mayakkamuṟṟu, makarayāḻiṉ vāṉkōṭu taḻīi - makara yāḻiṉatu ciṟanta kōṭṭit taḻuvi, vaṭṭikic ceytiyiṉ varinta pāviyiṉ eṭṭikumaraṉ iruntōṉ taṉṉi - eḻutu kōliṉāl eḻutappaṭṭa ōviyap pāvi pōla acivaṟṟu irunta eṭṭikumaraṉi,
āṭakam-nālvakip poṉṉuḷ oṉṟu.poḷitta -  tuḷiceyta. kālpōku peruvaḻiyākiya poḷitta cāḷaram eṉka. makarayāḻ - nālvaki yāḻiṉuḷoṉṟu; patiṉēḻu narampuṭiyatu eṉpar. kōṭu - yāḻttaṇṭu. vaṭṭiki - eḻutukōl. eṭṭi - vaṇikar peṟum paṭṭap peyar; "eṭṭip pūppeṟ ṟirumup patiṟṟiyāṇ ṭoṭṭiya celvat tuyarntō ṉāyiṉaṉ." (22; 113-4) eṉpar. piṉṉum; 1"eṭṭi kāvitip paṭṭan tāṅki," eṉpatuṅ kāṇka; 2'eṭṭi kāvitiyeṉpaṉa tēya vaḻakkākiya ciṟappup peyar" eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
 59-64.	mātar taṉṉoṭu mayaṅkiṉi iruntōy yātu nī uṟṟa iṭukkaṇ eṉṟalum - mātiṉōṭum mayakkamuṟṟirup pōy nī aṭinta tuṉpam yātu eṉa viṉavutalum, āṅkatu kēṭṭu vīṅkiḷa muliyoṭu pāṅkiṟceṉṟu tāṉ toḻutu ētti-ataṉik kēṭṭu ak kārikiyuṭaṉ avaṉ pakkaliṟ ceṉṟu vaṇaṅkit tutittu, maṭṭu aviḻ alaṅkal maṉṉa kumaraṟku-tēṉ aviḻum malarmāliyiṉi yuṭiya araca kumaraṉukku, eṭṭi kumaraṉ eytiyatu urippōṉ - tāṉ uṟṟa tuṉpattiṉ kāraṇattik kūṟukiṉṟa eṭṭi kumaraṉ;
mayaṅkiṉi: eccamuṟṟu. mayaṅki mātar taṉṉoṭum iruntōy eṉka. pāṅkil - muṟimiyāl eṉṟumām.
 65-71.	vakivaric ceppiṉuḷ vikiya malarpōl - tiṟappaṭa aminta ceppiṉ uḷḷē vikkappaṭṭa naṟumalarip pōla,  taki nalam vāṭi - mikka aḻaku vāṭṭamuṟṟu, malarvaṉam pukūum - pūmpoḻilukkuc cellum, mātavi payanta maṇimēkali yoṭu kōvalaṉ uṟṟa koṭuntuyar tōṉṟa - mātavi peṟṟa maṇimēkaliyik kaṇṭatum kōvalaṉ aṭinta koṭiya tuṉpam niṉivil vara aḵtu, neñcu iṟikoṇṭa nīrmiyi nīkki - maṉam taṅkutal koṇṭa taṉmiyi nīkki, vempaki narampiṉ eṉ kic celuttiyatu, koṭiya paki narampiṉ kaṇ eṉ kiyic celuttiyatu - itu yāṉ uṟṟa iṭumpi eṉṟalum - ituvē yāṉ aṭinta tuṉpam eṉṉalum;
ceppiṉuḷ vikiya malar puḻukkattāl vāṭum; 3"maṭimāṇ ceppiṟṟamiya vikiya, peyyāp pūviṉ mey cāyiṉaḷē," 4"vaki varic ceppiṉuḷ vikiya kōtiyēm," 5"vēyātu ceppiṉaṭittut tami vikum vīyiṉaṉṉa, tīyāṭi ciṟṟam palamaṉiyāḷ," eṉpaṉa kāṇka. maṇimēkaliyik kaṇṭavuṭaṉ kōvalaṉuṟṟa tuṉpam niṉiviṟku vantamiyiṉ, ‘maṇimēkali yoṭu...tuyartōṉṟa,' eṉṟāṉ iṟikoṇṭa-yāḻilē taṅkiya. paki narampu-niṉṟa narampiṟku āṟāvatu narampu;āṟāvatum mūṉṟāvatumākiya narampukaḷ eṉṟu murippar; "niṉṟa narampiṟ kāṟu mūṉṟuñ, ceṉṟu peṟa niṟpatu kūṭamākum," eṉpatu kāṇka. kōvalaṉ taṉ kulattōṉṟalākaliṉ avaṉuṟṟa tuyari niṉintu mayaṅkiṉēṉeṉṟāṉeṉka.
72-76. 	matumalart tārōṉ maṉamakiḻvu eyti-tēṉ poruntiya malar māliyiṉiyuṭiya utayakumaraṉ uḷamakiḻcciyaṭintu, āṅkavaḷ taṉṉi eṉ aṇittēr ēṟṟi - am maṇimēkaliyi eṉ aḻakiya tēriṉmītēṟṟi, īṇṭu yāṉ varuvēṉ eṉṟu avaṟku urittāṅku-īṇṭu yāṉ varuvēṉ eṉṟu eṭṭi kumaraṟkukkūṟi, ōṭu maḻi kiḻiyum matiyampōla - ōṭukiṉṟa mukil kiḻitaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya matiyampōla, māṭavītiyil maṇittēr kaṭii - māṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiya vītiyiṉkaṇ aḻakiya tēric celutti, kār aṇi pūmpoḻiṟ kaṭimukam kuṟuka - vāṉaḷāviya pūñcōliyiṉ vāyiliṉiṭam aṭiya;
muṉivar uṟiviṭattiṉīṅki malarvaṉamaṭinta maṇimēkaliyi iṉi eḷitiṉeytalāmeṉa maṉamakiḻntāṉeṉka. māṭaṅkaṭku maḻiyum tērukku matiyamum uvami; matiyiṉi utayakumaraṉukku uvami yākkalumām . kali nirampiṉamiyiṉ; itaṟku matiyam pōlak kuṟuka veṉṟiyikka. kār aṇi - mēkatti mītu aṇinta.
 77-85.	at tēr oli mātar cevimutal icittalum - antat tēr oliyāṉatu maṇimēkaliyiṉ ceviyiṭam ceṉṟu icittalum, cittirāpatiyōṭu utayakumaraṉ uṟṟu eṉmēl vitta uḷḷattāṉeṉa-utayakumaraṉ eṉpāl vitta maṉamuṭiyāṉ eṉṟu cittirāpatiyiṉiṭamāka aṟintu, vayantamāli mātavikku orunāḷ kiḷanta māṟṟam kēṭṭēṉ ātaliṉ - vayantamāli mātavipāl orunāḷ kūṟiya moḻiyik kēṭṭirukkiṉṟēṉākaliṉ, āṅkavaṉ tēroli pōlum āyiḻi īṅku eṉ cevimutal icittatu-cutamati av vutayakumaraṉatu tērōciyē pōlum ippoḻutu eṉ ceviyiṭam olittatu, eṉ ceyku eṉa amutuṟu tīñcol āyiḻi urittalum - itaṟku eṉ ceyvēṉ eṉa amutiṉu miṉiya moḻiyiṉiyuṭiya maṇimēkali kūṟutalum;  
cittirāpatiyōṭu uṟṟu eṉak kūṭṭic cittirāpatiyāl aṟintu eṉṟurikka; vayantamāli cittirāpatiyuṭaṉ aṭintu eṉṟalumām. āyiḻi: viḷi. 
86-90. cutamati kēṭṭut tuḷakkuṟu mayilpōl - cutamati atu kēṭṭut taḷarcciyuṟṟa mayilippōla, paḷikkaṟi maṇṭapam pāviyip pukuka eṉṟu oḷittu aṟi tāḻkōttu uḷḷa kattu irīi-maṇimēkaliyip paḷikkaṟi maṇṭapattiṉ uḷḷiṭattē pukuka eṉṟu kūṟi oḷittu irutti aṟiyiṉ tāḻik kōttu, āṅkatu taṉakku ōr aiviliṉ kiṭakki nīṅkātu niṉṟa nēriḻitaṉṉi - appaḷikku maṇṭapattiṟku aintu viṟkiṭi tūrattil nīṅkāmal niṉṟa cutamatiyi; 
oḷittu aṟi-oḷiyiyuṭiyatākiya aṟiyeṉṟumām. paṇiyāḷar aintuviṟkiṭi tūrattē niṟṟal marapu; 1"aiviliṉakala niṉṟāṅ kaṭitoḻu taṟiñciṉāṟku," eṉpatu kāṇka. nīṅkātu niṉṟaṉaḷ; aṅṅaṉam niṉṟa nēriḻiyi eṉa aṟutturittu, nēriḻiyik kaṇṭu eṉa oru col viritturikka. 
 91-96.	kalleṉ tāṉiyoṭu kaṭuntēr niṟutti - oliyiṉi yuṭiya cēṉiyoṭu virinta celaviṉiyuṭiya tēriyum niṟutti, paṉmalarp pūmpoḻil pakal muḷittatu pōl-pala malarkaḷiyuṭiya pūñcōliyil ñāyiṟu tōṉṟiyatu pōla, pūmaraccōliyum puṭiyum poṅkarum - pūmpoḻililum pakkalilum kaṭṭumalikaḷilum, tāmaric ceṅkaṇ parappiṉaṉ varūum araciḷaṅkumaraṉ - tāmari malar pōṉṟa civanta kaṇkaḷāl nōkkivarukiṉṟa maṉṉiḷam putalvaṉ, ārum il oru ciṟi oru taṉi niṉṟāy uṉ tiṟam aṟintēṉ - yārumillāta oru pakkattil nī oruttiyāy niṟkiṉṟāy niṉ iyalpiṉi aṟintēṉ ; 
poṅkar - īṇṭuc ceykuṉṟu. araciḷaṅkumaraṉ cutamatiyika kaṇṭu uṉ tiṟam aṟintēṉ eṉṟu kūṟiyeṉka. 
  97-104.	vaḷariḷa vaṉamuli maṭanti melliyal - vaḷar kiṉṟa vaṉappuṭiya iḷaṅ koṅkikaḷiyum meṉmit taṉmiyiyum uṭiya maṇimēkali, taḷar iṭi aṟiyum taṉmiyaḷ kollō - āṭavar taḷarnta camayatti yaṟiyum taṉmiyi yuṭiyavaḷō, viḷiyā maḻali viḷintu melliyal muḷi yeyiṟu arumpi muttu nirit taṉakol-mutirāta maḻalimoḻi mutirntu melliyalukku muḷi eyiṟukaḷ arumpi muttukkaḷi nirittaṉa pōṉṟaṉavō, ceṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇ cevimaruṅku ōṭi veṅkaṇi neṭuvēḷ viyappu urikkumkol-civanta kayal mīṉ pōṉṟa nīṇṭa kaṇkaḷ ceviyiṉ pakkattē ōṭik koṭiya kaṇikaḷiyuṭiya kāmaṉiṉ viyappiṉi urik kiṉṟaṉavō, mātavar uṟiviṭam orīi maṇimēkali tāṉē tamiyaḷ iṅku eytiyatu uri eṉa-maṇimēkali caṅkattār uṟikiṉṟa viṭat tiṉiṉṟum nīṅkit tāṉē taṉiyaḷāy ivaṇ eytiya kāraṇatti urippāyāka eṉa;
iṭi- celvi; 1"uṭiyār pōla iṭiyiṉṟu kuṟuki" eṉpatu kāṇka. muḷi eyiṟu - muḷipōlum eyiṟu; muḷitta eyiṟumām. veṅkaṇi-viruppattic ceyyuṅ kaṇiyumām. viyappu- viyakkuñ ceyti. cevimaruṅkōṭutal - urippatu pōlu meṉṟāṉeṉka. caṅkatti nīṅkiṉaḷātalit tamiyaḷ eṉṟāṉ.
 105-110	potiyaṟip paṭṭōrpōṉṟu uḷam varunti matu malark kūntaṟ cutamati urikkum - tēṉ poruntiya malar kaḷiyaṇinta kuḻaliṉiyuṭiya cutamati cāḷaramillāta nilavaṟiyiṟ paṭṭōr pōla maṉam varuntik kūṟukiṉṟāḷ, iḷami nāṇi mutumi eytu urimuṭivu kāṭṭiya uravōṉ marukaṟku iḷamip paruvatti nāṇi mutumipparuvatti aṭintu tammuḷ māṟu koṇṭu vantār iruvaruṭiya colli ārāyntu aṟintu avarkaṭku avaṟṟiṉ muṭivi viḷakkiya pēraṟivuṭiyōṉākiya karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉatu vaḻittōṉṟalākiya niṉakku, aṟivum cālpum araciyal vaḻakkum ceṟivaḷi makaḷir ceppalum uṇṭō-nallaṟiviṉiyum amitiyiyum araciyal nītiyiyum ceṟinta vaḷiyiyumuṭiya makaḷir kūṟumāṟum uṇṭō;
kaṟikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉ uri muṭivu kāṭṭiya ivvaralāṟu 2uri muṭivukāṇā ṉiḷamiyōṉeṉṟa, narimutu makkaḷuvappa - nari muṭittuc collāṉ muṟiceytāṉ cōḻaṉ kula vicci, kallāmaṟ pākam paṭum" eṉṉum paḻamoḻi veṇ pāvālum, "tam muḷ maṟutaliyāyiṉā riruvar tamakku muṟi ceyya vēṇṭi vantu cila coṉṉāl ac coṉ muṭivu kaṇṭē ārāyntu muṟi ceyya aṟivu nirampāta iḷamip paruvattāṉeṉṟikaḻnta narimutu makka ḷuvakkumvaki nari muṭittuvantu, muṟivēṇṭivanta irutiṟattārum colliya coṟkoṇṭē ārāyntaṟintu muṟiceytāṉ karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉeṉṉuñ cōḻaṉ; ātalāl tattam kulattukkut takka viccikaḷ kaṟpataṟku muṉṉē cempāka muḷavām eṉṟavāṟu" eṉṉum ataṉ uriyālum aṟiyappaṭum: porunarāṟ ṟuppaṭiyilum 1"mutiyō, ravipuku poḻutiṟṟam pakimuraṇ celavum" eṉa itu kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. marukaṟku muṉṉiliyiṟ paṭarkki.
111-125.	aṉiyatu āyiṉum yāṉ oṉṟu kiḷappal viṉi viḷaṅku taṭakki viṟalōy kēṭṭi - aṅṅaṉamāyiṉum pōriṉkaṇ viḷaṅkukiṉṟa taṭantōḷ valiyuṭiyāy yāṉ oṉṟu kūṟuvēṉ ataṉik kēṭpāyāka, viṉiyiṉ vantatu viṉikku viḷivāyatu - kaṉmattāl uṇṭāṉatu kaṉmattiṟku viḷi nilamākavuḷḷatu. puṉivaṉa nīṅkil pulāl puṟattu iṭuvatu - puṉiyap paṭuvaṉavākiya maṇap poruḷkaḷ nīṅkap paṭumāṉāl pulāl nāṟṟatti veḷikkuk kāṭṭuvatu, mūttu viḷivuṭiyatu - mutumi yaṭintu cātaliyuṭiyatu, tīppiṇi irukki - koṭiya nōykaṭku iruppiṭamākavuḷḷatu, paṟṟiṉ paṟṟiṭam - paṟṟukkaḷukkup paṟṟum iṭamāyatu, kuṟṟak koḷkalam - kuṟṟaṅkaṭkuk koḷkalamāyuḷḷatu, puṟṟu aṭaṅku araviṉ ceṟṟac cēkki - pāmpu aṭaṅkiyuḷḷa puṟṟippōlac ceṟṟattiṟkut taṅkumiṭamāyatu, avalak kavali kiyāṟu aḻuṅkal tavalā uḷḷam taṉpāl uṭiyatu-avalam mutaliya nāṉkum nīṅkātatākiya uḷḷattit taṉṉiṭat tuṭiyatu, makkaḷ yākki ituveṉa uṇarntu mikkōy itaṉip puṟamaṟippārāy - mēlōṉē makkaḷ uṭampu ittakiyateṉpataṉi aṟintu itaṉip puṟamaṟiyākap pārppāyāka, eṉṟu avaḷ uritta-eṉṟu avaḷ kūṟiya, icippaṭu tīñcol-pukaḻiṉiyuṇṭākkukiṉṟa iṉiya moḻikaḷ, ceṉṟu avaṉ uḷḷam cērāmuṉṉar - utayakumaraṉ uḷḷattiṟ ceṉṟu aṭivataṟku muṉṉarē, paḷiṅku puṟattu eṟinta pavaḷappāviyiṉ - paḷiṅkaṟiyiṉ puṟattē viḷaṅkukiṉṟa pavaḷappāviyippōla, iḷaṅkoṭi tōṉṟumāl iḷaṅkō muṉ eṉ - iḷami poruntiya koṭiyaṉiya maṇimēkaliyiṉ uruvam iḷaṅkōviṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟiya teṉka.
yākki viṉiyiṉ kāriyamāyum, viṉikkuk kāraṇamāyum uḷḷa teṉṟapaṭi; "piṟavip peruṅkaṭal" "eṉpataṉuriyil, kāraṇa kāriyat toṭarcciyāyk kariyiṉṟi varutaliṉ piṟavip peruṅkaṭaleṉṟār" eṉap parimēlaḻakar kūṟiyatu īṇṭu aṟiyaṟpālatu. puṉivaṉa - cantaṉam, malar mutaliyaṉa. mūppu eṉpatum pāṭam. aravu aṭaṅku puṟṟiṉ eṉa māṟuka. ceṟṟam - pakimi neṭuṅkālam nikaḻvatākiya ciṉam, avalam - varut tantōṉṟi niṟṟal; kavali - yātu ceyvaleṉṟal; kiyāṟu - mūrccittal; aḻuṅkal - vāyviṭ ṭaḻutal; itu araṟṟu eṉavum paṭum. puṟamaṟip pārāy - puṟamaṟiyākap pārppāyāka; eṉṟatu, piyi uḷpuṟamāka maṟittup pārttal pōl uṭampiyum pārkkaveṉṟapaṭi; pārppiṉ itaṉ tūytu aṉmi viḷaṅkum eṉṟavāṟu; 1"maṟṟataṉip pimmaṟiyāp pārkkappaṭum" 2"pimmaṟiyā nōkkap paruntārkkun takimittu" eṉpaṉa kāṇka. eṉṟāḷ; eṉṟu avaḷuritta col eṉa vēṟaṟut turikka. "paḷiṅku......pāvi" eṉṉuṅ karuttu; 3"paḷikkaṟip pavaḻap pāvi pariceṉat tikaḻuñ cāyal" eṉac cīvaka cintā maṇiyil vantuḷḷami kāṇka.
cutamati kāṭṭa maṇimēkali malarvaṉam kāṇpuḻi, yāṉiyiṉ kaṭāttiṟam aṭakkivaruvōṉākiya utayakumaraṉ eṭṭikumaraṉi nōkkiyātu nī yuṟṟa iṭukkaṇ eṉṟalum, avaṉ itu yāṉuṟṟa iṭumpi yeṉṟalum, tārōṉ makiḻveytip pūmpoḻiṟ kaṭimukam kuṟuka, tēroli icittalum, eṉceyku eṉa āyiḻiyurittalum, pāviyi irīi niṉṟa, cutamatiyik kaṇṭu araciḷaṅkumaraṉ maṇimēkali eytiyatu uriyeṉa, avaḷ varunti makkaḷ yākki itu veṉa uṇarntu puṟamaṟippārāy eṉṟuritta col avaṉuḷḷañ cērā muṉṉar iḷaṅkoṭi iḷaṅkōmuṉ tōṉṟum eṉa muṭikka.
	paḷikkaṟi pukka kāti muṟṟiṟṟu.	


5. maṇimēkalāteyvam vantu 
tōṉṟiya kāti 
maṇimēkaliyiṉ uruvatti ōviyameṉak karuti ōviyaṉatu kittiṟatti viyanta utayakumaraṉ piṉpu ataṉi avaḷ vaṭiveṉat tuṇintu, paḷikkaṟiyuṭ pukutaṟku ataṉ vāyil kāṇap paṭāmiyāl paḷikkuc cuvariṉik kiyālē taṭavik koṇṭu varupavaṉ cutamatiyi nōkki, ‘maṇimēkali ettiṟattiṉaḷ ? kūṟuti' eṉṟāṉ. eṉṉalum, cutamati, ‘avaḷ tavavoḻukkiṉaḷ; kuṟṟam purintāric capikkum āṟṟalumuṭiyavaḷ; kāma vikāram illātavaḷ ; nī avaḷi virumputal takkataṉṟu' eṉṟu kūṟiṉaḷ. kūṟalum avaṉ, ‘ciṟiyumuṇṭō ceḻumpuṉal mikkuḻi, niṟiyumuṇṭō kāmam kāḻkkoḷiṉ? avaḷ eppaṭiyum eṉakkuriyavaḷāvaḷ' eṉṟu collik koṇṭu celpavaṉ, camaṇa muṉivarkaḷiṉ iruppiṭattil ōr vittiyātaraṉāl iṭappaṭṭavaḷeṉṟu kūṟappaṭum cutamati putta caṅkattic cārnta mātavi makaḷuṭaṉ vanta varalāṟṟi avaḷi viṉāvit terintukoṇṭu, ‘maṇimēkaliyic cittirāpatiyāl iṉi aṭital kūṭum' eṉṟu collip pōyiṉaṉ. pōṉavuṭaṉ maṇimēkali veḷiyē vantu cutamatiyi nōkki, aṉpilaḷ; tavavuṇarcciyillātavaḷ, poruḷ viliyāṭṭi,' eṉṟu eṉṉi ikaḻntaṉaṉ eṉṉātu avaṉ piṉṉē eṉ neñcu cellaluṟṟatu; ‘ituvō kāmattiyaṟki ; itaṉ taṉmi keṭuvatāka;' eṉṟu collikkoṇṭu niṉṟāḷ. appoḻutu intira viḻāvik kāṇutaluṟṟa maṇimēkalā teyvam avarkaṭkut terinta ōr maṭanti vēṭam pūṇṭu appoḻiliyaṭintu, paḷikkaṟiyiluḷḷa pātapīṭikiyi valaṅkoṇṭu mēl eḻumpi niṉṟu palavāṟu tutittatu. appoḻutu pakaṟpoḻutu nīṅka antimāli vantuṟṟatu. (itaṉkaṇ cutamati varalāṟu kūṟum vāyilākac camaṇariṉum puttarkaḷ aruḷuṭiyō reṉpatu valiyuṟuttappaṭukiṉṟatu. putta tēvaṉip paravutal naṉkamin tuḷḷatu. mēṟṟiciyil ñāyiṟu vīḻak kīḻtticiyil niṟi mati tōṉṟutali uruvakappaṭutti yiruppatum, mālippoḻutiṉ nikaḻccikaḷ kūṟuvatum kaviyiṉ kaṟpaṉit tiṟattiṟkuc ciṟanta eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷām.)
		iḷaṅkōṉ kaṇṭa iḷampoṟ pūṅkoṭi
viḷaṅkoḷi mēṉi viṇṇavar viyappap	
porumukap paḷiṅkiṉ eḻiṉi vīḻttut
tiruviṉ ceyyōḷ āṭiya pāviyiṉ
5		virimala riṅkaṇi mīṉa vilōtaṉattu
uruvi lāḷaṉo ṭuruvam peyarppa
ōviya ṉuḷḷat tuḷḷiyatu viyappōṉ
kāviyaṅ kaṇṇi yākutal teḷintu
tāḻoḷi maṇṭapan taṉkiyil taṭiic
10		cūḻvōṉ cutamati taṉmuka nōkkic
cittirak kiviṉi ticitoṟuñ ceṟintaṉa
ettiṟat tāḷniṉ iḷaṅkoṭi yuriyeṉak
kurukupeyark kuṉṟaṅ koṉṟō ṉaṉṉaniṉ
murukac cevvi mukantutaṉ kaṇṇāl
15		parukā ḷāyiṉip pintoṭi naṅki
ūḻtaru tavattaḷ cāpa caratti
kāmaṟ kaṭanta vāymiya ḷeṉṟē
tūmalark kūntaṟ cutamati yurippac
ciṟiyu muṇṭō ceḻumpuṉal mikkuḻi
20		niṟiyu muṇṭō kāmaṅ kāḻkkoḷiṉ
cevviya ḷāyiṉeṉ cevviya ḷākeṉa
avviya neñcamō ṭakalvō ṉāyiṭi
añceñ cāya larāntā ṇattuḷōr
viñciya ṉiṭṭa viḷaṅkiḻi yeṉṟē
25		kalleṉ pērūrp pallō ruriyiṉi
āṅkava ruṟiviṭam nīṅki yāyiḻi
īṅkivaḷ taṉṉo ṭeytiya turiyeṉa
vārkaḻal vēntē vāḻkaniṉ kaṇṇi
tīneṟip paṭarā neñciṉi yākumati
30		īṅkivaḷ taṉṉō ṭeytiya kāraṇam
vīṅkunīr ñāla māḷvōy kēṭṭaruḷ
yāppuṭi yuḷḷat temmaṉi yiḻantōṉ
pārppaṉa mutumakaṉ paṭima vuṇṭiyaṉ
maḻivaḷan tarūum aḻalōm pāḷaṉ
35		paḻaviṉip payattāl piḻimaṇa meytiya
eṟkeṭut tiraṅkit taṉtaka vuṭimiyiṉ
kuraṅku ceykaṭaṟ kumariyam peruntuṟip
parantuceṉ mākkaḷoṭu tēṭiṉaṉ peyarvōṉ
kaṭaṉmaṇṭu peruntuṟik kāviri yāṭiya
40		vaṭamoḻi yāḷaroṭu varuvōṉ kaṇṭīṅ
	kiyāṅṅaṉam vantaṉi yeṉmaka ḷeṉṟē
tāṅkāk kaṇṇī reṉṟali yutirttāṅ
kōta lantaṇark kovvē ṉāyiṉum
kātala ṉātaliṟ kiviṭa līyāṉ
45		irantūṇ talikkoṇ ṭinnakar maruṅkil
parantupaṭu maṉitoṟun tirivō ṉorunāḷ
puṉiṟṟāp pāynta vayiṟṟup puṇṇiṉaṉ
kaṇaviri māli kikkoṇ ṭeṉṉa
niṇanīṭu peruṅkuṭar kiyakat tēnti
50		eṉmaka ḷirunta iṭameṉ ṟeṇṇit
taṉṉuṟu tuṉpan tāṅkātu pukuntu
camaṇīr kāḷnuñ caraṇeṉ ṟōṉi
	ivaṇī rallaveṉ ṟeṉṉoṭum vekuṇṭu
miyaṟu paṭivattu mātavar puṟatemik
55		kiyutirk kōṭaliṉ kaṇṇiṟi nīrēm
aṟavō ruḷirō ārumi lōmeṉap
puṟavōr vītiyiṟ pulampoṭu cāṟṟa
maṅkuṟōy māṭam maṉitoṟum pukūum
aṅkiyiṟ koṇṭa pāttira muṭiyōṉ
60		katircuṭum amayattup paṉimati mukattōṉ
poṉṉiṟ ṟikaḻum polampū vāṭiyaṉ
eṉṉuṟ ṟaṉirō eṉṟemi nōkki
aṉpuṭa ṉaḷiiya aruṇmoḻi yataṉāl
añcevi niṟittu neñcakaṅ kuḷirppittut
65		taṉkip pāttira meṉkit tantāṅ
kentik kuṟṟa iṭumpi nīṅka
eṭuttaṉaṉ ṟaḻīik kaṭuppat taliyēṟṟi
mātava ruṟiviṭaṅ kāṭṭiya maṟiyōṉ
cātuyar nīkkiya talivaṉ ṟavamuṉi
70		caṅka tarumaṉ tāṉemak karuḷiya
eṅkō ṉiyalkuṇaṉ ētamil kuṇapporuḷ
ulaka nōṉpiṟ palakati uṇarntu
taṉakkeṉa vāḻāp piṟarkkuri yāḷaṉ
iṉpac cevvi maṉpati yeyta
75		aruḷaṟam pūṇṭa oruperum pūṭkiyiṉ
aṟakkati rāḻi tiṟappaṭa uruṭṭik
kāmaṟ kaṭanta vāmaṉ pātam
takipā rāṭṭuta lallatu yāvatum
mikinā villēṉ vēntē vāḻkeṉa
80		añco lāḻiyi niṉtiṟa maṟintēṉ
vañci nuṇṇiṭi maṇimē kalitaṉic
cittirā patiyāṟ cērtalu muṇṭeṉ
ṟappoḻi lāṅkavaṉ ayarntu pōyapiṉ
paḷikkaṟi tiṟantu paṉimati mukattuk 
85		kaḷikkayal piṟaḻāk kāṭciya ḷākik
kaṟput tāṉilaḷ naṟṟava uṇarvilaḷ
varuṇak kāppilaḷ poruḷvili yāṭṭiyeṉ
ṟikaḻntaṉa ṉāki nayantō ṉeṉṉātu
putuvōṉ piṉṟip pōṉateṉ ṉeñcam
90		ituvō aṉṉāy kāmat tiyaṟki
ituvē yāyiṟ keṭukataṉ ṟiṟameṉa
matumalark kuḻalāḷ maṇimē kalitāṉ
cutamati taṉṉoṭu niṉṟa elliyuḷ
intira kōṭaṇi viḻāvaṇi virumpi
95		vantu kāṇkuṟuu maṇimēkalā teyvam
patiyakat tuṟiyumōr pintoṭi yāki
maṇiyaṟip pīṭiki valaṅkoṇ ṭōṅkip
pulavaṉ ṟīrttaṉ puṇṇiyaṉ purāṇaṉ
ulaka nōṉpiṉ uyarntō yeṉkō 
100		kuṟṟaṅ keṭuttōy ceṟṟañ ceṟuttōy
muṟṟa uṇarnta mutalvā eṉkō
kāmaṟ kaṭantōy ēma māyōy
tīneṟik kaṭumpaki kaṭintōy eṉkō
āyira vārat tāḻiyaṉ ṟiruntaṭi
105		nāvā yiramilēṉ ēttuva tevaṉeṉ
ṟerimaṇip pūṅkoṭi irunila maruṅkuvan
torutaṉi tirivatot tōtiyi ṉotuṅki
nilavari yiṟantōr muṭaṅkunā nīṭṭum
pulavari yiṟanta pukāreṉum pūṅkoṭi
110		paṉmalar ciṟanta naṉṉī rakaḻip
puḷḷoli ciṟanta teḷḷaric cilampaṭi
ñāyi liñci nakimaṇi mēkali
vāyiṉmaruṅ kiyaṉṟa vāṉpaṇit tōḷi
tarunila vacciram eṉiru kōṭṭam
115		etireti rōṅkiya katiriḷa vaṉamuli
ārpuṉi vēntaṟkup pēraḷa viyaṟṟi
ūḻi yeṇṇi nīṭuniṉ ṟōṅkiya
oruperuṅ kōyiṟ ṟirumuka vāṭṭi
kuṇatici maruṅkiṉ nāṇmutir matiyamum
120		kuṭatici maruṅkiṟ ceṉṟuvīḻ katirum
veḷḷiveṇ tōṭṭoṭu poṟṟō ṭāka
eḷḷaṟu tirumukam poliyap peytalum
aṉṉac cēval ayarntuviḷi yāṭiya
taṉṉuṟu peṭiyit tāmari yaṭakkap
125		pūmpoti citiyak kiḻittup peṭikoṇ
ṭōṅkirun teṅkiṉ uyarmaṭa lēṟa
aṉṟiṟ pēṭi arikkura laḻiic
ceṉṟuvīḻ poḻutu cēvaṟ kicippap
pavaḷac ceṅkāṟ paṟavik kāṉattuk
130		kuvaḷi mēynta kuṭakkaṭ cētā
mulipoḻi tīmpāl eḻutuka ḷavippak
kaṉṟuniṉi kurala maṉṟuvaḻip paṭara
anti yantaṇar centīp pēṇap
pintoṭi makaḷir palarviḷak keṭuppa
135		yāḻōr marutat tiṉṉaram puḷarak
kōvalar mulli kuḻaṉmēṟ koḷḷa
amaraka maruṅkiṟ kaṇavaṉi yiḻantu
tamarakam pukūum orumakaḷ pōlak
katirāṟṟup paṭutta mutirāt tuṉpamō
140		ṭanti yeṉṉum pacalimey yāṭṭi
vantiṟut taṉaḷāl mānakar maruṅkeṉ.
uri 
1-6.		iḷaṅkōṉ kaṇṭa iḷampoṟ pūṅkoṭi-araciḷaṅ kumaraṉ kaṇṇuṟṟa aḻakiya iḷam pūṅkoṭiyaṉiya maṇimēkali, viḷaṅku oḷi mēṉi viṇṇavar viyappa-viḷaṅkukiṉṟa pēroḷi poruntiya tirumēṉiyiyuṭiya vāṉavarkaḷum viyakkumāṟu, porumukap paḷiṅkiṉ eḻiṉi vīḻttu - paḷiṅkiṉālāya porumuka veḻiṉiyi vīḻttu, tiruviṉ ceyyōḷ āṭiya pāviyiṉ - tirumakaḷāṭiya pāviyippōla, virimalar aiṅkaṇi mīṉa vilōtaṉattu uruvilāḷaṉoṭu uruvam peyarppa - maṇam poruntiya malarākiya aintampukaḷiyum mīṉakkoṭiyiyum uṭiya anaṅkaṉuṭaṉ taṉ uruvatti veḷippaṭutta;
poṟpūṅkoṭi - kāmavalliyumām; itu poṉṉulakat tuḷḷa teṉpar. eḻiṉi - tiriccīli; porumuka veḻiṉi-mūvakit tiric cīlikaḷuḷ oṉṟu; araṅkiṉ valattūṇ iraṇṭilum uruvu tiri yāy amivatu; eḻiṉi mūṉṟaṉiyum 1"orumuka veḻiṉiyum - porumuka veḻiṉiyum; karantuvara leḻiṉiyum purintuṭaṉ vakuttu"eṉ pataṉālaṟika. tiruviṉ ceyyōḷ - tiruvākiya ceyyōḷ; tirumakaḷ- iṉ: cāriyi. āṭiya pāviyiṉ - āṭutaṟkuk koṇṭa pāvi yuruvip pōla; kollip pāviyurukkoṇṭu āṭiṉamiyiṉ ak kūttum pāvi yeṉappaṭum; atu pōr ceytaṟkuc caminta kōlattuṭaṉ avuṇar mōkittu viḻumpaṭi tirumakaḷāṭiyatu; patiṉōrāṭaluḷoṉṟu; 2"ceruveṅ kōla mavuṇar nīṅkat, tiru viṉ ceyyō ḷāṭiya pāviyum' eṉpatu kāṇka. "uruvi lāḷaṉo ṭuruvam peyarppa" eṉpataṉ karuttu ivaḷuruvik kaṇṭavuṭaṉ iḷaṅkōvaṉukkuk kāmavupāti uṇṭāyiṟ ṟeṉpatām. 
7-12.	ōviyaṉ uḷḷattu uḷḷiyatu viyappōṉ - (maṇimēkaliyiṉ uruvatti ōviyam eṉa niṉintu) ōviyakkāraṉ ataṉi eḻutumāṟu maṉatil amittati viyakkiṉṟavaṉāy utayakumaraṉ, kāviyaṅ kaṇṇi yākutal teḷintu - avvaṭivam nīlamalaraṉiya kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya maṇimēkali yātalit teḷintu, tāḻ oḷi maṇṭapam taṉ kiyil taṭiic cūḻvōṉ - taṅkiya oḷiyuṭiya paḷikku maṇṭapattit taṉ kiyālē taṭavip pārttuc cuṟṟi varupavaṉ, cutamati taṉmukam nōkki - cutamatiyiṉ mukattip pārttu, cittira kiviṉi ticitoṟum ceṟintaṉa ettiṟattāḷ niṉ iḷaṅkoṭi uri eṉa - cittirak kittoḻil eppakkamum niṟintirukkiṉṟaṉa niṉ iḷaṅkoṭi pōlpavaḷ evviṭattāḷ kūṟuka eṉa;
tāḻtal-taṅkutal. vāyil kāṇṭaṟku maṇṭapattit taṭavic cūḻntaṉaṉeṉka. cūḻntu varuṅkāl maṇimēkaliyiṉ uruvam etticiyiṉum veḷippaṭṭamiyiṉ "cittirak kiviṉi ticitoṟuñ ceṟintaṉa" eṉṟāṉ ; maṇṭapattiluḷḷa ōviyappāvi palavaṟṟuṭaṉ maṇimēkaliyiṉ uruvam vēṟupāṭiṉṟi yiruntamiyiṉ aṅṅaṉam kūṟiṉāṉeṉalumām; 1"mēviya paḷiṅkiṉ viruntiṟ pāviyiḵ tōviyac ceytiyeṉ ṟoḻivēṉ muṉṉar" 2"īṅkim maṇṇīṭṭiyāreṉa vuṇarkēṉ" eṉap piṉvaruvaṉa kāṇka. tiṟam-iyalpumām. cutamati kāvaṟ peṇṭākaliṉ 'niṉ iḷaṅkoṭi' eṉṟāṉ. ōviyaṉ eṉpataṟku utayakumaraṉ cittiram pōṉṟavaṉāy niṉṟeṉṟurittal īṇṭikkup poruntāmiyōrka.
13-18.	kuruku peyark kuṉṟaṅ koṉṟōṉ aṉṉa niṉ murukac cevvi - kiravuñca maliyi eṟinta murukavēḷi yotta niṉatu iḷami yaḻakiṉi, mukantu taṉ kaṇṇāl parukāḷ 3āyiṉ pintoṭi naṅki-ārāyumiṭattup paciya vaḷiyalkaḷi yaṇinta maṇimēkali taṉ kaṇkaḷāl mukantu parukāḷ, ūḻtaru tavattaḷ - muṟiyākap peṟṟa tavattiṉiyuṭiyaḷ, cāpacaratti-cāpamākiya ampiyuṭiyavaḷ, kāmaṟ kaṭanta vāymiyaḷ eṉṟē - kāmaṉi veṉṟa meymmiyi yuṭiyavaḷ eṉṟu, tūmalark kūntal cutamati urippa-tūya malaraṇinta kūntaliyuṭiya cutamati kūṟa;
kuruku-aṉṟil; kiravuñcam; ataṉ peyar peṟṟa kuṉṟameṉka; 4"kurukupeyarkkuṉṟaṅ koṉṟa neṭuvēlē" "kurukupeyarkkuṉ ṟaṅkoṉṟāṉ" eṉpaṉa kāṇka. muruku-iḷami; īṟu tirintatu; ūḻtaru tavattaḷ- ūḻiṉālē tarap paṭṭa tavattiṉaḷ eṉṟumām; eṉṉi? 5tavamun tavamuṭiyārk kākum" eṉpavākaliṉ. cāpacaratti - villiyum ampiyum uṭiyāḷ eṉpatōr poruḷum tōṉṟa niṉṟatu; cāpam - vil. āyiṉ, naṅki parukāḷ; tavattaḷ; caratti; vāymiyaḷ; eṉṟu urippaveṉka.
19-27. ciṟiyum uṇṭō ceḻumpuṉal mikkuḻi- vaḷaviya nīr mikunta viṭattu ataṉit tāṅkum araṇum uṇṭō, niṟiyum uṇṭō kāmam kāḻkkoḷiṉ-avvāṟē kāmam aṭippaṭiṉ ataṉi niṟuttum taṉmiyum uḷatākumō, cevviyaḷ āyiṉ eṉ cevviyaḷ ākeṉa - cevviyi yuṭiyaḷāyiṉ eṉṉi? avaḷ cevviyaḷākaṭṭum eṉṟu, avviya neñcamoṭu akal vōṉ-poṟāmi koṇṭa uḷḷattōṭum nīṅkuvōṉ, āyiṭi-appoḻutu, amceñ cāyal - aḻakiya civantacāyaliyuṭiyāy arāntāṇattuḷ-camaṇap paḷḷiyil, ōr viñciyaṉ iṭṭa viḷaṅkiḻi eṉṟē-oru viñciyaṉāliṭappaṭṭa melliyal eṉṟē, kalleṉ pērūrp pallōr uriyiṉi-kalleṉṉum oliyiṉiyuṭiya nakariṉkaṇ pallōrāluṅ kūṟappaṭuvāy, āṅkavar uṟiviṭam nīṅki āyiḻi-āyiḻāy nī accamaṇa muṉivar vāḻviṭatti nīṅki, īṅkivaḷ taṉṉōṭu eytiyatu uri eṉa - im maṇimēkaliyuṭaṉ īṇṭu eytiya kāraṇattik kūṟuvāyāka eṉa;
ciṟi-aṇi. niṟi-kāmatti uḷḷēyaṭakki niṟuttutal; maṉatti niṟuttalumām; kāḻkkoḷiṉ-viramēṟiṉ ; mutirntāl eṉṟapaṭi; 1 "nīr mikiṟ ciṟiyumilli" 2"nīrmikiṉilli ciṟi" 3"ciṟiyeṉpa tillic cevvē cempuṉal peruku māyiṉ, niṟi yeṉpa tillik kāma nērniṉṟu ciṟakkumāyiṉ" 4"piṟitu mākupa kāmaṅkāḻk koḷiṉē" eṉpaṉa īṇṭu aṟiyaṟpālaṉa. tavattaḷ, caratti, vāymiyaḷ eṉpavaṟṟic cevviyaḷ eṉa aṭakkik kūṟiṉāṉ. ākeṉa, vikāram, avviyam-piṟark kuriyaḷātal kūṭā teṉṉum poṟāmi; kōṭṭamumām. añceñ cāyal, viḷi. arāntāṇam eṉpataṟku mēl (3 : 87) urittami kāṇka. 
28-31.	vār kaḻal vēntē vāḻka niṉ kaṇṇi - nīṇṭa vīrakkaḻali yaṇinta aracē niṉ kaṇṇi vāḻvatāka, tīneṟippaṭarā neñciṉi ākumati-tīyavaḻiyiṟ cellāta uḷḷamuṭiyi āvāyāka, īṅkivaḷ taṉṉōṭu eytiya kāraṇam vīṅkunīrñālam āḷvōy kēṭṭaruḷ-im maṇimēkaliyuṭaṉ yāṉ vanta kāraṇattik kaṭalcūḻnta nilavulakiṉiyāḷum maṉṉavaṉē kēṭṭaruḷ vāyāka;
32-40.	yāppuṭi uḷḷattu em aṉi iḻantōṉ pārppaṉa mutumakaṉ-eṉ tāyi iḻantōṉum uṟutiporuntiya uḷḷatti yuṭiya pārppaṉa mutiyōṉum, paṭima uṇṭiyaṉ-paṭṭiṇiviṭṭuṇṇum nōṉpiṉi yuṭiyōṉum, maḻivaḷam tarūum aḻal ōmpāḷaṉ - maḻivaḷattit tarum muttīyōmpu vōṉumākiya enti, paḻaviṉip payattāl piḻimaṇam eytiya eṟkeṭuttu iraṅki-muṉ ceyviṉiyiṉ payaṉāl māruta vēkaṉiṭam akappaṭṭup piḻimaṇam eytiya eṉṉik kāṇāmiyāl varunti, taṉ takavuṭimiyiṉ - taṉatu takuti yuṭimiyāl, kuraṅkucey kaṭal kumariyam peruntuṟip parantu cel mākka ḷoṭu-kuraṅku ceyta kumarikkaṭaliṉ periya tuṟikkaṇ nīrāṭutaṟku mikkuc cellum makkaḷōṭē, tēṭiṉaṉ peyarvōṉ - eṉṉit tēṭic celpavaṉāy, kaṭal maṇṭu peruntuṟik kāviri āṭiya-iṭiyē kāviri kaṭaliṟ kalakkum periya caṅka mukattuṟiyil nīrāṭutaṟku, vaṭamoḻi yāḷaroṭu varuvōṉ- pārppaṉarōṭu varupavaṉ;
maḻivaḷan tarūum aḻal eṉka; 1"maḻikkaru vuyirkku maḻaṟṟika ḻaṭṭiṉ, maṟiyō rākkiya vāvuti naṟumpuki" eṉpatu kāṇka. māruta vēkaṉukkut tāṉ cilanāḷ uriyaḷāṉatu paṟṟi, "piḻimaṇa meytiya" eṉṟāḷ. kumari-kaṉṉiyākumari ; 2"toṭiyōḷ puvamum" eṉpataṉ uri nōkkuka. vaṭamoḻiyāḷar-pārppaṉar ; pārppaṉiyi "vaṭamoḻiyāṭṭi" (13:73) eṉṟu piṉṉark kūṟuvar.
40-46.	kaṇṭu īṅku-eṉṉi innakarattiṟ kaṇṭu, yāṅṅaṉam vantaṉi eṉmakaḷ eṉṟē tāṅkāk kaṇṇīr eṉṟali utirt tāṅku-eṉ makaḷē īṇṭu evvāṟu vantāy eṉṟu kūṟip perukiya kaṇṇīri eṉmītu corintu, ōtal antaṇarkku ovvēṉ āyiṉum-yāṉ maṟiyōtum antaṇarkaḷuṭaṉirut taṟkut takāta vaḷāyiṉum, kātalaṉ ātaliṉ kiviṭalī yāṉ - eṉmītu mikunta aṉpuṭiyaṉātalāl eṉṉik kiviṭāṉāy, irantūṇ talikkoṇṭu-irantuṇṭu vāḻtali mēṟkoṇṭu, innakar maruṅkil-iv'd2vūriṉ kaṇ, parantupaṭu maṉitoṟum tirivōṉ - parantu tōṉṟum illaṅkaḷ tōṟum ceṉṟu ēṟpōṉ; 
kiviṭalīyāṉ-kiviṭāṉ; "kāvalaṉ makaṉō kiviṭalīyāṉ" (19:32) eṉpar piṉṉum; "kāṭṭiyatātaliṟ kiviṭalīyāṉ" (13:85) eṉpatu cilappatikāram.
46-52.	orunāḷ puṉiṟṟuā pāynta vayiṟṟup puṇṇiṉaṉ-oru nāḷ īṉṟaṇimiyiyuṭiya pacuvoṉṟu pāyntamiyāl vayiṟṟiluṇṭāṉa puṇṇiṉi yuṭiyaṉāy, kaṇaviri māli kik koṇṭeṉṉa - alarimāliyik kiyiṟ
koṇṭāṟpōla,  niṇamnīṭu peruṅkuṭar kiyakattu ēnti-niṇattuṭaṉ nīṇṭa periya kuṭarik kiyil ēntik
koṇṭu, eṉmakaḷ irunta iṭa meṉṟu eṇṇi-paṇṭu eṉmakaḷ irunta iṭamākum eṉṟu niṉintu, taṉ uṟu tuṉpam tāṅkātu pukuntu - taṉatu mikka tuṉpattiṉit tāṅka viyalāmaṟ pukuntu, camaṇīrkāḷ num caraṇ eṉṟōṉi - camaṇarkaḷē ummuṭiya aṭikkalam eṉṟu kūṟiyavaṉi; 
pāynta puṇ: peyareccam kāraṇap poruṭṭāyatu; āṟu ceṉṟa viyar eṉpuḻippōla. 1"niṇavarik kuṟintavatar toṟuṅ, kaṇavira māli yiṭūukkaḻin taṉṉi, puṇṇumiḻ kuruti parippak kiṭantōr" eṉa uvamaṅ kūṟiyiruppatu aṟiyaṟpālatu. iṭameṉṟu-iṭa mākaliṉ purantaruḷvareṉṟu.
53-57.	ivaṇ nīr alla eṉṟu eṉṉoṭum vekuṇṭu - iv viṭattiṟkuriya taṉmi aṉṟeṉṟu kūṟi eṉṉiyum ciṉantu, miyaṟu paṭivattu mātavar puṟattu emik kiyutirk kōṭaliṉ- kuṟṟamaṟṟa tavavaṭivatti yuṭiya camaṇa muṉivarkaḷ puṟattē pōkumāṟu emmik kiyi acittuk kuṟittaliṉāl, kaṇ niṟi nīrēm-kaṇkaḷil niṟinta nīriṉiyuṭiyēmāy, aṟavōr uḷīrō ārum ilōm eṉa - aṟampurivīr irukkiṉṟīrō pātukāppōr yārumillātēm eṉṟu, puṟavōr vītiyil pulampoṭu cāṟṟa - puṟattiluḷḷa oru vītiyil varuttattuṭaṉ kūṟa;
ivaṇīralla - ivviṭattiṟkuriyīrallīr eṉṟumām ; alla eṉpatu pālkuṟiyātu vaḻakkuppaṟṟi niṉṟatu. eṉṉoṭum - eṉṉiyum; ummi: eccam; tantiyi eṉṉoṭum eṉṟumām. paṭivam - tava vēṭam. ōr puṟavītiyi leṉka ; puṟavōr - puṟattiluḷḷōr eṉṟurippārumuḷar.
58-70.	maṅkul tōy māṭa maṉitoṟum pukūum aṅkiyiṟ koṇṭa pāttiram uṭiyōṉ - vāṉaḷāviya māṭaṅkaḷi yuṭiya maṉikaḷtōṟum cellum kiyiṟkoṇṭa piccip pāttirattiyuṭiyavaṉum, katircuṭum amayattup paṉimati mukattōṉ-ñāyiṟu kāyum naṇpakaṟ poḻutil kuḷircci poruntiya matipōlum mukattiṉi yuṭiya vaṉum, poṉṉil tikaḻum polampū āṭiyaṉ-poṉ pōl viḷaṅkum aḻakiya āṭiyi uṭiyavaṉumāki, eṉ uṟṟaṉirō eṉṟu emi nōkki-eṉṉa tuṉpam eytiṉīr eṉṟu eṅkaḷi nōkki, aṉpuṭaṉ aḷiiya aruḷ moḻi ataṉāl - aṉpoṭu kalanta aruḷ mikunta iṉ moḻikaḷāl, añcevi niṟittu neñcakam kuḷirppittu - ceviyakatti niṟittu uḷḷattik kuḷiracceytu, taṉkip pāttiram eṉkit tantu-taṉ kiyiluḷḷa pāttiratti eṉ kiyil koṭuttuviṭṭu, entikku uṟṟa iṭumpi nīṅka - eṉ tantikku eytiya tuṉpam nīṅkumāṟu, eṭuttaṉaṉ taḻīik kaṭuppat taliyēṟṟi-viriyat taḻuvi eṭuttut taṉ uṭampiṉiṭattē cumantu ceṉṟu, mātavar uṟiviṭam kāṭṭiya maṟiyōṉ-putta muṉivarkaḷ uṟiviṭattik kāṭṭiya maṟiyavaṉākiya, cātuyar nīkkiya talivaṉ-entiyiṉ iṟapput tuṉpatti nīkkik kāppāṟṟiya talivaṉ, tavamuṉi caṅkatarumaṉ - caṅkatarumaṉ eṉṉum tavattiyuṭiya muṉivaṉāvāṉ; 
illaṟattōr yāvarum uṇṭapiṉ uccippoḻutiṟ piccikkuc celvatu puttamatat tuṟaviyar iyalpākaliṉ, "katircuṭumama yattu" eṉṟār ; "veṅkati ramayattu viyaṉpoḻi lakavayiṉ, vantu tōṉṟalum," "veyilviḷaṅ kamayattu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟiya, cātu cakkaraṉ ṟaṉṉiyāṉūṭṭiya" (10 : 27 - 8 ; 11 : 102 -3) eṉap piṉṉar varutaluṅ kāṇka. marutan tuvaril tōykkappaṭṭu mañcaḷ niṟamuḷḷatākaliṉ "poṉṉiṟ ṟikaḻuma polampū vāṭi" eṉap paṭṭatu. aṉpum aruḷumuṭiya moḻiyā leṉka. kaṭuppa-viriya. tali-iṭam. āṭiyaṉāy nōkki niṟittuk kuḷirppittut tantu ēṟṟik kāṭṭiya maṟiyōṉākiya talivaṉ caṅkatarumaṉ āvāṉeṉka. 
70-79.	tāṉ emakku aruḷiya -avaṉ emakku aṟivuṟutta, eṅkōṉ iyalkuṇaṉ ētamil kuṇapporuḷ - emmuṭiya iṟivaṉ iyalpākavē tōṉṟiya kuṇaṅkaḷi yuṭiyavaṉ kuṟṟamaṟṟa kuṇaṅkaḷiṉ poruḷāyuḷḷōṉ, ulaka nōṉpiṉ palakati uṇarntu taṉakkeṉa vāḻāp piṟarkkuriyāḷaṉ - ulakacīlattuṭaṉ pala piṟappukkaḷilum piṟantu piṟantu uṇarntu uyirkaṭku nallaṟivu pukaṭṭiya taṉakkeṉṟu uyir vāḻāta piṟarkkē urimipūṇṭoḻukupavaṉ, iṉpaccevvi maṉpati eyta-niruvāṇamaṭitaṟkuriya paruvatti uyirppaṉmikaḷ aṭiya, aruḷ aṟam pūṇṭa oru perum pūṭkiyiṉ - aruḷākiya aṟattiṉippūṇṭa oppaṟṟa periya mēṟkōḷiṉālē, aṟakkatir āḻi tiṟappaṭa uruṭṭi-aṟamākiya katirkaḷiyuṭiya āḻiyi ulakattiṟ palavakiyāka uruṭṭi, kāmaṟ kaṭanta vāmaṉ pātam - māraṉi veṉṟa puttaṉatu tiruvaṭikaḷi, taki pārāṭṭutal allatu yāvatum mikinā illēṉ - pārāṭṭit tutittaliyaṉṟi vēṟuporuḷkaḷip paṟṟic ciṟitum pēcāta nāviṉiyuṭiyēṉ, vēntē vāḻka eṉa-maṉṉava vāḻvāyāka eṉṟu cutamati kūṟa; 
ulaka nōṉpu-ilvāḻkkiyiliruntu ceyyum nōṉpu, oḻukkam; ataṉāl illaṟattār ulakanōṉpikaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. kati- piṟappu. puttaṉ aruḷālē palavakip piṟappum eṭuttāṉeṉap puttacātakak katikaḷ kūṟāniṟkum: 1"eṟumpukaṭi yayaṉmutalā veṇṇiṟanta veṉṟurikkap piṟantiṟanta yōṉitoṟum piriyātu cūḻpōki, evvuṭampi levvuyirkkum yātoṉṟā liṭareytiṉ, avvuṭampi luyirkkuyirā yaruḷpoḻiyun tiruvuḷḷam" 2"vāṉāṭum pariyāyu mariṇa māyum vaṉkēḻaṟ kaḷiṟāyu meṇkāṟ puṇmāṉ, tāṉāyum paṇiyerumi yoruttalāyun taṭakkiyiḷaṅ kaḷiṟāyuñ caṭaṅka māyum, mīṉāyu muyalāyu maṉṉa māyu mayilāyum piṟavāyum velluñ ciṅka, māṉāyuṅkoli kaḷavu kaṭpoy kāmam varintavartā muṟintapati māṉā 'd2vūrē" eṉpaṉa kāṇka. puttaṉ taṉak keṉa vāḻāp piṟarkkuriyāḷaṉ eṉṟa karuttu "piṟarkkaṟa muyalum periyōy," "taṉṉuyirk kiraṅkāṉ piṟavuyi rōmpu ; maṉṉuyir mutalvaṉ" (11 ; 45 ;55; 114-7) eṉa innūluḷḷē piṉṉa rum vantuḷḷaṉa; 3"iruṭpāra viṉinīkki yevvuyirkkuṅ kāvaleṉa, aruṭpāran taṉicumanta vaṉṟumuta liṉṟaḷavum, matuvoṉṟu malaraṭikkīḻ vantaṭintōr yāvarkkum, potuvaṉṟi niṉak kurittō puṇṇiyaniṉ ṟirumēṉi," "tītiyal puliyatu pacikeṭu vakiniṉa tiruvuru varuḷiya tirumali perumiyi," eṉpaṉavum kāṇka. ciṟukuṭikiḻāṉ paṇṇaṉ eṉpāṉic ciṟappikku miṭattu akappāṭṭilē (54) ittoṭar muḻutum vantuḷḷatu; aruḷaṟam- aruḷākiya aṟam ; aruḷē ellā aṟaṅkaḷilum mēlāyatu eṉpatu 1"oṉṟāka nallatu kollāmi" "ahimsā paramōtarma:" eṉpa vaṟṟāṉ aṟiyappaṭum. katir - tikiriyiṉ ār. aṟatti āḻiyeṉṟa taṟkēṟpa ‘uruṭṭi' eṉṟār ; palavaki yālum avaṉ aṟavupatēcañ ceytamiyiṉ ‘tiṟampaṭa' eṉṟār; "āti mutalva ṉaṟavāḻi yāḷ vōṉ" (10 : 31 eṉpār piṉṉum; itupaṟṟiyē puttaṉit taruma cakkarap piravarttaṉācāriyaṉ eṉpar. kāmaṉ - māraṉ; aṟattiṟku māṟāṉa viruppaṅkaḷi maṉattiluṇṭākkum oru tēvaṉ; māpōti virukkattiṉ kīḻē nōṟṟirukkiyil ivaṉ ceyta iṭiyūṟukaḷi yellām veṉṟu viḷaṅkiṉamiyiṉ "kāmaṟkaṭanta vāmaṉ" eṉṟār. vāmaṉ - aḻakuḷḷavaṉ ; puttaṉ. takipāraṭṭutal-tutittal; oru col. miki nā - mikikkiṉṟa nā ; 2"kāmaṉi veṉṟō ṉāyiratteṭṭu, nāma mallatu navilā teṉṉā" eṉpatu aṟiyaṟ pālatu.
80-83.	añcol āyiḻi niṉtiṟam aṟintēṉ-aḻakiya moḻiyiyuṭiya āyiḻāy niṉ varalāṟṟiṉi aṟintēṉ, vañcinuṇ iṭi maṇimēkalitaṉi - koṭipōlum nuṇṇiya iṭiyiyuṭiya maṇimēkaliyi. cittirāpatiyāl cērtalum uṇṭeṉṟu-cittirāpatiyāl aṭitaluṅkūṭum eṉṟu kūṟi, ap poḻil āṅkavaṉ ayarntu pōyapiṉ - utayakumaraṉ ap poḻiliṉiṉṟum kāmattāl taḷarcci yuṭiyaṉāyc ceṉṟa piṉṉar; 
84-93.	paḷikkaṟi tiṟantu-maṇimēkali paḷikku maṇṭapattit tiṟantu, paṉimati mukattuk kaḷikkayal piṟaḻāk kāṭciyaḷ  āki- kuḷirmati pōlum mukattilē matartta kayalpōluṅ kaṇkaḷ piṟaḻāta kāṭciyiyuṭiyaḷāy, kaṟputtāṉ ilaḷ naṟṟava uṇarvilaḷ varuṇak kāppu ilaḷ poruḷ viliyāṭṭi eṉṟu-kaṟpillātavaḷ nalla tavavuṇarcciyillātavaḷ marapiṟkēṟṟa kāvalaṟṟavaḷ poruḷ koṭuppārkkut taṉṉi viṟkum vilimakaḷ eṉṟu ivvāṟāka, ikaḻntaṉaṉ āki nayantōṉ eṉṉātu-paḻitturit tavaṉāy virumpiṉōṉ eṉa niṉiyāmal, putuvōṉ piṉṟip pōṉatu eṉ neñcam - ētilāṉ piṉṉē eṉṉuṭiya uḷḷam ceṉṟatu; ituvō aṉṉāy kāmattu iyaṟki - aṉṉiyē iṅṅaṉa muḷḷatō kāmattiṉ iyalpu, ituvē āyiṉ keṭuka taṉtiṟam eṉa-ivvāṟāyiṉ itaṉvali keṭuvatāka eṉṟu, matumalark kuḻalāḷ maṇimēkali tāṉ cutamati taṉṉoṭu niṉṟa elliyuḷ-tēṉaviḻum malarkaḷi yaṇinta kūntaliyuṭiya avaḷ cutamatiyōṭum kūṟiniṉṟa poḻutil;
cutamatiyi nōkkiya pārvi vilakātu niṉṟāḷeṉpar ‘kaḷik kayal piṟaḻāk kāṭciyaḷāki' eṉṟār. varuṇak kāppu - varuṇa mākiya kāval eṉṟumām. maṇimēkali tiṟattu kāṭciyaḷākik keṭuka taṉṟiṟameṉa niṉṟa velliyuḷ eṉka.
94-97.	intira kōṭaṇi viḻāaṇi virumpi vantukāṇkuṟūum maṇimēkalāteyvam - intirakōṭaṇiyākiya viḻāviṉ eḻilik kāṇutaṟku virumpi vanta maṇimēkalā teyvam, patiyakattu uṟiyum ōr pintoṭiyāki - appatiyiṉ kaṇirukkiṟa oru peṇ vaṭivattuṭaṉ, maṇiyaṟip pīṭiki valaṅkoṇṭu ōṅki - paḷikkaṟiyiluḷḷa pīṭikiyi valamvantu vicumpil uyarntu; 
kōṭaṇi - muḻakkam; palavaki āravāraṅkaḷi yuṭimiyiṉ, intira viḻā-intira kōṭaṇi yeṉappaṭṭatu. "intira kōṭaṇi yinnakark kāṇa," "intira kōṭaṇi viḻavaṇi varunāḷ" (7. 17, 17; 59) eṉap piṉ varutal kāṇka. maṇi-paḷiṅku, 
98-108.	pulavaṉ-meyyaṟi vuṭiyōṉē, tīrttaṉ-tūyōṉē, puṇṇiyaṉ - aṟavōṉē, purāṇaṉ - paḻiyōṉē, ulaka nōṉpiṉ uyarntōy-ulaka nōṉpiṉāl mēm paṭṭavaṉē, eṉkō - eṉpēṉō, kuṟṟam keṭuttōy - mukkuṟṟaṅkaḷiyum aḻittōy, ceṟṟam ceṟuttōy - kaṭuñciṉattik kaṭintōy, muṟṟa uṇarnta mutalvā-muḻutum uṇarnta mutaṉmi yuṭiyōy, eṉkō-eṉpēṉō, kāmaṟ kaṭantōy - māraṉi veṉṟōy, ēmam āyōy-iṉpa mayamāy uḷḷōy, tī neṟik kaṭumpaki kaṭintōy-tīya neṟiyākiya kaṭiya pakiyi nīkkiṉōy, eṉkō-eṉpēṉō, āyirārattu āḻiyaṉ tiruntaṭi- āyiram āraṅkaḷik koṇṭa aṟavāḻiyiṉiyuṭiya niṉcevviya tiruvaṭikaḷi, nāāyiram ilēṉ ēttuvatu evaṉ eṉṟu - āyiram nāvillā yāṉ evvāṟu tutikka viyalum eṉṟu, eri maṇip pūṅkoṭi irunila maruṅkuvantu-cuṭar viṭukiṉṟa māṇikkap pūṅkoṭiyāṉatu periya pūmiyiṉ pakkalil vantu, oru taṉi tirivatu ottu-taṉṉan taṉiyē tirivatupōla, ōtiyiṉ otuṅki- meyññāṉat tuṭaṉ otuṅki, nilavari iṟantu ōr muṭaṅkunā nīṭṭum-nilavelliyik kaṭantu taṉatu vaḷinta nāvi nīṭṭu kiṉṟāḷ; 
kuṟṟam-kāma vekuḷi mayakkaṅkaḷ. ēmam - pātukāvalumām. ‘āḻiyan tiruntaṭi' eṉṟa pāṭattiṟku āyiram āraṅkaḷiyuṭiya cakkara rēki poruntiya tiruntiya aṭi eṉṟurikka. nāvāyira milēṉ ēttuva tevaṉ eṉṟatu eṉ oru nāvil aṭaṅkuva taṉṟu eṉṟapaṭi. ōti - mukkāla uṇarvu. nilavari iṟantu - puviyiṉ mēlē uyarntu. ōr: aci. nānīṭṭum - collum.
109-118.	pula vari iṟanta pukār eṉum pūṅkoṭi - aṟiviṉ elliyik kaṭanta pukār eṉṉuma pūṅkoṭi, paṉmalar ciṟanta nalnīr akaḻip puḷ oli ciṟanta teḷ aric cilampu aṭi - pala malarkaḷum ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa nalla nīriṉiyuṭiya puṭkaḷiṉ oli mikka akaḻiyākiya ciṟanta teḷḷiya ariyiṉiyuṭiya cilam paṇinta aṭiyiyum, ñāyil iñci naki maṇimēkali - ñāyiliyuṭiya matilākiya oḷḷiya maṇikaḷālāya mēkaliyiyum, vāyil maruṅku iyaṉṟa vāṉ paṇittōḷi - vāyiliṉ pakkalil aminta periya tōraṇa kampamākiya tōḷiyum uṭiyāḷ, tarunili vacciram eṉa iru kōṭṭam etir etir ōṅkiyakatir  iḷavaṉamuli-etiretir uyarnta kaṟpakat taru niṟkum kōyil vaccirappaṭi niṟkum kōyil eṉṉumiraṇṭu mākiya oḷi poruntiya iḷiya aḻakiya koṅki kaḷiyum, ār puṉi vēntaṟkup pēraḷavu iyaṟṟi - āttimāli cūṭum cōḻaṉukkup periya aḷaviṉatāka iyaṟṟappaṭṭu; ūḻi eṇṇi nīṭu niṉṟu ōṅkiya - eṇṇappaṭum pala ūḻikkālamāka nilipeṟṟuyarnta, oru perum kōyil tirumuka vāṭṭi-oppaṟṟa kōyilākiya tirumukattiṉiyum uṭiyāḷ: 
pula vari - aṟiviṉ elli; 1"pulavari yiṟanta pukaḻcāl tōṉṟal" 2"pulavari yiṟantōy pōkutal poṟēeṉ" eṉ paṉa kāṇka. malar cilampum, puḷḷoḷi cilampoliyum, akaḻi aṭi yumām. ñāyil - matiliṉ ōruṟuppu; ēppuḻikku naṭuvāy eytumaṟiyuñ cūṭṭeṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar; kuruvit taliyeṉpar aṭiyārkku nallār. paṇi - mūṅkil; īṇṭut tōraṇa kampam. etiretirōṅkiya irukōṭṭameṉka. vaccirali eṉa virittuk koḷka. vēntaṟkēṟpap periya aḷaviṉatāka iyaṟṟappaṭṭa kōyil; 1"perumpeyar maṉṉark koppa maṉi vakuttu" eṉpatuṅ kāṇka-cilampu, mēkali eṉpavaṟṟiṟkēṟpa aṭikaḷ puṇartti yuḷḷār. 
119-122.	kuṇatici maruṅkil nāḷ mutir matiyamum-kīḻtticiyil nāṉ nirampiya muḻu matiyamum, kuṭatici maruṅkil ceṉṟu vīḻ katirum-mēṟṟiciyil ceṉṟu paṭikiṉṟa ñāyiṟum, veḷḷi veṇ tōṭṭoṭu poṉ tōṭāka-veḷḷiyālākiya veṇ tōṭṭuṭaṉ poṉṉālāya tōṭumāka, eḷḷaṟu tirumukam poliyap peytalum-ikaḻtalaṟṟa aḻakiya mukam poliyumāṟu aṇitalum; 
nāḷ-kali. niṟiyuvā nāḷil mati kīḻtticikkaṇ utittum, pariti mēṟṟiciyiṟ paṭintum kōyiliṉ iru maruṅkum oruṅku tōṉṟutaliṉ, avaṟṟip pukārc celvi kōyilākiya mukattiṉ iru maruṅkum tōṭāka aṇintaṉaḷ; eṉṟāḷ; eṉavē antip poḻutu vantami peṟṟām; itu pariyāyavaṇi. tirumukavāṭṭi peytalum eṉka. 
123-141.	aṉṉac cēval ayarntu viḷiyāṭiya taṉṉuṟu peṭiyit tāmari aṭakka - aṉṉac cēvalāṉatu meymmaṟantu viḷiyāṭiya taṉatu pēṭiyit tāmari malar kuvintu taṉṉuḷ aṭakkik koḷḷa pūmpoti citiyak kiḻittup peṭi koṇṭu ōṅku irun teṅkiṉ uyar maṭal ēṟa-pūliṉatu kaṭṭu aḻiyumāṟu kiḻittup pēṭṭiṉik koṇṭu uyarnta periya teṉṉiyiṉatu vaḷarnta maṭaliṟ cēravum, aṉṟil pēṭiarik kural aḻiic ceṉṟu vīḻpoḻutu cēvaṟku icippa-aṉṟiliṉ pēṭu melliya kuralāl cēvali aḻittu ñāyiṟuceṉṟu maṟiyum antip poḻuti urikkavum, pavaḷac ceṅkāl paṟavik kāṉattuk kuvaḷi mēynta kuṭakkaṭ cētā-pavaḷampōṟ civanta kālkaḷiyuṭiya aṉṉappuṭkaḷ niṟinta kāṉattil kuvaḷi malari mēynta tiraṇṭa kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya cevviya pacukkaḷ, mulipoḻi tīmpāl eḻu tukaḷ avippa - maṭiyiliruntu poḻikiṉṟa iṉiya pāl nilattiṉiṉṟum eḻukiṉṟa puḻutiyi avippa, kaṉṟu niṉi kurala maṉṟu vaḻip paṭā-kaṉṟukaḷi niṉinta kurali yuṭiyaṉavāy maṉṟukaḷiṉ vaḻiyē cellavum, anti antaṇar centīp pēṇa - antaṇarkaḷ antic cen tīyi vaḷarkkavum, pintoṭi makaḷir palar viḷakku eṭuppa - paciya vaḷiyalaṇinta makaḷir palar viḷakkukaḷi ēṟṟavum, yāḻōr marutattu iṉṉaram puḷara - yāḻiṉiyuṭiya pāṇar yāḻiṉ narampiṉi varuṭi iṉiya marutap paṇṇi yeḻuppavum, kōvalar mulli kuḻal mēṟkoḷḷa - kōvalar mullip paṇṇi vēyṅ kuḻaliṉiṭamāka icippavum, amaraka maruṅkil kaṇavaṉi iḻantu-pōrkkaḷattil kaṇavaṉi iḻantu, tamar akam pukūum oru makaḷ pōla-tammavariṭam cellum oru maṅkiyippōla, katir āṟṟuppaṭutta mutirāt tuṉpamōṭu - ñāyiṟṟiṉip pōkkiya muṭivillāta tuṉpattuṭaṉ, anti eṉṉum pacali meyyāṭṭi - antip poḻutu eṉkiṉṟa pacali pōrtta mēṉiyi yuṭiyāḷ, vantu iṟuttaṉaḷāl mānakar maruṅku eṉ - periya nakarattiṉ maruṅkē vantu taṅkiṉaḷ eṉka. 
ayarntu - virumpi eṉṟumām. aṭakka - aṭakkalāl. iraviṟ ciṟitum pirintiruttalarumiyiṉ ‘cēvaṟ kicippa' eṉṟār. kāṉam - īṇṭu nīrnili. cētā - cevvip pacu ; cenniṟamuṭiya pacuvumām. kaṉṟiṉi niṉintamiyāṟ pāl poḻivaṉavāyiṉa. maṉṟu-āṉiṉaṅkaḷi aṭittu vikkum vēli naṭuvaṇatāya veḷiyiṭam; koṭṭilumām. pakalil mēyacceṉṟa āṉiṉaṅkaḷ mālippoḻutil kaṉṟi niṉintu aḻikkuṅ kuralōṭu pōntu maṉṟilē pukutal iyalpu; 1''āṉkaṇam, kaṉṟu payirkurala maṉṟuniṟi pukutara,'' 2"matavu naṭi nallāṉ, vīṅkumāṇ cerutta ṟīmpāl piliṟṟak, kaṉṟu payir kurala maṉṟuniṟi pukutaru, māliyum'' eṉpaṉavuṅ kāṇka. eṭuttal - koḷuttal, 'yāḻōr...puḷara' - īṇṭu marutattiṟkuriya iyipu pulappaṭṭilatu; ivvaṭi iṭippukuttup pōlum. 'mullik kuḻal' eṉṟa pāṭattiṟku mulliyik kuḻalukku aṭiyākkuka ; eṉṉi? 3"mullik koṭiyāl muppuriyākat teṟṟiya vaḷiyi vaḷivāyk kaṭceṟittūtaliṉ mullik kuḻalāyiṟṟu" eṉa aṭiyārkku nallār urittaliṉ eṉka. tamar-tanti, tamiyaṉ mutaliyōr. mutirtal īṇṭu muṭital; mēl mutirtaṟku iṭaṉillāta eṉṟumām. kaṇavaṉip pirintāṭku mey pacakkumākaliṉ ‘pacali meyyāṭṭi' eṉṟār; īṇṭup pacaliyāvatu antip poḻutiṉ puṟkeṉṟa niṟam. tirumuka vāṭṭi tōṭākap peytalum pacali meyyāṭṭi mānakar maruṅku vantiṟuttaṉaḷ eṉka. 
pūṅkoṭi uruvam peyarppa, cūḻvōṉ cutamati muka nōkki uriyeṉa, urippa, akalvōṉ eytiyatu uriyeṉa, avaḷ caṅkatarumaṉ aruḷiya vāmaṉ pātattip pārāṭṭu
talallatu mikinā illēṉ eṉa, avaṉ pōyapiṉ, maṇimēkali cutamatiyōṭu niṉṟa velliyuḷ, maṇimēkalā teyvam pin toṭiyākip pīṭikiyi valaṅkoṇṭu ōṅki otuṅki nānīṭṭum ; nīṭṭuḻi, tirumukavāṭṭi peytalum, pacali meyyāṭṭi mutirāt tuṉpamoṭu mānakar maruṅku vantiṟuttaṉaḷ eṉṟu muṭikka. 
maṇimēkalā teyvam vantu tōṉṟiya 
kāti muṟṟiṟṟu. 


6. cakkaravāḷakkōṭṭa muritta kāti 
antimāli vantu nīṅkiyatu. tiṅkaḷmaṇṭilam veḷḷik kuṭattiṉiṉṟu pāl corivatupōl taṇkatiri eṅkaṇum poḻintatu; maṇimēkalā teyvam mīṭṭum putta tēvaṉatu pātapīṭikiyi ētti, aṅku niṉṟa cutamatiyi nōkki, 'nīṅkaḷ iṅkē niṟṟaṟkuk kāraṇam yātu? eṉṉa tuṉpamuṟṟīrkaḷ?' eṉa viṉava, avaḷ utayakumaraṉ vantu kūṟic ceṉṟatic colliṉaḷ. collalum, atteyvam avaḷi nōkki, "utayakumaraṉukku maṇimēkalipāluḷḷa vēṭki ciṟitum taṇintilatu; aṟavōr vaṉameṉṟu itiṉiṉṟu avaṉ akaṉṟaṉaṉāyiṉum, itaṉik kaṭantu celliṉ puṟattuḷḷa vītiyil ivaḷi akappaṭuttuvaṉ; nīṅkaḷ ivvaṉattic cūḻnta matiliṉ mēṟṟicikkaṇṇatākiya ciṟiya vāyil vaḻiyē ceṉṟu mātavar uṟiyum cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭatti aṭiyiṉ yātoru tuṉpamum aṇukātu: ākaliṉ aṅkē ceṉmiṉ." eṉṟurittatu. urittalum, cutamati, 'yāvarum ataṉic cuṭukāṭṭuk kōṭṭa meṉṟu kūṟāniṟpar; māruta vēkaṉum nīyumē cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭa meṉṟu kūṟuvīr; ataṟkuk kāraṇam yātu? colluka.' eṉṟāḷ.
