eṉṟalum at teyvam, kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉam tōṉṟiya kālattu uṭaṉṟōṉṟiyatāṉa appuṟaṅkāṭṭiṉ iyalpaṉittiyum viḷaṅkak kūṟi, "tavattuṟi mākkaḷ mikapperuñ celvar, īṟṟiḷam peṇṭir āṟṟāp pālakar, mutiyōr eṉṉāṉ iḷiyōr eṉṉāṉ, koṭun toḻilāḷaṉ koṉṟaṉaṉ kuvippa iv aḻalvāyc cuṭali tiṉṉak kaṇṭum, kaḻiperuñ celvak kaḷḷāṭṭayarntu, mikka nallaṟam virumpātu vāḻum, makkaḷiṟ ciṟanta maṭavō ruṇṭō?" eṉa aṟañ ceyyātār poruṭṭu iraṅki, "attaṉmiyatāṉa cuṭukāṭṭil taṉiyē ceṉṟu pēykōṭpaṭṭu iṟanta cārṅkala ṉeṉṉum pārppaṉac ciṟuvaṉ tāyākiya kōtamiyiṉ muṟiyīṭṭik kēṭṭuvanta campāpati yeṉṉum teyvam, "aṇaṅkum pēyum āruyir kavarā ; niṉ makaṉatu aṟiyāmiyē paṟṟukkōṭāka iḻavūḻ avaṉuyirik kavarntuceṉṟatu; niṉ uyiri vāṅkikkoṇṭu avaṉuyiri aḷittal iyalpaṉṟu ! uyir uṭampiṉiṉṟu nīṅkiṉ atu viṉivaḻiyē ceṉṟu vēṟu piṟappaṭivatil aiyamilli; ‘ulaka maṉṉavarkku uyirkkuyir īvōr ilarō, inta īmap puṟaṅkāṭṭu, aracark kamintaṉa āyiraṅkōṭṭam' allavō ? ic cakkaravāḷat tuḷḷa tēvarkaḷil yārēṉum nī kēṭṭa varam koṭuppāruḷarāyiṉ yāṉum ataṉit tarutaṟ kuriyēṉāvēṉ," eṉṟurittu, pirama kaṇaṅkaḷ mutalākac cakkaravāḷattiluḷḷa varaṅkoṭukkum āṟṟaluṭiya tēvarkaḷi yellāmvaruvittuk kōtamikku muṉṉiṟutti, ivaḷuṟṟa tuṉpam itu ; ivaḷatu varuttattip pōkkuvīrāka, eṉṟu nikaḻntavaṟṟic colla, attēvaraṉivarum campāpatikūṟiya vāṟē kūṟiṉar; ataṉik kēṭṭu uṇmi yaṟintu oruvāṟu varutta moḻinta kōtami makaṉip puṟaṅkāṭṭiliṭṭu iṟantu pōyiṉaḷ.
piṉpu campāpatiyiṉ āṟṟali yāvarkkum pulappaṭuttal vēṇṭi, ellāt tēvarkaḷum kūṭiyaviṭattu avarkaḷ kūṭiyataṟku aṟikuṟiyāka, ulakiṉ naṭuvēyuḷḷa mērumaliyum, ataṉic cūḻnta eḻuvakik kuṉṟaṅkaḷum nāṉku peruntīvukaḷum, iraṇṭāyiram ciṟu tīvukaḷum, ēṉiya iṭa vakikaḷum ākiya ivaṟṟip pulappaṭutti, āṅkāṅku vāḻum uyirkaḷiyum maṇṇīṭṭiṉāl vakuttu mayaṉāl nirumikkappaṭṭa tākaliṉ cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭameṉap peyarpeṟṟatu; itu cuṭukāṭṭic cūḻnta matiṟ puṟattuḷḷatākaliṉ ‘cuṭukāṭṭukkōṭṭa'meṉṟu yāvarāṉum kūṟappaṭum; itaṉ varalāṟu ituvākum" eṉṟurikkak kēṭṭukkoṇṭirunta maṇimēkali ‘makkaḷ vāḻkki ittakiyatu,' eṉa iraṅkikkūṟi yirukkiyil, cutamati tūṅkutaluṟṟaṉaḷ. appoḻutu maṇimēkalā teyvam maṇimēkaliyit taḻuvi yeṭuttu, ākāyavaḻiyē muppatu yōcaṉi teṟkē ceṉṟu, kaṭalcūḻnta maṇi pallavam eṉṉum tīvil avaḷi vittakaṉṟatu. (itil pukār nakariṉ puṟaṅ kāṭṭiṉiyalpukaḷum, akkālattil iṟantōri aṭakkañ ceyyumuṟikaḷum kūṟiyiruppaṉa ciṟappāka aṟiyat takkavi. 
		anti māli nīṅkiya piṉṉar
vantu tōṉṟiya malarkatir maṇṭilam
cāṉṟōr taṅkaṇ eytiya kuṟṟam
tōṉṟuvaḻi viḷaṅkun tōṟṟam pōla
5		mācaṟu vicumpiṉ maṟuniṟaṅ kiḷara
ācaṟa viḷaṅkiya vantīn taṇkatir
	veḷḷiveṇ kuṭattup pālcori vatupōl
	kaḷḷaviḻ pūmpoḻil iṭiyiṭi coriya
uruvu koṇṭa miṉṉē pōlat
10		tiruvi liṭṭut tikaḻtaru mēṉiyaḷ
āti mutalvaṉ aṟavāḻi yāḷvōṉ
pāta pīṭiki paṇintaṉa ḷēttip
patiyakat tuṟiyumōr pintoṭi yākic
cutamati nallāḷ taṉmukam nōkki
15		īṅku niṉṟīr eṉṉuṟ ṟīreṉa
āṅkava ḷāṅkavaṉ kūṟiya turittalum 
araciḷaṅ kumaraṉ āyiḻi taṉmēl
taṇiyā nōkkan tavirntila ṉāki
aṟattōr vaṉameṉ ṟakaṉṟaṉa ṉāyiṉum
20		puṟattōr vītiyiṟ poruntuta loḻiyāṉ
perunteru voḻittip peruvaṉañ cūḻnta
tirunteyiṟ kuṭapāṟ ciṟupuḻi pōki
mikka mātavar virumpiṉa ruṟiyum
cakkara vāḷak kōṭṭam pukkāl
25		kaṅkul kaḻiyiṉuṅ kaṭunavi yeytātu
aṅkunīr pōmeṉ ṟarunteyva murippa
vañca viñciyaṉ māruta vēkaṉum
añceñ cāyal nīyu mallatu
neṭunakar maruṅkiṉ uḷḷō rellāñ
30		cuṭukāṭṭuk kōṭṭa meṉṟala turiyār
cakkara vāḷak kōṭṭa maḵteṉa
mikkōy kūṟiya uripporu ḷaṟiyēṉ
īṅkitaṉ kāraṇa meṉṉi yōveṉa
āṅkataṉ kāraṇam aṟiyak kūṟuvaṉ
35		mātavi makaḷoṭu valliruḷ variṉum
nīkē ḷeṉṟē nēriḻi kūṟumin
nāmap pērūr taṉṉoṭu tōṉṟiya
īmap puṟaṅkā ṭīṅkita ṉayalatu
ūrā naṟṟēr ōviyap paṭuttut
40		tēvar pukutarūuñ ceḻuṅkoṭi vāyilum
nelluṅ karumpu nīruñ cōliyum
nalvaḻi yeḻutiya nalaṅkiḷar vāyilum
veḷḷi veṇcuti iḻukiya māṭat
	tuḷḷuru veḻutā veḷḷiṭi vāyilum
45		maṭitta cevvāyk kaṭutta nōkkiṉ
toṭutta pācattup piṭitta cūlattu
neṭunili maṇṇīṭu niṉṟa vāyilum
nāṟperu vāyilum pāṟpaṭ ṭōṅkiya
kāppuṭi yiñcik kaṭivaḻaṅ kāriṭi
50		uliyā uḷḷamō ṭuyirkkaṭaṉ iṟuttōr
talitūṅku neṭumaran tāḻntupuṟañ cuṟṟip
pīṭiki yōṅkiya perumpali muṉṟil
kāṭamar celvi kaḻiperuṅ kōṭṭamum
aruntavark kāyiṉum aracark kāyiṉum
55		oruṅkuṭaṉ māynta peṇṭirk kāyiṉum
nālvēṟu varuṇap pālvēṟu kāṭṭi
iṟantōr maruṅkiṟ ciṟantōr ceyta
kuṟiyavu neṭiyavuṅ kuṉṟukaṇ ṭaṉṉa
cuṭuma ṇōṅkiya neṭunilik kōṭṭamum
60		aruntiṟaṟ kaṭavuṭ ṭiruntupalik kantamum

uṇṭukaṇ paṭukkum uṟiyuṭ kuṭikiyum
tūmak koṭiyuñ cuṭarttō raṇaṅkaḷum
65		īmap pantarum yāṅkaṇum parantu
cuṭuvō riṭuvōr toṭukuḻip paṭuppōr
tāḻvayi ṉaṭippōr tāḻiyiṟ kavippōr
iravum pakalum iḷivuṭaṉ ṟariyātu
varuvōr peyarvōr māṟāc cummiyum
70		eñciyōr maruṅkiṉ īmañ cāṟṟi
neñcu naṭukkuṟūum neyta lōciyum
tuṟavō riṟanta toḻuviḷip pūcalum
piṟavō riṟanta aḻuviḷip pūcalum
nīṇmuka nariyiṉ tīviḷik kūvum
75		cāvōrp payiruṅ kūkiyiṉ kuralum
pula'd2vūṇ poruntiya kurāliṉ kuralum
ūṇṭali tuṟṟiya āṇṭalik kuralum
naṉṉīrp puṇari naḷikaṭa lōtiyiṉ
iṉṉā iciyoli eṉṟuniṉ ṟaṟātu
80		tāṉṟiyum oṭuviyum uḻiñcilu mōṅkik
kāṉṟiyuñ cūriyuṅ kaḷḷiyu maṭarntu
kāypacik kaṭumpēy kaṇaṅkoṇ ṭīṇṭum
mālamar peruñciṉi vāki maṉṟamum
veṇṇiṇan taṭiyoṭu mānti makiḻciṟantu
85		puḷḷiṟi kūṟum veḷḷiṉ maṉṟamum
cuṭali nōṉpikaḷ oṭiyā vuḷḷamoṭu
maṭitī yuṟukkum vaṉṉi maṉṟamum
virata yākkiya ruṭitali tokuttāṅ
kiruntoṭarp paṭukkum iratti maṉṟamum
90		piṇantiṉ mākkaḷ niṇampaṭu kuḻiciyil
viruntāṭ ṭayarum veḷḷiṭi maṉṟamum
aḻaṟpey kuḻiciyum puḻaṟpey maṇṭiyum
veḷḷiṟ pāṭiyum uḷḷīṭ ṭaṟuviyum
parinta māliyum uṭinta kumpamum
95		nellum poriyuñ cilpali yariciyum
yāṅkaṇum paranta ōṅkirum paṟantali
tavattuṟi mākkaḷ mikapperuñ celvar
īṟṟiḷam peṇṭi rāṟṟāp pālakar
mutiyō reṉṉāṉ iḷiyō reṉṉāṉ
100		koṭun toḻi lāḷaṉ koṉṟaṉaṉ kuvippaviv
aḻalvāyc cuṭali tiṉṉak kaṇṭum

makkaḷiṟ ciṟanta maṭavō ruṇṭō
105		āṅkatu taṉṉiyō raruṅkaṭi nakareṉac
cārṅkala ṉeṉpōṉ taṉivaḻic ceṉṟōṉ
eṉpun taṭiyu mutiramu miyākkiyeṉ
ṟaṉpuṟu mākkaṭ kaṟiyac cāṟṟi
vaḻuvoṭu kiṭanta puḻuvūṉ piṇṭattu
110		alattakam ūṭṭiya aṭinari vāykkoṇ

115		uṭiyak kavvi yoṭuṅkā vōtiyum

120		karuntali vāṅkik kiyakat tēnti

kumiḻō mūkkō itaḻō kavirō

125		kaṇṭoṭ ṭuṇṭu kaviyaṭi peyarttut

viṇṭōr ticiyiṉ viḷittaṉaṉ peyarntīṅ
kemmaṉi kāṇāy īmac cuṭaliyiṉ
130		vemmutu pēykkeṉ uyirkoṭut tēṉeṉat

pārppāṉ ṟaṉṉoṭu kaṇṇiḻan tiruntavit
tīttoḻi lāṭṭiyeṉ ciṟuvaṉ ṟaṉṉi
yārumil tamiyē ṉeṉpatu nōkkātu
135		āruyi ruṇṭa taṇaṅkō pēyō
tuṟiyu maṉṟamun tolvali māṉum
uṟiyuḷuṅ kōṭṭamuṅ kāppāy kāvāy
takavili kollō campā patiyeṉa
makaṉmey yākkiyi mārpuṟat taḻīi
140		īmap puṟaṅkāṭ ṭeyiṟpuṟa vāyilil

145		poṉṉiṟ polinta niṟattāḷ tōṉṟa

150		aṇaṅkum pēyum āruyi ruṇṇā
piṇaṅkunūṉ mārpaṉ pētukan tāka
ūḻviṉi vantivaṉ uyiruṇṭu kaḻintatu

eṉṉuyir koṇṭivaṉ uyirtan taruḷiṉeṉ
155		kaṇṇil kaṇavaṉi ivaṉkāt tōmpiṭum
ivaṉuyir tanteṉ uyirvāṅ keṉṟalum
mutumū tāṭṭi iraṅkiṉaḷ moḻivōḷ
aiya muṇṭō āruyir pōṉāl
ceyviṉi maruṅkiṟ ceṉṟupiṟap peytutal
160		āṅkatu koṇarntuniṉ āriṭar nīkkutal
īṅkeṉak kāvatoṉ ṟaṉṟunī yiraṅkal
koliyaṟa māmeṉuṅ koṭuntoḻiṉ mākkaḷ
avalap paṭiṟṟuri yāṅkatu maṭavāy
ulaka maṉṉavark kuyirkkuyi rīvōr
165		ilarō inta īmap puṟaṅkāṭ
ṭaracark kamintaṉa āyiraṅ kōṭṭam
nirayak koṭumoḻi nīyoḻi yeṉṟalum
tēvar taruvar varameṉ ṟorumuṟi
nāṉmaṟi yantaṇar naṉṉū lurikkum
170		māperun teyva nīyaru ḷāviṭiṉ
yāṉō kāvēṉ eṉṉuyi rīṅkeṉa
ūḻi mutalvaṉ uyirtari ṉallatu
āḻit tāḻi yakavarit tirivōr
tāntariṉ yāṉun tarukuvaṉ maṭavāy
175		īṅke ṉāṟṟaluṅ kāṇpā yeṉṟē
nālvaki marapi ṉarūpap piramarum
nāṉāl vakiyi ṉurūpap piramarum
iruvakic cuṭarum irumū vakiyiṟ
peruvaṉap peytiya teyvata kaṇaṅkaḷum
180		palvaki yacurarum paṭutuya ruṟūum
eṇvaki narakarum iruvicum piyaṅkum
paṉmī ṉīṭṭamum nāḷuṅ kōḷum
taṉṉakat taṭakkiya cakkara vāḷattu
varantaraṟ kuriyōr tamimuṉ niṟutti
185		aranti keṭumiva ḷaruntuya rituveṉac
campā patitāṉ urittaam muṟiyē
eṅkuvāḻ tēvarum urippak kēṭṭē
kōtami yuṟṟa koṭuntuyar nīṅki
īmac cuṭaliyiṉ makaṉiyiṭ ṭiṟantapiṉ
190		campā patitaṉ āṟṟal tōṉṟa

cūḻkaṭal vaḷiiya āḻiyaṅ kuṉṟattu
naṭuvu niṉṟa mēruk kuṉṟamum
puṭiyi ṉiṉṟa eḻuvakik kuṉṟamum
195		nālvaki marapiṉ māperun tīvum
ōrī rāyirañ ciṟṟuṭit tīvum
piṟavum āṅkataṉ iṭavaki yuriyaṉa
peṟumuṟi marapiṉ aṟivuvarak kāṭṭi
āṅkuvā ḻuyirkaḷum avvuyi riṭaṅkaḷum
200		pāṅkuṟa maṇṇīṭṭiṟ paṇpuṟa vakuttu
mikka mayaṉāl iḻikkap paṭṭa
cakkara vāḷak kōṭṭamīṅ kitukāṇ
iṭupiṇak kōṭṭat teyiṟpuṟa mātaliṉ
cuṭukāṭṭuk kōṭṭa meṉṟala turiyār
205		itaṉvara vituveṉ ṟirunteyva murikka
mataṉiṉ neñcamoṭu vāṉṟuya reytip
piṟantōr vāḻkki ciṟantō ḷurippa
iṟantiruḷ kūrnta iṭiyiruḷ yāmattut
tūṅkutuyi leytiya cutamati yoḻiyap
210		pūṅkoṭi taṉṉip poruntit taḻīi
antaram āṟā āṟin tiyōcaṉit
teṉṟici maruṅkiṟ ceṉṟutiri yuṭutta
maṇipal lavattiṭi maṇimē kalāteyvam
aṇiyiḻi taṉṉivit takaṉṟatu tāṉeṉ.

1-8.		antimāli nīṅkiya piṉṉar - antippoḻutākiya mālik kālam kaḻinta piṉṉar, vantu tōṉṟiya malar katir maṇṭilam- vantu utitta paranta kiraṇaṅkaḷiyuṭiya tiṅkaḷ maṇṭilam, cāṉṟōr taṅkaṇ eytiya kuṟṟam - uyarkuṭit tōṉṟiya mēṉmakkaḷpālaṭinta kuṟṟamāṉatu, tōṉṟuvaḻi viḷaṅkum tōṟṟampōla - tōṉṟumiṭattu viḷaṅkumiyal piṉippōla, mācu aṟu vicumpil maṟu niṟam kiḷara - vicumpiṉkaṇ mācaṟṟa niṟattiṉiṭam kaḷaṅkam viḷaṅka, ācu aṟa viḷaṅkiya amtīm taṇ katir - kuṟṟamaṟṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa aḻakiya tīviya kuḷirnta katiriṉi, veḷḷi veṇkuṭattup pāl corivatupōl - veḷḷik kuṭat tiṉiṉṟu pālic corivatupōla, kaḷaviḻ pūmpoḻil iṭiyiṭi coriya - tēṉ aviḻukiṉṟa malarc cōliyil iṭiyiṭiyē poḻiya;
antimāli-antiyākiya māli. tōṉṟuvaḻi viḷaṅkum eṉṟatu cāṉṟōrpāl kuṟṟam uṇṭāya appoḻutē atu yāvarum aṟiya veḷippaṭum eṉṟavāṟām; avar veḷippaṭu miṭattu atuvum veḷippaṭum eṉṟumām: tiṅkaḷ tūymi uṭiyatākalāṉum ataṉkaṇ kaḷaṅkam ataṟku māṟāka iruttalāṉum atu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟiṟṟu. 1"kuṭippiṟantār kaṇviḷaṅkuṅ kuṟṟam vicumpiṉ, matikkaṇ maṟuppō luyarntu" eṉpataṉāṟ cāṉṟōriṭik kuṟṟam yāvarumaṟiya niṟṟal kāṇka. īṇṭup poruḷ uvamamākkap paṭṭatu; eṉṉi? 2poruḷē yuvamañ ceytaṉar moḻiyiṉum, maruḷaṟu ciṟappiṉaḵ tuvama mākum" eṉpavākalāṉ. cāṉṟōr kuṟṟam viḷaṅkun tōṟṟampōlak katir maṇṭilam maṟuniṟaṅ kiḷarat taṇ katirip pāl corivatupōl iṭiyiṭic coriya veṉka. tiṅkaḷukku veḷḷikkuṭamum taṇkatirkkup pālum uvami.
9-15. 	uruvu koṇṭa miṉṉē pōla-peṇṇuruvaṅkoṇṭa  miṉṉalip pōlavum, tiruvil iṭṭut tikaḻtaru mēṉiyaḷ - intira villippōlavum oḷiviṭṭu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa tirumēṉiyi yuṭiya atteyvam, ātimutalvaṉ aṟavāḻi āḷvōṉ pāta pīṭiki paṇintaṉaḷ ētti - aṟavāḻiyiṉi uruṭṭukiṉṟa āti mutalvaṉatu pātapīṭikiyi vaṇaṅkit tutittu, patiyakattu uṟiyum ōr pintoṭiyāki-ap patiyiluṟikiṉṟa oru peṇ vaṭivukoṇṭu, cutamati nallāḷ taṉmukam nōkki - cutamatiyiṉ mukatti nōkki, īṅku niṉṟīreṉ uṟṟīr eṉa-īṇṭu niṟkiṉṟavarkaḷē nīvir aṭinta tuṉpam yātu? eṉa viṉava;
uruvu koṇṭa miṉ:ilporuḷuvami. miṉpōlavum tiruvil pōlavum eṉka. tiruvil - vāṉavil. 3tiruvi liṭṭut tikaḻtaru mēṉiyaṉ" eṉa varutal kāṇka. kaṭavuḷar iṉṉa kālattu ivvāṟu tōṉṟuvar eṉpatu pulappaṭāmiyāṉ, aṅṅaṉam tōṉṟum vāṉavil avarkku uvamiyāyiṟṟu. tiruvil - aḻakiya oḷiyeṉṟumām. āti mutalvaṉ - puttaṉ. aṟavāḻi - aṟamākiya tikiri. pātapīṭiki- puttatēvaṉ tiruvaṭi yaminta pīṭam; uvavaṉattiṟ paḷikkaṟiyil uḷḷatu. paṇintaṉaḷ: muṟṟeccam. muṉṉarum 1"patiyakat tuṟiyumōr pintoṭi yāki" eṉa vantami kāṇka. niṉṟīr : viṉippeyar; atu maṇimēkaliyiyum uḷappaṭuttiṟṟu.
16. 		āṅku avaḷ āṅku avaṉ kūṟiyatu urittalum-akkālic cutamati av vutayakumaraṉ moḻintataṉik kūṟiya vaḷavil; avaṉ kūṟiyatu - cittirāpatiyāṟ cērtalu muṇṭu eṉpatu. 
17-26.	araciḷaṅ kumaraṉ āyiḻi taṉmēl taṇiyāvuḷḷam tavirntilaṉ āki - maṉṉavaṉ putalvaṉ maṇimēkaliyiṭattuk koṇṭa āci nīṅkāta uḷḷattiṉit tavirntilaṉāy, aṟattōr vaṉam eṉṟu akaṉṟaṉaṉ āyiṉum-tavattōr kaḷuṭiya poḻil eṉṟu iṅku niṉṟum nīṅkiṉaṉāyiṉum, puṟattōr vītiyil poruntutal oḻiyāṉ - puṟat tiluḷḷavarkaḷatu teru viṉkaṇṇē cērtali nīṅkāṉ, perunteru oḻittu ipperuvaṉañ cūḻnta - ātalāl perun teruvukaḷi viṭṭu intap periya cōliyic cūḻntuḷḷa, tiruntu eyiṟ kuṭapāl ciṟu puḻi pōki - tiruttamuṟṟa matiliṉ mēṟṟiciyiṉiṭat tēyuḷḷa ciṟiya vāyiliṉ vaḻiyē ceṉṟu, mikka mātavar virumpiṉar uṟiyum-tavattāṉ mikka muṉivarkaḷ virumpi yuṟiyum, cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭam pukkāl - cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭatti yaṭintāl, kaṅkul kaḻiyiṉum kaṭunavi eytātu - aṅkē iraviṉik kaḻippiṉum koṭiya tuṉpa muṇṭākātu, aṅkunīr pōm eṉṟu arunteyvam urippa - ākaliṉ nīvir aṅkē celvīrāka eṉṟu ariya teyvam kūṟiyaruḷa;
aṟattōr tuṟavikaḷāṉamiyiṉ, puṟattōr eṉpataṟku avarkkup puṟamākavuḷḷa illaṟa neṟiyiṉar eṉa vurittalum amiyum; puṟattu ōr vītiyil eṉṟalumām. puḻi-nuḻi vāyil. kaḻiyiṉum - kaḻippiṉum. iṉi, āṇṭē irākkālam kaḻivatāyiṉum eṉalumām kaṭunavi - utayakumaraṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭutal.
27-33.	vañca viñciyaṉ māruta vēkaṉum amceñcāyal nīyum allatu-vañcamuṭiya viñciyaṉākiya mārutavēkaṉ eṉpāṉum aḻakiya civanta meṉmit taṉmiyiṉiyuṭiya nīyum aṉṟi, neṭunakar maruṅkiṉ uḷḷōr ellām - ip periya nakariṉkaṇ uḷḷōraṉi varum, cuṭukāṭṭuk kōṭṭam eṉṟalatu uriyār - cuṭukāṭṭuk kōṭṭam eṉpatēyaṉṟi vēṟu peyar kūṟār, cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭam aḵtu eṉa - ataṉic cakkara vāḷakkōṭṭam eṉṟu, mikkōy kūṟiya uripporuḷ aṟiyēṉ - mēmpāṭuṭiya nī kūṟiya moḻiyiṉ poruḷi aṟiyēṉ, īṅku itaṉ kāraṇam eṉṉiyō eṉa - iṅṅaṉam kūṟuvataṉ kāraṇam yātō eṉṟu cutamati kēṭpa;
vañca viñciyaṉ eṉṟāḷ, taṉṉik 1kavarntu ceṉṟu piṉṉart taṉṉiviṭṭup pirintamiyāṉ. neṭunakar-kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉam.
34-36.	āṅku ataṉ kāraṇam aṟiyak kūṟuval - aḵtu appeyar peṟṟataṉ kāraṇattit teḷiya urippēṉ, mātavi makaḷoṭu valliruḷ variṉum nīkēḷ eṉṟē nēriḻi kūṟum - mikunta iruḷiyuṭiya yāmam variṉum nī mātavi makaḷuṭaṉ ataṉik kēṭpāyāka eṉṟu maṇimēkalā teyvam kūṟukiṉṟatu; 
36-38.	in nāmap pērūr taṉṉoṭu tōṉṟiya - pakivarkku accatti viḷikkum ip periya nakarattōṭē uṇṭāya, īmap puṟaṅkāṭu īṅku itaṉ ayalatu - piṇañcuṭum viṟakaṭuk kiṉiyuṭiya cuṭukāṭu iv vuvavaṉattiṉ pakkattatu; nāmam - pukaḻumām. itaṉ - iv vuvavaṉattiṉ. 
39-49.	ūrā naltēr ōviyappaṭuttu - ōviyampōla vimāṉam vicumpiṉkaṇṇē niṟkumāṟu ceytu, tēvar pukutarūum ceḻuṅ koṭi vāyilum - viṇṇavar nuḻintu celluñ ceḻuṅ koṭiyiṉiyuṭiya vāyilum, nellum karumpum nīrum cōliyum nalvaḻi eḻutiya nalaṅkiḷar vāyilum - neṟpayirum karumpum poykiyum poḻilum naṉkaṉa meḻutiya nalañciṟanta vāyilum, veḷḷi veṇcuti iḻukiya māṭattuḷ - mikka veṇmi yuṭiya cutiyāṟ pūcappaṭṭa māṭattil, uru eḻutā veḷḷiṭi vāyilum- vaṭivaṅkaḷ eḻutappeṟāta veḷiyāṉa iṭattiṉiyuṭiya vāyilum, maṭitta cevvāyk kaṭutta nōkkil toṭutta pācattup piṭitta cūlattu - maṭikkappaṭṭa civanta vāyiyum ciṉanta nōkkiyum piṟarittoṭutta pācattiyum kiyiṟ piṭitta cūlappaṭiyiyumuṭiya, neṭunili maṇṇīṭu niṉṟa vāyilum - nīṇṭa tōṟṟattiyuṭiya pūtavaṭivam niṟkiṉṟa vāyilum ākiya, nāṟperu vāyilum pāṟpaṭṭu ōṅkiya - nāṉku periya vāyilkaḷum pakutippaṭṭu uyarnta, kāppuṭi iñcik kaṭi vaḻaṅku āriṭi - kāvaluṭiya matil cūḻnta pēykaḷ naṭamāṭukiṉṟa ariya iṭattil;
ūrāt tēr-pākarāṟ celuttappaṭātu tāṉē cellun tēr, vimāṉam. vimāṉattuṭaṉ tēvar pukuvatāka ōviyappaṭutta vāyil eṉṟalumām. nīr - poyki mutaliyaṉa. maṇṇīṭu - cutiyāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa pāvi; "maṇṇīṭṭiṟ paṇpuṟa vakuttu," "īṅkim maṇṇīṭṭu" (6: 200; 18 - 156.) eṉa mēl innūluḷ varutal kāṇka. kaṭi - pēy. 
50-53.	uliyā uḷḷamōṭu uyirkkaṭaṉ iṟuttōr-taḷarāta uḷḷattōṭu uyirākiya kaṭaṉik koṭuttōr kaḷiṉ, tali tūṅku neṭumaram tāḻntu puṟañcuṟṟi - talikaḷ toṅkukiṉṟa nīṇṭa maraṅkaḷ tāḻntu puṟattē cūḻappeṟṟu, pīṭiki ōṅkiya perum pali muṉṟil kāṭamar celvi kaḻiperuṅ kōṭṭamum- periya palipīṭam ōṅkiya muṉṟiliyuṭiya kāṭukiḻāḷiṉ mikap periya kōyilum;
uyirkkaṭaṉ iṟuttalic cilappatikārattu 1intira viḻa'd2vūreṭutta kātiyāṉum aṟika. iḵtu avippali eṉappaṭum. pali koṭuppōr cikiyi marattil muṭintuviṭṭut taliyi arivarākaliṉ "talitūṅku neṭumaram" eṉṟār ; 2"vīṅkutali neṭuṅkaḻiyiṉ micitōṟun ticitōṟum viḻittu niṉṟu, tūṅkutali, cirippaṉa kaṇ ṭuṟaṅkutali maṟantirukkuñ cuḻalkaṭ cūrppēy" eṉpatu kāṇka. kāṭamar celvi - kāṭukiḻāḷ ; turkki.
54-59. 	aruntavarkku āyiṉum aracarkku āyiṉum, oruṅkuṭaṉ māynta peṇṭirkku āyiṉum-ariya tavattāṉuyarn tōrkkum aracarkkum kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ oruṅkē uyirnītta kaṟpuṭi makaḷirkkum, nālvēṟu varuṇap pālvēṟu kāṭṭi - nālvaki marapiṉ pakuti vēṟupāṭṭiṉik kāṭṭi, iṟantōr maruṅkil ciṟantōr ceyta-iṟantōrkaḷiṉ uṭalip putittaviṭattē avarkaṭkuc ciṟantōr amitta, kuṟiyavum neṭiyavum kuṉṟu kaṇṭaṉṉa cuṭumaṇ ōṅkiya neṭunilik kōṭṭam-kuṟiyaṉavum neṭiyaṉavum ākiya kuṉṟukaḷik kaṇṭāṟpōṉṟaṉavāyc ceṅkaṟkaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu uyarnta kōṭṭaṅkaḷum;
āyiṉum eṉpatu ōr eṇṇiṭiccol; 1"potiyi lāyiṉum imaya māyiṉum......pukārēyāyiṉum" eṉpuḻippōla. ciṟantōr - urimiyuṭiyōr. cuṭumaṇ-ceṅkal. kōṭṭam-aṟi; kōyilumām.
60-65.	aruntiṟal kaṭavuḷ tiruntu palik kantamum - ariya tiṟaliyuṭiya kaṭavuḷuṭiya tiruntiya paliyiṭutaṟkuriya tūṇkaḷum, niṟikal teṟṟiyum - niṟuttappaṭṭa kallālākiya tiṇṇiyum, miṟikkaḷac cantiyum-vaḷinta iṭattiṉi yuṭiya pala vaḻikaḷuñ cērnta cantukaḷum, taṇṭum maṇṭiyum piṭittuk kāvalar uṇṭu kaṇpaṭukkum uṟiyuṭ kuṭikiyum - kiyiṟ piṭikkum kōlum uṇṇum kalamum piṭittu īmaṅ kāppōr uṇṭu tuyilum uṟiviṭamākiya kuṭilkaḷum, tūmakkoṭiyum cuṭart tōraṇaṅkaḷum-pukiyoḻuṅ kākiya koṭiyum cuṭarākiya tōraṇaṅkaḷum, īmappantarum yāṅkaṇum parantu - īmattil iṭṭa pantarkaḷum evviṭattum parakkappeṟṟu;
kantiṉkaṇ teyvamuṟitali 2"kalikeḻu kaṭavuḷ kantaṅ kiviṭa" eṉpataṉāṉum aṟika. miṟi - vaḷivu ; 3miṟikkoḷi tiruttiṉāṉē" eṉṟār piṟarum. maṇṭi-uṇṭaṟkuriya maṇkalam; irakkuṅ kalamumām; 4"ēlātu kaviḻnta eṉ iravaṉ maṇṭi" eṉpatu puṟam. kuṭiki-kuṭici. cuṭar-tīkkoḻuntu; viḷakkumām.
66-69.	cuṭuvōr iṭuvōr toṭukuḻip paṭuppōr - piṇaṅkaḷic cuṭuvōrum vāḷā iṭṭuppōvōrum tōṇṭappaṭṭa kuḻiyiliṭuvōrum, tāḻvayiṉ aṭippōr tāḻiyiṟ kavippōr - tāḻnta iṭaṅkaḷil aṭittu vippōrum tāḻiyiṉālē kavippōrum, iravum pakalum iḷivuṭaṉ tariyātu - iravum pakalum aruvaruppuṭaṉ taṅkāmal, varuvōr peyarvōr māṟāc cummiyum - varuvōrum pōvōru mākiyavarkaḷiṉ nīṅkāta oliyum;
tāḻvayiṉ - paḷḷamākiya iṭam. tāḻiyiṟ kavittalip puṟa nāṉūṟṟiṉ 227, 237, 255, 363-ām ceyyuṭkaḷāl aṟika. itu mutumakkaṭ ṭāḻi eṉappaṭum. cuṭuvōr mutaliyōrāy varuvōr peyarvōr eṉka.
70-79. 	eñciyōr maruṅkiṉ īmañcāṟṟi neñcu naṭukkuṟūum neytal ōciyum-uyir vāḻntiruppōriṭam īmam uṇṭeṉpatik kūṟi uḷḷam naṭuṅkumāṟu puriyum neytaṟ paṟiyiṉ oliyum, tuṟavōr iṟanta toḻuviḷip pūcalum-tuṟaviyar iṟantataṉāṟ pōnta tutimuḻakkamum, piṟavōr iṟanta aḻuviḷip pūcalum-illaṟattōr iṟantataṉā lākiya aḻuki yoliyum, nīḷmuka nariyiṉ tīviḷikkūvum-nīṇṭa mukattiyuṭiya nariyiṉ tīya oliyiṉiyuṭiya kūppīṭum. cāvōrp payirum kūkiyiṉ kuralum-iṟappōri yaḻikkum pērāntiyiṉ kuralum, pula'd2vūṇ poruntiya kurāliṉ kuralum - pulālākiya uṇavip poruntiya kōṭṭāṉiṉ oliyum, ūṇtali tuṟṟiya āṇṭalik kuralum - uṇavākat tali mūḷiyik kaṭittuṇṭa āṇṭalip paṟaviyiṉ muḻakkamum, naṉṉīrp puṇari naḷikaṭal otiyiṉ iṉṉā icioli eṉṟum niṉṟu aṟātu-tūnīrc cērkki yiṉiyuṭiya ceṟinta kaṭal ōciyippōl iṉṉātaṉavākiya muḻakkam eṉṟum niṉṟu nīṅkāmal;
neytal - cāppaṟi ; aḵtu īmañcāṟṟutali 1"maṇaṅ koṇṭīṇ, ṭuṇṭuṇṭuṇ ṭeṉṉum uṇarviṉāṟ cāṟṟumē, toṇṭoṇṭoṇ ṇeṉṉum paṟi" eṉpataṉālumaṟika. muṉ tuṟavōr eṉṟamiyiṉ piṟavōr illaṟattārāyiṟṟu. toḻuviḷiyākiya pūcal, aḻuviḷiyākiya pūcal eṉka ; viḷi - ōci. payirtal - aḻittal, 2"pōḻvāykkūki, cuṭṭuk kuviyeṉac cettōrp payirum, kaḷḷiyam paṟantali" eṉpataṉāl pērānti iṟantōri aḻikkumeṉpatum pōtarum. āṇṭali - āṇmakaṉatu tali pōṉṟa vaṭiva muḷḷa oruvakip paṟavi; itaṉi, 3"nīṇṭa pali pīṭattilarintu vitta neṭuṅkuñcic cirattittaṉ ṉiṉameṉṟeṇṇi, āṇṭalippuḷ arukaṇintu pārkku mālō" eṉpataṉāṉaṟika. neytalōci mutaliya iṉṉā iciyoli kaṭalōtiyiṉ eṉṟum niṉṟaṟāteṉka.
80-91.	tāṉṟiyum oṭuviyum uḻiñcilum ōṅki - tāṉṟiyum oṭuviyum uḻiñcilum uyarntu, kāṉṟiyum cūriyuṅ kaḷḷiyum aṭarntu - kāṉṟiyuñ cūriyuṅ kaḷḷiyum ceṟintu, kāypacik kaṭumpēy kaṇaṅkoṇṭu īṇṭum - kāykiṉṟa paciyiyuṭiya koṭiya pēykaḷ kūṭṭamākat tiraṇṭirukkum, māl amar peruñciṉi vāki maṉṟamum - mukil taṅkukiṉṟa peruṅ kiḷi kaḷiyuṭiya vāki maram niṟkum maṉṟamum, veḷ niṇam taṭiyoṭu mānti makiḻ
ciṟantu - veḷḷiya niṇattiṉit taciyuṭaṉ uṇṭu makiḻcci mikuntu, puḷ iṟi kūrum veḷḷil maṉṟamum- paṟavikaḷ taṅkukiṉṟa viḷāmaram niṟkum maṉṟamum, cuṭali nōṉpikaḷ oṭiyā uḷḷamoṭu maṭitīyuṟukkum vaṉṉi
maṉṟamum - kāpālikar taḷarāta uḷḷattuṭaṉ cōṟaṭukiṉṟa tīyiyuṭiya vaṉṉimaram niṟkum maṉṟamum. virata yākkiyar uṭitali tokuttāṅku iruntoṭarp paṭukkum iratti maṉṟamum - virataṅ kākkum uṭampiṉi yuṭiyōr uṭinta talikaḷit tokuttup periya mālikaḷākac ceyyum ilantimaram niṟkum maṉṟamum, piṇam tiṉ mākkaḷ - piṇattiṉit tiṉṉum mākkaḷāyiṉār, niṇampaṭu kuḻiciyil viruntāṭṭu ayarum - niṇam poruntiya pāṉiyil viruntu ceykiṉṟa, veḷḷiṭi maṉṟamum - veḷiyāṉa iṭattiyuṭiya maṉṟamum ākiya ivviṭaṅkaḷil;
oṭuvi-oṭumaram. uḻiñcil - vāki; uṉṉamumām. tāṉṟi mutaliya mūṉṟum maramum, kāṉṟi mutaliya mūṉṟum ceṭiyumām. māl-mēkam. iṟikūrtal-taṅkutal. cuṭali nōṉpikaḷ-mayāṉattiliruntu nōṉpiyaṟṟuvōr; kāpālika camayattōr. virata yākkiyar -māvirata camayattōr. palar kūṭumiṭam maṉṟa meṉappaṭum; paṇṭi nāḷil maṉṟaṅkaḷ perumpālum marattaṭiyil iruntaṉa; pēykaḷ kūṭutaliṉ vākiyaṭi maṉṟa māyiṟṟu; iṅṅaṉamē ēṉiyavum koḷka.
92-96.	aḻal pey kuḻiciyum puḻal pey maṇṭiyum - tīppeyta pāṉiyum, puḻal eṉṉum paṇṇiyam iṭṭa kalaṉum, veḷḷila pāṭiyum uḷḷīṭṭaṟuviyum-veḷḷilākiya pāṭiyum uḷḷē paṇṭaṅkaḷ iṭap paṭṭa uṟiyum, parinta māliyum uṭinta kumpamum -pāṭiyiṟkaṭṭi aṟutteṟinta mālikaḷum uṭinta kuṭaṅkaḷum, nellum poriyum cilpali ariciyum-nellum poriyum ciṟupaliyākiya ariciyum, yāṅkaṇum paranta ōṅkirum paṟantali - evviṭattum parantu kiṭakkum mikapperiya pāḻiṭattil;
puḻal-oruvakic ciṟṟuṇṭi. veḷḷiṟpāṭi: irupeya roṭṭu. uḷḷīṭṭaṟuvi-uḷḷīṭṭiyuṭiya kiḻiyumām. cilpali - ciṟupali; 
1"tūveḷḷarici, cilpalic ceytu" eṉpatu kāṇka. vāki maṉṟam mutaliya maṉṟaṅkaḷ yāṅkaṇum kuḻici mutaliyaṉa paranta paṟantali yeṉka. 
97-104.	tavattuṟi mākkaḷ mikap peruñ celvar-tava neṟiyiṟ cellun tuṟaviyar mikka peruñ celva muṭiyōr, īṟṟiḷam peṇṭir āṟṟāppālakar - īṉṟaṇimiyi yuṭiya iḷamakaḷir āṟṟāta iḷañciṟār, mutiyōr eṉṉāṉ iḷiyōr eṉṉāṉ - āṇṭil mutirntōr eṉṉāmalum iḷiyōr eṉṉāmalum, koṭun toḻilāḷaṉ koṉṟaṉaṉ kuvippa - koṭun toḻili yuṭiya kālaṉ koṉṟu kuvippa, iv aḻalvāyc cuṭali tiṉṉakkaṇṭum - aḻal vāyiṉiyuṭiya cuṭali tiṉṉak kaṇṭum, kaḻiperuñcelvak kaḷḷāṭṭu ayarntu - mikka peruñcelvamākiya kaḷḷiyuṇṭu viḷiyāṭutalic ceytu, mikka nallaṟam virumpātu vāḻum - mēṉmi tarum nallaṟaṅkaḷi virumpāmal vāḻkiṉṟa, makkaḷil ciṟanta maṭavōr uṇṭō - makkaḷiluñ ciṟanta aṟivilikaḷ uḷarō;
eṉṉāṉ eṉpataṉit tavattuṟimākkaḷ mutaliya vaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. koṉṟaṉaṉ; muṟṟeccam. aḻalākiya vāy eṉa uruvakam. ikaraccuṭṭu ataṉ koṭumiyi uṇarttiṟṟu. āriṭiyil paṟantaliyil cuṭali (49, 66, 101) eṉka. paṟantali yākiya cuṭali yeṉṉalumām. aṟivi mayakkutaliṉ celvattik kaḷ eṉṟār. ‘tavat tuṟimākkaḷ......maṭavōruṇṭō' eṉpatu teyvaṅ kūṟiyatu ; kavi kūṟiyatumām.
105-106.	āṅkatu taṉṉi ōr aruṅkaṭi nakar eṉa - aṅṅaṉamākiya puṟaṅkāṭṭi ōr ariya kāppaminta nakarameṉṟeṇṇi, cārṅkalaṉ eṉpōṉ taṉivaḻic ceṉṟāṉ - taṉivaḻiyē ceṉṟa cārṅkalaṉ eṉṉum ciṟuvaṉ;
107-109.	eṉpum taṭiyum utiramum yākki eṉṟu - eṉpun taciyum kurutiyumākiyavaṟṟi uṭampu eṉṟu, aṉpuṟu mākkaṭku aṟiyac cāṟṟi - aṉpu vikkiṉṟa makkaḷukku aṟiyakkūṟi, vaḻuvoṭu kiṭanta puḻu'd2vūṉ piṇṭattu - vaḻuvuṭaṉ kiṭanta puḻukkaḷ malinta ūṉ piṇṭamākiya uṭalil;
aṉpuṟu mākkaḷ-uṭampiṉiṭattu aṉpumikka mākkaḷ; mākkaḷ eṉṟār aimpula vuṇarcciyaṉṟi maṉavuṇarcci yillātavar eṉaṟku, vaḻu - vaḻumpu. yākki eṉpatu eṉpu mutaliyaṉavē yeṉa mākkaṭkuc cāṟṟikkiṭanta piṇṭam eṉka;    
	1"kuṭaruṅ koḻuvuṅ kurutiyu meṉpum     	toṭaru narampoṭu tōlum--iṭiyiṭiyē vitta taṭiyum vaḻumpumā maṟṟivaṟṟuḷ  ettiṟattā ḷīrṅkōti yāḷ."
eṉpatu ottu nōkkaṟpālatu.
110-119.	alattakam ūṭṭiya aṭi nari vāykkoṇṭu - cem pañcuk kuḻampu ūṭṭappeṟṟa aṭikaḷi nari vāyilē kavvik koṇṭu, ulappil iṉpamōṭu uḷikkum ōtiyum - kēṭillāta iṉpattuṭaṉ ūḷiyiṭukiṉṟa oliyum, kalippuṟa alkul kaḻuku kuṭintuṇṭu nilattali neṭuviḷi eṭukkum ōtiyum - kaliyi yoḻitta alkulik kaḻuku kuṭintu uṇṭu nilattiṉ kaṇ periya kūvutalic ceyyum ōciyum, kaṭakam ceṟinta kiyit tī nāy uṭiyak kavvi oṭuṅkā ōtiyum-kaṭakamaṇinta kiyit tīya nāy uṭiyumāṟu kavvikkoṇṭu iṭum oṭuṅkāta muḻakkamum, cāntam tōynta ēntiḷa vaṉamuli - cantaṉam pūcappeṟṟa nimirnta iḷaṅ koṅkiyi, kāynta paci eruvi kavarntūṇ ōtiyum-mikka paciyiyuṭiya paruntu kavarntuṇṇukiṉṟa ōciyum, paṇpukoḷ yākkiyiṉ veṇpali araṅkattu- iṉimiyuṭiya uṭalkaḷ venta cāmpaṟ kuviyākiya araṅkil, maṇkaṇi muḻavam āka - mārccaṉi ceṟinta taṇṇumi muḻakkamāka;
kali - uṭi; mēkaliyumām. nilittali eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ taṉṉiṭattu nilipeṟṟa eṉka. tī nāy - puṟaṅkāṭṭiṟ ṟiriyum oru vaki nāyumām. paṇpu - iṉimi ; aḻakumām. ōti palavum muḻavamāka veṉka.
119-121.	āṅku ōr karuntali vāṅkik kiyakattu ēnti - aṅkuḷḷa ōr peṇpiṇattiṉ kariya taliyip paṟittuk kiyilē ēnti, irumpēr uvakiyiṉ eḻuntu ōr pēy makaḷ-ōr peṇ pēy mikka periya makiḻcciyuṭaṉ eḻuntu ;
122-126.	puyalō kuḻalō-mukilō aṉṟik kūntalō, kayalō kaṇṇō - kayal mīṉō aṉṟik kaṇṇō, kumiḻō mūkkō - kumiḻampūvō aṉṟi mūkkō, itaḻō kavirō - utaṭō aṉṟi muruṅki malarō, pallō muttō - paṟkaḷō aṉṟi muttukkaḷō, eṉṉātu iraṅkātu-eṉṟu karutāmalum iraṅkāmalum, kaṇtoṭṭu uṇṭu - kaṇkaḷit tōṇṭi uṇṭu, kavi aṭi peyarttu - kavitta aṭikaḷip peyarttu, taṇṭāk kaḷippiṉ āṭum kūttu - taṇiyāta kaḷippuṭaṉ āṭukiṉṟa kūttiṉi;
eṉṉātu - eṉṟu karuti aticayikkātu ; iḵtu aiyavati cayattiṉ pāṟpaṭum: 1"kaviyaṭip pēymakaḷ" eṉṟār piṟarum.
127-131. 	kaṇṭaṉaṉ verīi - kaṇṭu añci, kaṭu navi eyti - ap pēyāṟ piṭikkappaṭṭu, viṇṭu - avviṭattiṉ nīṅki, ōr ticiyiṉ viḷittaṉaṉ peyarntu - ōr ticiyiṟ kūppiṭṭuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu, īṅku emmaṉi kāṇāy - em aṉṉiyē iṅkē kāṇpāyāka, īmaccuṭaliyiṉ - īmamākiya mayāṉattiṉ kaṇ uḷḷa, vemmutu pēykku eṉ uyir koṭuttēṉ eṉa - koṭiya mutu pēykku eṉ uyirik koṭuttuviṭṭēṉ eṉṟu kūṟi, tam aṉi taṉ muṉ vīḻntu mey vittalum - taṉ tāyiṉ muṉṉē vīḻntu uyir tuṟakkavum;
cārṅkala ṉeṉpōṉ taṉivaḻic ceṉṟōṉ kaṇṭaṉaṉ verīi eṉka. kaṭu navi-mikka tuṉpam ; āvatu-pēyk kōṭpaṭutal. 2"kaḻalkaṭ kūḷik kaṭunavip paṭṭōr" eṉpatukāṇka. īmac cuṭali:irupeyaroṭṭu; īmattiyuṭiya cuṭaliyeṉṟumām. īmam- piṇañ cuṭum viṟakaṭukku. mey vittal - uṭampip pōkaṭutal; iṟattal.
132-141.	pārppāṉ taṉṉoṭu kaṇ iḻantirunta - kaṇavaṉōṭu kaṇ iḻantirunta, it tīt toḻilāṭṭi eṉ ciṟuvaṉ taṉṉi- ik koṭuviṉiyēṉuṭiya putalvaṉi, yārum il tamiyēṉ eṉpatu nōkkātu - yāṉ yārumaṟṟa ēḻi yeṉpataṉik karutāmal, ār uyir uṇṭatu aṇaṅkō pēyō - aṇaṅkō aṉṟip pēyō ariya uyiriyuṇṭatu, tuṟiyum maṉṟamum tol vali maraṉum - nīrttuṟikaḷum maṉṟaṅkaḷum paḻiya vali yuṭiya maraṅkaḷum, uṟiyuḷum kōṭṭamum kāppāy - taṅku miṭaṅkaḷum kōyilkaḷu mātiyavaṟṟiṉkaṇiruntu kāttu varuvōy, kāvāy takavili kollō campāpati eṉa-campāpatiyē nī eṉ makaṉuyirik kāvāmaliruntāy takaviṉmiyi yuṭiyiyō eṉa aḻittu, makaṉ mey yākkiyi mārpuṟat taḻīi - makaṉatu uṭalākiya uyiraṟṟa yākkiyi mārpuṭaṉ taḻuvikkoṇṭu; īmap puṟaṅkāṭṭu eyilpuṟa vāyilil - iṭu kāṭṭiṉ matiṟ puṟattuḷḷa vāyilil niṉṟu, kōtami eṉpāḷ koṭun tuyar cāṟṟa - kōtami eṉṉum pārppaṉi koṭiya tuṉpattiṉik kūṟa;
pārppāṉ - cārṅkalaṉuṭiya tanti; avaṉ kaṇṇiḻan tiruntami "kaṇṇil kaṇavaṉi" (6:155.) eṉap piṉ varutalāṉum tuṇiyappaṭum. tīttoḻilāṭṭi-pārppaṉi yeṉṟumām; 1tīttiṟam purintōṉ" eṉap pārppaṉaṉ kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka; aṅki kāriyam ceypavaṉ eṉṟapaṭi. kaṇavaṉum kaṇṇiḻan tāṉākaliṉ ciṟuvaṉi yaṉṟit taṉakkuk kaḷikaṇ illi yeṉpāḷ ‘yārumil tamiyēṉ' eṉṟāḷ. nōkkātu - karutātu; nōkkaṉōkkam. maṉṟam - palarkūṭum veḷiyiṭam. takavu ili kollō-niṉṉiṭam takavu illiyō eṉṟumām; takavu - naṭuvu nili; evviṭattum yāriyuṅ kākkun teyvam īṇṭu ivaṉik kāvāmiyiṉ ‘takavili kollō' eṉṟāḷ. kōtami takavili kollō eṉa mārpuṟat taḻīi vāyiliṟ cāṟṟaveṉka.
142-145. 	kaṭivaḻaṅku vāyilil kaṭuntuyar eyti iṭiyiruḷ yāmattu eṉṉi īṅku aḻittaṉi - iruḷiyuṭiya iṭi yāmattil pēykaḷ naṭamāṭukiṉṟa iv vāyiliṉ kaṇiruntu koṭiya tukkattiṉiyaṭintu eṉṉi ippoḻutu aḻittāy, eṉ uṟṟaṉi eṉakku uri eṉṟē-nī eṉṉa tuṉpameytiṉi ataṉi eṉakkuk kūṟuvāyāka eṉṟu, poṉṉiṟ polinta niṟattāḷ tōṉṟa -poṉṉip
pōl viḷaṅkum niṟattiṉi yuṭiya campāpati tōṉṟa ;
kaṭi - pēy. poṉ-cūriyaṉ eṉpatu arumpatavuri. tōṉṟi uriyeṉṟu kūṟa eṉa vikuti pirittoṭṭutalumām.
146-149.	ārumilāṭṭi eṉ aṟiyāp pālakaṉ - ārumaṟṟa vaḷākiya eṉṉuṭiya aṟiyāc ciṟuvaṉ. īmap puṟaṅkāṭṭu eytiṉōṉ taṉṉi - īmattiṉiyuṭiya puṟaṅkāṭṭiṉ vaḻiyē vantōṉi, aṇaṅkō pēyō āruyir uṇṭatu-aṇaṅkō pēyō ariya uyiri uṇṭatu, uṟaṅkuvāṉ pōlak kiṭantaṉaṉ kāṇ eṉa- itō uṟaṅkupavaṉ pōlak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉaṉ kāṇpāyāka eṉṟu pārppaṉi urittu varunta ;
150-153.	aṇaṅkum pēyum āruyir uṇṇāt - aṇaṅkum pēyum ariya vuyiri uṇṇamāṭṭā, piṇaṅku nūl mārpaṉ pētu kantāka -neruṅkiya muppuri nūli yaṇinta mārpiṉiyuṭiyacārṅkalaṉatu aṟiyāmiyē paṟṟuk kōṭāka, ūḻviṉi vantu ivaṉ uyiruṇṭu kaḻintatu - ūḻviṉiyāṉatu vantu ivaṉatu uyiri uṇṭu nīṅkiyatu, māperun tuṉpam nī oḻivāy eṉṟalum-niṉatu mikapperiya tuyaratti nīṅkuvāyāka eṉṟu teyvamurittalum; 
154-156. 	eṉ uyirkoṇṭu ivaṉ uyir tantaruḷiṉ-eṉṉuṭiya uyirip peṟṟu eṉ makaṉatu uyirit tantaruḷvāyāṉāl, eṉ kaṇṇil kaṇavaṉi ivaṉ kāttu ōmpiṭum-kaṇṇiḻanta eṉ kaṇavaṉi ivaṉ pātukāppāṉ ākaliṉ, ivaṉ uyir tantu eṉ uyir vāṅku eṉṟalum-ivaṉ uyiri aḷittu eṉatu uyiri vāṅku vāyāka eṉṟu kōtami kūṟiyavaḷavil : kāttu ōmpiṭum: oruporuṭ paṉmoḻi.
157-167. 	mutu mūtāṭṭi iraṅkiṉaḷ moḻivōḷ - campāpati irakkamuṟṟuk kūṟukiṉṟavaḷ, aiyam uṇṭō āruyir pōṉāl ceyviṉi maruṅkila ceṉṟu piṟappu eytutal-ariya uyirāṉatu uṭampi viṭṭu nīṅkiṉāl tāṉ ceyta viṉiyiṉvaḻiyēceṉṟu vēṟu piṟappaṭitalil aiyam uṇṭō, āṅkatu koṇarntu niṉ āriṭar nīkkutal-ceṉṟa avaṉuyirik koṇṭuvantu niṉṉuṭiya ariya tuṉpatti nīkkutal, īṅku eṉakku āva toṉṟu aṉṟu - eṉakku iyalvatoru kāriyamaṉṟu, nī iraṅkal-nī varuntaṟka, koli aṟam ām eṉum koṭuntoḻil mākkaḷ avalappaṭiṟṟu uri āṅkatu maṭavāy - maṭantiyē nī kūṟiya atu koli purivati aṟam eṉṟu kūṟum koṭun toḻiliyuṭiya mākkaḷiṉ tuṉpattit tarum poyyuri yākum, ulaka maṉṉavarkku uyirkku uyir īvōr ilarō - ulakattiyāḷkiṉṟa aracarkaṭku uyirkku uyir koṭup pōr oruvarumilarō, inta īmap puṟaṅkāṭṭu aracarkku amintaṉa āyiram kōṭṭam - intac cuṭukāṭṭiṉkaṇ aracarkaḷukku aminta kōṭṭaṅkaḷ aḷavaṟṟaṉa ākaliṉ, nirayak koṭu moḻi nī oḻi eṉṟalum-nirayattiṟkētuvākiya koṭiya moḻikaḷi nī viṭuvāyāka eṉṟu teyvam kūṟutalum;
mutu mūtāṭṭi-mikka mutumiyi uṭiyāḷ. maruṅku - vaḻi; 1"ceyviṉi vaḻittā yuyircelu meṉpatu, poyyil kāṭciyōr poruḷuri yātaliṉ" eṉpatu īṇṭaṟiyaṟpālatu. maṉṉavarkku uyirkkuyi rīvōr - aracar uyirukku māṟākat tammuyirik koṭuppōr. āyiram-aḷavillaṉa eṉṉumporuṭṭu. kōṭṭam-aṟi.
168-171. 	tēvar taruvar varam eṉṟu orumuṟi nāṉmaṟi antaṇar naṉṉūl urikkum - tēvarkaḷ varantaruvar eṉṟu antaṇar kaḷatu nalla nūlākiya nāṉmaṟi orupeṟṟiyē kūṟā niṟkum; aṅṅaṉamākavum, māperun teyvam nī aruḷāviṭiṉ- mikapperun teyvamākiya nī aruḷpuriyāviṭṭāl, yāṉō kāvēṉ eṉ uyir īṅkeṉa - yāṉ eṉ uyiri īṇṭuk kāppāṟṟāmal viṭuvēṉ eṉṟu kōtami kūṟa;
172-175.	ūḻimutalvaṉ uyirtariṉ allatu-ūḻimutalvaṉākiya iṟivaṉ uyiri aḷittālallatu, āḻittāḻi akavarit tirivōr tām tariṉ-cakkaravāḷamākiya tāḻiyiṉkaṇ tiriyun tēvarkaḷ tara vallavarāyiṉ, yāṉum tarukuvaṉ maṭavāy- maṭavāy yāṉum niṉ makaṉuyirit taruvēṉ, īṅku eṉ āṟṟalum kāṇpāy eṉṟu - eṉṉuṭiya āṟṟaliyum nī ippoḻutu kāṇpāyāka eṉṟu kūṟi;
ūḻimutalvaṉ - ulaka mutalvaṉ; ivaṉi makāppiramā eṉpar. tāḻipōṟ kavintuḷa teṉpār tāḻi yeṉṟār. mutalvaṉ tariṉ allatu tirivōr tārār. avar tariṉ yāṉun tarukuvaṉ eṉa viritturikka. 
176-185.	nālvaki marapiṉ arūpap piramarum - nāṉku vakip paṭṭa muṟimiyiṉiyuṭiya arūpap piramarkaḷiyum, nāṉāl vakiyiṉ urūpap piramarum-patiṉāṟu vakippaṭṭa urūpap piramarkaḷiyum, iruvakic cuṭarum-ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ kaḷiyum, iru mūvakiyil peruvaṉappu eytiya teyvata kaṇaṅkaḷum - āṟu vakiyiṉiyuṭiya mikka aḻaku viḷaṅku kiṉṟa teyva kaṇaṅkaḷiyum, palvaki acurarum paṭutuyar uṟūum eṇvaki narakarum- pala tiṟappaṭṭa acurarkaḷiyum mikka tuṉpatti aṉupavikkiṉṟa eṇvakippaṭṭa narakar kaḷiyum, iru vicumpu iyaṅkum paṉmīṉ īṭṭamum nāḷum kōḷum-akaṉṟa vāṉiṉkaṇ tiriyum pala mīṉkaḷiṉ tokutikaḷiyum nāṭkaḷiyum kōṭkaḷiyum, taṉ akattu aṭakkiya cakkaravāḷattu - taṉ uḷḷē aṭakkik koṇṭirukkum cakkaravāḷattil, varan taraṟkuriyōrtami muṉ niṟutti -varantaruvataṟ kuriyavarkaḷākiya tēvarkaḷik kōtamikku muṉṉē niṟutti, aranti keṭum ivaḷ aruntuyar ituveṉa - ivaḷuṟṟa ariya tuyar itu ittuyariṉip pōkkuvīrāka eṉṟu campāpati kūṟa;
ulakam muppattoṉṟu eṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷ mutalā vatākiya naraka lōkattiṉ pakutikaḷākiya makānirayam, iruravam, kālacūttiram, cañcīvaṉam, makāvīci, tapaṉam, campiratāpaṉam, caṅkatam eṉṉum eṭṭu narakaṅkaḷilum narakarkaḷ vacippareṉṟum, āṟāvatu mutaliya aṟuvaki ulakaṅkaḷilum aṟuvakit teyvakaṇaṅkaḷ vacippareṉṟum, paṉṉiraṇṭāvatu mutaliya patiṉāṟu ulakaṅkaḷil patiṉāṟuvaki uruvappiramarkaḷum, irupatteṭṭāvatu mutaliya nāṉku ulakaṅkaḷil nālvaki aruvappiramarkaḷum vacippareṉṟum kūṟuvar. oṉṟu mutal mēṉmēlulakaṅkaḷil vacippōr paṭippaṭiyē uyarntōrāva reṉpatu aṟika. keṭum : ceyyumeṉṉuñ col; ēvalil vantatu. 
186-189. 	campāpati tāṉ uritta ammuṟiyē - kāvaṟṟeyva mākiya campāpati kūṟiya muṟippaṭiyē, eṅku vāḻ tēvarum urippak kēṭṭē - ulakeṅkaṇumuḷḷa tēvarkaḷum kūṟak kēṭṭu, kōtami uṟṟa koṭuntuyar nīṅki - kōtami tāṉ aṭinta koṭiya tuṉpattiṉiṉṟum nīṅki, īmaccuṭaliyil makaṉi iṭṭu iṟantapiṉ - iṭukāṭṭil aṭukkappaṭṭa viṟakiṉi yuṭiya aḻaliṉkaṇ makaṉi iṭṭut tāṉum iṟanta piṉṉar; 
campāpati kūṟiyatāvatu ūḻi mutalvaṉ uyirtariṉallatu ēṉōr tārār eṉṟatu. tēvar eṉṟatu aruvappiramar iṟuti
yākavuḷḷa mēlulakattavar aṉivarkkum potuppeyar. 
190-202.	campāpati taṉ āṟṟal tōṉṟa - campāpatiyiṉuṭiya pērāṟṟal veḷippaṭumāṟu, eṅku vāḻ tēvarum kūṭiya iṭantaṉil-eṅkumuṟikiṉṟa tēvarka ḷellōrum kūṭiya anta iṭattil, cūḻkaṭal vaḷiiya āḻiyaṅkuṉṟattu - perumpuṟak kaṭalāṟ cūḻappaṭṭa cakkaravāḷa maliyiṉ uṭpaṭṭa iṭattil, naṭuvu niṉṟa mērukkuṉṟamum - naṭu viṭattē niṉṟatākiya mērumaliyum, puṭiyiṉniṉṟa eḻu vakik kuṉṟamum ataṉ pakkattē cūḻntu niṉṟa eḻuvakik kulamalikaḷum, nālvaki marapiṉ māperuntīvum-nāṉku vakippaṭṭa marapiṉiyuṭiya mikapperiya tīvukaḷum, ōrī rāyiram ciṟṟiṭittīvum - avaṟṟic cūḻntuḷḷa iraṇṭāyiram ciṟu tīvukaḷum, piṟavum āṅkataṉ iṭavaki uriyaṉa-piṟavumākiya ataṉ uriya iṭavakikaḷi, peṟu muṟi marapiṉ aṟivuvarak kāṭṭi-avi aminta muṟippaṭi kāṇpōrkku aṟivuri amittuk kāṭṭi, āṅkuvāḻ uyirkaḷum avvuyir iṭaṅkaḷum-āṅkē vāḻum uyirkaḷiyum avi vacikkum iṭaṅkaḷiyum, pāṅkuṟa maṇṇīṭṭil paṇpuṟa vakuttu-aḻaku porunta maṇṇīṭukaḷāka ilakkaṇappaṭi vakuttu, mikka mayaṉāl iḻikkappaṭṭa - aṟiviṉ mēmpaṭṭa teyvat taccaṉāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa, cakkaravāḷakkōṭṭam īṅkitukāṇ-cakkara vāḷakkōṭṭam eṉpatu ituvākum: 
tōṉṟakkūṭiya iṭam eṉka; tōṉṟa iḻikkappaṭṭa  eṉ ṟiyittalum poruntum. mērumali eṇpattīrāyiram yōcaṉi uyaramum, ataṟkut takka parumiyum uḷḷateṉṟum, ataṉic cūḻak kulakirikaḷ ēḻum, avaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟaṉiyum cūḻntu avvakkiri yiṉ uyaravaḷavuḷḷa āḻamum akalamumuṭiya ēḻu kaṭalkaḷum uḷḷaṉaveṉṟum, iviyellāvaṟṟiyum puṟañ cūḻntukiṭakkum perumpuṟakkaṭalil camputtīvu mutaliya nāṉku māperuntīvukaḷum, aviovvoṉṟiyum ainnūṟu ainnūṟu ākaccūḻnta iraṇṭāyiram ciṟu tīvukaḷum uḷḷaṉa eṉṟum, camputtīvu teṟkiluḷḷatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar; ippakutikku ṭākṭar u.. vē. cāminātiyar eḻutiya arumpatavuri kāṇka. 
ciṟu tīvukaḷ antarattīvukaḷ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum; "antarat tīviṉum akaṉperun tīviṉum" (25 : 224) eṉṟu piṉvarutal kāṇka. uyirkaḷ eṉṟatu avaṟṟukku nilikkaḷamākiya uṭampu kaḷi. iṭam eṉṟatu nīr, nilam mutaliyavaṟṟi. maṇṇīṭu-cutiyāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa pāvi mutaliyaṉa. mayaṉ - acurattaccaṉ eṉpārumuḷar.
203-205.	iṭupiṇakkōṭṭattu eyiṟpuṟam ākaliṉ - piṇaṅkaḷi yiṭukiṉṟa puṟaṅkāṭṭiṉ matiṟ puṟattiluḷḷatu ātalāl itaṉi, cuṭukāṭṭukkōṭṭam eṉṟalatu uriyār - cuṭukāṭṭuk kōṭṭa meṉṟē kūṟuvar, itaṉvaravu itu eṉṟu irunteyvam urikka - itaṉuṭiya varalāṟu iḵtu eṉṟu maṇimēkalā teyvam moḻintaruḷa;
iṭutal, cuṭutal iraṇṭiṉiyum oṉṟāka aṭakkip peyar kūṟiṉār. 
206-214. 	mataṉil neñcamōṭu vāṉtuyar eyti - valiyaṟṟa maṉattuṭaṉē mikka tuṉpattiyaṭintu, piṟantōr vāḻkki ciṟantōḷ urippa-pūmiyiṟ piṟantōrkaḷatu vāḻkkiyi maṇimēkali kūṟa, iṟantiruḷ kūrnta iṭiyiruḷ, yāmattu - mikavuñceṟinta iruḷ mikunta iṭiyāmattil, tūṅkutuyil eytiya cutamati oḻiya - mikka uṟakkattiṉiyaṭinta cutamati avviṭattil nīṅka, pūṅkoṭitaṉṉip poruntit taḻīi - maṇimēkaliyic cērat taḻuvikkoṇṭu, antaram āṟā āṟintu yōcaṉit teṉṟici maruṅkil ceṉṟu-vicumpiṉ vaḻiyāka muppatu yōcaṉi tūram teṉṟicikkaṭ ceṉṟu, tiriyuṭutta maṇi pallavattiṭi - kaṭalāṟ cūḻappaṭṭa maṇipallava meṉṉum tīviṉkaṇ, maṇimēkalā teyvam aṇiyiḻi taṉṉi vittu akaṉṟatutāṉ - maṇimēkaliyi vittuviṭṭu maṇimēkalā teyvamtāṉ nīṅkiyatu eṉka. 
mataṉ-cerukkumām. kōṭṭattiṉiyalpu kēṭṭamiyiṉ neñcam mataṉaḻintateṉka. vāḻkki yeṉṟatu ataṉ niliyāmiyi. 
maṇṭilam coriya, mēṉiyaḷ ētīti nōkki eṉṉuṟṟīreṉa, avaḷ avaṉ kūṟiya turittalum; arun teyvamurippa, cutamati eṉṉi yōveṉa, nēriḻi kūṟum ; aṅṅaṉam kūṟukiṉṟa teyvam itaṉ varavu ituveṉṟurikka, ciṟantōḷurippa, teyvam iṭi yiruḷ yāmattuc cutamatiyoḻiyap pūṅkoṭitaṉṉit taḻīic ceṉṟu maṇipallavattil vittu akaṉṟatu eṉa muṭikka.
cakkaravāḷakkōṭṭamuritta kāti muṟṟiṟṟu. 


7. tuyileḻuppiya kāti  
maṇipallavattiṉiṉṟum nīṅkiya maṇimēkalā teyvam, ‘iravu kaḻintāl maṇimēkali eṉki yakappaṭuvāḷ' eṉṟu vēṭki nōyāl tuyilātirunta utayakumaraṉ muṉtōṉṟi, "maṉṉavaṉ makaṉē, kōl nili tirintiṭiṉ kōḷnili tiriyum, kōḷnili tirintiṭiṉ māri vaṟaṅkūrum, māri vaṟaṅkūriṉ maṉṉuyir illi, maṉṉuyirellām maṇṇāḷ vēntaṉ taṉṉuyireṉṉum takutiyiṉ ṟākum, tavattiṟam pūṇṭōḷ taṉmēl vitta, avattiṟam oḻika" eṉṟu avaṉukku aṟivuri kūṟiviṭṭu, uvavaṉañ ceṉṟu aṅkē tuyilum cutamatiyi eḻuppi, "yāṉ maṇimēkalā teyvam; intira viḻāk kāṇṭaṟku vantēṉ ; nī-añcātē, maṇimēkalikkup puttaṉ aṟa neṟiyiṟ cellum naṟpoḻutu vantuṟṟatākaliṉ avaḷi yeṭuttuc ceṉṟu maṇipallavattil vittēṉ; avaḷ taṉatu paḻam piṟappiṉ nikaḻcciyiyum aṟintukoṇṭu iṟṟikku ēḻāvatu nāḷil iṅku vantu cērvāḷ. innakaril vēṟuvaṭivam koḷ vāḷāyiṉum uṉakku avaḷ oḷippāḷallaḷ; avaḷ ippatiyiṟ pukumnāḷil pala aṟputaṅkaḷ nikaḻum; nāṉ vantatiyum maṇimēkali nalvaḻiyiṟ ceṉṟatiyum mātavikkuc colka: avaḷ muṉṉarē eṉṉi yaṟivāḷ; kōvalaṉ ‘namatu kula teyvattiṉ peyari ikkuḻantikku iṭuka' eṉṟu colli, eṉ peyari ivaḷukkiṭṭa nāḷiṉ iravil mātaviyiṉ kaṉaviṟ ceṉṟu, ‘kāmaṉ ceyalaṟṟēṅka māperun tavakkoṭi yīṉṟaṉi' eṉṟu naṉaviṟ kūṟiyatupōla nāṉ kūṟiyatuṇṭu; itaṉiyum avaḷukku niṉippūṭṭuti," eṉṟu kūṟippōyiṟṟu. 
piṉpu cutamati yeḻuntu maṇimēkaliyiṉ pirivāl varunti. avvaṉattic cūḻnta matiliṉ mēṟpuṟattiluḷḷa ciṟiya vāyil vaḻiyē ceṉṟu, cakkaravāḷakkōṭṭatti yaṭintu, āṅkuḷḷa ulakavaṟaviyiṉ orupuṭi yiruntāḷ. appoḻutu avviṭattē kantiṉi iṭamākakkoṇṭuḷḷa teyvappāviyāṉatu cutamati maruḷumpaṭi avaḷatu muṟpiṟappiṉ varalāṟṟiyum ippiṟappiṉ varalāṟṟiyum kūṟi viḷittu, ‘maṇimēkali taṉatu muṟpiṟappiyum uṉatu muṟpiṟappiyum aṟintukoṇṭu iṟṟikku ēḻāmnāḷiravil in nakarkkaṇ varuvāḷ; avaḷ pirintatu paṟṟi nī añcal' eṉṟu kūṟiṟṟu. ataṉik kēṭṭu neñcamnaṭuṅkiya cutamati, iravu muḻutum avviṭattiruntu, katiravaṉ utittavuṭaṉ eḻuntu vītimaruṅkiṟ ceṉṟu mātaviyi aṭintu, mutalnāḷiravu nikaḻntavaṟṟic coṉṉavuṭaṉ avaḷ māṇikkatti iḻanta nākam pōṉṟu mikka tuyarat tōṭiruntāḷ. cutamati maṇimēkaliyiṉ pirivāl iṉṉuyiriḻanta yākkipōlac ceyalaṟṟiruntāḷ. (itil, irāppoḻutiṉ nikaḻccikaḷum, viṭiyal  nikaḻccikaḷum āciriyaruṭiya ulakiyalaṟiviyum, kaṟpaṉittiṟattiyum pulappaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa.)  
		maṇimē kalitaṉi maṇipal lavattiṭi
maṇimē kalāteyvam vittu nīṅki
maṇimē kalitaṉi malarppoḻiṟ kaṇṭa
utaya kumaraṉ uṟutuya reytik
5		kaṅkul kaḻiyileṉ kiyakat tāḷeṉap 
poṅkumel lamaḷiyiṟ poruntā tiruntōṉ 
muṉṉart tōṉṟi maṉṉavaṉ makaṉē
kōlnili tirintiṭiṟ kōḷnili tiriyum
kōḷnili tirintiṭiṉ mārivaṟaṅ kūrum
10		mārivaṟaṅ kūriṉ maṉṉuyi rilli 
maṉṉuyi rellām maṇṇāḷ vēntaṉ
taṉṉuyi reṉṉun takutiyiṉ ṟākum
tavattiṟam pūṇṭōḷ taṉmēl vitta
avattiṟam oḻikeṉ ṟavaṉvayi ṉurittapiṉ
15		uvavaṉam pukuntāṅ kuṟutuyil koḷḷum
cutamati taṉṉit tuyiliṭi nīkki
intira kōṭaṇi innakark kāṇa
vantē ṉañcal maṇimē kaliyāṉ
āticāṉ muṉivaṉ aṟavaḻip paṭuum
20		ētu mutirnta tiḷaṅkoṭik kātaliṉ
viñciyiṟ peyarttuniṉ viḷaṅkiḻi taṉṉiyōr
vañcamil maṇipal lavattiṭi vittēṉ
paṇṭip piṟappum paṇpuṟa uṇarntīṅ
kiṉṟēḻ nāḷil innakar maruṅkē
25		vantu tōṉṟum maṭakkoṭi nallāḷa
kaḷippumāṇ celvak kāvaṟ pērūr
oḷitturu veytiṉum uṉtiṟa moḷiyāḷ
āṅkavaḷ innakar pukunta annāḷ
īṅku nikaḻvaṉa ētup palavuḷa
30		mātavi taṉakkiyāṉ vanta vaṇṇamum
ētamil neṟimakaḷ eytiya vaṇṇamum
uriyāy nīyavaḷ eṉtiṟam uṇarum 
tiriyirum puvattut teyvamoṉ ṟuṇṭeṉak
kōvalaṉ kūṟiyik koṭiyiṭi taṉṉiyeṉ
35		nāmañ ceyta naṉṉāḷ naḷḷiruḷ.
kāmaṉ kiyaṟak kaṭunavi yaṟukkum
māperun tavakkoṭi īṉṟaṉi yeṉṟē
naṉavē pōlak kaṉavakat turittēṉ
īṅkiv vaṇṇam āṅkavaṭ kuriyeṉ
40		ṟantarat teḻuntāṅ karunteyvam pōyapiṉ
ventuya reytic cutamati eḻuntāṅ
kakaṉmaṉi yaraṅkat tāciriyar tammoṭu
vakiteri mākkaṭku vaṭṭaṇi kāṭṭi
āṭal puṇarkkum araṅkiyaṉ makaḷiril
45		kūṭiya kuyiluvak karuvikaṇ tuyiṉṟu
paṇṇukkiḷi payirum paṇṇiyāḻt tīntoṭi
koḷival lāyamō ṭicikūṭ ṭuṇṭu
vaḷicēr ceṅki melvira lutitta
vemmivey tuṟātu taṉmiyiṟ ṟiriyavum
50		paṇpil kātalaṉ parattami nōṉātu
uṇkaṇ civantāṅku olkukoṭi pōṉṟu
teruṭṭavun teruḷā tūṭaloṭu tuyilvōr
virippūm paḷḷi vīḻtuṇi taḻuvavum
taḷarnaṭi yāyamoṭu taṅkā tōṭi
55		viḷiyāṭu ciṟutēr īrttumey varunti
amaḷit tuñcum aimpaṭit tālik
kutalic cevvāyk kuṟunaṭip putalvarkkuk
kāvaṟ peṇṭir kaṭippaki yeṟintu
tūpaṅ kāṭṭit tūṅkutuyil vatiyavum
60		iṟiyuṟi puṟavum niṟinīrp puḷḷum
kāvuṟi paṟaviyum nāvu ḷaḻunti
viḻavukkaḷi yaṭaṅki muḻavukkaṇ tuyiṉṟu
paḻaviṟaṉ mūtūr pāyalkoḷ naṭunāḷ
kōmakaṉ kōyiṟ kuṟunīrk kaṉṉaliṉ
65		yāmaṅ koḷpavar ēttoli yaravamum
uṟiyuṇiṉ ṟoṭuṅkiya uṇṇā uyakkattu 
niṟiyaḻi yāṉi neṭuṅkū viḷiyum
tērvaḻaṅku teruvuñ ciṟṟiṭi muṭukkarum
ūrkāp pāḷar eṟituṭi ōtiyum
70		muḻaṅkunīr muṉṟuṟik kalampuṇar kammiyar
tuḻantaṭu kaḷḷiṉ ṟōppiyuṇ ṭayarntu
paḻañceruk kuṟṟa aṉantarp pāṇiyum
aravāyk kaṭippaki aiyavik kaṭippaki
viraviya makaḷir ēntiya tūmattup
75		putalvarip payanta puṉiṟunīr kayakkam
tīrviṉi makaḷir kuḷaṉā ṭaravamum
valitta neñciṉ āṭava riṉṟiyum
pulikkaṇat taṉṉōr pūta catukkattuk
koṭittēr vēntaṉ koṟṟaṅ koḷkeṉa
80		iṭikkuraṉ muḻakkat tiṭumpali yōtiyum
īṟṟiḷam peṇṭir āṟṟāp pālakar
kaṭuñcūṉ makaḷir neṭumpuṇ uṟṟōr
tantuyar keṭukkum mantira mākkaḷ
maṉṟap pēymakaḷ vantukik koḷkeṉa
85		niṉṟeṟi paliyiṉ neṭuṅkura lōtiyum
palvē ṟōtiyum parantoruṅ kicippak
kēṭṭuḷaṅ kalaṅki ūṭṭiru ḷaḻuvattu
muruntēr iḷanaki nīṅkip pūmpoḻil
tirunteyiṟ kuṭapāṟ ciṟupuḻi pōki
90		mikkamā teyvam viyanteṭut turitta
cakkara vāḷak kōṭṭat tāṅkaṇ
palarpukaḻ tiṟanta pakuvāy vāyil
ulaka aṟaviyiṉ orupuṭi iruttalum
kantuṭi neṭunilik kāraṇam kāṭṭiya
95		anti leḻutiya aṟputap pāvi
mittaṭaṅ kaṇṇāḷ mayaṅkiṉaḷ veruvat
tippiya murikkun teyvak kiḷaviyiṉ
iravi vaṉmaṉ oruperu makaḷē
turakat tāṉit tuccayaṉ ṟēvi
100		tayaṅkiṇark kōti tāri cāvuṟa 
mayaṅki yāṉimuṉ maṉṉuyir nīttōy
kārāḷar caṇpiyiṟ kucikaṉ makaḷē
māruta vēkaṉō ṭinnakar pukuntu
tāri tavvi taṉṉoṭu kūṭiya
105		vīri yākiya cutamati kēḷāy
iṉṟēḻ nāḷil iṭiyiruḷ yāmattut
taṉpiṟap pataṉōṭu niṉpiṟap puṇarntīṅku
ilakkumi yākiya niṉakkiḷi yāḷvarum
añcaleṉ ṟuritta tavvuri kēṭṭu
110		neñcam naṭukkuṟūum nēriḻi nallāḷ
kāva lāḷar kaṇṭuyil koḷḷat
tūmeṉ cēkkit tuyilkaṇ viḻippa
valampuric caṅkam vaṟiteḻun tārppap
pulampuric caṅkam poruḷōṭu muḻaṅkap
115		pukarmuka vāraṇam neṭuṅkū viḷippap
poṟimayir vāraṇaṅ kuṟuṅkū. viḷippap
paṇinilip puravi palaveḻun tālap
paṇinilip puḷḷum palaveḻun tālap
pūmpoḻi lārkip puḷḷoli ciṟappap
120		pūṅkoṭi yārkip puḷḷoli ciṟappap
kaṭavuṭ pīṭikip pūppali kaṭikoḷak
kalampakar pīṭikip pūppali kaṭikoḷak
kuyiluvar kaṭitoṟum paṇṇiyam paranteḻak
koṭuppōr kaṭitoṟum paṇṇiyam paranteḻa
125		ūrtuyi leṭuppa uravunī raḻuvattuk
kāriruḷ cīttuk katiravaṉ muḷittalum
ēvuṟu maññiyiṉ niṉintaṭi varunta
mānakar vīti maruṅkiṟ pōkip
pōya kaṅkuliṟ pukuntati yellām 
130		mātavi taṉakku vaḻuviṉ ṟurittalum
naṉmaṇi iḻanta nākam pōṉṟavaḷ
taṉmakaḷ vārāt taṉittuya ruḻappa
iṉṉuyi riḻanta yākkiyi liruntaṉaḷ
tuṉṉiya turitta cutamati tāṉeṉ. 
uri
1-7.		maṇimēkalitaṉi maṇipallavattiṭi maṇimēkalā teyvam vittu nīṅki-maṇimēkalā teyvam maṇimēkaliyi maṇipallavattiṉkaṇ vittu ataṉiviṭṭu nīṅka, maṇimēkalitaṉi malarppoḻiṟ kaṇṭa utayakumaraṉ - maṇimēkaliyip pūñcōliyiṟ kaṇṭa utayakumaraṉ, uṟutuyar eyti - mikka tuṉpa muṟṟu, kaṅkul kaḻiyileṉ kiyakattāḷ eṉa - iravu nīṅkiṉāl maṇimēkali eṉ kiyiṉiṭattē uḷḷāḷ eṉṟu, poṅku mel amaḷiyil poruntātu iruntōṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟi- ciṟanta melliya paṭukkiyiṭattuk kaṇṭuyilātu irukkiṉṟavaṉ muṉṉē tōṉṟi,  
maṇimēkaliyi eytap peṟāmiyāṉ uṟutuyar eytiṉāṉ eṉka. poruntātu-kaṇ poruntātu : uṟaṅkātu. iruntōṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟi eṉka. 
7-14. 	maṉṉavaṉ makaṉē-aracaṉ putalvaṉē, kōl nili tirintiṭiṉ kōḷ nili tiriyum-aracaṉatu kōl kōṭu vatāyiṉ kōṭ kaḷiṉ nili vēṟupaṭum, kōḷnili tirintiṭiṉ māri vaṟamkūrum- aṅṅaṉam kōṭkaḷ nili tavaṟiṉāl maḻi peyyāmaṟ kaḻiyuṅkālam mikum, māri vaṟaṅ kūriṉ maṉṉuyir illi - maḻi vaḷaṅ kuṟintāl nilipeṟṟa uyirkaḷ illiyām, maṉṉuyir ellām maṇṇāḷ vēntaṉ taṉuyir eṉṉum takuti iṉṟu ākum- nilipeṟṟa uyirkaḷ aṉittum maṇṇulakatti āḷkiṉṟa maṉṉavaṉatu uyireṉṟu kūṟappaṭum takimiilatām ākaliṉ, tavattiṟam pūṇṭōḷ taṉmēl vitta - tavattiṉ kūṟupāṭṭiṉi mēṟkoṇṭa maṇimēkalipāl vitta, avattiṟam oḻi keṉṟu avaṉ vayiṉ urittapiṉ-tīya viruppiṉ taṉmiyi oḻivāyāka eṉṟu utayakumaraṉiṭam moḻinta piṉṉar;  
kōlnili tiriyiṉ kōḷnili tirital mutaliyaṉa nikaḻumeṉ pataṉi, 1"kōṇili tiriyāk kōlō ṉākuka,' 2"kōṇilitirintu nāḻi kuṟipaṭap pakalkaṇ miñci, nīṇila māri yiṉṟi viḷivaḵkip paciyu nīṭip, pūṇmuli makaḷir poṟpiṟ kaṟpaḻin taṟaṅkaṇ māṟi, āṇiyiv vulaku kēṭā maracukōl kōṭi ṉeṉṟāṉ" eṉpavaṟṟāṉum aṟika. avattiṟam - kēṭu ceyyum tīya viḻivu. 
15-22.   uvavaṉam pukuntu āṅku uṟutuyil koḷḷum-uvavaṉattiṟ pukuntu āṇṭu mikunta uṟakkattuṭaṉirukkum, cutamati taṉṉit tuyiliṭi nīkki - cutamatiyi uṟakkat tiṉiṉṟum eḻuppi, intirakōṭaṉi innakark kāṇa vantēṉ añcal maṇimēkali yāṉ-yāṉ maṇimēkalā teyvam añcātē innakariṉ intira viḻākkāṇa vantēṉ, āticāṉ muṉivaṉ aṟa vaḻippaṭūum-putta tēvaṉiṉ aṟaneṟiyiṟ cellum, ētu mutirntatu iḷaṅkoṭikku ātaliṉ- ētu nikaḻcci maṇimēkalikku muṟṟiyatākaliṉ viñciyiṟ peyarnnu niṉ viḷaṅkiḻitaṉṉi-niṉ melliyali eṉ vittiyiṉālē peyarttu, ōr vañcam il maṇipallavattiṭi vittēṉ-oru vakiyāṉa vañcaṉiyum illāta maṇi pallavattiṉ kaṇ vittuḷēṉ;  tuyiliṭi : urupu mayakkam, kōṭaṇi - muḻakkam ; palvaki muḻakkattiṉiyuṭiya viḻāviṟkāyiṟṟu. viṉikaḷ tam payaṉiyūṭṭa etirppaṭuvataṉi, ētu nikaḻcci allatu ētu eṉpatu puttanūl vaḻakku. 
23-29.	paṇṭip piṟappum paṇpuuṟa uṇarntu-muṟpiṟaviyiyum naṉkaṟintu, īṅku iṉṟu ēḻunāḷil innakar maruṅkē- iṟṟikku ēḻām nāḷilē ivviṭattu innakarattiṉkaṇ, vantu tōṉṟum maṭakkoṭi nallāḷ - maṭappam poruntiya koṭipōlum maṇimēkali vantu tōṉṟuvaḷ, kaḷippu māṇ celvak kāvaṟ pērūr - celvak kaḷippāṟ ciṟanta kāvaliyuṭiya ip periya nakarattil. oḷittu uruvu eytiṉum uṉ tiṟaṉ oḷiyāḷc - taṉ vaṭivatti maṟittu vantālum niṉṉiṭam maṟiyāḷ, āṅku avaḷ innakar pukunta annāḷ - maṇimēkali innakarattiṟku mīḷavanta annāḷil, īṅku nikaḻvaṉa ētup palavuḷa - īṇṭu nikaḻvaṉa vākiya ētu nikaḻccikaḷ palavuṇṭu;
 tōṉṟum: muṟṟu. kaḷippumāṇ celvam eṉpataṟkuk kaḷippu māṭci yuṟutaṟkuk kāraṇamāya celvam eṉa urippiṉum amiyum. pukunta eṉpatu kāla vaḻuvamiti.
 30-40. 	mātavi taṉakku yāṉ vanta vaṇṇamum-mātavikku yāṉ vanta vakiyiyum, ētamil neṟi makaḷ eytiya vaṇṇamum- maṇimēkali kuṟṟamaṟṟa vaḻiyiṟ cērnta taṉmiyiyum, uri yāy nī - nī kūṟuvāyāka, avaḷ eṉ tiṟam uṇarum-am mātavi eṉ varalāṟṟi uṇaruvāḷ, tiriyirum puvattut teyvam oṉṟu uṇṭu eṉak kōvalaṉ kūṟi ikkoṭiyiṭi taṉṉi eṉ nāmañ ceyta nalnāḷ - kōvalaṉ alikaḷiyuṭiya periya kaṭaliṉiṭattut teyvam oṉṟu uṇṭeṉṟu kūṟi ip peṇ makaḷukku eṉ peyariyiṭṭa nallanāḷil, naḷiruḷ-iraviṉ naṭuyāmattē, kāmaṉ kiyaṟak kaṭunavi aṟukkum - kāmaṉ ceyalaṟṟuppōkap periya tuṉpaṅkaḷi aṟukkavalla, māperun tavakkoṭi īṉṟaṉi eṉṟē-mikap periya tavamuṭiya makaḷip peṟṟaṉi eṉṟu, naṉavēpōlak kaṉavakattu urittēṉ -  nēriṟ kūṟuvatip pōlak kaṉaviṉkaṇ moḻintēṉ, īṅku ivvaṇṇam āṅku avaṭku uri eṉṟu - ivaṟṟiyellām mātavikkuk kūṟuvāyāka eṉṟurittu antarattu eḻuntu āṅku arunteyvam pōyapiṉ - vicumpil eḻuntu avvariya maṇimēkalā teyvam pōyiṉa piṉṉar; 
ētam il neṟi eytiyatu eṉṟatu puttaṉ aṟavaḻippaṭutal eṉka; itaṉi vicuttimārkkam eṉpa. teyvam-eṉkula mutalvaṉik kēṭṭiṉiṉṟum nīkkip pātukātta teyvam eṉa viritturittuk koḷka. koṭiyiṭi taṉṉi-koṭiyiṭikku; koṭiyiṭi: maṇimēkali. naḷḷiruḷ eṉpatu ceṟinta iruḷiyuṭiya iraviṉi yuṇarttiṟṟu. kaṭunavi - avā mutaliyaṉa. naṉṉāḷ naḷḷiruṭ kaṉavakattu urittēṉ eṉka. 
41.		ventuyar eytic cutamati eḻuntāṅku-cutamati koṭiya tuṉpa maṭintu eḻuntu;  
42-49.  	akalmaṉi araṅkattu āciriyar tammoṭu- akaṉṟa vīṭākiya araṅkiṉkaṇ āciriyaṉmāroṭu niṉṟu, vaki teri māk kaṭku vaṭṭaṇi kāṭṭi-aviṉaya mutaliya kūṟupāṭukaḷiyaṟikiṉṟa makkaḷukkuk kamalavarttaṉi kāṭṭi, āṭal puṇarkkum araṅkiyal makaḷiril-āṭal puriyum nāṭaka makaḷirippōla, kūṭiya kuyiluvak karuvi kaṇ tuyiṉṟu-oruṅkiyinta kuralyāḻ mutaliya kuyiluvak karuvi kaḷellām kaṇṭuyiṉṟu, paṇṇuk kiḷipayirum paṇṇi yāḻt tīntoṭi-paṇṇiṉ vakikaḷi icikkum yāḻiṉ iṉiya narampukaḷ, koḷival āyamōṭu ici kūṭṭuṇṭu- pāṭṭil valla makaḷiruṭaṉ iciyic cērntaṉupavittu, vaḷi cēr ceṅki melviral utitta-vaḷikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya civanta kikaḷiṉ melliya viralkaḷāl teṟitta, vemmi veytuṟātu taṉmiyil tiriyavum- vemmi naṉku uṟāmiyāl iyalpi ṉiṉṟu tiriyāniṟkavum;  
āciriyar-iyalāciriyaṉ, āṭalāciriyaṉ, yāḻāciriyaṉ, iciyā ciriyaṉ, kuḻalāciriyaṉ, taṇṇumi yāciriyaṉ eṉpōr. vaṭṭaṇi- varttaṉi; kamala. varttaṉi; āvatu kittalaṅ kāṭṭal. kiḷi-tiṟamu mām. tīntoṭi tiriyavum eṉka. 
50-53.	paṇpil kātalaṉ parattami nōṉātu. kuṇamaṟṟa kātalaṉuṭiya parattamiyip poṟāmal, uṇ kaṇ civantāṅku olku koṭipōṉṟu - acikiṉṟa koṭi pōla miyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷ civantu, teruṭṭavum teruḷātu ūṭaloṭu tuyilvōr-teḷi vikkavum teḷiyāmal ūṭaluṭaṉ tuyilkiṉṟa makaḷir, virip pūmpaḷḷi vīḻtuṇi taḻuvavum - maṇam poruntiya malaraṇiyil tammāl virumpappaṭukiṉṟa koḻunarit taḻuvavum;  
paṇpu - īṇṭu aṉpu. parattami-parattaṉ taṉmi; parattiyi maruvi yoḻukuvōṉ parattaṉ; 1"peṇṇiyalā rellāruṅ kaṇṇiṟ potuvuṇpar, naṇṇēṉ parattaniṉ mārpu" eṉpatu kāṇka. ūṭaloṭu tuyilvōr tāmē tuṇiyit taḻuvavu meṉka. 
54-59.	taḷarnaṭi āyamoṭu taṅkātu ōṭi viḷiyāṭu ciṟutēr īrttu meyvarunti - taḷarnaṭic ciṟārkaḷuṭaṉ ōriṭattil taṅkāmal ōṭi viḷiyāṭuñ ciṟu tēriṉi iḻuttu uṭalvarunti, amaḷit tuñcum-paṭukkiyil uṟaṅku kiṉṟa, jampaṭit tālik kutalic cevvāyk kuṟunaṭip putalvarkku - jampaṭit tāli yaṇinta mārpum maḻalic cevvāyum taḷarnaṭiyumuṭiya iḷam putalvarkku, kāvaṟ peṇṭir kaṭippaki eṟintu tūpam kāṭṭit tūṅku tuyil vatiyavum - cevilittāyar kaṭippaki vīcip puki kāṭṭit tūṅkukiṉṟa uṟakkattiṟ poruntavum; 
aimpaṭi - tirumāliṉ pañcāyuta vaṭivamuṭiya aṇikalaṉ; "cintupu ciṉṉī rimpaṭi naṉippa" (3;138) eṉpuḻi urittami nōkkuka. kaṭippaki - pēykkup paki; vēmpum, veṇciṟu kaṭukum; ik kātiyuḷ mēlvarutal kāṇka. kāvaṟpeṇṭir tuyil vatiyavum eṉka. 
60-63.	iṟiyuṟi puṟavum niṟinīrp puḷḷum - iṟappukaḷil vāḻ kiṉṟa puṟākkaḷum niṟinta nīriluḷḷa paṟavikaḷum, kā uṟi paṟavikaḷuma nā uḷ aḻunti-poḻilkaḷil uṟi kiṉṟa puṭkaḷum nā uḷḷē aḻunti, viḻavukkaḷi aṭaṅki muḻavukkaṇ tuyiṉṟu- viḻāviṉāluṇṭākiya kaḷippu aṭaṅki muḻavukaḷiṉ kaṇ tuyiṉṟu, paḻaviṟal mūtūr pāyal koḷ naṭunāḷ - paḻiya vali yiṉiyuṭiya toṉṉakaram tuyiliṉikkoṇṭa iṭiyāmattil; 
nāvuḷḷaḻuntalāvatu : oliyātiruttal. aḻunti, aṭaṅki, tuyiṉṟu eṉṉum iṭattu nikaḻporuḷiṉ ṟoḻilkaḷ iṭattiṉ mēl niṉṟaṉa; aḻunti mutaliyavaṟṟic ceyavaṉeccamākat tirittalumām.
64-65.	kōmakaṉ kōyil kuṟunīrk kaṉṉaliṉ - aracaṉ māḷikiyil kuṟiya nīriṉik koṇṭa nāḻiki vaṭṭilāl, yāmam koḷpavar ēttuoli aravamum-nāḻikik kaṇakkiṭu vōr ēttukiṉṟa oliyākiya muḻakkamum; aracaṉi ētti nāḻiki kūṟuvarākaliṉ ‘ēttoliyaravamum' eṉṟār. oliyākiya aravameṉka ; aravam tamiḻccollē.
 66-67.	uṟiyuḷ niṉṟu oṭuṅkiya uṇṇā uyakkattu-taṅku miṭattil niṉṟu oṭuṅkiya uṇṇāmiyā ṉuṇṭākiya varuttattiṉāl, niṟi aḻiyāṉi neṭuṅkūviḷiyum - niṟiyaḻinta yāṉiyiṉatu neṭiya kūppīṭākiya muḻakkamum;  
68-69.	tērvaḻaṅku teruvum ciṟṟiṭi muṭukkarum - tērkaḷ cellukiṉṟa periyavītikaḷilum ciṟiya iṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiya muṭuk kut terukkaḷilum, ūrkāppāḷar eṟituṭi ōtiyum - ūr kāvalar aṭikkiṉṟa tuṭiyoliyum; 
70-72.	muḻaṅku nīr muṉṟuṟik kalampuṇar kammiyar - olik kiṉṟa nīriṉiyuṭiya kaṭaṟṟuṟikaḷil marakkalattiṟ cērntu toḻil purivōr, tuḻantaṭu kaḷḷiṉ tōppiyuṇṭu ayarntu - tuḻāvic camitta kaḷ vakikaḷil nellāṟ camitta kaḷḷiyuṇṭu tammi maṟantu, paḻañcerukkuṟṟa aṉantarp pāṇiyum - paḻiya cerukkiṉiyaṭinta mayakkat tuṭaṉ pāṭum pāṭṭiṉōciyum; 
kaḷḷākiya tōppi yeṉṟumām. tōppi - nellāṟ camitta kaḷ. paḻañcerukku-mutirnta kaḷippu. aṉantar-kaḷḷuṇṭa mayakkam. 
73-76.	aravāyk kaṭippaki aiyavik kaṭippaki viraviya makaḷir ēntiya tūmattu-arattiṉ vāypōṉṟa vēmpākiya kaṭip pakiyum veṇciṟukaṭukākiya kaṭippakiyum kalantu iṭappaṭṭu makaḷirāl ēntappaṭṭa pukiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟu, putalvarip payanta puṉiṟu tīr kayakkam tīrviṉi makaḷir kuḷaṉ āṭu aravamum - putalvarip peṟṟa puṉiṟu tīrumāṟu kayaṅkutal tīrkiṉṟa makaḷir kuḷaṅkaḷil nīrāṭukiṉṟa oliyum; 
kaṭippaki-pēykkup pakiyeṉṉum poruḷatu, makaḷir ēntiya aravāyk kaṭippakiyuṭaṉum aiyavik kaṭippaki viraviya tūmat tuṭaṉum eṉṟurittalumām. puṉiṟu tīrnta makaḷir mayakkantīrnta makaḷir eṉat taṉittaṉi muṭittalumām. putalvarp payanta puṉiṟu tīrnta makaḷir iraviṟceṉṟu kuḷattil nīrāṭutal 1"kaṇava ruvappap putalvarp payantu, paṇittēn tiḷamuli yamutamūṟap, pulavuppuṉiṟu tīrntu polinta cuṟṟamoṭu, vaḷamaṉi makaḷir kuḷanī rayara" eṉpataṉālum aṟiyappaṭum. pattāvatu iravil nīrāṭavēṇṭum eṉpar. 
77-80.	valitta neñciṉ āṭavar iṉṟiyum - pakimiyik karutiya uḷḷamuṭiya vīrar illātirukkavum, pulikkaṇattu aṉṉōr-pulikkūṭṭattiṉi yotta vīrarkaḷ, pūtacatuk kattuk koṭittēr vēntaṉ koṟṟam koḷka eṉa-pūtattiyuṭiya  nāṟcantiyilē koṭiyaṇinta tēriṉi yuṭiya em aracaṉ veṟṟipeṟuka eṉṟu, iṭikkural muḻakkattu iṭumpali ōtiyum iṭiyaṉiya kural muḻakkat tuṭaṉ paliyiṭukiṉṟa ōciyum;
valitta-pōriṉik karutiya eṉṟumām. āṭavar-īṇṭup pakivar.  
81-85.   īṟṟiḷam peṇṭir āṟṟāp pālakar - īṉṟaṇimiyi yuṭiya iḷamakaḷir āṟṟāc ciṟuvar, kaṭuñcūl makaḷir neṭumpuṇ uṟṟōr - mutaṟ cūliyuṭiya maṅkiyar periya puṇkaḷāl varuntiṉōr, tam tuyar keṭukkum mantira mākkaḷ - ākiya ivarkaḷiṉ tuṉpaṅkaḷi nīkkukiṉṟa mantiravātikaḷ, maṉṟap pēy makaḷ vantu kikkoḷka eṉa-maṉṟattiṉkaṇuḷḷa pēymakaḷ vantu peṟuka eṉṟukūṟi, niṉṟu eṟi paliyiṉ neṭuṅkural ōtiyum-niṉṟu pali eṟikiṉṟa nīṇṭa kural muḻakkamum; 
kaṭuñcūl - mutaṟcūl; 1"kaṭuñcūṉ muṇṭakam katirmaṇi kaḻāalavum" eṉṟār piṟarum. paliyiṭumpoḻutu neṭuṅkura leṭuttukkūppiṭuvarākaliṉ ‘paliyiṉeṭuṅkural' eṉṟār: 
86.		palvēṟu ōtiyum parantu oruṅkicippa - ākiya palavēṟu vakippaṭṭa ōciyum parantu oṉṟāka muḻaṅka;  
87-93.   kēṭṭu uḷam kalaṅki ūṭṭiruḷ aḻuvattu-avaṟṟi yellāṅ kēṭṭu maṉaṅkalaṅki ūṭṭiṉāṟpōṉṟa iruṭ parap pilē, muruntu ēr iḷanaki nīṅkip pūmpoḻil - mayiṟpīliyiṉ muḷipōṉṟa aḻakiya paṟkaḷiyuṭiya cutamati uvavaṉattiṉiṉṟum nīṅki, tiruntu eyiṟ kuṭapāl ciṟu puḻi pōki - tiruntiya matiliṉ mēṟṟiciyiluḷḷa ciṟiya vāyiliṉ vaḻiyē ceṉṟu, mikka māteyvam viyantu eṭuttu uritta-perumiporuntiya maṇimēkalāteyvam viyantu eṭuttukkūṟiya, cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭat tāṅkaṇ - cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭattiṇkaṇ, palar pukat tiṟanta pakuvāy vāyil-palaruñ cellumāṟu tiṟakkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟa periya vāypōlum vāyiliyuṭiya, ulaka aṟaviyiṉ orupuṭi iruttalum-ūrampalattil orupakkattil iruttalum; 
ūṭṭutal - pūcutal. aṟavi - ampalam cutamati eḻuntu pal vēṟu ōtiyum oruṅkicippak kēṭṭu uḷaṅkalaṅki nīṅkip pōki orupuṭi yiruttalum eṉka. 
94-97.	kantuṭi neṭunilik kāraṇam kāṭṭiya antil eḻutiya aṟputap pāvi - neṭiya tōṟṟattiyuṭiya tūṇākiya av viṭattē paḻaviṉiyākiya kāraṇatti aṉivarukkum aṟivikkum poruṭṭu mayaṉ eṉṉum teyvat taccaṉāl eḻutappaṭṭu aṉivarukkum viyappi yuṇṭupaṇṇum pāviyāṉatu, mittaṭaṅ kaṇṇāḷ mayaṅkiṉaḷ veruva-cutamati añci mayaṅkumāṟu, tippiyam urikkum teyvak kiḷaviyiṉ - etirkālattil nikaḻ vaṉavaṟṟiyum kūṟum tippiyamākiya teyvak kiḷaviyiṉālē; 
neṭunili yuṭik kantākiya antil eṉak kūṭṭuka. antil-āṅkeṉṉum poruṭṭu. kāṭṭiya : ceyyiyaveṉṉum viṉiyeccam. varuvataṉi urippatu teyvat taṉmiyām eṉṟu koṇṭu 'tippiya murikkum' eṉṟār. teyvakkiḷavi - teyvattiṉ moḻi. 
98-105.	iravivaṉmaṉ oru perumakaḷē - acōtara nakarattaraca ṉākiya iravivaṉmaṉatu oru perumakaḷē, turakat tāṉit tuccayaṉtēvi - kutiric cēṉikaḷi yuṭiyavaṉum kaccaya nakarat taracaṉumākiya tuccayaṉuṭiya maṉiviyē, tayaṅku iṇarkkōti tāri cāvuṟa-viḷaṅkukiṉṟa pūṅkot tiṉālāya māliyiyuṭiya tāri iṟakkumāṟu, mayaṅki yāṉimuṉ maṉṉuyir nīttōy- yāṉiyiṉ muṉṉē mayaṅki uyir tuṟantōy, kārāḷar caṇpiyil kucikaṉ makaḷē - kārāḷaruṭiya caṇpi nakarattil uḷḷa kucikaṉ eṉpavaṉuṭiya putalviyē, mārutavēkaṉōṭu innakar pukuntu - mārutavēkaṉ eṉṉum viñciyaṉuṭaṉ in nakarattiṟ cērntu, tāri tavvi taṉṉoṭu kūṭiya - tamakki yākiya tāriyuṭaṉ kūṭiya, vīriyākiya cutamati kēḷāy - vīri yākiya cutamatiyē kēṭpāyāka ; 
iravivaṉmaṉ - cutamatiyiṉ muṟpiṟappilē tantiyāyiruntavaṉ. tuccayaṉ - cutamatiyiṉ muṟpiṟappiṟ kaṇavaṉāyiruntavaṉ. tāri- cutamatiyiṉ muṟpiṟappil tamakkiyāyiruntavaḷ. vīri yāṉiyāl iṟantatu kēṭṭu ivaḷ iṟantamiyāl ‘tāri cāvuṟa' eṉṟāḷ ; cāvuṟa : etirkālattil vantatu. kārāḷar - vēḷāḷar ; caṇpi - cīkāḻi ; caṇpi yeṉpatu aṅkanāṭṭiṟ kaṅkik kariyiluḷḷa tākiya campā nakaram eṉṟum, kārāḷar eṉpatu karāḷar eṉpataṉ tiripeṉṟum kūṟuvārumuḷar; tāri, vīri, ilakkumi eṉṉum mūṉṟu cakōtarikaḷuḷ, tāri mātaviyākavum, ilakkumi maṇimēkali yākavum kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattiṟ piṟan tiruttalāṉum, intiraviḻāk kāṇṭaṟku vanta mārutavēkaṉ eṉṉum viñciyaṉ tāṉ pūk koyyumpoḻutu taṉṉi eṭuttēkiṉaṉeṉṟu mēl (3:28-39) cutamati kūṟutalāṉum pukār nakarukku aṇittākiya cīkāḻiyeṉṟu koḷvatē poruttamām. kucikaṉ - cutamatiyiṉ tanti.  
106-110.	iṉṟēḻ nāḷil iṭiyiruḷ yāmattu - iṟṟikku ēḻāvatu nāḷil iruḷiyuṭiya iṭiyāmattiltaṉ piṟappataṉoṭu niṉ piṟappu uṇarntu īṅku ilakkumi yākiya niṉakku iḷiyāḷ varum - taṉatu muṟpiṟappuṭaṉ niṉatu muṟpippiṉiyum aṟintukoṇṭu niṉakku iḷi yāḷum ilakkumiyum ākiya maṇimēkali īṇṭu varuvāḷ, añcal eṉṟu urittatu - nī añcātē eṉṟu kūṟiyatu, avvuri kēṭṭu - ammoḻi kēṭṭu, neñcam naṭukkuṟūum nēriḻi nallāḷ - uḷḷam naṭuṅkiya nēriḻiyākiya cutamati;
pāvi teyvak kiḷaviyiṉ urittatu eṉka.  
111-126.	kāvalāḷar kaṇ tuyilkoḷḷa - ūrkāvalaruṭiya kaṇkaḷ uṟakkattip porunta, tūmeṉ cēkkit tuyilkaṇ viḻippa - tūya melliya paṭukkiyil tuyilkiṉṟa āṭavar makaḷiruṭiya kaṇkaḷ viḻippaṭiya, valampuric caṅkam vaṟitu eḻuntu ārppa-valampuriyākiya caṅkukaḷ poruḷiṉṟi, muḻaṅka, pulam puric caṅkam poruḷoṭu muḻaṅka-aṟiviṉmikka pulavar kūṭṭam poruḷoṭum muḻaṅka, pukarmuka vāraṇam neṭuṅkūviḷippa - puḷḷi poruntiya mukattiyuṭiya yāṉikaḷ nīṇṭa kūppīṭ ṭiṉic ceyya, poṟi mayir vāraṇam kuṟuṅkū viḷippa-poṟip poṟiyākiya mayirkaḷiyuṭiya kōḻikaḷ kuṟiya kūvutalicceyya, paṇinilip puravi pala eḻuntu āla - pantiyil niṟṟaliyuṭiya kutirikaḷ pala eḻuntu āṭa, paṇi nilip puḷḷum pala eḻuntu āla-kiḷikaḷiluḷḷa pala puṭkaḷum eḻuntu olikka, pūmpoḻil ārkip puḷoli ciṟappa-pūñcōliyil matuvuṇṇutaliyuṭiya vaṇṭu kaḷiṉ olimika, pūṅkoṭiyār kippuḷ oliciṟappa - pūṅkoṭi pōṉṟa makaḷiruṭiya kiyilaṇinta vaḷikaḷiṉ oli mika, kaṭavuṭ pīṭiki pūppali kaṭikoḷa - kaṭavuḷarkkuriya pīṭaṅkaḷiṟ koṭukkappaṭum polivupeṟṟa palikaḷ muṟṟup peṟa, kalampakar pīṭikip pūppali kaṭikoḷa-aṇikalaṅkaḷ viṟkap paṭukiṉṟa āvaṇa vītiyil malarāl ceyyappaṭum pūcaṉiyik kaṭiyiṭaṅkaḷ koḷḷa, kuyiluvar kaṭi toṟum paṇiyam parantu eḻa-tōṟ karuvikaḷip payil vōr vāyiltōṟum iciyuṭaṉ kūṭiya iyaṅkaḷiṉ oli paravi mēmpaṭa, koṭuppōr kaṭitoṟum paṇṇiyam parantu eḻa - koṭiyāḷarkaḷuṭiya vāyiltōṟum koṭuttaṟkuriya pala paṇṭaṅkaḷum parantu niṟiya, ūr tuyil eṭuppa-ūriṉit tuyil eḻuppumāṟu, uravu nīr aḻuvattuk kāriruḷ cīttuk katiravaṉ muḷittalum-kaṭaṟ parappilē kariya iruḷik keṭuttu ñāyiṟu tōṉṟutalum;  
kāvalāḷar iraviṭi viḻittōrākaliṉ viṭiyalil tuyilvā rāvar. pulampuriyākiya caṅkameṉka. pukarmuka vāraṇam kavaḷam vēṇṭik kūppiṭāniṟkum. puḷ-paṟavi. vaṇṭu, vaḷiyal, teyvaṅkaṭku iraviṟ pali koṭuppārākaliṉ atu muṭivuṟṟa teṉṟār, pū - polivu. illuṟi teyvattiṉ poruṭṭuk kāliyil pūviṭutal marapākaliṉ 'kalampakar pīṭikip pūppali kaṭikoḷa' eṉṟār ; kaṭi vāyilumām; 1"pūppali ceytu kāppukkaṭi niṟutti" eṉpatu kāṇka. paṇiyam-paṇṇappaṭṭa vāttiyam eṉṟumām. 
127-134.	ēvuṟu maññiyiṉ iṉintu - ampu paṭṭuruviya mayilippōla uḷḷam nintu, aṭi varunta mānakar vīti maruṅkil pōki - aṭikaḷ varuntak kaṭantu periya nakarattiṉ vītivaḻiyē ceṉṟu, pōya kaṅkulil pukuntati ellām-ceṉṟa iravil nikaḻntavaṟṟi ellām, mātavi taṉakku vaḻuviṉṟu urittalum-mātavikku vaḻuvātu kūṟutalum, naṉmaṇi iḻanta nākam pōṉṟu avaḷ-mātavi nalla maṇiyi iḻanta araviṉippōla, taṉmakaḷ vārāt taṉittuyar uḻappa - taṉ makaḷ vārātatāl oppaṟṟa tuṉpattāl varunta, iṉuyir iḻanta yākkiyiṉ iruntaṉaḷ tuṉṉiyatu uritta cutamatitāṉ eṉ - avaṭku nērntatik kūṟiya cutamatitāṉ iṉiya uyiriyiḻanta uṭalippōl aci vaṟṟiruntaṉaḷ eṉka. 
pukuntatiyellām: orumiyiṟ paṉmi. ‘naṉmaṇi yiḻanta nākam pōṉṟu' (25; 95) eṉa mēl innūluḷḷum, 2"iru nitik kiḻavaṉum perumaṉik kiḻattiyum, arumaṇi yiḻanta nākam pōṉṟatum" eṉac cilappatikārattilum maṇiyiḻanta nākamum, 3"iṉṉuyi riḻanta yākki yeṉṉat, tuṉṉiya cuṟṟam tuyark kaṭal vīḻntatum" eṉac cilappatikārattil uyiriḻanta yākkiyum uvamamāka varutal kāṇka.
maṇimēkalā teyvam nīṅkit tōṉṟi avaṉvayi ṉuritta piṉ pukuntu nīkki uriyeṉṟu pōyapiṉ, cutamati eḻuntu pal vēṟu ōtiyum icippak kēṭṭu uḷaṅkalaṅki nīṅkippōki oru puṭi iruttalum, pāvi teyvakkiḷaviyiṉ "varum, añcal" eṉ ṟurittatu ; ataṉik kēṭṭu naṭukkuṟu nallāḷ katiravaṉ muḷittalum pōki, mātavik kurittalum, avaḷ tuyaruḻappat tāṉ uyiriḻanta yākkipōl iruntaṉaḷ eṉa viṉi muṭikka.
tuyileḻuppiya kāti muṟṟiṟṟu. 


8. maṇipallavattut tuyaruṟṟa kāti  
 cutamati pukāriṉkaṇ ceyalaṟṟirukka, maṇipallavattiṟ kaṭalarukē maṇaliṟ ṟuyiṉṟa maṇimēkali, tuyiluṇarntu tāṉ muṉ paṟinta poruḷ oṉṟum kāṇappaṭātu aṟiyāta poruḷkaḷē kāṇappaṭutaliṉ vēṟiṭattiṟ ceṉṟu piṟanta uyir pōṉṟavaḷākit tikippuṟum poḻutu katiravaṉ utittāṉ. utittavuṭaṉ avaḷ 'ivviṭam uvavaṉattil muṉṉam kaṇṭaṟiyāta oru pakutiyō! itu naṉavō kaṉavō eṉpati aṟikiṉṟilēṉ; maṉam naṭuṅku kiṉṟatu: cutamati eṅkoḷittāy ? ōr māṟṟam tarukiṉṟili; iruḷ kaḻintatu; mātavi varuntuvaḷ ; itu viñciyuṭaṉ tōṉṟiya am maṭanti ceyta vañcamō! oṉṟum teriya villiyē! taṉiyē irukka mikavum añcukiṉṟēṉ; virintu varuvāyāka,' eṉṟu kūṟik koṇṭu nīrttuṟikaḷilum maṇaṟkuṉṟu kaḷilum eṅkaṇum ceṉṟu tēṭit taṉakkup pāṅkāyiṉār oruvariyum kāṇātavaḷākik kūvi aḻutu varuntupavaḷ, taṉ tantiyi niṉintu 'kōṟṟoṭi māta roṭu vēṟṟu nāṭaṭintu, vivāḷ uḻanta maṇippūṇ akalattu, aiyāvō!' eṉṟu aḻutaṉaḷ ; aḻupavaḷ muṉṉar, intiraṉāl iṭappaṭṭatum, taricittōrkkup paḻam piṟappip pulappaṭuttuvatumākiya putta pīṭiki tōṉṟiyatu.  
		īṅkiva ḷiṉṉaṇa māka iruṅkaṭal
vāṅkutiri yuṭutta maṇipal lavattiṭit
tattuvanī raṭikaric caṅkuḻu toṭuppiṉ
muttuviḷi kaḻaṉi muricem pavaḷamoṭu
5		virimara muruṭṭun tiriyulāp parappiṉ
ñāḻal ōṅkiya tāḻkaṇ acumpiṉ
āmpaluṅ kuvaḷiyum tāmpuṇarntu mayaṅki
vaṇṭuṇa malarnta kuṇṭunī rilañci
muṭakkāṟ puṉṉiyum maṭaṟpūn tāḻiyum
10		veyilvara voḻitta payilpūm pantar
aṟalviḷaṅku nilāmaṇal naṟumalarp paḷḷit
tuñcutuyi leḻūum añci lōti
kātaṟ cuṟṟam maṟantu kaṭikoḷa
vēṟiṭattup piṟanta uyirē pōṉṟu
15		paṇṭaṟi kiḷiyoṭu patiyuṅ kāṇāḷ
kaṇṭaṟi yātaṉa kaṇṇiṟ kāṇā
nīla mākkaṭal neṭṭiṭi yaṉṟiyuṅ
kāli ñāyiṟu katirvirittu muḷippa
uvavaṉa maruṅkiṉil ōriṭaṅ kollitu
20		cutamati oḷittāy tuyarañ ceytaṉi
naṉavō kaṉavō eṉpati aṟiyēṉ
maṉanaṭuk kuṟūum māṟṟan tārāy
valliruḷ kaḻintatu mātavi mayaṅkum
elvaḷi vārāy viṭṭakaṉ ṟaṉiyō
25		viñciyil tōṉṟiya viḷaṅkiḻi maṭavāḷ
vañcañ ceytaṉaḷ kollō aṟiyēṉ
orutaṉi yañcuveṉ tiruvē vāveṉat
tiritavaḻ paṟaviyum viriciṟip paṟaviyum
eḻuntuvīḻ cilliyum oṭuṅkuciṟi muḻuvalum
30		aṉṉac cēval araca ṉākap
paṉṉiṟap puḷḷiṉam parantoruṅ kīṇṭip
pācaṟi maṉṉar pāṭi pōla
vīcunīrp parappiṉ etireti rirukkum
tuṟiyun tuṟicūḻ neṭumaṇaṟ kuṉṟamum
35		yāṅkaṇun tirivōḷ pāṅkiṉaṅ kāṇāḷ
kuraṟṟalik kūntal kulintupiṉ vīḻa
araṟṟiṉaḷ kūuy aḻutaṉaḷ ēṅki
vīḻtuya reytiya viḻumak kiḷaviyil
tāḻtuya ruṟuvōḷ tantiyi uḷḷi
40		emmitiṟ paṭuttum vevviṉi yuruppak
kōṟṟoṭi mātaroṭu vēṟṟunā ṭaṭintu
vivā ḷuḻanta maṇippūṇ akalattu
aiyā vōveṉ ṟaḻuvōḷ muṉṉar
virintilaṅ kaviroḷi ciṟantukatir parappi
45		uripeṟu mummuḻam nilamici ōṅkit
ticitoṟum oṉpāṉ muḻanila makaṉṟu
vitimā ṇāṭiyiṉ vaṭṭaṅ kuyiṉṟu
patuma catura mīmici viḷaṅki
aṟavōṟ kaminta ācaṉa meṉṟē
50		naṟumala rallatu piṟamarañ coriyātu 
paṟaviyum mutirciṟi pāṅkuceṉ ṟatirātu
tēvarkō ṉiṭṭa māmaṇip pīṭiki
piṟappuviḷaṅ kaviroḷi aṟattaki yācaṉam
kīḻnila maruṅkiṉ nākanā ṭāḷum
55		iruvar maṉṉavar oruvaḻit tōṉṟi
ematī teṉṟē eṭukka lāṟṟār
tamperum paṟṟu nīṅkalum nīṅkār
ceṅkaṇ civantu neñcupuki uyirttut
tamperuñ cēṉiyoṭu veñcamam purināḷ
60		iruñceru oḻimiṉ ematīteṉṟē
peruntava muṉivaṉ iruntaṟa murikkum
poruvaṟu ciṟappiṟ puriyō rēttum
taruma pīṭiki tōṉṟiya tāṅkeṉ 
 uri                    
1-12.		īṅku ivaḷ iṉṉaṇamāka - pukār nakarilē cutamati ivvāṟu varunta, iruṅkaṭal vāṅku tiri uṭutta maṇi pallavat tiṭi-periya kaṭaliṉatu vaḷinta alikaḷāṟ cūḻappaṭṭa maṇipallavattiṉkaṇ, tattunīr aṭikaric caṅku uḻutoṭuppiṉ muttu viḷi kaḻaṉi-caṅkukaḷāl uḻutu vitikkappaṭṭa muttukkaḷ viḷikiṉṟa mōtunīr cūḻ aṭikariyākiya kaḻaṉiyil, muri cempavaḷamoṭu virimaram uruṭṭum tiri ulāpparappil-vaḷinta civanta pavaḷaṅkaḷuṭaṉ cantaṉam akil mutaliya maṇamporuntiya maraṅkaḷi uruṭṭukiṉṟa alikaḷ ulāvum kaṭaṟkariyil, ñāḻal ōṅkiya tāḻkaṇ acumpiṉ-pulinakak koṉṟi uyarnta tāḻnta iṭattiyuṭiya cēṟṟil, āmpalum kuvaḷiyum tām puṇarntu mayaṅki - alliyum kuvaḷiyu mākiya malarkaḷ cērntu kalantu, vaṇṭu uṇa malarnta kuṇṭu nīr ilañci-vaṇṭukaḷ uṇṇumāṟu malarappeṟṟa āḻamākiya nīriṉiyuṭiya poykik kariyil, muṭakkāl puṉṉiyum maṭaṟpūn  tāḻiyum - vaḷinta aṭiyi yuṭiya puṉṉiyum maṭalkaḷālākiya pūviṉiyuṭiya tāḻiyum, veyil varavu oḻinta payil pūmpantar-veyiliṉatu varavi nīkkiya aḻakiya pantariṉkaṇ, aṟal viḷaṅku nilāmaṇal naṟumalarp paḷḷi - aṟal viḷaṅkukiṉṟa nilāvip pōṉṟa veḷḷiya maṇalil naṟiya malarkaḷālākiya paṭukkiyi liruntu, tuñcu tuyil eḻūum añcil ōti - meymaṟanta uṟakkattiṉiṉṟum eḻunta aḻakiya cilavākiya kūntali yuṭiya maṇimēkali; 
aṭikarikkaṇ kaḻaṉiyil eṉṟumām. toṭuppu - vitippu; 1"toṭuppi ṉāyiram vittiyatu viḷiya" eṉpatu kāṇka; viḷāk koṇṭu uḻutalumām. muri eṉpataṟkuc citaṟiya eṉṟum, oḷiviṭu meṉṟum urittalumām. aṟal - nīr arittu oḻukuvatāl maṇal aṟṟu aṟṟu iruppatu. maṇipallavat tiṭip parappil ilañcik kariyil maṇaliṟ paḷḷiyil tuyileḻunta maṇimēkali yeṉka. 
13-27.	kātaṟ cuṟṟam maṟantu kaṭikoḷa vēṟiṭattup piṟanta uyirē pōṉṟu - aṉpiṉ mikka cuṟṟaṅkaḷi maṟantu muṭivu koḷḷa vēṟiṭattil tōṉṟiya uyirippōla, paṇṭu aṟi kiḷiyoṭu patiyum kāṇāḷ-muṉṉart taṉṉālaṟiyap paṭṭa cuṟṟattuṭaṉ nakarattiyum kāṇātavaḷāki, kaṇṭu aṟiyātaṉa kaṇṇiṟ kāṇā - muṉ kaṇṭaṟiyap paṭātaṉa vaṟṟik kaṇkaḷāṟ kaṇṭu, nīla māk kaṭal neṭṭiṭi aṉṟiyum - nīlaniṟamuṭiya periya kaṭalil aṇimikkaṇ, kāli ñāyiṟu katir virittu muḷippa - iḷañāyiṟu katirkaḷi virittu utikka, uvavaṉa maruṅkil ōriṭam kol itu-iḵtu uvavaṉattil ōriṭamō, cutamati oḷittāy- cutamatī  oḷintu koṇṭāyō, tuyaram ceytaṉi - tuṉpam ceytaṉi, naṉavō kaṉavō eṉpati aṟiyēṉ-itu naṉavō kaṉavō eṉpati aṟikiṉṟilēṉ, maṉam naṭukkuṟūum māṟṟam tārāy-uḷḷam naṭuṅkutaluṟukiṉṟatu maṟumoḻi taruvāy, valliruḷ kaḻintatu mātavi mayaṅkum-valitākiya iruḷ nīṅkiyatu mātavi mayaṅkuvāḷ, elvaḷi vārāy-oḷi poruntiya vaḷiyiṉi yuṭiyāy varuvāyāka, viṭṭu akaṉṟaṉiyō - eṉṉiviṭṭuc ceṉṟaṉiyō, viñciyil tōṉṟiya viḷaṅkiḻi maṭavāḷ - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa aṇikaḷuṭaṉ vittiyiṉālē tōṉṟiyamaṭanti, vañcam ceytaṉaṉ kollō aṟiyēṉ - vañcaṉi ceytaṉaḷō aṟiyēṉē, oru taṉi añcuvēṉ tiruvē vā eṉa-oppaṟṟa taṉimiyi añcukiṉṟēṉ tiruvaṉiyāy varuka eṉṟu;  
kaṭikoḷa - muṟṟa; viṉimuṭi veyta eṉṟumām. neṭṭiṭi aṉṟiyum - neṭuntūram aṉṟāka; aṇimiyil; um: aci. uvavaṉam eṉpatu mutal maṇimēkali kūṟṟu. ōriṭaṅkol-muṉ kaṇṭaṟiyāta ōriṭamō veṉka. vārāy - varukiṉṟili eṉavum, varātu eṉavum urittalumām. melvaḷi yeṉṟalumām.  
28-35.	tiri tavaḻ paṟaviyum viriciṟip paṟaviyum-aliyil tavaḻkiṉṟa puṭkaḷum virinta ciṟakiṉiyuṭiya paṟavikaḷum, eḻuntu vīḻ cilliyum oṭuṅkuciṟi muḻuvalum - eḻuntu vīḻkiṉṟa cilliyum oṭuṅkiya ciṟakiṉiyuṭiya muḻuvalum, aṉṉac cēval aracaṉākap paṉṉiṟap puḷḷiṉam parantu oruṅku īṇṭi - aṉṉaccēvali aracaṉākak koṇṭu pala  niṟaṅkaḷiyuṭiya paṟavi iṉaṅkaḷ parantu oṉṟākak kūṭi, pācaṟi maṉṉar pāṭi pōla - pakiyaracar iruvaruṭiya paṭi vīṭukaḷil iruvar paṭiyum etiretir iruttalpōla, vīcunīrp parappiṉ etir etir  irukkum -vīcukiṉṟa nīrpparappiṉ kaṇṇē etiretirē  irukkiṉṟa, tuṟiyum tuṟicūḻ neṭumaṇaṟ kuṉṟamum - nīrt tuṟikaḷum tuṟikaḷic cuṟṟilumuḷḷa periya maṇaṟ kuṉṟukaḷum ākiya,yāṅkaṇum tirivōṉ-evviṭattum tirikiṉṟavaḷ; 
tiritavaḻ paṟavi - kuḷuvi mutaliyaṉa. viriciṟip paṟavi - pōtā mutaliyaṉa. eḻuntuvīḻ cilli - ciral mutaliyaṉa. tiri tavaḻ paṟavi mutaliya nāṉkum nīrvāḻ paṟavikaḷ. paṟaviyum paṟaviyum cilliyum muḻuvalumākiya paṉṉiṟap puḷḷiṉam eṉka. pācaṟi maṉṉar - pōr kuṟittup pācaṟiyiṟ ceṉṟu taṅkiyirukkum maṉṉar. pāṭipōla - pāṭiyiṟ paṭikaḷ etiretir iruttalpōla - 1"kampuṭ kōḻiyum kaṉikural nāriyum, ceṅkā laṉṉamum piṅkāṟ kokkum, kāṉak kōḻiyum nīlaniṟak kākkiyum, uḷḷum ūralum puḷḷum putāvum, velpōr vēntar muṉiyiṭam pōlap. palvēṟu kuḻūukkural paranta vōtiyum" eṉpatu īṇṭu ottu nōkkaṟpālatu. 
35-43.	pāṅkiṉam kāṇāḷ-pakkattiluḷḷa iṉaṅkaḷik kāṇātavaḷāy, kuraltali kūntal kulintu piṉ vīḻa - pūṅkotti iṭattēyuṭiya kuḻal aviḻntu piṉṉē vīḻa, araṟṟiṉaḷ kūuy - kūvip pulampi, aḻutaṉaḷ ēṅki - ēṅki aḻutu, vīḻtuyar eytiya viḻumak kiḷaviyiṉ tāḻtuyar uṟuvōḷ - āḻamākiya tuṉpattiṉkaṇṇē poruntiṉōḷ mikka tuyaramaṭinta tuṉpattip pulappaṭuttum moḻikaḷāl, tantiyi uḷḷi - tantiyākiya kōvalaṉi niṉintu, em itil paṭuttum vevviṉi uruppa-emmi ivvakit tuṉpattiṉkaṇ akappaṭuttum koṭiya tīviṉiyāṉatu vantu uruppa, kōṟṟoṭi mātaroṭu vēṟṟu nāṭu aṭintu-tiraṭciyākiya vaḷiyalkaḷi yaṇinta eṉ aṉṉi kaṇṇaki yōṭu vēṟu nāṭṭi aṭintu, vivāḷ uḻanta maṇippūṇ akalattu aiyāvō eṉṟu aḻuvōḷ muṉṉar - kūriya vāḷiṉāl veṭṭuṇṭu varuntiya maṇippūṇ tāṅkiya mārpiṉiyuṭiya aiyāvō eṉṟu aḻukiṉṟavaḷ muṉṉē; 
kural - kūntaliṉ tiraṭciyumām. araṟṟiṉaḷ, aḻutaṉaḷ eṉpaṉa muṟṟeccam. uṟuvōḷ viḻumakkiḷaviyiṉ aḻuvōḷ eṉak kūṭṭuka. uruppa - mutira; vekuḷa eṉṟumām. ōkāram pulampalil vantatu. 
44-53.	virintu ilaṅku aviroḷi ciṟantu - virintu viḷaṅku kiṉṟa pēroḷi mikku, katirparappi-kiraṇaṅkaḷi virittu, uri peṟu mummuḻam nilamici ōṅki - pukaḻaminta nilattiṉ mītu mūṉṟu muḻa aḷaviṉukku uyarntu, ticitoṟum oṉpāṉ muḻanilam akaṉṟu-nāṟṟiciyilum oṉpatumuḻa aḷaviṉiyuṭiya iṭam akaṉṟu, vitimāṇ āṭiyiṉ vaṭṭam kuyiṉṟu -viti māṭcimippaṭṭa paḷiṅkiṉāl vaṭṭañ ceyyappaṭṭu, patuma caturam mīmici viḷaṅki-patumattiyuṭiya caturam mēliṭattil viḷaṅkappeṟṟu, aṟavōṟku aminta ācaṉam eṉṟē-puttaṉukku aminta ākaṉam eṉṟu, naṟumalar allatu piṟa marañ coriyātu - maraṅkaḷ naṟumalar kaḷi aṉṟi vēṟoṉṟiyum coriyā, paṟaviyum mutirciṟi pāṅku ceṉṟu atirātu - paṟavikaḷum appīṭikiyiṉ pakkattiṟ ceṉṟu ciṟiyoli ceyyā, tēvarkōṉ iṭṭa māmaṇippīṭiki - intiraṉāliṭappaṭṭa perumi poruntiya maṇikaḷāṉāya pīṭiki, piṟappu viḷaṅku aṟattaki ācaṉam-kāṇpōratu paḻam piṟappu viḷaṅkutaṟku ētuvākiya oḷi poruntiya aṟattiṉiyalpuṭiya ācaṉam;  
ciṟantu parappi ōṅki akaṉṟu kuyiṉṟu viḷaṅki aminta ācaṉam eṉka. viti - ceyki. patumam - puttaṉ pātamum ām. mīmici, oru poruṭ paṉmoḻi. maram piṟa coriyātu, paṟavi ciṟi atirātu, avvāṟākat tēvar kōṉ iṭṭa pīṭikiyeṉa virikka. 
54-63. 	kīḻnila maruṅkiṉ nākanāṭu āḷum - kīḻt ticikkaṇ uḷḷa nāka nāṭṭiṉi āḷum. iruvar maṉṉavar oruvaḻit tōṉṟi-maṉṉavar iruvar oruṅku tōṉṟi, ematu ītu eṉṟē eṭukkal āṟṟār tam perum paṟṟu nīṅkalu nīṅkār - iḵtu em muṭiyatu emmuṭiyatu eṉṟu kūṟi eṭukkavum iyalātavarkaḷāyt tammuṭiya periya viruppam nīṅku taliyum ceyyātavarkaḷāy, ceṅkaṇ civantu neñcupuki uyirttu-civanta kaṇkaḷ ciṉattāṟ civantu maṉaṅkotittu, tam perum cēṉiyōṭu veñcamam purināḷ-tammuṭiya peruñ cēṉikaḷuṭam koṭiya pōr puriyunāḷil, iruñceru oḻimiṉ ematu ītu eṉṟē - nīvīr numatu periya pōri nīṅkumiṉ; iḵtu emmuṭiyatu eṉṟu kūṟi, peruntava muṉivaṉ iruntu aṟam urikkum - periya tavamuṉi vaṉākiya puttaṉ iruntu aṟam uritta, poruvaṟu ciṟappil puriyōr ēttum-oppaṟṟa ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya uyarntōrkaḷ tutikkiṉṟa, taruma pīṭiki tōṉṟiyatu āṅkeṇ - taruma pīṭiki avviṭattil tōṉṟiyatu eṉka.  
nāka ilacciṉi yuṭimiyāl nākareṉap peyarpeṟṟa oruvaki makkaḷ vāḻum nāṭu nākanāṭu eṉpar. innāṭṭiṉ peyar piṉṉarum innūliṟ palaviṭattu vantuḷatu. tama, a-āṟaṉurupu. nīṅkalum nīṅkār eṉṉum aṭukkiṉuḷ piṉṉatu potuviṉiyākum ; muṉpu, "cuḻalaluñ cuḻalum, ōṭalu mōṭum oru ciṟi yotuṅki, nīṭalum nīṭum," (3 : 111-3) eṉa vantamiyuṅ kāṇka.
māmaṇip pīṭiki, aṟattakiyācaṉam, taruma pīṭiki eṉpaṉa oru poruḷmēl vantaṉa. aḻuvōḷ muṉṉar pīṭiki tōṉṟiya teṉka.
ivaḷ iṉṉaṇamāka, maṇipallavattiṭit tuyileḻum añci lōti, pōṉṟu kāṇāḷāyk kaṇṭu tiruvē vāveṉṟu aḻuvōḷ muṉṉart taruma pīṭiki āṅkut tōṉṟiyateṉka.  
maṇipallavattut tuyaruṟṟa kāti muṟṟiṟṟu. 


9. pīṭiki kaṇṭu piṟappuṇarnta kāti  
pīṭikiyik kaṇṭa maṇimēkali viyappiṉāl taṉṉi yaṟiyāḷāyiṉāḷ; avaḷ kikaḷ talimēṟ kuvintaṉa ; ataṉi mummuṟi valamvantu paṇintu eḻupavaḷ ataṉ kāṭciyāl taṉ paḻampiṟappiṉ nikaḻccikaḷi yuṇarntu, "mātava! kāyaṅkari yeṉṉum natikkariyil nī kūṟiyavellām uṇmiyātali aṟintēṉ; kāntāra nāṭṭiṉ aracaṉākiya attipati yeṉṉum aracaṟku mittuṉaṉākiya piramatarumaṉē! nī avaṉpāṟ ceṉṟu aṟamurikkiyil, ‘in nāvalan tīvil iṟṟikku ēḻām nāḷiṟ pūkampa muṇṭākum; appoḻutu in nakarum nākanāṭṭil nāṉūṟu yōcaṉip parappum pātalattil vīḻntu keṭṭoḻiyum: ātaliṉ itiṉiṉṟum nīṅkuka,' eṉṉa, aracaṉum nakariluḷḷa makkaṭkup paṟicāṟṟi yaṟivittu iṭavayameṉṉum appatiyi nīṅki, vaṭakkiluḷḷa avanti nakarkkuc celvōṉ iṭiyē kāyaṅkariyiṉ kariyiṟ pāṭiceytiruppa, nī kuṟitta nāḷil annakar aḻintatu, atukaṇṭa aracaṉum ēṉiyarum niṉṉic cūḻntu vaṇaṅka, nī avarkaṭku aruḷaṟattip pōtittuk koṇṭiruntaṉi; appoḻutu acōtara nakarattaracaṉākiya iravivaṉmaṉtēvi amutapati vayiṟṟiṟ piṟantu ilakkumi yeṉṟu peyareyti, attipati yeṉṉum aracaṟku nīlapati yeṉpavaḷpāṟ piṟanta irākulaṉukku maṉiviyākap pukka nāṉ eṉ kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ vantu aṟaṅ kēṭṭaṟku vaṇaṅkiyavuṭaṉ, nī eṉṉi nōkki, "ivvirākulaṉ iṟṟikkup patiṉāṟām nāḷil tiṭṭiviṭameṉṉum pāmpāl iṟappāṉ, nī ivaṉuṭaṉ tīyiṟpukuvāy; piṉpu kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉattiṟ ceṉṟu piṟappāy; aṅku niṉakku oru tuṉpam uṇṭākum; appoḻutu maṇimēkalā teyvam vantu naḷḷiravil uṉṉi eṭuttucceṉṟu teṉṟici maruṅkiluḷḷa tīvu oṉṟil vittuccellum; ceṉṟapiṉ āṅkuḷḷa putta pīṭikiyik kaṇṭu toḻuvāy; appoḻutē uṉatu muṟpiṟappil nikaḻnta ceytikaḷi aṟintu, iṉṟu yāṉ kūṟiya uriyiṉit teḷivāy,' eṉṟu colla, "eṉ kātalaṉ piṟappiyum terivikkavēṇṭum" eṉṟu kēṭṭēṉ; ‘uṉṉikkoṇṭu ceṉṟa teyvam mīṇṭun tōṉṟi, uṉakku avaṉip pulappaṭuttum,' eṉṟu kūṟiṉāy; at teyvam varātō?" eṉa ēṅki aḻutukoṇṭiruntaṉaḷ. 
		āṅkatu kaṇṭa āyiḻi aṟiyāḷ
kāntaḷañ ceṅki talimēṟ kuvintaṉa
talimēṟ kuvinta kiyaḷ ceṅkaṇ
mulimēṟ kaluḻntumut tattira ḷukuttatiṉ 
5		iṭamuṟi mummuṟi valamuṟi vārāk
koṭimiṉ mukiloṭu nilañcērn teṉṉa
iṟunucup palaca veṟunilañ cērntāṅku
eḻuvōḷ piṟappu vaḻuviṉ ṟuṇarntu
toḻutaki mātava tuṇiporu ḷuṇarntōy
10		kāyaṅ kariyiṉī yurittati yellām
vāyē yākutaṉ mayakkaṟa uṇarntēṉ
kāntāra meṉṉuṅ kaḻiperu nāṭṭup
pūruva tēyam poṟikeṭa vāḻum
atti patiyeṉum aracāḷ vēntaṉ
15		mittuṉa ṉākiya pirama tarumaṉ
āṅkavaṉ ṟaṉpāl aṇintaṟa ṉurippōy
tīṅkaṉi nāval ōṅkumit tīviṭi
iṉṟēḻ nāḷil irunila mākkaḷ
niṉṟunaṭuk keyta nīṇila vēntē
20		pūmi naṭukkuṟūum pōḻtat tinnakar
nākanaṉ ṉāṭṭu nāṉūṟi yōcaṉi
viyaṉpā talattu vīḻntukē ṭeytum
itaṉpā loḻikeṉa irunila vēntaṉum
māperum pērūr makkaṭ kellām
25		āvum māvuṅ koṇṭukaḻi keṉṟē
paṟiyiṟ cāṟṟi niṟiyarun tāṉiyō
ṭiṭavaya meṉṉum irumpati nīṅki
vaṭavayiṉ avanti mānakarc celvōṉ
kāyaṅ kariyeṉum pēriyāṟ ṟaṭikaric
30		cēyuyar pūmpoḻiṟ pāṭicey tiruppa
eṅkōṉ nīyāṅ kurittavan nāḷiṭit
taṅkā tannakar vīḻntukē ṭeytalum
maruḷaṟu pulavaniṉ malaraṭi yataṉi
aracoṭu makka ḷellām īṇṭic
35		cūḻntaṉar vaṇaṅkit tāḻntupala ēttiya
aruḷaṟam pūṇṭa orupē riṉpattu
ulakutuyar keṭuppa aruḷiya annāḷ
aravak kaṭaloli acōtaram āḷum 
iravi vaṉmaṉ oruperun tēvi
40		alattakac cīṟaṭi amuta pativayiṟṟu
ilakkumi yeṉṉum peyarpeṟṟup piṟantēṉ
atti patiyeṉum aracaṉ peruntēvi
cittipuram āḷuñ cītaraṉ tirumakaḷ
nīla patiyeṉum nēriḻi vayiṟṟil
45		kāli ñāyiṟṟuk katirpōṟ ṟōṉṟiya
irākulaṉ ṟaṉakkup pukkēṉ avaṉoṭu
parāvaru marapiṉiṉ pātam paṇitalum
eṭṭiru nāḷiliv virākulaṉ ṟaṉṉit
tiṭṭi viṭamuṇuñ celluyir pōṉāl
50		tīyaḻal avaṉoṭu cēyiḻi mūḻkuvi
ētu nikaḻcci īṅkiṉ ṟākaliṉ
kavēra kaṉṉip peyaroṭu viḷaṅkiya
tavākkaḷi mūtūrc ceṉṟupiṟap peytuti
aṇiyiḻi niṉakkōr aruntuyar varunāḷ
55		maṇimē kalāteyvam vantu tōṉṟi
aṉṟap patiyil āriruḷ eṭuttut
teṉṟici maruṅkilōr tīviṭi vittalum
vēka ventiṟal nākanāṭ ṭaracar
ciṉamā coḻittu maṉamācu tīrttāṅku
60		aṟaccevi tiṟantu maṟaccevi yaṭittup
piṟavippiṇi maruttuvaṉ iruntaṟa murikkum
tiruntoḷi yācaṉañ ceṉṟuki toḻuti
aṉṟip pakalē uṉpiṟap puṇarntīṅku
iṉṟiyā ṉuritta uriteḷi vāyeṉac
65		cātuyar kēṭṭut taḷarntuku maṉattēṉ
kātalaṉ piṟappuṅ kāṭṭā yōveṉa
āṅkuṉik koṇarnta arumperun teyvam
pāṅkiṟ ṟōṉṟip pintoṭi kaṇavaṉi
īṅkivaṉ eṉṉum eṉṟeṭut tōtiṉi
70		āṅkat teyvatam vārā tōveṉa
ēṅkiṉaḷ aḻūum iḷaṅkoṭi tāṉeṉ. 
uri                 
1-8.  		āṅkatu kaṇṭa āyiḻi aṟiyāḷ - pīṭikiyik kaṇṭa maṇimēkali taṉṉi aṟiyāḷāyiṉāḷ, kāntaḷ amceṅki tali mēṟ kuvintaṉa - kāntaḷ malarpōlum civanta kikaḷ talimītu kuvintaṉa, talimēṟ kuvinta kiyaḷ - talimītu kuvinta kiyi yuṭiyavaḷ, ceṅkaṇ mulimēṟ kaluḻntu muttat tiraḷukuttu-civanta kaṇkaḷ kalakkik koṅkikaḷiṉ mēlē nīrttuḷikaḷic corintu, atiṉ iṭamuṟi mummuṟi valamuṟi vārā - appīṭikiyiṉ iṭamāka viruntu mummuṟi valamāka vantu, koṭimiṉ mukiloṭu nilam cērnteṉṉa-miṉṉukkoṭi mukiluṭaṉ nilattiyaṭintāṟpōla, iṟu nucuppu alaca veṟunilam cērntāṅku - iṟumpaṭiyāṉa nuṇṇiya iṭi varuntumāṟu veṟu nilattiṟ cērntu vaṇaṅki, eḻuvōḷ - eḻukiṉṟavaḷ, piṟappu vaḻuviṉṟu uṇarntu-taṉ muṟpiṟaviyit tavaṟiṉṟi aṟintu; 
aṟiyāḷ - taṉṉi maṟantāḷ; itu putumipaṟṟi vanta maruṭki yeṉṉum meyppāṭu. muttam eṉṟatu nīrttuḷiyi. ceṅkaṇ kaluḻntu mulimēl ukutteṉa māṟuka. koṭimiṉ-miṉṉukkoṭi. miṉ uruvattiṟkum, mukil kūntalukkum uvami. nilañcērntu - vaṇaṅki yeṉṟapaṭi.  
9-15. 	toḻutaki mātava - vaṇaṅkuvataṟkut takka peruntava muṭiyōy, tuṇiporuḷ uṇarntōy-meynnūl kaḷāl tuṇiyappaṭṭa meypporuḷi uṇarntōy, kāyaṅkariyil nī urittati ellām - kāyaṅkari eṉṉum yāṟṟaṅkariyil nī moḻintavi aṉittum, vāyē ākutal mayakkaṟa uṇarntēṉ- meymmiyāku talit teḷivuṟa aṟintēṉ, kāntāram eṉṉum kaḻiperu nāṭṭup pūruvatēyam-kāntāram eṉṉum mikap periya nāṭṭil uḷḷa pūruvatēyamāṉatu, poṟikeṭa vāḻum-pāvaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum nīṅkumāṟu vāḻnta, attipati eṉṉum aracāḷ vēntaṉ-aracu purinta attipati eṉum maṉṉaṉukku, mittuṉaṉ ākiya pirama tarumaṉ -mittuṉaṉākiya pirama tarumaṉē;  
"toḻutaki mātava" eṉpatu mutal "eṉṟeṭut tōtiṉi" eṉpatu kāṟum uḷḷavi paḻam piṟappil piramatarumaṉ eṉṉum muṉivaṉ taṉakkuk kūṟiya vaṟṟip putta pīṭikik kāṭci yālaṟintu, maṇimēkali avaṉi etirpeytu kūṟiyavi. tuṇiporuḷ - teḷiyappaṭṭa poruḷ. urittati; ai: cāriyi, vāy - uṇmi. poṟi īṇṭu pāvaccumi. vāḻum vēntaṉ eṉka; vāḻum piramatarumaṉ eṉiṉumām. piramatarumaṉ: viḷi.  
16-23. 	āṅkavaṉ taṉpāl aṇintu aṟaṉ urippōy - ammaṉṉavaṉiṭam vantu aṟaṅkūṟum nī, tīṅkaṉi nāval ōṅkum ittīviṭi - iṉiya kaṉikaḷiyuṭiya nāval ōṅkiya ittīviṉkaṇ, iṉṟēḻ nāḷil-iṉṟikku ēḻāvatu nāḷil, irunila mākkaḷ niṉṟu naṭukkeyta nīḷnila vēntē pūmi naṭuṅkuṟūum pōḻ tattu - periya nilattiyāḷum aracaṉē periya pūmiyiluḷḷa makkaḷ niṉṟu naṭukka meytu māṟu pūmi naṭukkutaluṟum poḻutu; innakar nāka naṉṉāṭṭu nāṉūṟu yōcaṉi - inta nakarum naṉṟākiya nāka nāṭṭiṉkaṇ nāṉūṟu yōcaṉip parappum, viyaṉpātalattu vīḻntu kēṭeytum-akaṉṟa pātalattiṉkaṇ viḻuntu aḻivuṟum. ākaliṉ, itaṉpāl oḻikeṉa - innakarattiviṭṭu nīṅkuka eṉṟu kūṟa; 
nāvalōṅkun tīvu - nāvalan tīvu; camput tīvu. urikkum nī ‘vēntē! kēṭeytum ākalāl itaṉpāl oḻika'veṉa eṉka. 
23-30. 	irunila vēntaṉum - ataṉik kēṭṭa periya pūmiyi āḷum aracaṉum, māperum pērūr makkaṭku ellām - mikap periya nakarattiṉkaṇuḷḷa makkaḷuk kellām, āvum māvum koṇṭu kaḻika eṉṟē paṟiyiṟ cāṟṟi - pacukkaḷiyum ēṉiya vilaṅkukaḷiyum koṇṭu nakarattiviṭṭu nīṅkuka eṉṟu paṟiyaṟintu terivittu, niṟi arum tāṉiyoṭu iṭavayam eṉṉum irumpati nīṅki-pakimēṟ cellum pōtu niṟuttalariya cēṉikaḷuṭaṉ iṭavayam eṉṉum periya nakarattiṉiṉṟum nīṅki, vaṭavayiṉ avanti mānakarc celvōṉ - vaṭaticiyiluḷḷa avanti eṉṉum periya nakarattiṟkuc cellukiṉṟavaṉ; kāyaṅkari eṉum pēriyāṟṟu aṭikaric cēyuyar pūmpoḻil pāṭi ceytiruppa - kāyaṅkari eṉṉum periya yāṟṟiṉatu aṭikarikkaṇ mika uyarnta pūñcōliyiṟ pāṭi ceytu amarntirukka; 
iṭavayam - attipatiyiṉ nakaram. pāṭi -paṭivīṭu. vēntaṉum cāṟṟic celvōṉ iruppa veṉka.  
31-37.	eṅkōṉ nī āṅku uritta annāḷiṭi - emperu māṉākiya nī appōtu kūṟiya an nāḷilēyē, taṅkātu an nakar vīḻntu kēṭu eytalum-tāḻāmal an nakaram vīḻntu aḻintoḻitalum, maruḷ aṟu pulava niṉ malaraṭi ataṉi - mayakkamaṟṟa meyyaṟi viṉiyuṭiyōy niṉ malar pōlum tiruvaṭikaḷi, aracoṭu makkaḷ ellām īṇṭi-aracaṉuṭaṉ makkaḷaṉivarum kūṭi, cūḻntaṉar vaṇaṅkit tāḻntu pala ēttiya - cuṟṟiyavarkaḷāy vaṇaṅkip paṇintu palavāṟākat tutikka, aruḷaṟam pūṇṭa oru pēr iṉpattu ulaku tuyar keṭuppa aruḷiya annāḷ - aruḷākiya aṟattiṉikkoṇṭa oppaṟṟa pēriṉpatti ulaka makkaḷiṉ tuyaratti nīkkum poruṭṭu aruḷic ceyta an nāḷil; 
nakar kēṭeytiṉami kūṟavē nākanāṭṭu nāṉūṟu yōcaṉip parappuk kēṭeytiya teṉpatum peṟṟām. ēttiya - ētta; ēttiya annāḷ aruḷiya annāḷ eṉat taṉittaṉi muṭittalumām. iṉpattu iṉpatti: cāriyi niṟka urupu tokkatu. 
38-47.	aravakkaṭal oli acōtaram āḷum-kaṭal olipōṉṟa muḻakkattiyuṭiya acōtara    nakarattiyāḷum, iravivaṉmaṉ oru peruntēvi - iravivaṉmaṉuṭiya oppaṟṟa peruntēvi yākiya, alattakac cīṟaṭi amutapati vayiṟṟu-cempañcik kuḻampu tōynta ciṟiya aṭikaḷiyuṭiya amutapati eṉpavaḷ vayiṟṟil, ilakkumi eṉṉum peyar peṟṟup  piṟantēṉ-piṟantu ilakkumi eṉṉum peyar peṟṟēṉ, attipati eṉum aracaṉ peruntēvi -attipati eṉṉum aracaṉuṭiya peruntēviyum, cittipuram āḷum cītaraṉ tirumakaḷ-cittipuratti āṇṭa cītaraṉ eṉpavaṉuṭiya aḻakiya makaḷum ākiya, nīlapati eṉum  nēriḻi vayiṟṟil - nīlapati eṉkiṉṟa melliyaliṉ vayiṟṟil, kāli ñāyiṟṟuk katirpōl tōṉṟiya-katirkaḷuṭiya iḷamparitiyippōl utitta, irākulaṉ taṉakkup pukkēṉ - irākulaṉukku maṉiviyākap pukuntēṉ, avaṉoṭu parāvaru marapiṉ niṉ pātam paṇitalum-avvirākulaṉōṭu vaḻuttutaṟkuriya niṉ aṭikaḷip paṇitalum;
 kaṭal aravameṉa māṟuka. alattakaṉ - cevvarakku eṉpārumuḷar. piṟantu peyar peṟṟēṉ eṉa vikuti pirittuk kūṭṭuka. katir ñāyiṟu eṉka. 
48-50.	eṭṭiru nāḷil ivvirākulaṉ taṉṉit tiṭṭiviṭam uṇum-patiṉāṟu nāḷil inta irākulaṉit tiṭṭiviṭam eṉṉum pāmpu uṇṇum, celluyir pōṉāl tīyaḻal avaṉoṭu cēyiḻi mūḻkuvi - avaṉatu uyir pirintāl nīyum avaṉoṭu tīyiṭik kuḷippāy; 
tiṭṭi - tiruṣṭi; tiṭṭiviṭam-kaṇṇil nañcuṭiya pāmpu; atu pārttāl uyirkaḷ cām eṉpar. innūliṉkaṇ piṉpu (11; 100 ; 21 ; 11; 21: 40 ; 23 : 69 ; 23 : 84.) palaviṭattu itu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷami kāṇka. 1"tiṭṭiyiṉ viṭa maṉṉa kaṟpiṉ celviyi" eṉṟār kamparum. cēyiḻi:muṉṉili.  
51-53.	ētu nikaḻcci īṅku iṉṟātaliṉ-uyarnili eytutaṟkuriya ētu nikaḻcci ivviṭattil    illiyākaliṉ, kavēra kaṉṉip peyaroṭu viḷaṅkiya-kāviriyiṉ peyaroṭu viḷaṅkiya (kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉam eṉṉum), tavākkaḷimūtūr ceṉṟu piṟappu eytuti- aḻiyāta makiḻcciyiṉiyuṭiya mūtūriṉ kaṇ ceṉṟupiṟappiṉi yaṭivāy;
