1-6.   		pattiṉip peṇṭir pāttūṇ ēṟṟa - kaṟpiṟciṟanta ātiri nallāḷāṟ pakuttuṇṇum uṇaviṉip peṟṟa, piccip pāttirap peruñ cōṟṟu amali-amutacurapiyiluḷḷa periya cōṟṟuttiraḷi, aṟattiṉ īṭṭiya oṇporuḷ - aṟaneṟiyiṉālīṭṭappaṭṭa oḷḷiya poruḷ, aṟavōṉ tiṟattu vaḻippaṭūum ceyki pōla - aṟañ ceyvōṉ karuttiṉ vaḻiyē ceṉṟu payaṉpaṭumāṟu pōla, vāṅku ki varunta maṉṉuyirkku aḷittu- ēṟkum kikaḷ varuntumāṟu uyirkaṭku mika aḷittum, tāṉ tolivu illāt takimi nōkki-tāṉ kuṟivupaṭāttaṉmiyik kaṇṭu; 
 pāttu - pakuttu eṉpataṉ marūu. pāttūṇ - iyalpuṭiya mūvarkkum teṉpulattār mutaliya nālvarkkum pakuttuṇṭaṟ kuriya uṇavu ; itaṉāl ātiriyaḷitta uṇaviṉ takutiyum, ātiriyiṉ maṉiyaṟa māṇpum peṟappaṭṭaṉa; 1''paḻiyañcip pāttū ṇuṭittāyiṉ vāḻkki, vaḻiyeñca leññāṉṟu mil'' eṉpatu aṟiyaṟ pālatu. ātiripāṟ peṟṟataṟku aṟattiṉ īṭṭiyatum, maṇimēkali karutiyavāṟu maṉṉuyirkkaḷikka atu payaṉpaṭutaṟku aṟavōṉ tiṟattu vaḻippaṭutalum, uvami yāyiṉa. iṉi, aṟavōṉ eṉpataṟkuc caṟpāttiram eṉṟum, tiṟattu vaḻippaṭum eṉpataṟkup peṟuvōr perumikkut takkapaṭi perukumeṉṟum karuttukkoṇṭaṉar makā makōpāttiyāya ṭākṭar cāminātiyar; īṇṭu atu poruntu mēṟ koḷka. piṟa uyirkaṭkum aḷikkappaṭiṉum cōṟuṇṭali iyalpāka vuṭiyar makkaḷātaliṉ, 'vāṅkuki varunta' eṉṟār. ik karuttu muṉṉarum vantuḷḷami kāṇka. 
 7-16.   yāṉit tī nōy akavayiṟṟu aṭakkiya kāyacaṇṭiki eṉum kāriki vaṇaṅki - yāṉittī eṉṉum perumpaci nōyiṉit taṉ vayiṟṟiṟkoṇṭa kāyacaṇṭiki eṉṉum maṭanti maṇimēkaliyi vaṇaṅki, neṭiyōṉ mayaṅki nilamici tōṉṟi aṭalarumunnīr aṭitta ñāṉṟu - tirumāl mayakkattāl nilamici irāmaṉākat tōṉṟi vellutaṟkariya kaṭali aṭitta poḻutu, kuraṅku koṇarntu eṟinta neṭumali ellām-kuraṅkukaḷ koṇṭuvantu vīciya periya malikaḷellām, aṇaṅkuṭi aḷakkar vayiṟu pukkāṅku - varuttan tarum kaṭaliṉ vayiṟṟiṉuḷḷē pukuntāṟ pōla, iṭṭatu āṟṟāk kaṭṭaḻal kaṭumpaci paṭṭēṉ eṉṟaṉ paḻaviṉip payattāl - iṭṭa uṇavukaḷāl taṇiyāta aḻalpōṉṟa koṭiya paciyi eṉṉuṭiya muṉṉi viṉippayaṉāl aṭintēṉ, aṉṉikēḷ nī - tāyē nī kēṭpāyāka, āruyir maruttuvi tuṉṉiya eṉnōy tuṭippāy eṉṟālum - ariya uyirip pātukākka valla maruntiṉiyuṭiyāy neruṅkiya eṉatu pacippiṇiyik kaḷintaruḷvāy eṉa vēṇṭa; 
 yāṉit tī nōy - perumpaciyic ceyyum nōy; pasmaka viyāti eṉavum paṭum. mayaṅki - avicciyāṉ mayaṅki; 1‘iruṇīṅki yiṉpam payakkum maruṇīṅki, mācaṟu kāṭci yavarkku'' eṉpataṉāl avicci piṟappiṟkuk kāraṇamāta laṟika; neṭiyōṉ tōṉṟi mayaṅki eṉa māṟṟi, tirumāl irāmāvatārattil paruvata muṉivarum nāratarum amparīṭaṉuk kiṭṭa cāpamākiya iruḷit tāṉ ēṟṟuk koṇṭamiyāl ataṉāl mūṭappaṭṭut taṉṉi māyaṉeṉ ṟaṟiyātu mayaṅki yiruntaṉaṉ eṉṉum iliṅka purāṇak katiyi ēṟṟiyurippārumuḷar. iṉi mayaṅki yeṉpataṟkuc cītiyip pirintu kiyā ṟeyti yeṉ ṟurittalumām. munnīr - paṭittal, kāttal, aḻittal eṉṉum mūṉṟu nīrmiyi yuṭiyatu; kaṭal. malikaḷāl kaṭaliṉ vayiṟu nirampāmipōla uṇavukaḷiṉāl eṉ vayiṟu nirampiṟṟilli yeṉat tāṉuṇṭa uṇaviṉ mikutiyum paciyiṉ koṭumiyum pulappaṭa uvamaṅ kūṟiṉāḷ. iṭṭatu: viṉippeyar. āruyir maruntu- aṭicil, maruttuvi - maruntiṉiyuṭiyāḷ.
kāriki nōkki vaṇaṅki, 'eṉṉōy tuṭippā' yeṉṟalu meṉka.
 17-20.  eṭutta pāttirattu ēntiya amutam piṭittu avaḷ kiyil peytalum-maṇimēkali tāṉ kiyiṟ koṇṭa amuta curapiyiluḷḷa aṉṉatti eṭuttup piṭiyāka avaḷ kiyil iṭa, vayiṟukāy perumpaci nīṅki maṟṟavaḷ tuyaram nīṅkittoḻutaṉaḷ urikkum-kāyacaṇṭiki vayiṟṟil erikiṉṟa koṭiya paci nīṅkat taṉ tuyaramum nīṅki maṇimēkaliyit toḻutu kūṟukiṉṟāḷ; 
ēntiya - poruntiya; ēntiya kiyileṉak kūṭṭutalumām. paci nīṅki - paci nīṅka: eccattiripu.
21-26.	mācu il vāl oḷi vaṭaticic cēṭi - vaṭaticiyil mācaṟṟa veḷḷiya voḷiyiṉiyuṭiya viñciyarulakil, kācu il kāñcaṉapurak kaṭinakar uḷḷēṉ - kuṟṟamaṟṟa kāñcaṉapura meṉṉuṅ kāval poruntiya nakarattiluḷḷa yāṉ, viñciyaṉ taṉṉoṭu eṉ vevviṉi uruppa-eṉatu koṭuviṉiyāṉatu tōṟṟa eṉ kaṇavaṉōṭu, teṉṟicip potiyil kāṇiya vantēṉ-teṉṟiciyiluḷḷa potiyiṉ maliyiṉ vaḷaṅkāṇum poruṭṭu vantēṉ, kaṭuvaral aruvik kaṭumpuṉal koḻitta - virinta celaviṉi yuṭiya aruviyiṉatu vēkamuḷḷa nīr teḷḷiya, iṭumaṇal kāṉiyāṟṟu iyintoruṅku iruntēṉ- iṭumaṇali yuṭiya kāṉyāṟṟil eṉ kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ kūṭi oruṅkiruntēṉ; 
cēṭi - vittiyātara rulaku; atu veḷḷi maliyiluḷḷa tākaliṉ vāloḷiyiyuṭiyatāyiṟṟu; 1“veḷḷi mālvari viyaṉperuñ cēṭi'' eṉpatu kāṇka. uruppa - aḻalaveṉṟumām. kāṇiya: ceyyiya veṉṉum viṉiyeccam. vantēṉ : eccamuṟṟumām. iṭu maṇal - ekkar.
 27-32.	purinūl mārpil tiripuri vārcaṭi maravuri uṭiyaṉ viruccikaṉ eṉpōṉ - appoḻutu muṟukkiya pūṇūlaṇinta mārpum tirittu muṟukkiya nīṇṭa caṭiyum maravuri yāṭiyu muṭiya viruccikaṉ eṉṉum muṉivaṉ, peruṅkulip peṇṇik karuṅkaṉi aṉiyatōr iruṅkaṉi nāvaṟpaḻam oṉṟu ēnti - periya kuliyiyuṭiya paṉiyiṉatu kariya kaṉiyi yottatākiya perumi poruntiya kaṉinta nāvaṟpaḻam oṉṟik kiyilēnti vantu, tēkkili vittuc cēṇ nāṟu parappil pūkkamaḻ poyki āṭac ceṉṟōṉ - ataṉi oru tēkkiṉ iliyil vittuviṭṭu neṭuntūram nāṟumiyalpuṭiya pūkkaḷ kamaḻum parappiṉiyuṭiya poykiyil nīrāṭac ceṉṟāṉāka;
paṉaṅkaṉi parumaṉ paṟṟi uvamiyāyatu. ōr : aci. nāṟu pū eṉavum, parappiṟ poyki yeṉavum iyiyum.
viruccika ṉeṉpōṉ nāvaṟpaḻamoṉṟi ēnti vittup poykiyāṭac ceṉṟāṉāka eṉka. 
33-34.   tīviṉi uruttaliṉ cerukkoṭu ceṉṟēṉ - tīviṉi yāṉatu payaṉ koṭuppat tōṟṟutaliṉ yāṉ āṇṭut tarukkoṭu ceṉṟu, kālāl antak karuṅkaṉi citittēṉ - kāliṉālē anta nāvaṟpaḻattic citittēṉ; 
35-42.   uṇṭal vēṭkiyiṉ varūum viruccikaṉ - uṇṇum vēṭkiyuṭaṉ nīrāṭip pōnta viruccika muṉivaṉ, kaṇṭaṉaṉ eṉṉik karuṅkaṉic citivuṭaṉ - ap paḻattiṉic cititta tiṟattiṉōṭu eṉṉik kaṇṇuṟṟaṉaṉ, cīrtikaḻ nāvalil tippiya māṉatu-ciṟappu viḷaṅkum nāvalilē teyvattaṉmi yuṭiya toṉṟu, īrāṟu āṇṭil oru kaṉi taruvatu - paṉṉīrāṇṭiṟku oru paḻam koṭuppatu, ak kaṉi uṇṭōr - ap paḻattiṉi yuṇṭōr, āṟīrāṇṭu makkaḷ yākkiyiṉ varum paci nīṅkuvar- paṉṉīrāṇṭu makkaḷuṭali luṇṭākum paci nīṅkappeṟuvar, paṉṉīrāṇṭil oru nāḷ allatu uṇṇā nōṉpiṉēṉ - yāṉō paṉṉīrāṇṭil orunāḷ uṇpatallatu piṟanāḷil uṇṇāta nōṉpiṉiyuṭiyēṉ, uṇkaṉi citittāy-avviyalpuṭiya yāṉ iṉṟu uṇṭaṟkuriya ak kaṉiyik keṭuttāy;
citivu-citittu niṉṟanili; citintakaṉiyō ṭeṉṟumām. tippiyam-teyvalōkattiliruntu uṇṭāya teṉṟumām. uṇ kaṉi - tippiyamāṉa nāval tanta kaṉiyākiya yāṉ uṇṭaṟkuriya kaṉiyeṉka
 43-48.   antaram cellum mantiram iḻantu-ākaliṉ viṇmītu cellum mantiratti iḻantu, tantit tīyāl taṉittuyar uḻantu - yāṉittī yeṉṉum pacinōyāl oppaṟṟa tuṉpa maṭintu varunti, mun nālāṇṭil mutirkaṉi īṅku uṇṇum nāḷ uṉ uṟupaci kaḷika eṉa - iṉi varum paṉṉiraṇṭāva tāṇṭil mutirkiṉṟa nāvaṟkaṉiyiṉi nāṉ iṅku uṇṇukiṉṟa nāḷil niṉatu mikka paciyip pōkkuvāy eṉṟu, annāḷ āṅku avaṉ iṭṭa cāpam - yāṉ av varuṅ kaṉi cititta aṉṟu avviṭattil am muṉivaṉiṭṭa cāpatti, innāḷ pōlum iḷaṅkoṭi keṭuttaṉi - iḷaṅkoṭi pōlum nī iṉṟu keṭuttāy; 
paṉṉīrāṇṭu tāṉ pacittirukkac ceyta tīviṉi paṟṟi avvakiyē tuyaruḻakkumāṟu cāpamiṭṭaṉaṉeṉka. cāpam keṭuttaṉi yeṉṟiyiyum. pōlum : oppil pōli. cāpa muṭivelliyākiya nāḷ innāḷ eṉṟumām.
49-55. 	vāṭu paci uḻantu māmuṉi pōyapiṉ - vāṭutaṟkētu vākiya paciyāl varunti am muṉivaṉ ceṉṟapiṉ, pāṭu imiḻ aruvip payamali oḻintu-oli muḻaṅkukiṉṟa aruvi kaḷiyuṭiya payaṉuṭiya potiyiṉ maliyi aṭivati viṭuttu, eṉ alavalic ceytikku añciṉaṉ akaṉṟa - maṉattil tōṉṟiyati ārāyātu ceyta eṉ ceykikku añciṉavaṉāy nīṅkiya, ilaku oḷi viñciyaṉ viḻumamōṭu eyti-viḷaṅkum oḷiyuṭiya viñciyaṉ tuṉpamō ṭaṭintu, āraṇaṅkākiya aruntavaṉ taṉṉāl - ariya teyvat taṉmiyuṭiya aruntavaṉāl, kāraṇam iṉṟiyum kaṭunōy uḻantaṉi - kāraṇamillāmalum kaṭiya nōyāl varun taluṟṟaṉi, vāṉūṭu eḻuka eṉa - vicumpiṉ mītu eḻuvāyāka eṉṟu kūṟa ;
alavalic ceyti - ārāyātu ceyta ceyti; alamarutalic ceyyumceyti yeṉṟumām; 1"alavaliyuṭiyi" eṉpuḻi nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urittami kāṇka. vilakoḷi eṉap pirittalumām ; vilaku oḷi - viṭṭu viḷaṅkum oḷi. kāraṇa miṉṟiyē kaṉiyic citittuk kaṭunōyuḻantaṉi eṉa virit turittukkoḷka.
55-61.	mantiram maṟantēṉ - vāṉiṟ cellum mantiratti maṟantu viṭṭēṉ, ūṉ uyir nīṅkum uruppoṭu tōṉṟi - uṭampiṉiṉṟum uyir nīṅkutaṟkētuvākiya veppattuṭaṉ tōṉṟi, vayiṟukāy perumpaci varuttum eṉṟēṟku - vayiṟṟiṉik kāykiṉṟa perum paciyāṉatu mika varuttā niṉṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiya eṉporuṭṭu, tīṅkaṉi kiḻaṅku ceḻuṅkāy nallaṉa āṅkavaṉ koṇaravum āṟṟēṉāka-nallaṉavākiya iṉiya paḻaṅkaḷ kiḻaṅkukaḷ ceḻiya kāykaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi avaṉ koṇarntu taravum eṉatu paci taṇiyēṉāka, nīṅkal āṟṟāṉ neṭuntuyar eyti āṅkavaṉ āṅku eṉakku aruḷoṭum urippōṉ - eṉṉiviṭṭu nīṅkalāṟṟātavaṉākiya eṉ kaṇavaṉmikka tuyarattiyaṭintu avviṭattil eṉakku aruḷoṭum kūṟukiṉṟavaṉ; 
maṟantēṉ eṉavum varuttum eṉavum kūṟiṉēṟku eṉka. nallaṉaveṉpatik kaṉi mutaliya ovvoṉṟōṭuṅ kūṭṭuka. āṟṟēṉāka - paci taṇiyappeṟēṉāka. 
62-68.   camput tīviṉuḷ tamiḻaka maruṅkil - nāvalan tīviṉuḷ tamiḻakattilē, kampam illāk kaḻiperuñ celvar - naṭukkamillāta mikka peruñ celvattiyuṭiyōr, āṟṟā mākkaṭku āṟṟum tuṇiyāki - vaṟiyōrkaṭku vēṇṭiyaṉa koṭukkum tuṇivarāki, nōṟṟōr uṟiva tōr nōṉ nakar uṇṭāl - muṟpiṟappil tavampurintōr vāḻkiṉṟa perumiyuṭiya nakaramoṉṟu uṇṭu, pala nāḷāyiṉum nilaṉoṭu pōki - palanāṭkaḻiyiṉum nī naṭantu ceṉṟu, appatip pukuka eṉṟu avaṉ aruḷ ceyya-an nakarattiṟ cērvāy eṉṟu avaṉ kūṟiyaruḷa, ip patip pukuntu īṅku yāṉ uṟikiṉṟēṉ-yāṉ avaṉ col vaḻiyē ik kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉatti yaṭintu īṇṭu vatikiṉṟēṉ;
āṟṟutal-koṭuttal; 1"aṟṟārkkoṉ ṟāṟṟātāṉ celvam" eṉpatu kāṇka. nōṟṟōr peruñcelvar tuṇiyāki uṟivateṉka. nōṉmi-īṇṭup perumi, nilaṉoṭu pōtal - kālāl naṭantu cellutal.
69-74.	intirakōṭaṇi viḻavaṇi varunāḷ - intira kōṭaṇi yākiya aḻakiya viḻā naṭipeṟum nāḷil, vantu tōṉṟi immānakar maruṅkē - ip periya nakariṉ maruṅkē av viñciyaṉ vantu tōṉṟi, eṉṉuṟu perumpaci kaṇṭaṉaṉ iraṅki-eṉṉi varuttukiṉṟa perum paciyiṉik kaṇṇuṟṟu iraṅki, piṉvarum yāṇṭu avaṉ eṇṇiṉaṉ kaḻiyum-piṉṉar eṉatu cāpam kaḻikiṉṟa āṇṭukaḷi eṇṇikkoṇṭu avaṉ nīṅkuvāṉ, taṇivil vempaci tavirttaṉi - eṉṉuṭiya taṇitalillāta koṭiya paciyi nīkkiṉāy, vaṇaṅkiṉēṉ - niṉṉē vaṇakkañ ceytēṉ, maṇimēkali eṉ vāṉpatip paṭarkēṉ-maṇimēkaliyē iṉi eṉatu ciṟanta patiyiṉkaṭ celvēṉ;
intirakōṭaṇiyākiya viḻavu eṉka; palvaki muḻakka muṭimi paṟṟi viḻā kōṭaṇi yeṉappaṭṭatu; "intira kōṭaṇi viḻāvaṇi virumpi" (5:94) eṉa muṉṉar vantamiyuṅ kāṇka. āṇṭutōṟum intiraviḻā naṭakkumnāḷil vantu kaṇṭu iraṅkik kaḻiyum eṉka. piṉvarum yāṇṭu - cāpam niṟkum paṉṉīrāṇṭil eñciya āṇṭu. maṇimēkali tavirttaṉi vaṇaṅkiṉēṉ paṭarkēṉ eṉṟāḷeṉka. maṇimēkali: viḷi. 
75-83.   tukkam tuṭikkum tukaḷaṟu mātavar - tukkattip pōkkum kuṟṟamaṟṟa perun tavattōrkaḷ uṟikiṉṟa, cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭam uṇṭu-cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭam eṉpatoṉṟuṇṭu, āṅkatil palar pukat tiṟanta pakuvāy vāyil - ataṉkaṇ palarum cellumāṟu tiṟakkappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟa piḷanta vāypōlum vāyiliṉi yuṭiya, ulakavaṟavi oṉṟuṇṭu-ulakavaṟavi eṉṉum ūrampalam oṉṟu uṇṭu, ataṉiṭi-ataṉkaṇ, ūrūr āṅkaṇ uṟupaci uḻantōr- ūrtōṟum mikka paciyāl varuntiṉōrum, ārum iṉmiyiṉ arumpiṇi uṟṟōr - pātukāppōr oruvarum iṉmiyiṉāl ariya nōyuḻantōrumāy, iṭuvōrt tērntu āṅku iruppōr palarāl - aṉṉamiṭuvōri ārāyntu irukkiṉṟavar palarāvar, vaṭu vāḻ kūntal ataṉpāṟ pōkeṉṟu - vakir poruntiya kuḻaliṉi yuṭiyāy avviṭam celvāyāka eṉṟu kūṟi, āṅkavaḷ pōkiya piṉṉar-ak kāyacaṇṭiki ceṉṟa piṉpu;
tukkam - piṟavittuṉpam. ūrūr āṅkaṇ - pala ūrkaḷilum, āṅku, āl: acikaḷ. vaṭu-vakir. kūntal: viḷi.
83-90.	āyiḻi - maṇimēkali, ōṅkiya vītiyiṉ orupuṭi otuṅki-māṭaṅkaḷ ōṅkiya teruviṉkaṇṇē oru puṟamāka otuṅkic ceṉṟu, valamuṟi mummuṟi vantaṉi ceytu - vala muṟiyāka mūṉṟumuṟi vantu paṇintu, av vulakavaṟaviyiṉ oru taṉi ēṟi - anta ulaka vaṟaviyiṉkaṇ tāṉ taṉiyē ceṉṟu, patiyōr tammoṭu palar toḻutu ēttum - nakariluḷḷāruṭaṉ palarum tutittu vaṇaṅkum, mutiyōḷ kōṭṭam mummiyiṉ vaṇaṅki-mutiyavaḷākiya campāpatiyiṉ kōyili maṉam moḻi meykaḷāl vaṇakkañ ceytu, kantuṭi neṭu nilik kāraṇam kāṭṭiya tantuṇip pāviyit tāṉ toḻutu ētti - neṭiya niliyiṉiyuṭiya tūṇiṉ kaṇiruntu paḻaviṉiyākiya kāraṇaṅ kāṭṭum poruṭṭu eḻutap paṭṭa tam tuṇiyākiya kantiṟpāviyi vaṇaṅkittutittu;
mummiyiṉ vaṇaṅki-mūṉṟumuṟi vaṇaṅki yeṉṟumām. neṭu niliyuṭik kantu eṉa māṟuka. kāṭṭuvataṟku mayaṉāl eḻutappaṭṭaveṉa viritturikka. "kantuṭi neṭunilik kāraṇaṅ kāṭṭiya, anti leḻutiya vaṟputap pāvi" (7:94-5) eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattaṉavāka in nūluṭ piṟāṇṭu varuvaṉa kāṇka. maṇimēkaliyuṭaṉ maṟṟiyōriyum uḷappaṭutti, ‘tantuṇippāvi' eṉṟār.
91-98.   veyil cuṭa vempiya vēykari kāṉattu - ñāyiṟṟiṉ katirāṟ cuṭappaṭṭu vempiya mūṅkil karinta kāṭṭiṉkaṇ, karuvi māmaḻi tōṉṟiyatu eṉṉa - tokutiyākiya mukilkaḷ tōṉṟiṉāṟpōla; paci tiṉa varuntiya pital mākkaṭku - paciyāṉatu uṭalit tiṉṉa varuntiya tuṉpamuṭiya makkaḷukku, amutacurapiyōṭu āyiḻi tōṉṟi - maṇimēkali amutacurapiyōṭu tōṉṟi, āputtiraṉ ki amutacurapi iḵtu-āputtiraṉ kiyilirunta amutacurapiyākum itu, yāvarum varuka ēṟpōr tām eṉa - ēṟpōr yāvarum varuka eṉṟu kūṟa, ūṇ oli aravattuolieḻuntaṉṟē yāṇarp
pērūr ampalam maruṅkeṉ-uṇavuṇṇum āravāramākiya muḻakkam eḻuntatu putuvaruvā yiṉiyuṭiya pērūriṉ ampalattiṉkaṇ eṉka. 
tiṉa eṉṟatu ilakkaṇi vaḻakku. varukaveṉa aḻittuc coriya vantuṇṇum āravāram eṉa viritturikka. maḻi tōṉṟip poḻintāṟpōla āyiḻi tōṉṟic coriya veṉka. oliyaravam: oruporu ḷirucol. ampala maruṅku oliyeḻuntaṉṟeṉka.
kāyacaṇṭiki yeṉṉuṅ kāriki, nōkki vaṇaṅki, ‘eṉṉōy tuṭippāy' eṉṟalum, maṇimēlali peytalum, avaḷ paci nīṅkit toḻutu urikkum; urippavaḷ, ‘ulakavaṟavi yoṉṟuṇṭu; ataṉpāṟ pōka' eṉṟu kūṟip pōkiya piṉṉar, āyiḻi otuṅki vantu vantaṉi ceytu ēṟi vaṇaṅkit toḻutu ēttit tōṉṟi, ēṟpōr yāvarum  varuka' eṉa, ampala maruṅku oliyeḻuntaṉṟu eṉa viṉi muṭivu ceyka. 
 ulakavaṟavi pukka kāti muṟṟiṟṟu. 


18. utayakumaraṉ ampalam pukka kāti 
maṇimēkali pikkuṇik kōlaṅkoṇṭu pāttiramēnti aiyamēṟṟu ulaka vaṟaviyiṟ ceṉṟāḷeṉpatik kēṭṭa cittirāpati uḷaṅkotittu veytuyirttuk kalaṅki, "maṇimēkaliyiṉ ic ceykiyi oḻippēṉ" eṉṟu eṇṇikkoṇṭu, kūttiyal maṭantiya rellāriyum pārttu, "kōvalaṉ iṟantatu kēṭṭu mātavi muṉivarkaḷiṉ paḷḷiyi aṭintu tāpatak kōlam pūṇṭiruttal nakikkat takkatu; yām kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ iṟakkum pattiṉip peṇṭirallēm; pāṇaṉ iṟantapoḻutu avaṉuṭaṉ iṟavāta yāḻiṉip pōlvēm; maṟṟum tātiṉi yuṇṭu pūvit tuṟakkum vaṇṭupōlkuvam; tāpatak kōlan tāṅkuvatu namatu kula voḻukkattiṟku ottataṉṟu; mātavi makaḷ maṇimēkaliyiṉ pikkuṇikkōlatti māṟṟi avaḷ kiyilēntiya aiyak kaṭiñiyip picciyēṟpār piṟar kiyiṟ pōkki, avaḷip palanāḷāka virumpiyirukkum utayakumaraṉāl avaṉatu tērilēṟṟu vittu varuvēṉ; aṅṅaṉañ ceyyēṉāyiṉ, kuṭikkuṟṟappaṭṭu ēḻu ceṅkalit talimēlēṟṟik koṇṭu nāṭaka varaṅkic cuṟṟivantu paḻiyōṭirukkum nāṭaka makaḷirpōla iṉi nāṉ nāṭakak kaṇikiyar maṉikaṭkuc cellēṉākuka" eṉṟu cūḷuṟavu ceytu, taṉṉic cilar cūḻntuvarac ceṉṟu utayakumaraṉ iruppiṭatti yaṭintu, paḷiṅku maṇṭapattilē tūmalarp paḷḷiyil iruntōṉik kaṇṭu tutittu, maṇimēkali ulakavaṟaviyi aṭintirukkiṟāḷ eṉpataṉi avaṉukkuk kuṟippikka, avaṉ uvavaṉattil maṇimēkaliyik kaṇṭatu mutal nikaḻntavaṟṟik kūṟi avaḷpāluḷḷa ciṟapput taṉmiyip pārāṭṭiṉaṉ; cittirāpati avaṉ maṉatti vēṟupaṭuttum moḻikaḷ palavaṟṟik kūṟi muyala avaṉ uḷḷam piṟaḻntu tērēṟi ulakavaṟaviyi aṭintu, palarkkum uṇavaḷittuk koṇṭirukkum maṇimēkaliyik kaṇṭu arukiṟ ceṉṟu, "nī tāpatak kōlam pūṇṭatu yātu karuti?" eṉṟu viṉāviṉaṉ; maṇimēkali "paḻam piṟappiṟ kaṇavaṉākavirunta ivaṉi vaṇaṅkutal muṟiyākum" eṉṟu eṇṇi vaṇaṅki,  
	“		piṟattalu mūttalum piṇippaṭ ṭiraṅkalum
iṟattalu muṭiyatu iṭumpik koḷkalam
makkaḷ yākki yituveṉa vuṇarntu
mikka nallaṟam virumputal purintēṉ" 
eṉṟurittu, vēṟṟurukkoḷḷa niṉittu avaṉi nīṅkik kōyiliṉuḷḷē ceṉṟu campāpatiyi vaṇaṅki, muṉpu maṇimēkalā teyvam aruḷic ceyta mantiratti yōtik kāyacaṇṭiki vaṭiva muṟṟu amutacurapiyi ēnti veḷiyē vantu niṉṟāḷ. avaḷ aṅṅaṉam vantati aṟiyāta utayakumaraṉ "uḷḷē ceṉṟa maṇimēkali campāpati kōyiliṉuḷḷē oḷittukkoṇṭāḷ" eṉṟu niṉintu ceṉṟu campāpatiyi vaṇaṅki "piccip pāttirattik kāyacaṇṭikiyiṉ kiyiṟ koṭuttuviṭṭu oḷittuc ceṉṟa maṇimēkaliyi iṅkuḷḷa pāvikaḷuḷḷē yāṉ evvāṟuṇarvēṉ; nī avaḷi eṉakkuk kāṭṭāyāyiṉ paṉṉāḷāyiṉum nāṉ ivviṭattilēyē pāṭu kiṭappēṉ; maṇimēkaliyi iṅkē viṭuttu nāṉmaṭṭum pōkēṉ; uṉ tiruvaṭiyit toṭṭēṉ" eṉṟu cūḷurittāṉ.  
		āṅkatu kēṭṭāṅ karumpuṇ akavayiṉ
tīttuṟu ceṅkōl ceṉṟucuṭ ṭāṅkuk
kotitta uḷḷamoṭu kurampukoṇ ṭēṟi
vituppuṟu neñciṉaḷ veytuyirttuk kalaṅkit
5		tīrppaliv vaṟameṉac cittirā patitāṉ
kūttiyaṉ maṭantiyark kellāṅ kūṟum
kōvalaṉ iṟantapiṉ koṭuntuya reyti
mātavi mātavar paḷḷiyuḷ aṭintatu
nakutak kaṉṟē naṉṉeṭum pērūr
10		itutak keṉpōrk keḷḷuri yāyatu
kātalaṉ vīyak kaṭuntuya reytip
pōtal ceyyā uyiroṭu pulantu
naḷiyirum poyki yāṭunar pōla
muḷiyerip pukūum mutukuṭip piṟanta
15		pattiṉip peṇṭir allēm palartam
kittūṇ vāḻkki kaṭaviya maṉṟē
pāṇmakaṉ paṭṭuḻip paṭūum pāṉmiyil
yāḻiṉam pōlum iyalpiṉam aṉṟiyum
naṟuntā tuṇṭu nayaṉil kāli
20		vaṟumpūt tuṟakkum vaṇṭu pōlkuvam
viṉiyoḻi kālit tiruviṉ celvi
aṉiyē māki āṭavart tuṟappēm
tāpatak kōlan tāṅkiṉa meṉpatu
yāvarum nakūum iyalpiṉa taṉṟē
25		mātavi īṉṟa maṇimē kalivalli
pōtaviḻ celvi poruntutal virumpiya
utaya kumaraṉām ulakāḷ vaṇṭiṉ
citiyā uḷḷañ cevvitiṉ aruntak
kikkoṇ ṭāṅkavaḷ ēntiya kaṭiñiyip 
30 		picci mākkaḷ piṟarkik kāṭṭi
maṟṟavaṉ ṟaṉṉāl maṇimē kalitaṉip
poṟṟērk koṇṭu pōtē ṉākil
cuṭumaṇ ēṟṟi araṅkucūḻ pōki
vaṭuvoṭu vāḻum maṭantiyar tammōr
35		aṉiyē ṉāki araṅkak kūttiyar
maṉiyakam pukāa marapiṉa ṉeṉṟē
vañciṉañ cāṟṟi neñcupuki uyirttu
vañcak kiḷavi māṇpoṭu tērntu
ceṟivaḷi nallār cilarpuṟañ cūḻak
40		kuṟuviyar poṭitta kōlavāṇ mukattaḷ
kaṭuntēr vīti kāliṟ pōki
iḷaṅkō vēntaṉ iruppiṭam kuṟuki
arava vaṇṭoṭu tēṉiṉam ārkkum
tarumaṇaṉ ñemiriya tirunā ṟoruciṟip
45		pavaḻat tūṇattup pacumpoṟ ceñcuvart
tikaḻoḷi nittilac cittira vitāṉattu
viḷaṅkoḷi paranta paḷiṅkucey maṇṭapattut
tuḷaṅkumā ṉūrtit tūmalarp paḷḷi
veṇṭiri virinta veṇṇiṟac cāmari
50		koṇṭiru maruṅkuṅ kōtiyar vīca
iruntōṉ tiruntaṭi poruntiniṉ ṟēttip
tirunteyi ṟilaṅkac cevviyi ṉakkavaṉ
mātavi maṇimē kaliyuṭaṉ eytiya
tāpatak kōlan tavaṟiṉ ṟōveṉa
55		aritupeṟu ciṟappiṟ kurukukaru uyirppa
orutaṉi yōṅkiya tirumaṇik kāñci
pāṭalcāl ciṟappiṟ paratat tōṅkiya
nāṭakam virumpa naṉṉalaṅ kaviṉik
kāmar cevvik kaṭimalar aviḻntatu
60		utaya kumaraṉeṉum oruvaṇ ṭuṇīiya
virivoṭu vantēṉ viyaṉperu mūtūr
pāḻmam paṟantali ampalat tāyatu
vāḻkaniṉ kaṇṇi vāyvāḷ vēnteṉa 
ōṅkiya puvat tuṭikalap paṭṭōṉ
65		vāṉpuṇi peṟṟeṉa maṟṟavaṭ kurippōṉ
mēviya paḷiṅkiṉ viruntiṟ pāviyiḵtu
ōviyac ceytiyeṉ ṟoḻivēṉ muṉṉark
kāntaḷañ ceṅki taḷipiṇi viṭāa
ēntiḷa vaṉamuli iṟinerit tatūum 
70		ottoḷir pavaḷat tuḷḷoḷi ciṟanta
muttukkūrt taṉṉa muḷḷeyiṟ ṟamutam
aruntē mānta āruyir taḷirppa
viruntiṉ mūral arumpiya tūum
māyitaḻk kuvaḷi malarpuṟat tōṭṭik
75		kāyvēl veṉṟa karuṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇ
aṟivupiṟi tākiya tāyiḻi taṉakkeṉac
ceviyakam pukūuc ceṉṟa cevviyum
paḷiṅkupuṟat teṟinta pavaḷap pāviyeṉ
uḷaṅkoṇ ṭoḷittāḷ uyirkkāp piṭṭeṉṟu
80		iṭiyiruḷ yāmat tiruntēṉ muṉṉarp
poṉṟikaḻ mēṉi orutti tōṉṟic
ceṅkōl kāṭṭic ceytavam purinta
aṅkavaḷ taṉṟiṟam ayarppā yeṉṟaṉaḷ
teyvaṅ kollō tippiyaṅ kollō
85		eyyā miyalēṉ yāṉeṉ ṟavaṉcolac
cittirā patitāṉ ciṟunaki eyti
attiṟam viṭuvāy araciḷaṅ kuricil
kāmak kaḷḷāṭ ṭiṭimayak kuṟṟaṉa
tēvark kāyiṉuñ cilavō ceppiṉ
90		mātavaṉ maṭantikku varuntutuya reyti
āyirañ ceṅkaṇ amararkōṉ peṟṟatum
mēruk kuṉṟat tūṟunīrc caravaṇattu
aruntiṟaṉ muṉivark kāraṇaṅ kākiya
perumpeyarp peṇṭir piṉpuḷam pōkkiya
95		aṅki maṉiyāḷ avaravar vaṭivāyt
taṅkā vēṭki taṉiyavaḷ taṇittatūum
kēṭṭu maṟitiyō vāṭṭiṟaṟ kuricil
kaṉṉik kāvaluṅ kaṭiyiṟ kāvalum 
taṉṉuṟu kaṇavaṉ cāvuṟiṟ kāvalum
100		niṟiyiṟ kāttup piṟarpiṟark kāṇātu
koṇṭōṉ allatu teyvamum pēṇāp
peṇṭirtaṅ kuṭiyiṟ piṟantāḷ allaḷ
nāṭavar kāṇa nallaraṅ kēṟi
āṭalum pāṭalum aḻakuṅ kāṭṭic
105		curuppunāṇ karuppuvil aruppukkaṇi tūvac
cerukkayal neṭuṅkaṭ curukkuvalip paṭuttuk
kaṇṭōr neñcaṅ koṇṭakam pukkup
paṇṭēr moḻiyiṟ payaṉpala vāṅki
vaṇṭiṟ ṟuṟakkuṅ koṇṭi makaḷirip
110		pāṉmiyiṟ piṇittup paṭiṟṟuri yaṭakkutal
kōṉmuṟi yaṉṟō kumaraṟ keṉṟalum
utaya kumaraṉ uḷḷam piṟaḻntu
viripari neṭuntēr mēṟceṉ ṟēṟi
āyiḻi yirunta ampala meytik
115		kāṭamar celvi kaṭippaci kaḷiya
ōṭukik koṇṭuniṉ ṟūṭṭunaḷ pōlat
tīppaci mākkaṭkuc ceḻuñcō ṟīttup
pāttiram ēntiya pāviyik kāṇṭalum
iṭaṅkaḻi kāmamo ṭaṭaṅkā ṉāki
120		uṭampō ṭeṉṟaṉ uḷḷakam pukunteṉ
neñcaṅ kavarnta vañcak kaḷvi
nōṟṟūṇ vāḻkkiyiṉ nocitavan tāṅki
ēṟṟūṇ virumpiya kāraṇam eṉṉeṉat
tāṉē tamiyaḷ niṉṟōḷ muṉṉar
125		yāṉē kēṭṭal iyalpeṉac ceṉṟu
nallāy eṉkol naṟṟavam purintatu
collāy eṉṟu tuṇintuṭaṉ kēṭpa
eṉṉamar kātala ṉirākulaṉ īṅkivaṉ
taṉṉaṭi toḻutalum takaveṉa vaṇaṅki
130		aṟipōy neñcam avaṉpāl aṇukiṉum
iṟivaḷi muṉki īṅkivaṉ paṟṟiṉum
toṉṟu kātalaṉ colletir maṟuttal
naṉṟi yaṉṟeṉa naṭuṅkiṉaḷ mayaṅkik 
kēṭṭatu moḻivēṉ kēḷvi yāḷaril
135		tōṭṭa ceviyinī yākuvi yāmeṉil
piṟattalum mūttalum piṇippaṭ ṭiraṅkalum
iṟattalu muṭiya tiṭumpik koḷkalam
makkaḷ yākki ituveṉa uṇarntu
mikka nallaṟam virumputal purintēṉ
140		maṇṭamar murukkuṅ kaḷiṟaṉi yārkkup
peṇṭir kūṟum pēraṟi vuṇṭō
kēṭṭaṉi yāyiṉ vēṭṭatu ceykeṉa
vāṭṭiṟaṟ kuricili maṭakkoṭi nīṅki
mutti mutalvi mutiyāḷ irunta
145		kuccarak kuṭiki taṉṉakam pukkāṅku
āṭavar ceyti aṟikunar yāreṉat
tōṭalar kōtiyit toḻutaṉaḷ ētti
māya viñci mantiram ōtik
kāyacaṇ ṭikiyeṉuṅ kāriki vaṭivāy
150		maṇimē kalitāṉ vantu tōṉṟa
aṇimalart tārōṉ avaḷpāṟ pukkuk
kuccarak kuṭikik kumariyi marīip
piccip pāttiram perumpaci uḻanta
kāyacaṇ ṭikitaṉ kiyiṟ kāṭṭi
155		māyiyiṉ oḷitta maṇimē kalitaṉi
īṅkim maṇṇīṭ ṭiyāreṉa uṇarkēṉ
āṅkavaḷ ivaḷeṉ ṟaruḷā yāyiṭiṉ
paṉṉā ḷāyiṉum pāṭu kiṭappēṉ
iṉṉuṅ kēḷāy imiyōr pāvāy
160		pavaḷac cevvāyt tavaḷavāḷ nakiyum
añcaṉañ cērāc ceṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇum
muṟintukaṭi neriya vaḷintacilip puruvamum
kuvimuṭ karuviyum kōṇamum kūrnuṉik
kavimuṭ karuviyum ākik kaṭikoḷak
165		kalvip pākariṟ kāppuvali yōṭṭi
valvā yāḻiṉ mellitiṉ viḷaṅka
mutukkuṟi mutumoḻi eṭuttuk kāṭṭip
putukkōḷ yāṉi vēṭṭam vāytteṉa 
mutiyāḷ uṉṟaṉ kōṭṭam pukunta
170		mativāḷ mukattu maṇimē kalitaṉi
oḻiyap pōkēṉ uṉṉaṭi toṭṭēṉ
itukuṟi yeṉṟaṉaṉ iṟimakaṉ ṟāṉeṉ.  
uri
1-9.		āṅkatu kēṭṭu āṅku - maṇimēkali aṟattōr kōlam pūṇṭu picciyēṟṟu aṉivarukkum uṇavaḷip patik kēṭṭu appoḻutē, arumpuṇ akavayiṉ - ariya puṇṇiṉ uḷḷē, tīttuṟu ceṅkōl ceṉṟu cuṭṭāṅku - tīyiṉkaṇ paḻukkak kāytaluṟṟa cevviya kōlāṉatu ceṉṟu cuṭṭāṟpōla, kotitta uḷḷamoṭu - kotip puṟṟa uḷḷattuṭaṉ, kurampu koṇṭu ēṟi - varampu kaṭantu eḻuntu, vituppuṟu neñciṉaḷ veytuyirttuk kalaṅki - naṭukka meytiya maṉattaḷāyc cuṭu mūcceṟintu mayaṅki, tīrppal iv aṟameṉac cittirātipatitāṉ kūttiyal maṭantiyarkku ellām kūṟum-cittirāpatiyāṉavaḷ maṇimēkali koṇṭa ivvaṟattiṉi oḻittu viṭuvēṉ eṉa eṇṇi nāṭakak kaṇikiyar aṉivarkkum kūṟuvāḷ; 
tī tuṟu - tīyāṉatu paṟṟiya, kōl cuṭṭāṅkut tuṉpattāṟ kotitta uḷḷa meṉka. kurampu - varampu; koṇṭu - tāṇṭi yeṉṉum poruṭṭu; 1"kurampeḻuntu kuṟṟaṅkoṇ ṭēṟār" eṉpatu kāṇka. vituppu-virivumām. mēl varuvaṉa cittirāpati kūṟṟu.  
7-15.   kōvalaṉ iṟantapiṉ koṭuntuyar eyti mātavi mātavar paḷḷiyuḷ aṭintatum - mātavi taṉ kātalaṉākiya kōvalaṉ koliyuṇṭa piṉṉark koṭiya tuṉpattiyaṭintu aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ uṟiviṭatti aṭintatum, nakutakkaṉṟē nalneṭum pērūr - mikac ciṟanta pērūriṉkaṇ cirikkat takkatāyiṟṟu, itu takku eṉpōrkku eḷḷuvari āyatu - ivvuri takutiyuḷḷateṉṟu kūṟum aṟiñarkaṭku ikaḻuri yākiyatu, kātalaṉ vīyak kaṭuntuyar eyti-kaṇavaṉiṟakka mikunta tuyaratti yaṭintu, pōtal ceyyā uyirōṭu pulantu - nīṅkāta uyiruṭaṉ veṟuppuk koṇṭu, naḷiirum poyki āṭunarpōla - kuḷirnta nīriṉiyuṭiya periya kuḷattiṉkaṭ ceṉṟu nīrāṭuvār pōla, muḷi eri pukūum mutukuṭippiṟanta pattiṉip peṇṭir allēm-viḷakka mikka tīyiṭik kuḷikkum paḻaṅkuṭiyiṟ piṟanta kaṟpuṭi makaḷi rallēm yām; 
kōvalaṉ koliyuṇṭami kēṭṭu mātavi aṟavaṇavaṭi kaḷi yaṭintu tuṟavu pūṇṭataṉi, muṉṉar ūralaruritta kātiyiṟ kūṟiṉamiyāṉum, 1"pōtiyiṉkīḻ mātavar muṉ puṇṇiyatāṉam purinta, mātavi taṉṟuṟavuṅ kēṭṭāyō tōḻī" eṉpataṉālum aṟika. pērūr-pērūril. itu-maṇimēkali pikkuṇik kōlam pūṇṭu picci yēṟkiṉṟāḷ eṉṉum uri. takku-takkatu: vikāram. takkeṉpōr - takavuṭimiyi aṟintu kūṟum aṟiñar. muḷiyeri - kāynta viṟakiṟ paṟṟiya tī yeṉṟumām. 
15-24.	palar tam kittūṇ vāḻkki kaṭaviyam aṉṟē - palaruṭiya kiyatākiya uṇaviyuṇṭu vāḻtalākiya urimiyiyuṭiyēm; pāṇmakaṉ paṭṭuḻip paṭūum pāṉmi il yāḻiṉam pōlum iyalpiṉam-pāṇaṉiṟantaviṭattu uṭaṉiṟakkuntaṉmiyillāta yāḻip pōṉṟa iyalpuṭi yōm: aṉṟiyum-aḵtaṉṟiyum, naṟuntātu uṇṭu nayaṉ il kāli-maṇamuḷḷa makarantatti nukarntu paciyākiya tēṉ illātapoḻutu, vaṟumpūt tuṟakkum vaṇṭu pōlkuvam - vaṟitākiya pūvi viṭṭu nīṅkum vaṇṭiṉiyum pōlvēm; viṉi oḻi kālit tiruviṉ celvi aṉiyēm āki āṭavart tuṟappēm-maṟṟum nalviṉi nīṅkum kālattut tirumakaḷip pōṉṟēm āki āṭavari viṭṭu nīṅkuvēm; tāpatak kōlam tāṅkiṉam eṉpatu - pikkuṇik kōlam pūṇṭēm eṉpatu, yāvarum nakūum iyalpiṉatu aṉṟē - yāvarum nakikkum iyalpiyuṭiya tallavō ;  
nāṭakak kaṇikiyātaliṉ pāṇmakaṉiyum yāḻiyum uvami kūṟiṉāḷeṉka. nayaṉ il kāli - āṭavariṭattuc celvamillāta kālattil eṉṟumām. "vaṇṭiṟ ṟuṟakkuṅ koṇṭi makaḷiri" eṉa ik kātiyiṟ piṉpum kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷatu; maturik kāñci yuḷḷum 1"iḷampal celvar vaḷantapa vāṅki, nuṇṭā tuṇṭu vaṟumpūt tuṟakkum, meṉciṟi vaṇṭiṉa māṉa" eṉa iv vuvami vantuḷḷami aṟiyaṟpālatu. viṉi - nalviṉi. tiruviṉ celvi-tiruvākiya celvi. nalviṉi yulanta poḻutu tirumakaḷ nīṅkuvatu pōlum eṉṉum iv vuvami 2"tavantīr maruṅkiṟ ṟirumakaḷ pōlap, payantīr maruṅkiṟ paṟṟuviṭ ṭorīi" eṉap peruṅ katiyuḷ varutalaṟintu iṉpuṟaṟpālatu. tāpatakkōlam - tavakkōlam. 
25-32.   mātavi īṉṟa maṇimēkali valli - mātavi peṟṟa maṇimēkaliyākiya pūṅkoṭi, pōtaviḻ cevvi poruntutal virumpiya-pūkkum paruva maṭitali virumpiya, utayakumaraṉām ulakāḷ vaṇṭiṉ-utayakumaraṉ eṉṉum ulakiṉiyāḷ kiṉṟa vaṇṭiṉatu, citiyā uḷḷam cevvitiṉ arunta-vēṟoṉṟālum aḻiyāta maṉamāṉatu naṉṟāka aruntu māṟu, kikkoṇṭu - maṇimēkaliyik kiyakappaṭuttu, āṅkavaḷ ēntiya kaṭiñiyi - avaḷ  kiyil ēntiya ōṭṭiṉi, piccimākkaḷ piṟarkik kāṭṭi - ēṟṟuṇpōrākiya piṟa makkaḷiṉ kiyilē kāṭṭi, maṟṟavaṉ   taṉṉāl - avvutayakumaraṉāl, maṇimēkalitaṉip poṟṟērk koṇṭupōtēṉ ākil - avaḷip poṟṟēriṉmītu ēṟṟik koṇṭu vārēṉāyiṉ ; 
pōtaviḻ cevvi poruntutal virumpiya eṉpataṟkup pūkkum paruvattil ataṉi yaṭitaṟku virumpiya eṉṟurit talumām. ikkātiyuṭ piṉṉarum utayakumaraṉ vaṇṭāka uruvakikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉaṉ. talivaṉ makaḷir nalatti yuṇṭu tuṟattaṟku vaṇṭiṉi uvami kūṟutalumuṇṭu; 3"peṇṭir nalamvuvit taṇcāraṟṟātuṇṇum, vaṇṭiṟ ṟuṟappāṉ mali" eṉpatu kāṇka. piṟar kikkāṭṭi - piṟariṭam paṟittuk koṭutteṉṟapaṭi. 
33-42. 	cuṭumaṇ ēṟṟi araṅku cūḻpōki - ceṅkaṟkaḷit tali mītēṟṟi araṅkiṉic cuṟṟi vantu, vaṭuvoṭu vāḻum maṭantiyar tammō raṉiyēṉ āki - kuṟṟattuṭaṉ kūṭi vāḻum makaḷirip pōṉṟavaḷāki, araṅkak kūttiyar maṉiyakam pukāa marapiṉaṉ eṉṟē-araṅkiṉkaṇ āṭukiṉṟa nāṭaka makaḷiruṭiya illattiṉ kaṇ nuḻiyāta muṟimi yaḷāvēṉ eṉṟu, vañciṉam cāṟṟi neñcu puki uyirttu-vañciṉaṅkūṟi neñcu pukiyeḻap perumūc ceṟintu, vañcakkiḷavi māṇpoṭu tērntu - vañcam poruntiya colli naṉku ārāyntu, ceṟivaḷi nallār cilar puṟam cūḻa - neruṅkiya vaḷiyalkaḷi yaṇinta potu makaḷir cilar puṟattē cūḻa, kuṟuviyar poṭitta kōlavāṇ mukattaḷ - ciṟu viyarvinīr arumpiya aḻaku poruntiya oḷḷiya mukattiyuṭiyaḷāy, kaṭuntēr vīti kāliṟpōki- virinta celaviṉiyuṭiya tērkaḷ cellum vītiyil naṭantu ceṉṟu, iḷaṅkō vēntaṉ iruppiṭam kuṟuki - araciḷaṅ kumaraṉ iruppiṭatti aṭintu; 
ēṟṟi - ēṟṟappaṭṭu eṉṟumām. patiyilāriṟ kuṭikkuṟṟap paṭṭāri ēḻu ceṅkaṟ cumatti araṅku cūḻvittup puṟattu viṭutal marapākaliṉ, cittirāpati iṅṅaṉam vañciṉañ cāṟṟiṉa ḷeṉka ; 'ya ṅkuñ cūḻpōki' eṉap pāṭaṅ koṇṭu, ūric cuṟṟi vantu eṉṟurittalumām; 1"cuṭuma ṇēṟā vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ" eṉpatum ataṉ urikaḷum kāṇka. maṭantiyar tammōr; ōr: aci. marapiṉaṉ: aṉvikuti, taṉmikkaṇ vantatu; 'marapiṉaḷaṉṟeṉa' eṉpatum pāṭam vañcakkiḷavi - maṇimēkaliyi utayakumaraṉ vayamākkaṟkēṟṟa col. kōla mukattu viyarpoṭittavaḷāy eṉap pirittuk kūṭṭutalumām.  
43-51.	arava vaṇṭoṭu tēṉiṉam ārkkum-olikkiṉṟa vaṇṭukaḷuṭaṉ tēṉiṉamum āravārikkum, tarumaṇal ñemiriya tirunāṟu oru ciṟi - koṇṭuvantiṭṭa maṇal paranta muṉṟiliyuṭiya aḻaku tōṉṟum oru pakkattil, pavaḻat tūṇattup pacumpoṉ ceñcuvar-pavaḷaṅkaḷāṟ ceyta tūṇkaḷiyum paciya poṉṉālākiya cevviya cuvarkaḷiyum, tikaḻ oḷi nittilac cittira vitāṉattu - oḷi viḷaṅkum muttukkaḷākiya mēṟkaṭṭiyiyum uṭiya, viḷaṅku oḷip paranta paḷiṅku cey maṇṭapattu-viḷaṅku kiṉṟa pēroḷi paraviya paḷiṅku maṇṭapattil, tuḷaṅkumāṉ ūrtit tūmalarp paḷḷi - oḷi acikiṉṟa ariyaṇi yākiya naṟu malar irukkiyil, veṇtiri virinta veṇṇiṟac cāmari - veḷḷiya alikaḷ pōla virinta veṇṇiṟamuṭiya kavarikaḷi, koṇṭu iru maruṅkum kōtiyar vīca - makaḷir koṇṭu iru pakkamum vīca, iruntōṉ tiruntaṭi porunti niṉṟu ētti - amarntirunta utayakumaraṉuṭiya tiruntiya aṭikaḷi vaṇaṅki niṉṟu ētta;
 ārkkum oru ciṟi yeṉka; ārkkum eṉṟataṉāl maṇamuṭimi peṟappaṭṭatu. maṇal paranta muṟṟatti yuṭiyaveṉa viritturittuk koḷka ; 1"tarumaṇaṉ ñemiriya tirunakar muṟṟattu" eṉpatu kāṇka. nāṟutal-tōṉṟutal. muttālākiya calliyiyum tūkkiṉiyum uṭiya cittira vitāṉam eṉka; 2"ōviya vitāṉat turipeṟu nittilattu, mālit tāmam vaḷiyuṭa ṉāṟṟi" eṉṟār iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum. māṉ-arimāṉ. ūrti-ēṟutaṟkiṭamākavuḷḷa ātaṉam eṉṉum poruḷ kuṟittu niṉṟatu. maṇṭapattup paḷḷiyil iruntōṉ eṉka. ētti - ēttaveṉat tirikka. 
52-54.	tiruntu eyiṟu ilaṅkac cevviyiṉ nakku avaṉ - utayakumaraṉ aḻakiya paṟkaḷ viḷaṅkumāṟu cevviyākac cirittu, mātavi maṇimēkaliyuṭaṉ eytiya - mātavi maṇimēkaliyuṭaṉ koṇṭa, tāpatak kōlam tavaṟiṉṟō eṉa - tava vēṭam tavaṟiṉṟi yuḷatō veṉṟu kēṭka ;
tāpatakkōlam-tavavēṭam; eṉṟatu pikkuṇik kōlatti. 
55-63.	aritu peṟu ciṟappil kuruku karu vuyirppa - aritiṟ peṟṟa ciṟappiṉāl paṟavikaḷ īṉum vaṇṇam, oru taṉi ōṅkiya tirumaṇik kāñci - oppaṟṟuyarnta aḻakiya kariya kāñci maram, pāṭal cāl ciṟappil paratattu ōṅkiya-pāṭal cāṉṟa ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya parata kaṇṭattil uyarnta, nāṭakam virumpa naṉṉalam kaviṉi - nāṭukaḷiṉiṭam virumpumāṟu aḻakiya nalaṅkaḷ niṟintu. kāmar cevvik kaṭimalar aviḻntatu-virumpappaṭum cevviyiyuṭiya.  naṟiya malar viriyap peṟṟatu; utayakumaraṉ eṉum oru vaṇṭu uṇīiya - ataṉi utayakumaraṉākiya oppaṟṟa vaṇṭu uṇṇum poruṭṭu, viri voṭu vantēṉ-virintu vantēṉ; viyaṉperu mūtūrp pāḻmm paṟantali ampalattāyatu - atu mikka perumiyi yuṭiya im mūtūriṉ kaṇṇē pāḻiṭamākiya puṟaṅ kāṭṭic cārnta ulaka vaṟaviyil uḷḷatu ; vāḻka niṉ kaṇṇi vāyvāḷ vēntu eṉa-kūriya vāy poruntiya vāḷiṉi yuṭiya maṉṉava niṉ kaṇṇi vāḻvatāka eṉa vurippa; 
utayakumaraṉ avik kaḷattiruntā ṉākaliṉ cittirāpati tāṉ kūṟuvataṉik kuṟippi ṉuṇarntukoḷḷumāṟu cilēṭiyāṟ kūṟuvāḷāyiṉaḷ. kāñcimarattip puṉinturippatu pōṉṟa itaṉkaṇ, ‘kuruku' eṉpatu mātaviyiyum, 'maṇikkāñci' eṉpatu maṇimēkaliyiyum kuṟittu niṉṟaṉa. mātavipeṟa ōṅkiya maṇimēkali nāṭakam virumpap paruva meytiṉaḷeṉka. kuruku-kurukkatti ; itu mātaviyeṉavum paṭum. kāñci - mēkaliyātalit tivākaram mutaliyavaṟṟāṉaṟika, nāṭakam - nāṭṭiṉiṭam, nāṭakakkali. nāṭakam ivaḷāṟ ciṟappeytu mākaliṉ "nāṭakam virumpa" eṉṟār; 1"nāṭaka mēttu nāṭakak kaṇiki" eṉa varutal kāṇka. nalam kaviṉi - nalam niṟintu eṉṉum poruṭṭu. kāmar-viruppam. ‘utayakumaraṉeṉu moruvaṇṭu' eṉṟatu muṉṉilip puṟamoḻi. paṟantali - puṟaṅkāṭu. kuruku karuvuyirppa ōṅkiya kāñci, nalaṅ kaviṉik kaṭimala raviḻntatu; ataṉi utayakumaraṉākiya vaṇṭu uṇṇum poruṭṭu vantēṉ; ak kāñci ampalattāyatu eṉka. kaṇṇi-taliyiṟ cūṭum māli. niṉ kaṇṇi vāḻka veṉṟal marapu. 
64-65.	ōṅkiya puvattu uṭikalap paṭṭōṉ vāṉpuṇi peṟṟeṉa - periya kaṭalilē kappal uṭiyappaṭṭavaṉ ciṟanta teppattip peṟṟāṟpōlak karuti, maṟṟu avaṭku urippōṉ- cittirāpatikkuk kūṟupavaṉāy. 
uṭikalap paṭṭōṉ - kalam uṭiyap paṭṭōṉ eṉa māṟuka. "uṭikalap paṭṭāṅ koḻintōr tammuṭaṉ" (16:20) eṉa muṉ vantamiyuṅ kāṇka.  
66-73.	mēviya paḷiṅkiṉ viruntiṉ pāvi iḵtu ōviyac ceyti yeṉṟu oḻivēṉ muṉṉar-paḷikkaṟiyiṉkaṇ poruntiya putumiyiṉi yuṭiya pāviyākiya itu cittirac ceyki eṉṟu nīṅkuvēṉ muṉṉē, kāntaḷañ ceṅki taḷipiṇi viṭāa ēntu iḷavaṉamuli iṟi nerittatūum - kāntaḷ malar pōṉṟa civanta kikaḷ piṇitta piṇippu viṭāvāy ēntiya iḷiya aḻakiya muliyi iṟiyaḷavu neritta ceykiyum, ottoḷir .  pavaḷattu uḷ oḷi ciṟanta - pavaḷatti yottu oḷirukiṉṟa vāyiṉuḷḷē oḷimikka, muttuk kūrttaṉṉa muḷeyiṟṟu amutam - muttukkaḷ kūrmiyuṟṟālaṉṉa kūriya paṟkaḷiṉ kaṇ  ūṟukiṉṟa amutiṉi, arunta ēmānta āruyir taḷirppa-aruntu  taṟku ācippaṭṭa ariya uyirāṉatu taḷirkkumāṟu, viruntiṉ mūral arumpiyatūum - putiya puṉmuṟuval pūttatuvum ;
muṉpu maṇimēkali taṉṉik kaṇṭu añcip paḷik kaṟiyiṟ pukunta poḻutu avaḷi ōviyameṉṟu tāṉ eṇṇiyiruntāṉāka utayakumaraṉ kūṟiṉāṉeṉka. viṭā a:etirmaṟi viṉiyeccamuṟṟu. iṟi - ciṟitu. arunta eṉṉum peyareccat takaram tokkatu; "aruntē mānta vāruyir mutalvaṉi" (14 : 68) eṉa muṉṉarum iṅṅaṉam pōntatu. 
74-80. 	māyitaḻk kuvaḷi malar puṟattu ōṭṭik kāyvēl veṉṟa karuṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇ - kariya itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya kuvaḷi malarkaḷip puṟattē ōṭacceytu kāykiṉṟa vēliṉi veṉṟa kariya kayalmīṉip pōṉṟa nīṇṭa kaṇkaḷ, aṟivu piṟitākiyatu āyiḻi taṉakkeṉac ceviyakam pukūuc ceṉṟa cevviyum - maṇimēkalikku aṟivu vēṟupaṭṭateṉṟu aṟivikkumāṟu kātiṉiṭam ōṭic ceṉṟa aḻakum, paḷiṅku puṟatteṟinta pavaḷappāvi eṉ uḷam koṇṭu oḷittāḷ uyirk kāppiṭṭu eṉṟu-ākiya ivaṟṟikkoṇṭu paḷikkaṟiyiṉ puṟattē tōṉṟiya pavaḷappāvi uyirik kāttal ceytu eṉ uḷattil oḷittāḷ eṉṟu, iṭiyiruḷ yāmattu iruntēṉ muṉṉar- iruḷiyuṭiya iṭiyāmattil tuyilātirun tēṉ muṉpu;  
ōṭṭi veṉṟa kaṇ, kayal neṭuṅkaṇ eṉka. ceviyil urikkap pukkatupōṟ ceṉṟa veṉka. kaṇṇiṉ iyalpākiya nīṭciyiyō allatu mīṭciyiyō iṅṅaṉaṅ kaṟpittuk kūṟutalāl itutaṟkuṟippēṟṟam. koṇṭu - nerittatūum arumpiyatūum cevviyumākiya ivaṟṟik koṇṭu, pāvi koṇṭu uyirkkāppiṭṭu eṉ uḷattu oḷittāḷ eṉka. iruntēṉ muṉṉar - tuyilātiruntēṉuṭiya muṉpu; "poṅku mellamaḷiyiṟ poruntā tiruntōṉ, muṉṉart tōṉṟi"  (7: 6-7) eṉa muṉpu vantiruttal kāṇka. iruntēṉ ; aṅṅaṉa mirunta eṉ muṉṉar eṉa vēṟaṟutturikka. 
81-85.	poṉtikaḻ mēṉi orutti tōṉṟi - poṉpōla viḷaṅkukiṉṟa tirumēṉiyiyuṭiyoruttitōṉṟi, ceṅkōl kāṭṭi - ceṅkōṉmiyi aṟivuṟutti, ceytavam purinta aṅkavaḷ taṉtiṟam ayarppāy eṉṟaṉaḷ - tavaneṟiyiṟ cellum maṇimēkaliyiṉmītu koṇṭa eṇṇatti maṟappāy eṉṟu kūṟiṉaḷ, teyvaṅ kollō tippiyaṅ kollō. eyyā miyalēṉ yāṉ eṉṟu avaṉ cola-aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiyatu oru teyvantāṉō aṉṟi vāṉavar ulakil tōṉṟiya vēṟu poruḷō iṉṉateṉa aṟiyāta mayakkamuṭiyēṉ eṉṟu utayakumaraṉ kūṟa; 
orutti eṉṟatu maṇimēkalā teyvatti- eyyāmi - aṟiyāmi: uriccol, tippiyaṅ kollō - viyappiṉi viḷivikkum vēṟoṉṟō eṉṟumām. 
86-89. 	cittirāpati tāṉ ciṟu naki eyti - cittirāpati ciṟunakiyaṭintu, attiṟam viṭuvāy araciḷaṅkuricil-iḷavaracar peruntakiyē niṉ kaṉaviṟṟōṉṟi orutti kūṟiyataṉi viṭuvāyāka, kāmak kaḷḷāṭṭiṭi mayakkuṟṟaṉa tēvarkkāyiṉum cilavō ceppiṉ-kūṟuṅkāl vāṉavar kaḷiṭattum kāmak kaḷḷāṭṭiṉ kaṇ mayaṅkic ceyta ceykikaḷ cilavāmō; 
kāmak kaḷḷāṭṭiṭi - kāmamākiya kaḷḷi uṇṭavaḻi. aṟivi mayakkutalāṉum, veḷippaṭuntōṟum viruppatti yuṇṭākkutalāṉum kāmam kaḷḷiṉi yoppatāyiṟṟu; 1"kaḷittoṟuṅ kaḷḷuṇṭal vēṭṭaṟṟāṟ kāmam, veḷippaṭun tōṟu miṉitu" eṉpatu kāṇka. tēvarkaḷum mayaṅkic ceytaṉa pala veṉṟapaṭi.
90-98.	mātavaṉ maṭantikku varuntu tuyar eyti āyirañ ceṅkaṇ amararkōṉ peṟṟatum - kutama muṉivar maṉiviyākiya akalikiyiṉ mēluḷḷa vēṭkiyāl mikka tuyaruḻantu tēvarkkaracaṉākiya intiraṉ ammuṉivarāl āyirañ ceṅkaṇ aṭinta ceytiyum, mēruk kuṉṟattu ūrunīrc caravaṇattu-imayamaliyiluḷḷa paranta nīriṉiyuṭiya caravaṇap poykiyil, aruntiṟal muṉivarkku āraṇaṅkākiya - ariya vali poruntiya muṉivar eḻuvarkku ariya aṇaṅkupōlvārākiya, perum peyarp peṇṭir piṉpu uḷam pōkkiya - periya pukaḻiyuṭiya avar maṉiviyar piṉṉē uḷattic celuttiya, aṅki maṉiyāḷ - akkiṉitēvaṉ maṉivi, avaravar vaṭivāyt tāṅkā vēṭkitaṉi avaṇ taṇittatūum - am muṉivar maṉiviyariṉ vaṭivattit taṉittaṉi koṇṭu avaṉatu nīṅkāta vēṭkiyit taṇitta ceytiyum, kēṭṭum aṟitiyō vāṭṭiṟal kuricil- kēṭṭum aṟiyāyō vāḷvaliyuṭiya aracar peruntakiyē;
tuyareyti avaḷic cērntu ataṉāṟ peṟṟatum eṉa viritturittuk koḷka. intiraṉ kutama muṉivar maṉivi yākiya akalikiyi virumpic cērntu am muṉivariṭṭa cāpattāl āyiraṅkaṇ peṟṟāṉ eṉpatu purāṇakkati. mēru - imayam ; 1"imiyavil vāṅkiya" eṉpatu kāṇka. ūrtal - parattal. caravaṇam - taruppik kāṭu ; ataṉi yuṭiyatoru poykiyi uṇarttiṟṟu. muṉṉoru kālattil aṅkivāṉavaṉ eḻumuṉivar maṉiviyari virumpi avvētaṉi poṟukkalāṟṟātu kāṭṭiṟkucceṉṟapoḻutu, avaṉ eṇṇatti yaṟinta avaṉ maṉiviyākiya cuvākātēvi eḻu muṉivar maṉivikaḷuḷ aruntati yoḻintōr vaṭivatti muṟiyē eṭuttu, vēṟuvēṟu kālaṅkaḷiṟ cērntu avaṉ vēṭkiyit taṇittāḷ eṉpatum purāṇakkati.  
98-102.	kaṉṉik kāvalum kaṭiyiṟ kāvalum - kaṉṉip paruvattiṟ kāvalum maṇattiṉ piṟkāvalum, taṉuṟukaṇavaṉ cāvuṟiṟ kāvalum-taṉṉi aṭinta kaṇavaṉ iṟappiṟ kāvalum ākiya ivaṟṟi, niṟiyiṟ kāttup piṟarpiṟark kāṇātu-uḷattik kaṟpu neṟiyil niṟuttalāṟ kāttu ayalāri nōkkāmal, koṇṭōṉ allatu teyvamum pēṇā - kaṇavaṉiyaṉṟit teyvattiyum toḻāta, peṇṭirtam kuṭiyil piṟantāḷ allaḷ - kulamakaḷirtam marapil tōṉṟiyavaḷ allaḷ ; 
kulamakaḷir kaṉṉi mutaliya munniliyilum niṟiyāṟ ṟammikkāt toḻukuva reṉṟataṉāl potumakaḷirkku an nili vēṟupāṭum kāttalum illi yeṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu. 2"makaḷir, niṟikākkuṅ kāppē tali," "teyvan toḻāaḷ koḻunaṟ ṟoḻuteḻuvāḷ" eṉpaṉa kāṇka.  
103-111.	nāṭavar kāṇa nal araṅ kēṟi āṭalum pāṭalum aḻakum kāṭṭi-ilakkaṇappaṭi amitta nalla araṅku kaḷil ēṟi nāṭṭiṉar palarum kāṇum vaṇṇam āṭaliyum pāṭaliyum eḻiliyum pulappaṭutti, curuppu nāṇ karuppuvil aruppukkaṇi tūva - vaṇṭukaḷākiya nāṇiṉiyuṭiya karumpuvillikkoṇṭa anaṅkaṉ arumpukaḷākiya ampu kaḷip poḻiya, cerukkayal neṭuṅkaṇ curukku valip paṭuttu-pōrceyyuṅ kayalmīṉi yotta nīṇṭa kaṇ kaḷākiya curukku valiyāl akappaṭutti, kaṇṭōr neñcam koṇṭu akampukku - tamatāṭal mutaliyavaṟṟik kaṇṭōruṭiya uḷḷattik koḷḷikoṇṭu maṉiyiṟ ceṉṟu, paṇtēr moḻiyiṟ payaṉpala vāṅki - paṇpōlum iṉiya moḻikaḷāl poṉ āṭi aṇi mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷiyum vāṅki, vaṇṭiṉ tuṟakkum koṇṭi makaḷiri - tēṉiyuṇṭu malarit tuṟakkum vaṇṭiṉip pōlap poruḷ koṭuttārip piṉpu tuṟakkum parattiyari, pāṉmiyil piṇittup paṭiṟṟuri aṭakkutal - taṉ vaya mākap piṇittu avartam poyyurikaḷi aṭakkutal, kōlmuṟi aṉṟō kumaraṟku eṉṟalum - iḷaṅkōvukkuc ceṅkōṉmuṟimi allavō eṉa urittalum,  
nallaraṅku-1"eḻukō lakalat teṇkō ṉīḷat, toru kōluyarat tuṟuppiṉa tāki, uttarap palakiyō ṭaraṅkiṉ palaki, vitta viṭi nilam nāṟkō lāka, ēṟṟa vāyi liraṇṭuṭaṉ poliyat, tōṟṟiya varaṅku." nāṭaka makaḷirkku āṭal mutaliya mūṉṟiṉoṉṟuṅ kuṟiyalākā teṉpatu, 2"āṭalum pāṭalu maḻaku meṉṟik, kūṟiya mūṉṟi ṉoṉṟukuṟi paṭāmal" eṉpataṉā ṉaṟika ; curumpu, karumpu, arumpu eṉpaṉa valittal peṟṟaṉa. arumpu - moṭṭaṟāmalar. cerukkayal - oṉṟi yoṉṟu etirntu pōr ceyyuṅ kayal. koṇṭi makaḷir-poruḷik koḷḷum parattiyar, kōṉmuṟi-aracanīti eṉṟumām. kumaraṟku:muṉṉiliyiṟ paṭarkki. kuṭiyiṟ piṟantāḷallaḷ. koṇṭi makaḷiruṭ paṭṭāḷ. avaḷip piṇittu aṭakkutal kōṉmuṟi yaṉṟō veṉka. 
112-118.	utayakumaraṉ uḷḷam piṟaḻntu - utayakumaraṉ maṉam māṟutalaṭintu, viripari neṭuntēr mēṟceṉ ṟēṟi-virinta celaviṉiyuṭiya kutirikaḷ pūṭṭappeṟṟa periya tēriṉmītu ēṟic ceṉṟu, āyiḻi irunta ampalam eyti - maṇimēkaliyirunta ampalatti yaṭintu, kāṭamar celvi kaṭippaci kaḷiya ōṭukik koṇṭu niṉṟu ūṭṭunaḷ pōla - pēykaḷiṉ paciyi nīkkutaṟku ōṭṭiṉik kiyiṟ koṇṭu niṉṟu ūṭṭukiṉṟa kāṭu kiḻāḷip pōla, tīppaci mākkaṭkuc ceḻuñ cōṟu īttup pāttiram ēntiya pāviyik kāṇṭalum - koṭiya paciyiyuṭiya makkaḷukkuc ceḻuñcōṟaḷittuk kaṭiñi ēnti niṉṟa maṇimēkaliyik kaṇṭa vaḷavil;
kaṭi - pēy. ūṭṭunaḷākiya celvi pōla veṉka. 
119-127.	iṭaṅkaḻi kāmamoṭu aṭaṅkāṉ āki - varampiṉṟip perukiya kāmattōṭu aṭaṅkātavaṉāy, uṭampōṭu eṉṟaṉ uḷḷakam pukuntu eṉ neñcam kavarnta vañcak  kaḷvi - uṭaluṭaṉ eṉṉuṭiya neñciṉuṭ pukuntu eṉatu uḷḷattik kavarnta vañcak kaḷviyākiya maṇimēkali, nōṟṟūṇ vāḻkkiyiṉ nocitavam tāṅki - virataṅkaḷāṟ paṭṭiṉi viṭṭuṇṇum vāḻkkiyiyuṭiya nuṇṇiya tavattit tāṅki, ēṟṟūṇ virumpiya kāraṇam eṉeṉa - irantuṇṇutali virumpiya kāraṇam yātu eṉṟu, tāṉē tamiyaḷ niṉṟōḷ muṉṉar - tāṉē taṉiyaḷāy niṉṟōḷmuṉ, yāṉē kēṭṭal iyalpu eṉac ceṉṟu - yāṉē nēriṟ ceṉṟu kēṭpatu naṉṟu eṉap pōy, nallāy eṉkol naṟṟavam purintatu collāy eṉṟu tuṇintuṭaṉ kēṭpa - naṅkāy nī naṟṟavam ceyya mēṟkoṇṭatu eṉṉa kāraṇam ataṉik kūṟuvāyāka    eṉṟu tuṇintu kēṭka; 
iṭaṅkaḻi - varampu kaṭanta; 1"iṭaṅkaḻi neñcat tiḷami yāṉi" eṉpataṉ uri kāṇka, nocitavam-uṭal iḷittaṟ kētuvākiya tavameṉṟum, maṉam nuṇṇitātaṟ kētuvākiya tavameṉṟum ām; 2"nōṟṟuṇal yākki nocitavattīr'' eṉṟār iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum. utayakumāraṉ ampalameytip pāviyik kāṇṭalum aṭaṅkāṉāki, yāṉē kēṭṭal iyalpeṉac ceṉṟu, ‘purintatu eṉkol col lāy' eṉṟu kēṭpa eṉka. 
128-133.	eṉ amar kātalaṉ irākulaṉ īṅkivaṉ taṉ aṭi toḻutalum takaveṉa vaṇaṅki-ivaṉ eṉṉi virumpiya kaṇavaṉākiya irākulaṉākaliṉ ivaṉuṭiya aṭikaḷi vaṇaṅkutalum takutiyē eṉap paṇintu, aṟipōy neñcam avaṉpāl aṇu kiṉum-eṉ uḷḷam ōṭṭi pōy avaṉiṭam kiṭṭiṉum, iṟivaḷi muṉ ki īṅkivaṉ paṟṟiṉum - iṟiyiṉiyuṭiya vaḷi yaṇinta muṉ kiyi ivaṉ paṟṟiṉum, toṉṟu kātalaṉcol etir maṟuttal - paḻampiṟappiṟ kaṇavaṉuṭiya colli etir maṟuttal, naṉṟi aṉṟeṉa naṭuṅkiṉaḷ mayaṅki-naṉmi yaṉṟu eṉa naṭukkamuṟṟu mayaṅkiyavaḷāy; 
īṅkivaṉ : oru col. aṟipōtal - kīḻaṟuttuc cellutal, aṇukiṉum paṟṟiṉum eṉpaṉa etirkālattil vantaṉa. maṟut talāvatu viṭikūṟā toḻital. 
134-139.	kēṭṭatu moḻivēṉ kēḷviyāḷaril tōṭṭa ceviyi nī ākuvi āmeṉil-kēḷviyāḷarpōla nī tuḷikkap paṭṭa ceviyuṭiyi ākuviyāṉāl nī kēṭṭa taṟku maṟumoḻi kūṟuvēṉ; piṟattalum mūttalum piṇip paṭṭu iraṅkalum iṟattalum uṭiyatu - piṟattalum mutumi yeytalum nōyppaṭṭu varuntutalum iṟattalum uṭiyatu; iṭumpik koḷkalam- tuṉpattiṟkuk koḷ kalamāṉatu; makkaḷ yākki itu eṉa uṇarntu - makkaḷ uṭampākiya itu eṉa aṟintu, mikka nal aṟam virumputal purintēṉ-mēṉmiyuṭiya aruḷaṟattiṉi virumpu vēṉāyiṉēṉ;
ākuvi yāmeṉil moḻivēṉ eṉa māṟuka. kēḷvi yāḷar - uṇmip poruḷik kēṭṭuṇarpavar; 1"kēṭpiṉuṅ kēḷāt takiyavē kēḷviyāl tōṭkap paṭāta cevi'' eṉpatu īṇṭu uṇartaṟ pālatu. makkaḷ yākkiyākiya itu uṭiyatu koḷkalam eṉa vuṇarnteṉka.
nallaṟam-aruḷaṟam: tayātarumam, 2"viṉiyiṉ vantatu viṉikku viḷivāvatu,......, makkaḷ yākki yituveṉa uṇarntu'' eṉa innūluḷ muṉṉar yākkiyi ṉiḻipu kūṟappaṭṭiruttalum kāṇka. 
140-142.	maṇṭamar murukkum kaḷiṟu aṉiyārkkup peṇṭir kūṟum pēraṟivu uṇṭō-mikkuc cellukiṉṟa pōri yaḻikkum kaḷiṟṟiṉi yotta āṭavarkku makaḷir kūṟum pēraṟivum uṇṭō, kēṭṭaṉi āyiṉ vēṭṭatu cey keṉa - itaṉik kēṭṭā yātaliṉ iṉi nī virumpiyatic ceyka eṉṟu kūṟi; 
amariṉkaṇ pakivarik kollum eṉṟumām. kaḷiṟaṉi yārkku eṉṟatu muṉṉilippuṟam. "iḷami nāṇi mutumi yeyti ......ceṟivaḷi makaḷir ceppalu muṇṭō" (4: 107 - 10) eṉa muṉṉarc cutamati utayakumāraṉukku nīti kūṟiyavāṟuṅ kāṇka.  
143-150.   vāḷtiṟal kuricili maṭakkoṭi nīṅki -maṇimēkali vāḷ valiyuṭiya maṉṉavaṉ putalvaṉi nīṅki, mutti mutalvi mutiyāḷ irunta-aṉivarukkum muṟpaṭṭa mutalvi yākiya campāpati amarntirukkiṉṟa, kuccarak kuṭikitaṉ akam pukku āṅku - ciṟiya kōyiliṉuḷ nuḻintu av viṭattil, āṭavar ceyti aṟikunar yār eṉa - āṇmakkaḷiṉ ceyali aṟivōr yāvar eṉṟu eṇṇi, tōṭalar kōtiyit toḻutaṉaḷ ētti - itaḻ virikiṉṟa malar māliyi yaṇinta campāpatiyi vaṇaṅkit tutittu, māya viñci mantiram ōti - māyavitti yuṭiya mantiratti ōti, kāyacaṇṭiki eṉum kāriki vaṭivāy- kāyacaṇṭiki eṉṉum viñci makaḷiṉ vaṭivaṅkoṇṭu, maṇi mēkali tāṉ vantu tōṉṟa - maṇimēkali veḷiyē vantu tōṉṟa; 
 mutti: munti yeṉpataṉ tiripu. kuccaram - kūrccara nāṭu; kuccarak kuṭiki - kūrccara nāṭṭup paṇiyaminta ciṟiya kōyil eṉpar. āṅku:aci. āṭavar ceykiyi aṟikunar yār eṉṟatu maṇimēkali taṉ aṟivuriyik kēṭṭa piṉṉum utayakumaraṉ kāmattik kaṭakkalāṟṟātu taṉṉip paṟṟutaluṅ kūṭumeṉa eṇṇiṉāḷ eṉṉum kuṟippiṟṟu. māyaviñci mantiram eṉṟatu maṇimēkalā teyvam aruḷiya vēṟṟuru eytuvikkum mantiratti. 
151-158.    aṇimalart tārōṉ avaḷpāṟ pukku - aḻakiya malar māliyiṉiyuṭiya utayakumaraṉ kāyacaṇṭiki yuruk
koṇṭa naṅkiyiṭañ ceṉṟu, kuccarak kuṭikik kumariyi marī i-piṉpu kōyiliṉkaṇ uḷḷa camputteyvattip porunti niṉṟu, piccip pāttiram perumpaci uḻanta kāyacaṇṭiki taṉkiyil kāṭṭi - taṉ kiyilirunta tiruvōṭṭip perumpaciyāl varuntiya kāyacaṇṭikiyiṉ kiyiṟ koṭuttu, māyiyiṉ oḷitta maṇimēkalitaṉi-māyiyiṉāl maṟinta maṇimēkaliyi, īṅkim maṇṇīṭṭu yār eṉa uṇarkēṉ - īṇṭuḷḷa ip pāvikaḷuḷ yāreṉṟu aṟivēṉ, āṅkavaḷ ivaḷ eṉṟu    aruḷāy āyiṭiṉ -am maṇimēkali iṉṉaḷeṉa aṟivit taruḷāy āyiṉ,palnāḷ āyiṉum pāṭukiṭappēṉ - palanāṭkaḷāyiṉum īṇṭu varaṅkiṭappēṉ ;
maṇṇīṭu-cutiyāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa pāvi. "neṭunili maṇṇīṭu niṉṟa vāyilum" (6: 47) eṉa innūluḷ muṉṉarum, 1"kaṇṇuḷ viṉiñarum maṇṇīṭ ṭāḷarum" eṉac cilappatikārattum varutal kāṇka. āṅkavaḷ: oru col; 2"teḷivaṉ puṉaṟ ceṉṉi" eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷuriyil, "iṅkivi uṅkuvi yeṉpaṉa oru col" eṉap pērāciriyarurittami kāṇka. pāṭu kiṭattal-varam vēṇṭik kiṭattal; karutiyatu kikūṭum vari eḻātu kiṭattaleṉka; 3"pācaṇṭac cāttaṟkup pāṭukiṭan tāḷukku" eṉpatu kāṇka.  
159-172.	iṉṉum kēḷāy imiyōr pāvāy-vāṉōr taliviyē iṉṉuṅ kēṭpāyāka, pavaḷac cevvāyt tavaḷa vāḷ nakiyum - pavaḷampōṟ civanta vāyiṉkaṇuḷḷa veḷḷoḷi poruntiya paṟkaḷum, añcaṉam cērāc ceṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇum- miyeḻutappaṭāta civanta kayal pōlum nīṇṭa kaṇkaḷum, muṟintu kaṭi neriya varinta cilip puruvamum-kaṭi neriyumāṟu vaḷintu varinta villip pōṉṟa puruvaṅkaḷum, kuvimuṭ karuviyum kōṇamum kūrnuṉik kavimuṭ karuviyum ākik kaṭikoḷa kaḷiṟṟiṉi yaṭakkum kuvinta muḷḷiyuṭiya karuviyum tōṭṭiyum kūriyamuṉiyiyuṭiya kavitta muṭkaḷālākiya parik kōlumākik kāval koḷḷa, kalvip pākaril kāppuvali ōṭṭi-kalviyākiya pākarāl kāppiṉiyuṭiya valiyi vīci, valvāy yāḻiṉ mellitiṉ viḷaṅka - col vaṉmiyuṭiya vāyākiya yāḻiṉāl iṉitāka viḷaṅku māṟu, mutukkuṟi mutumoḻi eṭuttukkāṭṭi - pēraṟivi viḷakkum mutumoḻikaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭi, putukkōḷ yāṉi vēṭṭam vāytteṉa - putitākak koḷḷappaṭum yāṉi vēṭṭattiṉkaṇ akappaṭṭateṉṟu, mutiyāḷ uṉṟaṉ kōṭṭam pukunta - campāpatī niṉṉuṭiya kōyiliṟ pukunta, mativāḷ mukattu maṇimēkalitaṉi oḻiyap pōkēṉ - matipōlum oḷḷiya mukattiṉiyuṭiya maṇimēkaliyi īṇṭu viṭuttuc cellēṉ ; uṉ aṭi toṭṭēṉ - niṉ tiruvaṭikaḷit toṭṭuc cūḷuṟṟēṉ ; itu kuṟi eṉṟaṉaṉ iṟimakaṉ tāṉ eṉ - eṉakku vēṇṭum kāriyam ituveṉṟu utayakumaraṉ kūṟiṉa ṉeṉka.  
īṇṭu imiyōr pāvāy eṉṟatu pōlap piṉṉar "vāṉōr pāvāy" (25-147) eṉac cintātēviyi viḷittaluṅ kāṇka. pikkuṇiyātaliṉ kaṇṇukku mitīṭṭiṟṟila ḷeṉka. varinta cili - kaṭṭaminta vil; nāṇēṟṟiya villumām. kōṇam - tōṭṭiyeṉṉuṅ karuvi; kavimuṭ karuvi - kavitta muḷḷiyuṭiya parikkōl: 1"kōṇan tiṉṟa vaṭuvāḻ mukatta" 2"kavimuṭ karuviyiṉ vaṭamoḻi payiṟṟi" eṉpaṉa kāṇka. 
naki kuvimuṭ karuviyum, kaṇ kōṇamum, puruvam kavimuṭ karuviyumāki eṉa niraṉiṟiyākkuka. kāppu eṉṟatu valikku aṭi. kaliyākiya valiyeṉa uruvakam viritturikka. taṉ kalittiṟaṅkaḷāl puṟampōkātu taṭuttāḷ eṉṟapaṭi. vāyi yāḻ eṉṟataṟkēṟpa mutumoḻiyi iciyeṉa uruvakañ ceyka ; yāḻōci pōla mellitiṉ-viḷaṅka vāyāl mutumoḻi yeṭuttuk kāṭṭi eṉṟalumām ; yāṉi yāḻicikku vayamākumātaliṉ ivvāṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. 3"kāḻvari nillāk kaṭuṅkaḷiṟ ṟoruttal, yāḻvarit taṅki yāṅku" eṉpataṉāl yāṉi yāḻukku vayamāta laṟika. mutumoḻi - mutuvōr moḻi; nītimoḻi. utayakumaraṉ taṉṉi yāṉi yākavum, maṇimēkaliyiṉ naki mutaliyavaṟṟi yāṉiyi aṭakkuṅ karuvikaḷākavum uruvakañ ceytu, avaḷāl tāṉ piṇippuṇṭami kūṟiṉaṉeṉka. cūḷuṟuvār aṭitoṭutal vaḻakkātali 4"aṟava raṭitoṭiṉum" 5"kōṉaṭi toṭṭēṉ" 6"aṭalvali yeyiṉarniṉ aṭi toṭu kaṭaṉitu" eṉpavaṟṟāṉu maṟika.  
cittirāpati atu kēṭṭu eṟi veytuyirttuk kalaṅkit tīrppaleṉṟu kūttiyaṉ maṭantiyar ellārkkuṅ kūṟum; aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟukiṉṟavaḷ, vañciṉañ cāṟṟi uyirttut tērntu pōkik kuṟukip porunti niṉṟu ētta, avaṉ, ‘tavaṟiṉṟō' eṉa, avaḷ, ‘vēntē! niṉ kaṇṇi vāḻka; kāñci ampalat tāyatu' eṉa, atu kēṭṭu avaṭku urippōṉ, ‘eyyā miyalēṉ' eṉṟu colla, cittirāpati ciṟunaki yeyti, paṭiṟṟuri yaṭakkutal kōṉmuṟi yaṉṟō kumaraṟku?' eṉṟalum, utayakumaraṉ uḷḷam piṟaḻntu ēṟi eytik kāṇṭalum aṭaṅ kāṉākic ceṉṟu tuṇintu kēṭpa, avar vaṇaṅki naṭuṅki mayaṅki, ‘vēṭṭatu ceyka' eṉṟu kūṟi nīṅkip pukkuttoḻutu ētti ōtik kāyacaṇṭiki vaṭivāy vantu tōṉṟa, tārōṉ pukku marīi, ‘itu kuṟi' eṉṟaṉaṉ eṉa, viṉi muṭivu ceyka.  
utayakumaraṉ ampalam pukka kāti muṟṟiṟṟu. 
19. ciṟikkōṭṭam aṟakkōṭṭamākkiya kāti  
utayakumaraṉ campāpatiyi vaṇaṅkiiṅṅaṉam vañciṉaṅ kūṟukiyil avaṉ kēṭkumpaṭi, "nī em perumāṭṭiyiṉ muṉ ārāyntu pārāmaṟ cūḷuṟavu moḻintaṉi ; ataṉāl yātoru payaṉumilli" eṉṟu āṅkuḷḷa cittiraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟip poruntiyuḷḷa teyvaṅ kūṟiṟṟu. avvuri kēṭṭu avaṉ maṉaṅ kalaṅki varunti, "maṇimēkaliyi maṟappāyeṉṟu muṉṉaṅkūṟiya teyvattiṉ moḻiyum viyappit tarukiṉṟatu ; teyvattaṉmi yuḷḷatāka iruttaliṉ avaḷ ēntiya pāttiramum viyappit tarukiṉṟatu ; ic cittiram pēciyatum viyappi viḷikkiṉṟatu ; ivaṟṟiyellām maṇimēkaliyiṉ ceytiyi aṟintukoṇṭa piṉpu aṟivōm" eṉṟu tuṇintu mīṇṭu taṉ iruppiṭatti yaṭintāṉ. maṇimēkali, "nam vaṭivattōṭu tirintāl utayakumaraṉ viṭṭu nīṅkāṉ ; ākaliṉ nām kāyacaṇṭiki vaṭivaṅkoḷḷutalē naṉṟu" eṉṟu eṇṇi av vaṭivukoṇṭu campāpatiyiṉ kōyililiruntu amutacurapiyik kiyilēntik koṇṭu yāṅkaṇum ceṉṟu pacittuvanta yāvarukkum uṇavaḷittu varupavaḷ, oru nāḷ an nakarattuḷḷa ciṟikkōṭṭattiṟ pukuntu, aṅkē paciyāl varuntuvōri aruḷuṭaṉ pārttu iṉiya moḻi kūṟi uṇpippāḷāyiṉaḷ. avaḷ oru pāttirattiliruntē palarukku uṇavaḷittalik kaṇṭa kāvalāḷar mikka viyappaṭintu, "ip pāttirattiṉ mēṉmiyiyum ivaḷ ceyki yiyum aracaṉukkut terivippēm' eṉa niṉintu ceṉṟu, cīrtti yeṉṉum irācamātēviyuṭaṉ oru maṇṭapattil mikka ciṟappuṭaṉ vīṟṟirukkum vēntaṉuṭiya cevviyi nōkkic cēymiyil vaṇaṅki niṉṟu, "māvaṇkiḷḷi ūḻitō ṟūḻi oḷiyoṭu vāḻi" eṉṟu vāḻtti, "yāṉittī yeṉṉum nōyāl varunti uṭal melintu ip pakutiyilē tiriyum maṭanti yorutti ciṟikkōṭṭattuḷḷē vantu niṉṉi vāḻttik kiyiṉkaṇ piccip pāttiram oṉṟē koṇṭu aṅku vantu moykkiṉṟa yāvarukkum uṇavu curantūṭṭu kiṉṟaṉaḷ; iv vaticayattit terivikkavē vantōm" eṉṟār. ataṉik kēṭṭa aracaṉ, "am maṅkiyi iṅkē aḻittu varuka" eṉṟaṉaṉ. uṭaṉē kāvalāḷar vantu ataṉit terivikka, avaḷ ceṉṟu aracaṉik kaṇṭu vāḻtti niṉṟaṉaḷ. aracaṉ, "ariya tavamuṭiyāy ; nī yār ? kiyilēntiya pāttiram eṅkē kiṭittatu?" eṉṉa, avaḷ, "aracē! neṭuṅ kālam vāḻvāyāka; yāṉ viñci makaḷ; ip pakutiyilē vēṟṟuruk koṇṭu tirintēṉ; itu piccip pāttiram; itaṉi ampalattēyuḷḷa teyvamoṉṟu eṉakku aruḷiyatu ; itu teyvat taṉmiyuṭiyatu; yāṉittī yeṉṉum tīrāp pacinōyit tīrttatu; paciyāl melintavarkaḷukku uyir maruntākavuḷḷatu" eṉṟu kūṟiṉaḷ. piṉ, aracaṉ, "yāṉ ceyya vēṇṭuvatu yātu?" eṉṟu kēṭpa, avaḷ, "ciṟikkōṭṭatti yaḻittu aṟavōr vāḻuṅ kōṭṭamākac ceytal vēṇṭum" eṉṟāḷ. aracaṉ, avaḷ virumpiya vaṇṇamē ceyvittaṉaṉ. (itaṉkaṇ aracaṉatu poḻil viḷiyāṭṭuk kūṟumiṭattuḷḷa iyaṟkippuṉivu mutaliyaṉa mikka iṉpam payappaṉa.
		mutiyāḷ tiruntaṭi mummiyiṉ vaṇaṅki
matumalart tārōṉ vañciṉaṅ kūṟa
ēṭaviḻ tārōy eṅkō makaḷmuṉ
nāṭātu tuṇintunā nalkūrn taṉiyeṉa.
5		vittakar iyaṟṟiya viḷaṅkiya kiviṉic
cittira moṉṟu teyvaṅ kūṟalum
utaya kumaraṉ uḷḷaṅ kalaṅkip
potiyaṟip paṭṭōr pōṉṟumey varunti
aṅkavaḷ taṉṟiṟam ayarppā yeṉṟē
10    		ceṅkōl kāṭṭiya teyvamun tippiyam
piyara valkul palarpaci kaḷiyak
kiyi lēntiya pāttiram tippiyam
mutti mutalvi aṭipiḻit tāyeṉac
cittira muritta itūun tippiyam
15		innili yellām iḷaṅkoṭi ceytiyiṉ
piṉṉaṟi vāmeṉap peyarvōṉ ṟaṉṉi
akalvāy ñālam āriru ḷuṇṇap
pakalara cōṭṭip paṇiyeḻun tārppa
māli neṟṟi vāṉpiṟik kōṭṭu
20		nīla yāṉi mēlōr iṉṟik
kāmar ceṅki nīṭṭi vaṇṭupaṭu
pūnāṟu kaṭāañ cerukkik kālkiḷarntu
niṟiyaḻi tōṟṟamoṭu toṭara muṟimiyiṉ
nakara nampiyar vaḷiyōr tammuṭaṉ
25		makara vīṇiyiṉ kiḷinarampu vaṭitta
iḷipuṇa riṉcīr eḵkuḷaṅ kiḻippap
poṟāa neñciṟ pukiyeri pottip
paṟāak kurukiṉ uyirttavaṉ pōyapiṉ
uṟiyuṭ kuṭiki uḷvarik koṇṭa
30		maṟuvil ceyki maṇimē kalitāṉ
mātavi makaḷāy maṉṟam tiritariṉ
kāvalaṉ makaṉō kiviṭa līyāṉ
kāypaci yāṭṭi kāyacaṇ ṭikiyeṉa
ūrmuḻu taṟiyum uruvaṅ koṇṭē
35		āṟṟā mākkaṭ kāṟṟun tuṇiyāki
ēṟṟalum iṭutalum irappōr kaṭaṉavar
mēṟceṉ ṟaḷittal viḻuttakit teṉṟē
nūṟporuḷ uṇarntōr nuṉittaṉa rāmeṉa
mutiyāḷ kōṭṭat takaviyiṉ irunta
40		amuta curapiyi aṅkiyiṉ vāṅkip
patiyakan tiritarum pintoṭi naṅki
atirkaḻal vēntaṉ aṭipiḻit tāri
oṟukkun taṇṭat tuṟuciṟik kōṭṭam
viruppoṭum pukuntu veytuyirttup pulampi
45		āṅkup paciyuṟum āruyir mākkaḷi
vāṅku kiyakam varuntaniṉ ṟūṭṭalum
ūṭṭiya pāttiram oṉṟeṉa viyantu
kōṭṭaṅ kāvalar kōmakaṉ ṟaṉakkip
pāttira tāṉamum pintoṭi ceytiyum
50		yāppuṭit tāka icittumeṉ ṟēki
neṭiyōṉ kuṟaḷuru vāki nimirntutaṉ
aṭiyiṟ paṭiyi yaṭakkiya annāḷ
nīriṟ peyta mūri vārcili
māvali marumāṉ cīrkeḻu tirumakaḷ
55		cīrtti yeṉṉun tiruttaku tēviyoṭu
pōtaviḻ pūmpoḻil pukuntaṉaṉ pukkuk
kompart tumpi kuḻalici kāṭṭap
poṅkar vaṇṭiṉam nalliyāḻ ceyya
varikkuyil pāṭa māmayil āṭum
60		virippūm pantar kaṇṭuḷañ ciṟantum
puṇartuṇi nīṅkiya poyki aṉṉamoṭu
maṭamayiṟ pēṭiyun tōkiyuṅ kūṭi 
iruciṟi virittāṅ keḻuntuṭaṉ koṭpaṉa
oruciṟik kaṇṭāṅ kuṇmakiḻ veyti
65		māmaṇi vaṇṇaṉun tammuṉum piññiyum
āṭiya kuraviyiḵ tāmeṉa nōkkiyum
kōṅkalar cērnta māṅkaṉi taṉṉip
pāṅkuṟa virunta palpoṟi maññiyic
cempoṟ ṟaṭṭil tīmpā lēntip
70		piṅkiḷi yūṭṭumōr pāviyā meṉṟum
aṇimalarp pūmpoḻil akaviyi ṉirunta
piṇavukkuraṅ kēṟṟip perumatar maḻikkaṇ
maṭavōrk kiyaṟṟiya māmaṇi yūcal
kaṭuvaṉūk kuvatu kaṇṭunaki eytiyum
75		pācili ceṟinta pacuṅkāṟ kaḻiyoṭu
vālvīc ceṟinta marāaṅ kaṇṭu
neṭiyōṉ muṉṉoṭu niṉṟaṉa ṉāmeṉat
toṭicēr ceṅkiyiṟ ṟoḻutuniṉ ṟēttiyum
āṭaṟ kūttiṉō ṭavinayan terivōr
80		nāṭakak kāppiya naṉṉūl nuṉippōr
paṇṇiyāḻ narampiṟ paṇṇumuṟi niṟuppōr
taṇṇumik karuvik kaṇṇeṟi terivōr
kuḻaloṭu kaṇṭaṅ koḷaccīr niṟuppōr
85		āram parinta muttaṅ kōppōr
īram pularnta cāntan timirvōr
kuṅkuma varuṇaṅ koṅkiyiṉ iḻippōr
añceṅ kaḻunīr āyitaḻ piṇippōr
naṉṉeṭuṅ kūntal naṟuviri kuṭivōr
90		poṉṉiṉ āṭiyiṟ poruntupu niṟpōr
āṅkavar tammō ṭakaliru vāṉattu
vēntaṉiṟ ceṉṟu viḷiyāṭ ṭayarntu
kuruntun taḷavun tiruntumalarc ceruntiyum
murukuviri mulliyuṅ karuviḷam poṅkarum
95		poruntupu niṉṟu tiruntunaki ceytu
kuṟuṅkāl nakulamum neṭuñcevi muyalum
piṟaḻntupāy māṉum iṟumpakalā veṟiyum
vammeṉak kūuy makiḻtuṇi yoṭutaṉ
cemmalarc ceṅki kāṭṭupu niṉṟu
100		maṉṉavaṉ ṟāṉum malarkkaṇi mintaṉum
iṉṉiḷa vēṉilum iḷaṅkāṟ celvaṉum
entirak kiṇaṟum iṭuṅkaṟ kuṉṟamum
vantuvī ḻaruviyum malarppūm pantarum
parappunīrp poykiyuṅ karappunīrk kēṇiyum
105		oḷittuṟi yiṭaṅkaḷum paḷikkaṟip paḷḷiyum
yāṅkaṇun tirintu tāḻntuviḷi yāṭi
makata viṉiñarum marāṭṭak kammarum
avantik kollarum yavaṉat taccarum
taṇṭamiḻ viṉiñar tammoṭu kūṭik
110		koṇṭiṉi tiyaṟṟiya kaṇkavar ceyviṉip
pavaḷat tiraḷkāṟ paṉmaṇip pōtikit
tavaḷa nittilat tāman tāḻnta
kōṇac canti māṇviṉi vitāṉattut
tamaṉiyam vēynta vakipeṟu vaṉappiṟ
115		piñcēṟu meḻukāp pacumpoṉ maṇṭapattu
intira tiruvaṉ ceṉṟiṉi tēṟalum
vāyiluk kicittu maṉṉavaṉ aruḷāl
cēynilat taṉṟiyuñ cevvitiṉ vaṇaṅki
eñcā maṇṇacii ikaluḷan turappa
120		vañciyi ṉiruntu vañci cūṭi
muṟañcevi yāṉiyun tērum māvum
maṟaṅkeḻu neṭuvāḷ vayavaru miṭinta
talittārc cēṉiyoṭu malittuttali vantōr
cilikkayal neṭuṅkoṭi ceruvēṟ ṟaṭakki
125		ārpuṉi teriyal iḷaṅkōṉ ṟaṉṉāl
kāriyāṟṟuk koṇṭa kāval veṇkuṭi
valikeḻu taṭakki māvaṇ kiḷḷi
oḷiyoṭu vāḻi ūḻitō ṟūḻi
vāḻi yeṅkō maṉṉavar peruntaki
130		kēḷitu maṉṉō keṭukaniṉ pakiñar
yāṉit tīnōyk kayarntumey vāṭiyim 
mānakart tiriyumōr vampa mātar
aruñciṟik kōṭṭat takavayiṟ pukuntu
perumpeyar maṉṉa niṉpeyar vāḻtti
135		aiyap pāttiram oṉṟukoṇ ṭāṅku
moykoṇ mākkaṇ mocikka'd2vūṇ curantaṉaḷ
ūḻitō ṟūḻi ulakaṅ kāttu
vāḻi yeṅkō maṉṉava eṉṟalum
varuka varuka maṭakkoṭi tāṉeṉṟu
140		aruḷpuri neñcamo ṭaracaṉ kūṟaliṉ
vāyi lāḷariṉ maṭakkoṭi tāṉceṉṟu
āykaḻal vēntaṉ aruḷvā ḻiyaveṉat
tāṅkarun taṉmit tavattōy nīyār	
yāṅkā kiyativ vēntiya kaṭiñiyeṉṟu
145		aracaṉ kūṟalum āyiḻi urikkum
virittār vēntē nīnīṭu vāḻi
viñci makaḷyāṉ viḻavaṇi mūtūr
vañcan tirintēṉ vāḻiya peruntaki
vāṉam vāykka maṇvaḷam perukuka
150		tītiṉ ṟāka kōmakaṟ kīṅkītu
aiyak kaṭiñi ampala maruṅkōr
teyvan tantatu tippiya māyatu
yāṉit tīnōy arumpaci keṭuttatu
ūṉuṭi mākkaṭ kuyirmarun tituveṉa
155		yāṉceyaṟ pālateṉ iḷaṅkoṭik keṉṟu
vēntaṉ kūṟa melliyal urikkum
ciṟiyōr kōṭṭañ cīttaruḷ neñcattu
aṟavōrk kākkum atuvā ḻiyareṉa
aruñciṟi viṭṭāṅ kāyiḻi uritta
160		peruntavar tammāṟ perumporu ḷeytak
kaṟiyō rillāc ciṟiyōr kōṭṭam
aṟavōrk kākkiṉaṉ aracāḷ vēnteṉ 
uri 
1-6.  		mutiyāḷ tiruntaṭi mummiyiṉ vaṇaṅki - campāpatiyiṉ tiruntiya aṭikaḷi mummuṟi vaṇakkañceytu, matumalart tārōṉ vañciṉam kūṟa - tēṉ poruntiya malarmāli kaḷiyuṭiya utayakumaraṉ vañciṉaṅ kūṟutalum, ēṭu aviḻ tārōy eṅkōmakaḷ muṉ nāṭātu tuṇintu nā nal kūrntaṉi eṉa - itaḻvirikiṉṟa māliyi yuṭiyavaṉē em talimakaḷ muṉṉar ārāyātu tuṇivuṭaṉ cūḷuṟavu ceytu nā vaṟumi yuṟṟaṉi eṉṟu, vittakar iyaṟṟiya viḷaṅkiya kiviṉic cittiram oṉṟu teyvam kūṟalum-kival ciṟparāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭu viḷaṅkiya toḻil aminta cittiram oṉṟiṟ poruntiya teyvam urittalum ;
mummiyiṉ - uḷam uri uṭal eṉṉum muk karaṇaṅkaḷāl eṉṟumām ; muṟimiyiṉ eṉpatum pāṭam. nā nalkūrtalāvatu payaṉila moḻital. vittakar-ciṟpam vallōr; "vittaka riyaṟṟiya viḷaṅkiya kiviṉic, cittirac ceyki" (3: 167-8) eṉa muṉṉar vantamiyuṅ kāṇka. oṉṟu-poruntiya; atiṭṭitta.
7-16. 	utayakumaraṉ uḷḷam kalaṅki - utayakumaraṉ maṉak kalakka muṟṟu, potiyaṟip paṭṭōrpōṉṟu mey varunti - cāḷaramillāta kīḻaṟiyil akappaṭṭōrip pōla uṭal varunti, aṅkavaḷ taṉtiṟam ayarppāy eṉṟē - maṇimēkalimītu koṇṭa eṇṇatti maṟappāy eṉṟē, ceṅkōlkāṭṭiya teyvamum tippiyam - ceṅkōṉ muṟiyi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya teyvaṅ kūṟiyatum viyappuṭittu, piyara valkul palar pacikaḷiyak kiyilēntiya pāttiram tippiyam - maṇimēkali palaruṭiya paciyiyum nīkkutaṟkuk kiyil ēntiya kaṭiñiyum viyappuṭittu, mutti mutalvi aṭipiḻittāy eṉac cittiram uritta itūum tippiyam-em taliviyiṉ tiruvaṭiyil piḻi purintāy eṉṟu iv vōviyaṅ kūṟiya ituvum viyappuṭittu, innili ellām iḷaṅkoṭi ceytiyiṉ piṉ aṟivām eṉap peyarvōṉ taṉṉi - ivaṟṟiṉ uṇmiyi yellām maṇimēkaliyiṉ ceyalāl piṉpu aṟivōmeṉa eṇṇi aṅkuniṉṟum mīṇṭa utayakumaraṉi ;
potiyaṟi - puḻukkaṟi: ciṟu tuvāramumiṉṟi mūṭappaṭṭa kīḻaṟi; 1"pōtār piṟavip potiyaṟiyōr" eṉpatu kāṇka. teyvam-maṇimēkalā teyvam. teyvam kūṟiyatumeṉka. tippiyam- īṇṭu viyappeṉṉum poruṭṭu; teyvattaṉmiyumām. araviṉ pi yeṉa māṟuka. pi-paṭam. pāttiramum eṉa eṇṇummi virikka mutti : vikāram.
17-23. 	akalvāy ñālam āriruḷ uṇṇa-iṭamakaṉṟa pūmiyi niṟinta iruḷāṉatu uṇṇumāṟu, pakal aracu ōṭṭi - katiravaṉākiya aracaṉi ōṭṭi, paṇieḻuntu ārppa - paṟiyeḻuntu olikka, māli neṟṟi vāṉpiṟik kōṭṭu- antik kālamākiya neṟṟiyum vāṉiluḷḷa piṟiyākiya maruppum uṭiya, nīlayāṉi- kariya niṟamuṭiya iravākiya yāṉi, mēlōr iṉṟi - pākar oruvarum iṉṟi, kāmar ceṅkinīṭṭi-viruppamākiya tutikkiyi nīṭṭi, vaṇṭupaṭu pū nāṟu kaṭāam cerukki - vaṇṭu moykkum pūviṉ maṇam poruntiya matanīric corintu kaḷittu, kāl kiḷarntu niṟiyaḻi tōṟṟamoṭu toṭara - kāṟṟip pōla eḻuntu kāvalik kaṭanta tōṟṟattuṭaṉ toṭarntu vara ;
muṉṉuḷḷa vicēṭaṇaṅkaḷāl ‘nīlayāṉi' eṉṟatu iravāyiṟṟu. irāttiriyi yāṉiyāka uruvakippavar ataṟ kiyipupaṭap piṟavaṟṟiyum uruvakañ ceykiṉṟār. "ñālam āriruḷ uṇṇa' eṉpatu 1"uṇṭaṟkuriya vallāp poruḷi, yuṇṭaṉa pōlak kūṟalumarapē" eṉpataṉāṉ amikkappaṭum. kolkaḷiṟṟiṉ celkiyi aṟivittaṟkup paṟiyaṟivit taluṇmiyiṉ 'paṇiyeḻuntārppa' eṉṟār. 2"verūup paṟi nuvalum parūup perun taṭakki, veruvaru celaviṉ vekuḷi vēḻam" eṉpatu kāṇka; veṉṟi muracuṅ koḷka. mēlōriṉṟi eṉṟatu pākari vīci yeṉṟapaṭi; 3"mēlōr vīci" eṉṟār piṟa cāṉṟōrum, kāmar - kāmam eṉṉum poruṭṭu. kaṭakkaḷiṟu kivīcic cellumākaliṉ ' kāmar ceṅki nīṭṭi' eṉṟār. pū - vēṅkippū ; ēḻilip pāliyiṉ malarumām; yāṉiyiṉ matam. appūkkaḷiṉ maṇamuṭiyatātali, 4"vilakkaruṅ karimatam vēṅki nāṟuva" 5"pātta yāṉiyiṟ pataṅkaḷiṟ paṭumata nāṟak, kātta vaṅkuca nimirntiṭak kālpiṭit tōṭip, pūtta vēḻilip pāliyip poṭip poṭi yākak, kātti raṅkaḷāṟ ṟalat toṭun tēytta tōr kaḷiṟu" eṉpavaṟṟāṉaṟika. 6"kālkiḷarn taṉṉa vēḻamēl koṇṭu" eṉpataṉāl yāṉi kāṟṟuppōṟ kiḷarnteḻu meṉpataṟika.
peyarvōṉṟaṉṉi nīlayāṉi toṭara veṉka : iravu vara eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu.
23-28. 	muṟimiyiṉ nakara nampiyar vaḷiyōr tammuṭaṉ - naka rattiluḷḷa kātalañcelvar makaḷir tammōṭu muṟimi yāka, makara vīṇiyiṉ kiḷi narampu vaṭitta iḷipuṇar iṉcīr eḵku uḷam kiḻippa - makarayāḻiṉ kiḷinarampu kaḷit teṟitta iciyuṭaṉ cērnta iṉiya tāḷa voṟṟākiya vēl uḷattikkiḻippa, poṟāa neñcil pukieri potti-poṟukkalāṟṟāta uḷḷattiṉkaṇ pukikiṉṟa kāmattīyāṉatu mūṭṭappaṭṭu, paṟāak kurukiṉ uyirttu avaṉ pōyapiṉ-kollaṉ uli mūkkiṉip pōla veytuyirttu iḷaṅkō ceṉṟapiṉṉar; 
vaḷiyōr-vaḷiyaṇinta makaḷir. nampiyarum vaḷiyōrum vaṭitta iḷipuṇar iṉcīr eṉka. makara vīṇi - makarayāḻ; nālvaki yāḻuḷoṉṟu; patiṉēḻu narampukaḷiyuṭiyatu. kiḷi - aintām narampu; uḻi caṭcamākaliṉ, caṭcakkiramattil ataṟkintāva tākiya kural kiḷiyākum; aṅṅaṉamē iḷi, tuttam, viḷari, kikkiḷi eṉpaṉa kural mutal oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu kiḷiyākum; ākaliṉ ivvintaṉiyumē kiḷinaram peṉṟalumuṇṭu; "kiḷiyeṉap paṭuva kiḷakkuṅ kālik, kuralē yiḷiyē tuttam viḷari, kikkiḷi yeṉavin tāku meṉpa" eṉṉum paḻiya nūṟpāvuṅ kāṇka. vaṭittal - narampi uruvutal; teṟittaluk kāyiṟṟu. iḷiyeṉpatu ēḻunarampiṉuḷ oṉṟaṉ peyarātalaṉṟi, iciyik kuṟikkum potup peyarumām, cīruṭaṉkūṭiya iḷiyākiya eḵku eṉka. paṟāak kuruku - kollaṉ ulimūkku; pūvā vañci mutaliyaṉa pōla veḷippaṭiyeṉṉum
ilakkaṇattatu. avaṉ - utayakumaraṉ. 
29-35. 	uṟiyuṭ kuṭiki uḷvarik koṇṭa - uṟiviṭa
mākiya ciṟiya kōyiliṉkaṇ vēṟṟuruva meytiya, maṟuvil ceyki maṇimēkalitāṉ - kuṟṟamaṟṟa ceykiyiyuṭiya maṇimēkali, mātavi makaḷāy maṉṟam tiritariṉ-mātaviyiṉ makaḷākavē ulaka aṟaviyiṟ cuṟṟikkoṇṭiruntāl, kāvalaṉ makaṉō kiviṭalī yāṉ - maṉṉavaṉ putalvaṉ kiviṭāṉ eṉa niṉintu, kāypaci yāṭṭi kāyacaṇṭiki eṉa ūr muḻutaṟiyum uruvaṅkoṇṭē - ūriluḷḷōr aṉivarālum naṉkaṟiyappaṭṭa yāṉittī nōyiṉiyuṭiya kāyacaṇṭikiyip pōla uruvaṅkoṇṭu, āṟṟā mākkaṭku āṟṟum tuṇiyāki-vaṟiyōrkku utavipuriyum aruntuṇiyāki ; 
uḷvari - vēṟṟuru; poyvēṭam. viṭalīyāṉ: viṉittiri col. kiviṭēṉeṉa niṉintu eṉa oru col varuvitturikka.
36-41.	ēṟṟalum iṭutalum irappōr kaṭaṉ - vāṅkutalum koṭuttalum irappōr kaṭaṉām ākaliṉ, avar mēṟ ceṉṟu aḷittal viḻuttakittu eṉṟē - avar ev viṭattum valintu ceṉṟu koṭuttal ciṟappuṭiyatu eṉṟu, nūṟporuḷ uṇarntōr nuṉittaṉarāmeṉa-nūṟporuḷkaḷi aṟintōr ārāynturittaṉar eṉṟu karuti, mutiyāḷ kōṭṭattu akavayiṉ irunta - campāpati kōyiliṉ uḷḷē irunta, amutacurapiyi aṅkiyiṉ vāṅki - amutacurapiyi aḻakiya kiyiṟkoṇṭu, patiyakam tiritarum pintoṭi naṅki - nakarattiṉkaṇṇē cuṟṟukiṉṟa paciya vaḷiyalkaḷi yaṇinta maṇimēkali; 
mēṟceṉṟu - valitiṟ ceṉṟu. nuṉittal - nuṇṇitiṉ uṇartal. eṉa - eṉṟu karuti. pintoṭi maṭanti eṉpatum pāṭam.
42-46.	atirkaḻal vēntaṉ aṭipiḻittāri - olikkiṉṟa vīrak kaḻaliyuṭiya maṉṉavaṉ aṭikkup piḻi ceytāri, oṟukkum taṇṭattu uṟuciṟik kōṭṭam - taṇṭikkum taṇṭattiṉiyuṭiya ciṟic cāliyiṉkaṇ, viruppoṭum pukuntu - viruppattuṭaṉ ceṉṟu, veytuyirttup pulampi - veytāka uyirttu varunti, āṅkup paciyuṟum āruyir mākkaḷi-avviṭattilē paciyuṟṟirukkum ariya uyiri yuṭiya makkaḷi, vāṅku kiyakam varunta niṉṟu ūṭṭalum- vāṅkukiṉṟa kiyiṉiṭam varuntumāṟu niṉṟu uṇpittalum;
iṟivaṉukkum periyārkkum piḻiceytārit tiruvaṭi piḻittāreṉṟu kūṟutal marapu. taṇṭattu uṟu - taṇṭat tiṉip porunti nukarum. paciyuṟṟu veytuyirttup pulampum mākkaḷeṉa māṟuka. āruyir mākkaḷ eṉṟatu, peṟutaṟkariya makkaḷuyirip peṟṟār iṅṅaṉam varuntukiṉṟaṉarē yeṉa iraṅkik kūṟiyapaṭi. īṇṭuk kūṟiyavāṟu "vāṅkunar kiyakam varuttuta lallatu" (11 : 49) "vāṅkuki varunta maṉṉuyi rōmpaliṉ" (14 : 23) "vāṅkuki varunta maṉṉuyirk kaḷittu" (17 : 5) eṉa muṉṉarp pōntamiyuṅ kāṇka.
47-50.	ūṭṭiya pāttiram oṉṟeṉa viyantu-iṅṅaṉam palariyum uṇpitta pāttiram oṉṟē eṉa viyappuṟṟu, kōṭṭaṅ kāvalar - ciṟikkōṭṭaṅ kāppavar, kōmakaṉ taṉakku - aracaṉukku, ip pāttira tāṉamum pintoṭi ceytiyum - ip pāttirattiṉ tāṉac ciṟappiyum itaṉik koṇṭa iḷaṅkoṭiyiṉ ceyaliyum, yāppuṭittāka icittum eṉṟu ēki - uṟuti yuṭittākak kūṟuvōm eṉac ceṉṟu ;
kōṭṭaṅ kāvalar viyantu tāṉamum ceytiyum kōmakaṉukku icittumeṉ ṟēki yeṉka,
51-56.	neṭiyōṉ kuṟaḷ uruvāki nimirntu taṉ aṭiyiṟ paṭiyi aṭakkiya-tirumāl vāmaṉa vaṭivaṅkoṇṭu piṉ pēruruvamāyp pūmiyit taṉ tiruvaṭiyiṉkaṇ aṭakka, annāḷ-aṟṟināḷil, nīriṟpeyta - pūmiyi nīruṭaṉ aḷitta, mūri vār cili māvali marumāṉ cīrkeḻu tirumakaḷ-valiya periya villiyuṭiya māvaliyiṉ vaḻittōṉṟalākiya aracaṉuṭiya pukaḻmikka tirumakaḷākiya, cīrtti eṉṉum tiruttaku tēviyoṭu - cīrtti eṉṉum peyaruṭiya celvamikka tēviyuṭaṉ, pōtu aviḻpūmpoḻil pukuntaṉaṉpukku - pūkkaḷ virinta aḻakiya cōliyiṟ ceṉṟu pukuntu ;  
kuṟaḷ - kuṟiya vaṭivamuṭiya vāmaṉaṉ ; kācipaṉukkum atitikkum piṟantavaṉ ; tirumāliṉ aintāvatu tōṟṟamāyavaṉ; ivaṉ māpaliyiṭattu mūṉṟaṭimaṇ irantu peṟṟu, puviyiyum vāṉiyum īraṭiyāl aḷantu, mūṉṟāmaṭiyip paliyiṉ taliyil vittu avaṉip pātalattil iruttiṉaṉ eṉpatu varalāṟu. aṭakkiya-aṭakka: ceyyiya veṉṉum viṉiyeccam; peyareccamākkōṭalumām. nīriṟ peyta-nīruṭaṉ peyta; tāri vārttaḷitta eṉṟapaṭi. māvali: māpaliyeṉpataṉ tiripu. pāṇa kulattaracarkaḷ māvaliyiṉ vaḻiyiṉareṉak kūṟappaṭuvar. iṉṉōr vāṇakappāṭi mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu aracupurin tōrāvar. ak kulattaracaruḷoruvaṉ makaḷeṉka.
57-60. 	kompart tumpi kuḻalici kāṭṭa - kompu kaḷiluḷḷa tumpikaḷ vēyṅkuḻa lōciyik kāṭṭa, poṅkar vaṇṭiṉam nalliyāḻceyya - cōliyiluḷḷa vaṇṭiṉaṅkaḷ nalla yāḻiṉi ciyi murala, varikkuyil pāṭa māmayil āṭum - varikaḷiyuṭiya kuyilkaḷ pāṭa aḻakiya mayilkaḷ āṭukiṉṟa, virippūm pantar kaṇṭu uḷam ciṟantum-maṇam poruntiya malarppantarik kaṇṭu maṉamakiḻcci mikkum;
vari - kīṟṟu; varippāṭṭumām. pantaril āṭutalik kaṇṭu eṉa māṟutalumām. ‘kuḻalici tumpi koḷuttik kāṭṭa, maḻali vaṇṭiṉam nalliyāḻ ceyya, veyiṉuḻi paṟiyāk kuyiṉuḻi potumpiṉ, mayilā ṭaraṅkiṉ' (4 : 3-6) eṉa muṉṉar vantiruttal aṟiyaṟ pālatu.
61-66.	puṇartuṇi nīṅkiya poyki aṉṉamoṭu - taṉ tuṇiyākiya pēṭṭiṉip pirinta kuḷattil vāḻum aṉṉappuḷḷuṭaṉ, maṭamayiṟ pēṭiyum tōkiyum kūṭi - maṭappam poruntiya mayiṟpēṭiyum āṇ mayilum cērntu, iruciṟi virittāṅku. eḻuntuṭaṉ koṭpaṉa-iraṇṭu ciṟakukaḷiyum virittu oruṅkē eḻuntu cuḻalvaṉavaṟṟi, oru ciṟik kaṇṭāṅku uḷmakiḻvu eyti - āṇṭu oru pakkattiṟ kaṇṭu maṉak kaḷippuṟṟu, mā maṇi vaṇṇaṉum tam muṉum piññiyum - nīla maṇipōṉṟa niṟamuṭiya kaṇṇaṉum avaṉ muṉṉavaṉākiya palatēvaṉum nappiṉṉiyum, āṭiya kuravi iḵtāmeṉa nōkkiyum - tuvarā patiyil erumaṉṟattē āṭiya kuraviyākum iḵtu eṉṟu nōkki viyantum;
veṇṇiṟa muṭimiyāl āṇ aṉṉattiṟkup pala tēvaṉum, karuniṟamuṭimiyāl āṇ mayilukkum peṇ mayilukkum muṟiyē kaṇṇaṉum nappiṉṉiyum uvamikaḷ. kuravi - viṉōtak kūttu  āṟiṉuḷ oṉṟu; kāmamum veṉṟiyum poruḷākak kuravic ceyyuḷ pāṭṭāka eḻuvarēṉum eṇmarēṉum oṉpatiṉmarēṉum kipiṇintāṭuvatu. kaṇṇaṉ mutaliyōr kuraviyāṭiyataṉi,
1"	māyavaṉṟam muṉṉiṉoṭum varivaḷikkip piṉṉiyoṭum 
kōvalartañ ciṟumiyarkaḷ kuḻaṟkōti puṟañcōra 
āyvaḷiccīrk kaṭipeyarttiṭ ṭacōtiyār toḻutēttat 
tāterumaṉ ṟattāṭuṅ kuraviyō takavuṭittē."
eṉpataṉāṉaṟika.
67-70. 	kōṅkalar cērnta māṅkaṉi taṉṉip pāṅkuṟa irunta palpoṟi maññiyi - kōṅkam pūviṉuṭaṉ cērntirunta māṅkaṉiyiṉ pakkattil irunta pala poṟikaḷiyuṭiya mayili, cempoṉ taṭṭil tīmpāl ēnti-civanta poṟṟaṭṭil iṉiya pāli ēntik koṇṭu, piṅkiḷi ūṭṭum ōr pāviyām eṉṟum - paciya kiḷiyi uṇpikkum oru aḻakiya peṇṇākum eṉṟum;
kōṅkalarkkup pāliyuṭiya poṟṟaṭṭum, māṅkaṉikkuk kiḷiyum, mayilukkup peṇṇum uvamikaḷ. māṅkaṉikkuk kiḷi vaṭivum vaṇṇamum paṟṟi uvamiyākum ; 1"vaṇṭaḷir mā attuk, kiḷi pōl kāya kiḷittuṇar" eṉpatuṅ kāṇka. maṟṟum, māvaṭuviṟkuk kiḷi uvamiyātalum, makaṭūu orutti poṉvaḷḷattilē pāliyēntik kiḷiyi uṇpippatumākiya karuttukkaḷ 2"cēṭiyal vaḷḷattup peytapāl cila kāṭṭi, ūṭumeṉ ciṟukiḷi yuṇarppavaḷ mukampōla,...kaṭikayat tāmarik kamaḻmuki karimāviṉ, vaṭitīṇṭa vāyviṭūum vayalaṇi nallūra" eṉṉuñ cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷil amin tiruttal kāṇka.
71-74. 	aṇimalarp pūmpoḻil akavayiṉ irunta - aḻakiya malarkaḷiyuṭiya pūñcōliyil uḷḷiṭattirunta, piṇavuk kuraṅku ēṟṟip perumatar maḻikkaṇ maṭavōrkku iyaṟṟiya māmaṇi ūcal - matartta nōkkamuṭiya maḻi pōlum periya kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya makaḷirukkuc ceyyappaṭṭa aḻakiya maṇikaḷāṉākiya ūcalil peṇ kuraṅkiṉi ēṟṟi, kaṭuvaṉ ūkkuvatu kaṇṭu naki eytiyum - āṇ kuraṅku āṭṭuvatu kaṇṭu makiḻcci yaṭintum;
piṇavuk kuraṅku - peṇ kuraṅku; 3"paṉṟi pulvāy nāyeṉa mūṉṟum, oṉṟiya veṉpa piṇaviṉ peyark koṭi" eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, 'oṉṟiya' eṉṟataṉāl, piṇavu eṉpatu kuraṅkukkum koḷḷappaṭṭatu. kaṭuvaṉ ūcalil piṇavuk kuraṅki yēṟṟi ūkkuvatu kaṇṭu nakiyeytiyum eṉka. 
75-78.	pācili ceṟinta pacuṅkāṟ kaḻiyoṭu - paciya ilikaḷ neruṅkiya paciya taṇṭiṉiyuṭiya mūṅkiluṭaṉ, vāl vīceṟinta marāam kaṇṭu-veḷḷiya pūkkaḷ neruṅkiya veṇkaṭampiṉik kaṇṇuṟṟu, neṭiyōṉ muṉṉoṭu niṉṟaṉaṉ āmeṉa - kaṇṇaṉ taṉ muṉṉōṉoṭu niṟ kiṉṟaṉaṉām eṉṟu, toṭicēr ceṅkiyil toḻutu niṉṟu ēttiyum-vīravaḷi yaṇinta civanta kikaḷāṟ kumpiṭṭu niṉṟu tutittum ;
muṉ - tamiyaṉ ; palatēvar. kaḻikkuk kaṇṇaṉum veṇ kaṭampiṟkup palatēvarum uvamikaḷ. veṇkaṭampiṟkup palatēvar uvamiyātal 1"orukuḻi yoruvaṉpō liṇarcērnta marāamum" 2nāñcil valava ṉiṟampōlap pūñciṉic, ceṅkāṉ marāan takintaṉa" eṉpavaṟṟuḷḷum kāṇappaṭumāṟaṟika.
79-92. 	āṭaṟ kūttiṉōṭu avinayam terivōr-kati taḻuvātu āṭutaliyuṭiya kūttiṉōṭu pāṭṭiṉ poruḷ tōṉṟak ki kāṭṭum avinayattiṉi aṟivōrum, nāṭakak kāppiya naṉṉūl nuṉippōr - nāṭakak kāppiyamākiya nalla nūlkaḷik kūrntu ārāyvōrum, paṇ yāḻ narampil paṇṇumuṟi niṟuppōr - paṇkaḷiyuṭiya yāḻ narampil paṇkaḷi muṟiyē niṟuttuvōrum, taṇṇumik karuvik kaṇ eṟi terivōr - tōṟ karuviyākiya mattaḷattiṉ mukattil aṭittali ārāyvōrum, kuḻaloṭu kaṇṭam koḷaccīr niṟuppōr-vēyṅ kuḻaluṭaṉ miṭaṟṟōciyum poruntum vaṇṇam tāḷavaṟuti ceyvōrum, paḻu niya paṭāl palaroṭu makiḻvōr-muṟṟup peṟṟa icip pāṭalkaḷip palaruṭaṉ pāṭi makiḻvōrum, āram parinta muttamkōp pōr-mālikaḷaṟupaṭutalāṟ cintiya muttuk kaḷik kōppōrum, īram pularnta cāntam timirvōr - īram ularnta cantaṉattit timirvōrum, kuṅkuma varuṇam koṅkiyiṉ iḻip pōr - civanta toyyiṟ kuḻampik koṅkikaḷil eḻutuvōrum, am ceṅkaḻunīr āyitaḻ piṇippōr-aḻakiya ceṅkaḻunīr malariṉ aḻakiya itaḻkaḷik kaṭṭuvōrum, nalneṭuṅ kūntal naṟuviri kuṭivōr - nalla nīṇṭa kuḻalil naṟumaṇaṅkaḷip pukutti appuvōrum, poṉṉiṉ āṭiyiṟ poruntupu niṟpōr -poṉṉālākiya cuṟṟuvaṭṭattiṉiyuṭiya kaṇṇāṭiyiṟ poruntik kaṇṭu niṟpōrum ākiya, āṅkavar tammōṭu - avarkaḷuṭaṉ, akaliru vāṉattu vēntaṉiṟ ceṉṟu viḷiyāṭṭu ayarntu - akaṉṟa periya viṇṇulakattu vēntaṉākiya intiraṉip pōlac ceṉṟu viḷiyāṭṭi virumpic ceytu; 
nāṭakam - kati taḻuvivaruṅ kūttu. nāṭakak kāppiyamākiya naṉṉūleṉka; iṉpat tuṟiyil niṟpavarākaliṉ nāṭakak kāppiyaṅ kūṟiṉār. muṉṉiṉṟa paṇ yāḻukku aṭi. yāḻ-pēriyāḻ mutaliya nālvaki yāḻ. narampu - kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi, uḻi, iḷi, viḷari, tāram eṉpaṉa. paṇ - pāli, kuṟiñci, marutam, cevvaḻi eṉṉum perumpaṇkaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ tiṟaṅkaḷum, avviru kūṟṟiṉ akanili, puṟanili, arukiyal, perukiyal eṉṉum iṉa vēṟupāṭukaḷum ākiya paṇkaḷ. eṟi: mutaṉilit toḻiṟ peyar; 1"paṇṇami muḻaviṉ kaṇ
ṇeṟiyaṟintu" eṉpatuṅ kāṇka. paḻuniya - ici muṟṟiya. timirtal-taṭavi yutirttal; kuṭivōr - tuvariṉum ōmālikiyiṉum ūṟiya naṉṉīrāl kūntali āṭṭuvōr eṉṟumām. avinayan terivōr mutalākiya avartammōṭu ceṉṟu viḷiyāṭṭa
yarntu eṉka.
93-99. 	kuruntum taḷavum tiruntu malarc ceruntiyum - kuruntum cemmulliyum aḻakiya pūkkaḷiyuṭiya ceruntiyum, murukuviri mulliyum karuviḷam poṅkarum - maṇam virikiṉṟa mulliyum karuviḷa maracceṟivum, poruntupu niṉṟu tiruntu nakiceytu- amarntiruntu iṉpa naki purintu, kuṟuṅkāl nakulamum neṭuñcevi muyalum - kuṟiya kālkaḷiyuṭiya kīriyiyum nīṇṭa kātukaḷiyuṭiya muyaliyum, piṟaḻntu pāy māṉum iṟumpu akalā veṟiyum - tuḷḷik kutikkiṉṟa māṉiyum kuṟuṅkāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu nīṅkāta āṭṭiyum, vammeṉak kūuy makiḻtuṇiyoṭu taṉ cemmalarc ceṅki kāṭṭupu niṉṟu - vārumeṉa aḻittup peruntēviyuṭaṉ taṉ civanta malar pōṉṟa cevviya kiyāṟ kāṭṭi niṉṟu;
cerunti, karuviḷi yeṉpaṉa mara vēṟupāṭukaḷ. poṅkar- cōli; cōli pōlum marac ceṟivu. iṟumpu - kuṟuṅkāṭu. veṟi- āṭu. makiḻtuṇi - irācamātēvi. makiḻtuṇiyoṭu poruntupu niṉṟu nakiceytu vammeṉak kūuyc ceṅki kāṭṭupu niṉṟu eṉka.
100-106.	maṉṉavaṉtāṉum malarkkaṇi mintaṉum - aracar perumāṉum malarākiya ampukaḷiyuṭiya kāmavēḷum, iṉṉiḷa vēṉilum iḷaṅkāṟ celvaṉum - iṉpam payakkum iḷavēṉilum teṉṟaṟ kāṟṟākiya celvaṉum, entirak kiṇaṟum iṭuṅkaṟ kuṉṟamum - nīri niṟikka vēṇṭumāyiṉ niṟittup pōkka vēṇṭumāyiṟ pōkku taṟkuriya entiram aminta kiṇaṟumcey kuṉṟum, vantu vīḻ aruviyum malarppūmpantarum - vantu viḻukiṉṟa aruviyum malarkaḷākiya aḻakiya pantarum, parappunīrp poykiyum karappu nīrkkēṇiyum - nīrpparappiṉiyuṭiya vāviyum maṟintirukkiṉṟa nīriyuṭiya kiṇaṟum, oḷittu uṟi iṭaṅkaḷum paḷikkaṟip paḷḷiyum - oḷintu vāḻkiṉṟa maṟiviṭaṅkaḷum paḷikkaṟiyākiya iṭamum ākiya, yāṅkaṇum tirintu tāḻntu viḷiyāṭi- evviṭattum tirintu taṅki viḷiyāṭi ;
iḷavēṉil - cittiriyum vikāciyumākiya iru tiṅkaḷkaḷ. iḷaṅkāl-iḷan teṉṟaṟkāṟṟu, mantamārutam. iḷavēṉiṟ kālattilē iḷan teṉṟal vīcāniṟkak kāmavēṭki viñca maṉṉavaṉ viḷiyāṭiṉāṉ eṉpār, 'maṉṉavaṉṟāṉum mintaṉum vēṉilum celvaṉum viḷiyāṭi' eṉṟār. 1"antak kēṇiyu mentirak kiṇaṟum, taṇpūṅ kāvun talittōṉ ṟaruviya, veṇcutik kuṉṟoṭu vēṇṭuva piṟavum, iḷiyōrk kiyaṟṟiya viḷiyāṭ ṭiṭatta, cittirap pūmi vittaka nōkki" eṉa varuvatu īṇṭu aṟiyaṟpālatu.
107-116.	makataviṉiñarum marāṭṭak kammarum-makata nāṭṭiṟ piṟanta maṇi vēlikkārarum makārāṭṭirattiṟ piṟanta poṟkammiyarum, avantikkollarum yavaṉattaccarum-avantināṭṭuk kollarum yavaṉanāṭṭut taccarum, taṇṭamiḻ viṉiñar tammoṭu kūṭi-taṇṇiya tamiḻnāṭṭut tōṉṟiya toḻil vallāruṭaṉ kūṭi, koṇṭiṉitu iyaṟṟiya kaṇkavar ceyviṉi-uḷḷaṅ koṇṭu naṉku ceytamitta kaṇkaḷik kavarum toḻiṟ ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya, pavaḷat tiraḷkāl paṉmaṇip pōtiki - pavaḷattāṟ camitta tiraṭciyuṭiya tūṇkaḷum palvaki maṇikaḷālākiya pōtikik kaṭṭikaḷum, tavaḷa nittilat tāmam tāḻnta-veḷḷiya muttumālikaḷ toṅkaviṭappaṭṭa, kōṇac canti māṇviṉi vitāṉattu - kōṇamākiya cantiṉi yuṭiya māṭcimippaṭṭa toḻilaminta mēṟkaṭṭiyum aminta, tamaṉiyam vēynta vakipeṟu vaṉappiṉ-poṉṉāl vēyappeṟṟa vakiyaminta aḻakiṉiyuṭiya, piñcēṟu meḻukāp pacum poṉ maṇṭapattu-paciya cāṇattāṉ meḻukappaṭāta pacum poṉ maṇṭapattiṉkaṇ, intira tiruvaṉ ceṉṟu iṉitu ēṟalum - intirac celvat tiṉiyuṭiya maṉṉavaṉ iṉitu ceṉṟu ēṟutalum ;
marāṭṭam : makārāṭṭiram eṉpataṉ marūu. yavaṉam eṉpatu parata kaṇṭattiṉ puṟattatākiya ōr nāṭu. iṅṅaṉam ovvoru nāṭṭu viṉiñar ovvoru toḻiliṟ ciṟantiruntaṉa reṉpatu, 1"yavaṉat taccarumavantik kollarum, makatattup piṟanta maṇiviṉik kārarum, pāṭalip piṟanta pacumpoṉ viṉiñarum, kōcalat tiyaṉṟa vōviyat toḻilarum, vatta nāṭṭu vaṇṇak kammarum" eṉpataṉālum aṟiyappaṭum. tamiḻ - tamiḻ nāṭu; tamiḻ viṉiñar eṉap potup paṭak kūṟiṉamiyiṉ avar pala toḻiliṉum vallunarātal peṟṟām. pōtiki-tūṇiṉ mēl uttaram mutaliyavaṟṟit tāṅkuṅ kaṭṭi; kōṇac canti - kūṭalvāy. piñcēṟu - kōmayam; āviṉcāṇam. 2"piñcēṟu meḻukiya naṉṉakar" eṉṟār piṟarum. poṉ maṇṭapa mākiyāṟ piñcēṟṟāl meḻukap paṭātāyiṟṟu; cantaṉattāl meḻukappaṭṭa teṉṉalumām; 3"maṅkala veḷḷi vaḻittu muttīrttapiṉ" eṉa varutal kāṇka. ceyviṉiyiyuṭiya maṇṭapam, kāl mutaliyavaṟṟiyuṭiya poṉ maṇṭapameṉka.
117-130. 	vāyilukku icittu maṉṉavaṉ aruḷāl - vāyil kāp pōrkku urittu aracaṉ paṇiyāl. cēynilattaṉṟiyum cevviyiṉ vaṇaṅki - tūramāṉa iṭattil tām niṉṟa taṉṟiyum muṟimiyāl vaṇakkañceytu, eñcā maṇ nacii ikaluḷam turappa - kuṟiyāta nilatti virumpi māṟupāṭāṉatu uḷḷattic celutta, vañciyiṉ iruntu vañci cūṭi-vañci nakarattiṉ kaṇṇiruntu vañcimāli cūṭi, muṟañcevi yāṉiyum tērum māvum-muṟam pōṉṟa kātukaḷiyuṭiya yāṉiyum tērum kutiriyum, maṟaṅkeḻu neṭuvāḷ vayavarum miṭinta-periya vāṭpaṭiyik kiyiṟkoṇṭa āṇmimikka vīrarum ceṟinta, talit tārc cēṉiyoṭu-mutaṉmiyākiya tūcip paṭiyōṭu, malittut talivantōr - porutaṟkuvanta iruperu vēntarākiya cērapāṇṭiyaruṭiya, cilikkayal neṭuṅ koṭi - villum kayalumākiya neṭiya koṭikaḷi, ceru vēl taṭakki ārpuṉi teriyal iḷaṅkōṉ taṉṉāl - pōrpuriyum vēṟpaṭiyik koṇṭa periya kikaḷi yuṭiya āttimalarāl toṭukkappaṭṭa kaṇṇiyiyuṭiya iḷaṅkōviṉāl, kāriyāṟṟuk koṇṭa - kāri yāṟṟiṉ kaṇ kippaṟṟiya’ kāval veṇkuṭi valikeḻu taṭakki māvaṇkiḷḷi - uyirkaḷik kākkum veṇ kuṭi yiṉiyum valimikka peruṅ kikaḷiyum uṭiya māvaṇ kiḷḷi, oḷiyoṭu vāḻi ūḻitō ṟūḻi - nī pallūḻi kālam oḷiyuṭaṉ vāḻ vāyāka, vāḻi eṅkō maṉṉavar peruntaki - em talivaṉākiya aracar peruntakiyē vāḻvāyāka, kēḷitu maṉṉō keṭukaniṉ pakiñar - niṉ pakiñar oḻika ! aracē itaṉik kēṭpāyāka;
(48) kōṭṭaṅ kāvalar (50) icittu meṉṟēki, (117) vāyilukkicittu, maṉṉavaṉaruḷāl (138) maṉṉava eṉṟalumeṉa muṭiyum ; maṉṉavaṉaruḷāl vaṇaṅki yeṉṟalumām. pakivar maṇṇiṉi virumpi vañcicūṭip pōrukkuc cellutal vañcit tiṇiyākum itaṉi, 1"eñcā maṇṇaci vēntaṉi vēntaṉ, añcutakat talicceṉ ṟaṭalkuṟit taṉṟē" eṉpataṉāṉaṟika. vañciyiṉiruntu vañci cūṭic cēṉiyoṭu talivantōr eṉka. malintu eṉpataṉi maliyaveṉat tirikka. iḷaṅkōṉ-tampiyākiya nalaṅkiḷḷi. māvaṇkiḷḷi : peyar; mikka vaṇmiyi yuṭiya kiḷḷi yeṉṟumām; ivaṉ kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ eṉappaṭu pavaṉ. kiḷḷi : viḷi. kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ pakivaṉākiya kāriyāṟṟut tuñciya neṭuṅkiḷḷiyiyum, avaṉukkut tuṇiyāka vantetirtta cēra pāṇṭiyarkaḷiyum taṉ tampiyākiya nalaṅkiḷḷiyāl kāriyāṟu eṉṉum yāṟṟiṉ pāṅkar veṉṟaṉa ṉeṉka. kāriyāṟu ōr nati eṉpataṉi, 2"celkatimuṉ ṉaḷippārtan tirukkārik karipaṇintu" eṉpataṉāṉaṟika, oḷi - kaṭavuṭṭaṉmi; 3"uṟaṅku māyiṉu maṉṉavaṉ ṟaṉṉoḷi, kaṟaṅku teṇṭiri viyakaṅ kākkumāl" eṉpatu kāṇka : parimēlaḻakarum im mēṟ kōḷ koṇṭē 4"iḷiyariṉamuṟiya reṉṟikaḻār niṉṟa, oḷiyō ṭoḻukap paṭum" eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷuriyil 'oḷi - uṟaṅkā niṟkavum ulakaṅ kākkiṉṟa avaratu kaṭavuṭṭaṉmi,' eṉṟurittār. maṉṉum ōvum acikaḷ.
131-138. 	yāṉittī nōykku ayarntu mey vāṭi im mānakart tiriyum ōr vampa mātar - yāṉittī eṉṉum pacinōyāl cōrntu uṭal vāṭṭamuṟṟu ip periya nakarattiṉkaṇ cuṟṟikkoṇṭirunta oru putiya maṅki, aruñciṟik kōṭṭattu akavayiṟ pukuntu - ariya ciṟiccāliyiṉ uḷḷē nuḻintu, perum peyar maṉṉa niṉpeyar vāḻtti - periya pukaḻuṭiya aracē niṉatu peyari vāḻtti, aiyap pāttiram oṉṟukoṇṭu āṅku moykoḷ mākkaḷ mocikka ūṇ curantaṉaḷ - orē piccip pāttirattik koṇṭu aṅku neruṅkutaliyuṭiya makkaḷ aṉivarum uṇṇumāṟu uṇaviṉic curantaḷittaṉaḷ, ūḻitōṟūḻi ulakam kāttu vāḻi eṅkō maṉṉava eṉṟalum- ūḻiyūḻiyaḷavum ulakiṉik kāttu em talivaṉākiya aracē vāḻvāyāka eṉa vurittalum;
nōykku ayarntu - nōyāl ayarntu. vampu - putumi; īṟu tirintatu. perumpeyar-mikka pukaḻ. mocikka-uṇṇa; 1"miyūṉ mocitta vokkaloṭu" eṉpatu kāṇka.
139-145.	varuka varuka maṭakkoṭitāṉ eṉṟu aruḷpuri neñca moṭu aracaṉ kūṟaliṉ - aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ av viḷaṅkoṭi īṇṭu varuka varuka eṉṟu aracaṉ aruṇmikunta uḷatti ṉōṭum urittaliṉ, vāyilāḷariṉ maṭakkoṭitāṉ ceṉṟu- vāyil kāvalarāl maṇimēkali aracaṉmuṉ ceṉṟu, āykaḻal vēntaṉ aruḷ vāḻiya eṉa - ārāyntu vīrak kaḻali yaṇinta maṉṉavaṉatu aruḷ vāḻka eṉṟu kūṟa, tāṅkarum taṉmit tavattōy nī yār - poṟuttaṟkariya taṉmikaḷiyuṭiya tavattiṉi yuṭiyōy nī yār, yāṅ kākiyatu iv vēntiya kaṭiñi eṉṟu - niṉ kiyilēntiya ip pāttiram yāṇṭuk kiṭittatu eṉṟu, aracaṉ kūṟalum - vēntaṉ viṉavutalum, āyiḻi urikkum - avaḷ kūṟuvāḷ;
aṭukku virivu paṟṟiyatu. aḻittu varuka veṉṟu vāyilāḷarkkuk kūṟa veṉka. tāṅkarun taṉmi-piṟarāṟ poṟuttaṟkariya taṉmi.
146-154.	virittār vēntē nī nīṭuvāḻi - maṇam poruntiya māliyi yaṇinta maṉṉava! nī nīṭūḻi vāḻvāyāka, viñci makaḷ yāṉ - yāṉ ōr vittiyātara makaḷ, viḻavaṇi mūtūr vañcan tirintēṉ - viḻāvālākiya eḻil niṟinta it toṉṉakaril vañcattāl tirintēṉ, vāḻiya peruntaki - aracar peruntaki vāḻka, vāṉam vāykka - maḻi tavaṟātu poḻika, maṇvaḷam perukuka - puviyil vaḷamperukuka, tītiṉṟāka kōmakaṟku - aracaṉukkut tītīṉṟi naṉmi yuṇṭākuka, īṅkitu aiyak kaṭiñi-iḵtu ōr piccip pāttiram, ampala maruṅku ōr teyvam tantatu - ulaka vaṟaviyiluḷḷa oru teyvattāṟṟarap paṭṭatu, tippiya māyatu - teyvattaṉmi yuṭiyatu, yāṉittī nōy arumpaci keṭuttatu - yāṉittī yeṉṉum pōkkutaṟ kariya pacinōyip pōkkiyatu, ūṉ uṭi mākkaṭku uyir maruntu itu eṉa-iḵtu uṭampu melinta makkaḷukku uyiri niṟuttum maruntāvatu eṉṟu kūṟa ;
viñci makaḷ eṉpataṟku mantirattāl urumāṟiya makaḷ eṉṟu maṇimēkalik kēṟpap poruḷkoḷka. vañcat tirintēṉ eṉpataṟkum eṉatu tīviṉiyāl tirintēṉ eṉṟum, vēṟṟurukkoṇṭu tirintēṉ eṉṟum iruporuḷ koḷka. uṭi: viṉittoki; uṭiyaveṉṟumām; piṟitoṉṟu millāta veṉṟapaṭi
155-158.	yāṉ ceyaṟpālatu eṉiḷaṅkoṭikku eṉṟu vēntaṉ kūṟa - aracaṉ yāṉ niṉakkuc ceyyavēṇṭiyatu yātu eṉa viṉava, melliyal urikkum - avaḷ kūṟuvāḷ, ciṟiyōr kōṭṭam cīttu aruḷ neñcattu aṟavōrkku ākkum atu vāḻiyar eṉa - ciṟiccāliyi aḻittu aruḷ uḷḷamuṭiya aṟavōr vāḻuṅ kōṭṭamākkum ac ceyalē vāḻvāyāka eṉṟurikka ; 
iḷaṅkoṭikku muṉṉiliyiṟ paṭarkki. ākkumatu nī ceyaṟ pālateṉa eṉka.
159-162. 	aruñciṟi viṭṭu āṅku āyiḻi uritta peruntavar tammāl perumporuḷ eyta - ariya ciṟiyiluḷḷōri viṭuttu avviṭattil maṇimēkali kūṟiya perun tavamuṭiyōr kaḷāl perum poruḷi eytumāṟu, kaṟiyōr illāc ciṟiyōr kōṭṭam aṟavōrkku ākkiṉaṉ aracāḷ vēntu eṉ-kaṟippaṭṭōrillāta ciṟikkōṭṭatti aṟakkōṭṭa mākkiṉāṉ aracupuriyum vēntaṉ eṉka.
perum poruḷ - aṟam ; ñāṉamumām. kaṟiyōr - kaṭami (iṟi) celutta vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ. kaṟiyōrillā eṉṟamiyāl aracaṉ kaṟi vīṭu ceytami peṟṟām. ceṅkōl vēntar kaṟivīṭu ceyvarātali. 1"ciṟippaṭu kōṭṭañ cīmiṉ yāvatuṅ, kaṟippaṭu mākkaḷ kaṟivīṭu ceymmiṉ......kōṉmuṟi yaṟinta koṟṟa vēntaṉ" 1"ciṟiyōr kōṭṭañ cīmiṉ yāṅkaṇuṅ, kaṟikeḻu nāṭu kaṟivīṭu ceymmeṉa" eṉpavaṟṟāṉaṟika. ciṟiyōr kōṭṭam - ciṟik kōṭṭam.
tārōṉ vaṇaṅki vañciṉaṅkūṟat teyvaṅ kūṟalum avaṉ uḷḷaṅ kalaṅki varunti aṟivāmeṉap peyarvōṉṟaṉṉi (iravākiya) nīlayāṉi toṭara eḵku uḷaṅkiḻippa avaṉ potti uyirttup pōyapiṉ, maṇimēkali nuṉittaṉareṉṟu karuti vāṅkik kōṭṭam pukuntu mākkaḷi ūṭṭalum, kāvalar viyantu kōmakaṉukku icittumeṉṟēki, tiruvaṉ ceṉṟu ēṟalum, vāyilukkicittu vaṇaṅki, ‘maṉṉava, vampamātar ūṇ curantaṉaḷ' eṉṟalum, ‘varuka varuka eṉṟu aracaṉ kūṟaliṉ, maṭakkoṭi vāyilāḷariṟ ceṉṟu, 'niṉ aruḷ vāḻiya' eṉa 'yāṅkākiyatu" eṉṟu aracaṉ kūṟalum, āyiḻi urippavaḷ ‘uyir maruntu itu' eṉa, ‘yāṉ ceyaṟpālatu eṉ' eṉṟu vēntaṉ kūṟa, melliyal urippavaḷ, ‘nī ceyaṟpālatu aṟavōrkku ākkumatu' eṉa, vēntu perumporu ḷeytutaṟkuk kōṭṭatti aṟavōrkku ākkiṉaṉ eṉa viṉi muṭivu ceyka. 
ciṟikkōṭṭam aṟakkōṭṭamākkiya  kāti muṟṟiṟṟu.

20. utayakumaraṉi vāḷāleṟinta kāti  
(aracaṉ paṇiyāl ciṟiccāli palavaki aṟaṅkaḷum nikaḻtaṟkuriya cāliyāy viḷaṅkiṟṟu. utayakumaraṉ in nikaḻcci
kaḷik kēṭṭu, "maṇimēkali ulakavaṟaviyi nīṅki veḷiyē vantapoḻutu, avaḷik kippaṟṟik koṇarntu tērilēṟṟi, avaḷ kaṟṟa viñcikaḷiyum avaḷ kūṟum mutumoḻikaḷiyum kēṭpēṉ" eṉṟu taṉṉuḷḷē eṇṇikkoṇṭu ceṉṟu avaḷirukkum ulakavaṟaviyil ēṟiṉaṉ. kāñcaṉaṉeṉṉum viñciyaṉ, "kāyacaṇṭikikku viruccika muṉivaṉ iṭṭa cāpatti avaḷ nukartaṟkuriya paṉṉīrāṇṭum ceṉṟaṉa; avaḷ iṉṉum vārāmikkuk kāraṇam yātō" eṉṟu mikka kavali yuṟṟu, taṉatu patiyi nīṅki vicumpiṉ vaḻiyē vantu kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉattil iṟaṅkip pūtacatukkam, pūñcōlikaḷ, mātavariṭaṅkaḷ, maṉṟaṅkaḷ, potiyilkaḷ ākiya iṭantōṟum ceṉṟu ceṉṟu tēṭit tirintu, kāyacaṇṭiki vēṭam pūṇṭu uṇavaḷittu māntar palaruṭiya paciyiyum māṟṟik koṇṭirukkum maṇimēkaliyik kaṇṭu, avaḷik kāyacaṇṭiki yeṉṟē tuṇintu arukiṟ ceṉṟu avaḷi nōkki, "niṉ kiyil ēntiya pāttiram oṉṟēyāyiṉum uṇpōr palarāka vuḷḷaṉar ; uṉṉi varuttiya yāṉit tīyākiya nōyi oḻittaṟkut tēvarkaḷ itaṉi aḷittār kaḷō?" eṉṟu kūṟip, piṉpu, paḻiya naṭpiṉip pulappaṭuttum moḻikaḷ palavaṟṟic collip pārāṭṭavum, avaḷ avaṟṟic ciṟitum matiyāmal avaṉi nīṅki utayakumaraṉi aṭintu avaṉarukē niṉṟu, iḷamiyiṉ niliyillāmiyi avaṉukku aṟivuṟutta niṉintu, aṅkē iyalpāka vanta narimūtāṭṭi yoruttiyik kāṭṭi, muṉpu vaṉappuṭiyaṉavāyirunta avaḷuṭiya uṟuppukkaḷ palavum iyalpu tirintu aḻaku keṭṭu veṟukkat takkaṉavā yiruttali naṉku pulappaṭutti,
	"	pūviṉuñ cāntiṉum pulālmaṟit tiyāttut tūciṉu maṇiyiṉum tollōr vakutta
vañcan teriyāy maṉṉavaṉ makaṉē"
